<!-- User intent: the step-by-step build of the 2026-10-11 mods UX overhaul (band, Session tab, phase strip, review list, handovers, restore) so fresh executors can ship it stage by stage with a live check after each. -->

# Taskmaster mods UX overhaul Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Bring every taskmaster-mods surface to cm-restore's bar in the Reality Reprojection design system: a 2-line band on its own ground plus a Session tab, a shared two-way phase strip, review as a paged card list with bulk actions, handovers with phases, and a Taskmaster-tuned restore pane.

**Architecture:** rr-tui gains two static recipes (`badgeProps`, `tint`); taskmaster-mods gains an interactive kit (`hooks/kit.tsx`) and a pure phase model (`hooks/phases.ts`) that every pane draws with. Each surface lives in its own file (`band.tsx`, `session.tsx`, `review.tsx`, `handovers.tsx`, `restore/*`); `draw.tsx` is emptied and removed by Stage 4. Restore reads transcripts through a Python scanner (facts only) and resolves status, tasks and phases from tm with one `backlog_query`.

**Tech Stack:** Claude Code mods API (TSX hooks modules, `claude plugin test` / `validate --strict`, `tsc`), `$.mcp.call('plugin:taskmaster:tm', …)`, Python 3 + pytest for the scanner.

**Spec:** `docs/specs/2026-10-11-mods-ux-design.md` (read it with this plan; §14 records probe outcomes and the key-map deviation).

**How this plan was verified:** every stage was replayed task by task on copies of both mods, cumulatively: Stages 0–1 → 2 → 3 → 4 → 5 applied in order on one copy end green (taskmaster-mods 247 → 477 tests, rr-tui 24 → 27, scanner pytest 11; `validate --strict` and `tsc` clean after every task; `claude -p` smoke ok). Code blocks were rendered from the replayed files. Apply tasks **in order**: later anchors quote lines earlier tasks add. Existing files are CRLF; new files may be LF. Under machine load `claude plugin test` can hit random 5 s timeouts — re-run once before investigating.

## Global Constraints

- Design: Reality Reprojection via `$.rr` only; no hard-coded hex in taskmaster-mods.
- No coloured left rails, no motion on hover (colour/border/background only), no box-shadows; surface stepping (page < raised < overlay).
- No Taskmaster server, schema or skill change; data only via `plugin:taskmaster:tm` tools (`backlog_query` for joins; result cells are cut at 80 chars).
- Do not touch `hooks/handover-guard.ts` (owned by a parallel session); rebase onto its commits rather than overwrite.
- Every new file starts with a 1–3 line `// User intent: …` header.
- Mods validator rules: never name a variable `h` in .tsx; functions taking `$` declared at file top; every `$.state` key declared in `types/index.d.ts` `PluginState`; atom/update/read sources are consts in the same file; `$` never crosses an import.
- Hotkeys must match `/^[a-z0-9]$/` (engine refuses others). Key map: band/panes `1` review, `2` handovers, `3` session, `4` restore; panes `h`/`l` phase step, `f` phase overlay, `0`–`9` phase jump, `p`/`n` page, `u` refresh, `s` select, `j`/`k` move, `b`/`g` bulk primary/secondary; review `t`+digit ticks a check.
- Bump `DEMO_SEED` in `hooks/demo.ts` when demo data changes shape (Stage 2 → 5, Stage 4 → 7, Stage 5 → 8; Stage 3 leaves it).
- No push and no install-copy sync without the user's sign-off. Each stage ends with a live check; stop there and wait.

## Review Focus

Failure modes the spec implies that unit tests can't fully pin (each names where it is exercised):

1. **Big real backlogs** (CodeMaestro: ~758 in-review tasks, 388 archived handovers) — phase/refs/handover-list queries must stay under tm's 3–5 s bound or fall back (handovers pane → the snapshot's 30 rows). Exercised only in the live checks 3.5, 4.6, 5.8 against CodeMaestro; treat a slow query there as a blocker, not a nit.
2. **Band keys while typing** — `c`, `d`, `x`, `1`–`4` must not fire while the user types a prompt (spec §14 item 3). Live check 2.7 tests it by hand; the harness cannot route keystrokes.
3. **Key collisions inside a pane** — confirm-row `n` vs pager `n`, tick-mode digits vs phase digits. Tests assert draw order and that no digit sits on two Buttons (3.3, Stage 1 confirmRow); real routing ("later wins") is checked live in 3.5.
4. **Launcher quoting** — cwd with spaces, quotes or `;` must survive `wt.exe`/pwsh `-EncodedCommand` and the POSIX/AppleScript paths. Covered by the ported quoting tests in 2.1; the real `claude --resume` keeping its session id is confirmed live in 5.8.
5. **Scanner on odd transcripts** — truncated last line, non-UTF-8 bytes, a session whose cwd is a worktree, `claude -p` runs. Pinned in 5.1: `-p` and worktree fixtures, plus `test_a_truncated_last_line_and_bad_bytes_are_skipped` (the scanner reads with `errors='replace'` and skips undecodable lines).

Known nit, deliberately left: the dimmed bulk keycap toasts "Nothing selected to done" (kit composes `to <verb>`); reword in a follow-up so the verified tests stay byte-identical.

## Stage 0: Setup and probes

**Shell and paths for every task in this plan.** Commands are written for Git Bash, with forward-slash absolute paths. Never `cd`. Each Bash call starts with:

```bash
WT=C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/taskmaster/.worktrees/mods-ux
```

`$WT/mods/taskmaster-mods` is the mod most tasks touch; `$WT/mods/rr-tui` is the RR noun.

**Mod checks**, run per mod after every implementation step:

```bash
WT=C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/taskmaster/.worktrees/mods-ux
claude plugin test $WT/mods/<mod>
claude plugin validate --strict $WT/mods/<mod>
npx -y -p typescript@5.6.3 tsc -p $WT/mods/<mod>
```

`tsc` needs the engine-laid types in `<mod>/.claude-plugin/types/`. These are gitignored, so a new worktree has none, and they must be laid again after `rr-tui/types/index.d.ts` changes. Lay them with:

```bash
WT=C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/taskmaster/.worktrees/mods-ux
env -u ANTHROPIC_API_KEY claude -p --model haiku --plugin-dir $WT/mods/rr-tui --plugin-dir $WT/mods/taskmaster-mods "Reply with the single word ok."
```

Always run `claude -p` this way: `env -u ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` and `--model haiku`. A global API key would otherwise bill the run to the API.

**Verified while writing this plan (2026-10-11, `claude plugin test` on a throwaway mod).** These facts shape the key map below:

- A Button `hotkey` must be one digit or one lowercase letter. A hotkey of `"["`, `"/"`, `"R"` or `" "` fails the render hook (`$.ui.mount: no implementation for ui.render`). Shift+r arrives as `r`.
- A plain Button with `hotkey: 'd'` and `label: ''` is accepted.
- A `position: "absolute"` Box beside a Button validates on both the terminal and the desktop.
- A test can build element trees outside a render: `h('Button', props, ...children)` with a tag name gives the engine's own plain data. `hover` is hoisted out of `props`, `onPress` becomes a `press` handle and `null` children are dropped.

**Key map deviation (recorded in spec §14 by Task 0.2).** The brief's `[` / `]`, `/`, `R` and space cannot be hotkeys. The d.ts also says arrows scroll a focused Pane and Tab walks its Buttons. Every stage therefore uses:

| Spec / brief | This plan | Why |
|---|---|---|
| `[` / `]` phase step | `h` / `l` | letters only |
| `/` phase overlay | `f` (also on the `+N ▾` tab) | letters only; `f` was the old phase key |
| `R` refresh | `u` | `R` arrives as `r`, which clashes with handovers' `r` resume |
| space select | `s` on the focused card | letters only; review's `s` skip is removed (spec §8) |
| ↑↓ move | `j` / `k`, plus the Tab ring via a `ui.focus` hook | arrows scroll a pane |
| `0` All, `1`–`9` tabs, `p` / `n` page, `y` / `n` confirm | unchanged | already valid |

`kit.keycap` throws on any other hotkey, so a bad key fails in a test, never live.

### Task 0.2: Probe the open items (spec §14) and record the answers

The orchestrator runs this task with the user in the loop: items 2, 4 and 5 are judged by eye. Every answer is written into spec §14, and the switch it sets is a named const that Stage 1 code reads. Either outcome of each probe has a coded path below, so later stages proceed either way.

**Files:**
- Create (throwaway, outside the repo, never committed): `C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/dev-mods/mods-ux-probe/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, `.../hooks/hooks.json`, `.../hooks/register.tsx`
- Modify: `$WT/docs/specs/2026-10-11-mods-ux-design.md` §14 (lines 257–262; append items 5 and 6)

**Interfaces:** Consumes Task 0.1's worktree. Produces the answers that set four consts, which Tasks 1.2 and 1.6 create with the defaults shown:

| Spec §14 item | Const (file) | Default in code | Set to the other value when |
|---|---|---|---|
| 2 overlay placement | `OVERLAY_PLACEMENT: 'swap' \| 'absolute'` (kit.tsx) | `'swap'` | the absolute overlay paints cleanly over the cards **and** Tab stays inside it |
| 2 overlay list | `OVERLAY_LIST: 'buttons' \| 'select'` (kit.tsx) | `'buttons'` | `Select` draws as a vertical list that ↑↓ move and Enter picks |
| 4 panel title | `PANEL_TITLE: { terminal, desktop }` (kit.tsx) | `{ terminal: 'border', desktop: 'row' }` | terminal → `'row'` if the border title lands off the border row; desktop → `'border'` if the user confirms it renders there |
| 5 keycap face | `KEYCAP_FACE: 'overlay' \| 'accent'` (kit.tsx) | `'overlay'` | `'accent'` if the overlay keycap does not cover the engine's `k: ` cleanly |

Item 1 sets no const. If `task_ids` is missing from the handover list, Stage 4 joins through `backlog_query` (`handover_tasks`) instead of the list reply. The phase counts never depend on it: they come from Task 1.5's SQL.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the probe mod**

`C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/dev-mods/mods-ux-probe/.claude-plugin/plugin.json`:

```json
{ "name": "mods-ux-probe", "version": "0.0.1", "description": "Throwaway probes for the mods UX overhaul (plan Task 0.2); delete after.", "author": { "name": "gruku" } }
```

`C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/dev-mods/mods-ux-probe/hooks/hooks.json`:

```json
{ "modules": ["./register.tsx"] }
```

`C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/dev-mods/mods-ux-probe/hooks/register.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: throwaway probes for the mods UX overhaul (plan Task 0.2) — overlay placement, arrows over Buttons, Select's
// look, a title set into a border, and the keycap face — judged by eye, then deleted. Hex here is a probe's, never shipped.
import type { Elements, Register } from 'claude-code'

const C = { page: '#141413', raised: '#1d1d1b', overlay: '#272725', border: '#4d4c48', sig: '#8a9eeb', cap: '#8a9eeb', ink: '#0d0d0c', bold: '#f5f3ed', ok: '#3a9a5b' }
const PROBES = ['probe-overlay', 'probe-swap', 'probe-select', 'probe-panel', 'probe-keycap']
const PICKS = ['All 31', 'patch-1-9 · active 12', 'release-2 4', 'patch-1-8 ✓ 0', 'no task 3']

export const register: Register = on => {
  on('session.start', async ($, e, next) => {
    for (const name of PROBES) {
      try {
        await $.command.register({ name, description: `mods-ux probe: ${name}` })
      } catch {
        // registered by an earlier load
      }
    }
    return next(e)
  })

  for (const id of PROBES) {
    on('command.run', { command: id }, async $ => {
      await $.ui.open({ id, title: id, focus: true, closeOnEscape: true })
      return { text: `${id} opened` }
    })
  }

  on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: 'probe-overlay' }, async ($, e) => {
    const { Box, Text, Button } = $.ui.resolve(e)
    const cards = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6].map(n => (
      <Box key={`card-${n}-box`} flexDirection="row" backgroundColor={C.raised} paddingX={1}>
        <Button key={`card-${n}`} plain label={`card ${n}: a row the overlay covers`} onPress={() => $.ui.toast(`card ${n}`)} />
      </Box>
    ))
    const picks = PICKS.map((label, i) => (
      <Box key={`pick-${i}-box`} flexDirection="row">
        <Button key={`pick-${i}`} plain hotkey={String(i)} label={label} {...(i === 1 ? { autoFocus: true as const } : {})} onPress={() => $.ui.toast(`picked ${label}`)} />
      </Box>
    ))
    return (
      <Box flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={C.page} flexGrow={1}>
        <Text color={C.sig}>STRIP · All 31 │ patch-1-9 12 │ +3 ▾</Text>
        <Box flexDirection="column">
          {cards}
          <Box position="absolute" top={0} left={2} flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={C.overlay} borderStyle="round" borderColor={C.border} paddingX={1}>
            {picks}
          </Box>
        </Box>
      </Box>
    )
  })

  on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: 'probe-swap' }, async ($, e) => {
    const { Box, Text, Button } = $.ui.resolve(e)
    return (
      <Box flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={C.page} flexGrow={1}>
        <Text color={C.sig}>STRIP · All 31 │ patch-1-9 12 │ +3 ▾</Text>
        <Box flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={C.overlay} borderStyle="round" borderColor={C.border} paddingX={1}>
          {PICKS.map((label, i) => (
            <Box key={`swap-${i}-box`} flexDirection="row">
              <Button key={`swap-${i}`} plain hotkey={String(i)} label={label} {...(i === 1 ? { autoFocus: true as const } : {})} onPress={() => $.ui.toast(`picked ${label}`)} />
            </Box>
          ))}
        </Box>
      </Box>
    )
  })

  on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: 'probe-select' }, async ($, e) => {
    const { Box, Select } = $.ui.resolve(e) as unknown as Pick<Elements['terminal'], 'Box' | 'Select'>
    return (
      <Box flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={C.page} flexGrow={1}>
        <Select key="pick" label="phase" options={PICKS.map((label, i) => ({ value: String(i), label }))} value="1" autoFocus onSelect={(value: string) => $.ui.toast(`selected ${value}`)} />
      </Box>
    )
  })

  on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: 'probe-panel' }, async ($, e) => {
    const { Box, Text } = $.ui.resolve(e)
    const frame = { flexDirection: 'column' as const, borderStyle: 'round', borderColor: C.border, backgroundColor: C.raised, paddingX: 1, width: Math.min(60, e.props.bodyColumns) }
    return (
      <Box flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={C.page} flexGrow={1} rowGap={1}>
        <Box {...frame}>
          <Box position="absolute" top={-1} left={1}>
            <Text color={C.sig} backgroundColor={C.raised} bold>
              {' ◆ TASKMASTER · SESSION '}
            </Text>
          </Box>
          <Box position="absolute" top={-1} right={1} backgroundColor={C.raised} paddingX={1}>
            <Text color={C.ok}>● tm ok</Text>
          </Box>
          <Text color={C.bold}>border title: the title should sit IN the top border, after ╭─</Text>
          <Text color={C.bold}>and the health signal at the right end of the same border row.</Text>
        </Box>
        <Box {...frame}>
          <Box flexDirection="row">
            <Text color={C.sig} bold>
              ◆ TASKMASTER · SESSION
            </Text>
            <Box flexGrow={1} />
            <Text color={C.ok}>● tm ok</Text>
          </Box>
          <Text color={C.bold}>row title: the fallback, a first row inside the frame.</Text>
        </Box>
      </Box>
    )
  })

  on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: 'probe-keycap' }, async ($, e) => {
    const { Box, Text, Button } = $.ui.resolve(e)
    const glyph = (key: string) => (
      <Text backgroundColor={C.cap} color={C.ink} bold>
        {` ${key} `}
      </Text>
    )
    return (
      <Box flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={C.page} flexGrow={1} rowGap={1}>
        <Text color={C.bold}>A overlay: an RR keycap laid over the engine&apos;s `d: ` (expect ` d  done`)</Text>
        <Box key="a-box" flexDirection="row">
          <Button key="a" plain hotkey="d" label="done" onPress={() => $.ui.toast('A pressed')} />
          <Box position="absolute" left={0} top={0}>
            {glyph('d')}
          </Box>
        </Box>
        <Text color={C.bold}>B accent: the engine&apos;s own `a: back` (the fallback)</Text>
        <Box key="b-box" flexDirection="row">
          <Button key="b" plain hotkey="a" label="back" onPress={() => $.ui.toast('B pressed')} />
        </Box>
        <Text color={C.bold}>C label-less: a keycap-filled box around a hotkey Button with an empty label, then the word</Text>
        <Box key="c-box" flexDirection="row" columnGap={1}>
          <Box backgroundColor={C.cap}>
            <Button key="c" plain hotkey="c" label="" onPress={() => $.ui.toast('C pressed')} />
          </Box>
          <Text color={C.bold}>copy</Text>
        </Box>
      </Box>
    )
  })
}
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Item 1 — does `backlog_handover_list` JSON carry `task_ids`; do the phase queries run?**

What is already known: the captured native fixture (`mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/fixtures/captured/native.ts`) holds `"task_ids": ["epic-j-009"]`, and `parseHandovers` already reads it into `TmHandover.taskIds`. Confirm it live from claude-tools (this repo's tm server is a phantom, B-093). In the same run, check that Task 1.5's queries run under the server's 5 s cut-off and return cells under 80 characters. Run from PowerShell (no `cd`: `-WorkingDirectory` does it):

```powershell
$env:ANTHROPIC_API_KEY = $null
$date = "CASE WHEN p.status IN ('done','archived') THEN substr(coalesce(json_extract(p.doc,'`$.completed'),''),1,10) ELSE substr(coalesce(json_extract(p.doc,'`$.start_date'),''),1,10) END"
$open = "(SELECT count(DISTINCT ht.handover_id) FROM handover_tasks ht JOIN entities t ON t.kind='task' AND t.id=ht.task_id AND t.deleted=0 JOIN entities ho ON ho.kind='handover' AND ho.id=ht.handover_id AND ho.deleted=0 AND ho.status='open' WHERE json_extract(t.doc,'`$.phase')=p.id)"
$closed = $open.Replace("ho.status='open'", "ho.status='closed'")
$sqlHandovers = "SELECT json_array(p.id, p.status, json_extract(p.doc,'`$.order'), $open, $closed, $date) AS phase, json_extract(p.doc,'`$.name') AS name FROM entities p WHERE p.kind='phase' AND p.deleted=0 ORDER BY json_extract(p.doc,'`$.order') DESC"
$unlinked = "(SELECT count(*) FROM entities ho WHERE ho.kind='handover' AND ho.deleted=0 AND ho.status='open' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM handover_tasks ht WHERE ht.handover_id=ho.id))"
$sqlNoTask = "SELECT json_array('~none', 'none', 0, $unlinked, $($unlinked.Replace("ho.status='open'", "ho.status='closed'")), '') AS phase, 'no task' AS name"
$prompt = @"
<task>Call these Taskmaster MCP tools in order and print each raw result exactly as returned, inside its own tag. Do not summarise, reformat or explain.</task>
<calls>
<call tag="handovers">mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__backlog_handover_list with format "json", status "open", limit 3</call>
<call tag="handover_phases">mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__backlog_query with limit 200 and sql: $sqlHandovers</call>
<call tag="no_task">mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__backlog_query with limit 200 and sql: $sqlNoTask</call>
</calls>
"@
Start-Process -FilePath claude -ArgumentList @('-p', '--model', 'haiku', '--allowedTools', 'mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__backlog_query,mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__backlog_handover_list', $prompt) -WorkingDirectory C:\Users\gruku\Files\Claude\claude-tools -NoNewWindow -Wait -RedirectStandardOutput C:\Users\gruku\Files\Claude\dev-mods\mods-ux-probe\item1.txt
Get-Content C:\Users\gruku\Files\Claude\dev-mods\mods-ux-probe\item1.txt
```

Expected: `<handovers>` shows JSON whose entries carry a `task_ids` array. `<handover_phases>` is a table of `["id","status",order,open,closed,"YYYY-MM-DD"]  name` lines ending in `N rows`. `<no_task>` is one `["~none","none",0,N,M,""]  no task` line and `1 row`. Repeat once from `C:\Users\gruku\Files\Work\CodeMaestro` (the big store) to check the 5 s cut-off. A refusal naming `UNION`, `NOT EXISTS` or a timeout is an answer too: record it. Stage 4 then reads the no-task count with its own fallback (see the table at the end of this task).

**Fallback designs by outcome** (every row has code in Stage 1 or a named owner):

| Item | Outcome | Design |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | `task_ids` present (expected) | Stage 4 reads linked tasks from `TmHandover.taskIds` (already parsed) and phases from Task 1.5's `readPhases(host, 'handovers', …)`. |
| 1 | `task_ids` absent | Stage 4 joins with one extra `backlog_query`: `SELECT json_array(ht.handover_id, ht.task_id) AS link FROM handover_tasks ht JOIN entities ho ON ho.kind='handover' AND ho.id=ht.handover_id AND ho.deleted=0 AND ho.status IN ('open','closed')`. Phase counts are unaffected. |
| 1 | `NOT EXISTS` (no-task row) refused | Task 1.5's `readPhases` already reads the no-task row in its own call. On a refusal it keeps the phases and leaves out the no-task tab (Task 1.5 code), and Stage 4 counts no-task handovers client-side from `taskIds`. |
| 2 | Absolute overlay paints cleanly and Tab stays inside | `OVERLAY_PLACEMENT = 'absolute'`: `phaseSection` lays the overlay over the body. |
| 2 | Bleed-through, or Tab reaches covered cards (expected) | `'swap'` (default): while the overlay is open it replaces the pane body below the strip, so nothing hides behind it. |
| 2 | Select is a ↑↓ list | `OVERLAY_LIST = 'select'`: one `Select` (`phase-select`) replaces the pick Buttons. `f` closes it; Esc closes the pane, as the engine does. |
| 2 | Select is one line, or arrows scroll | `'buttons'` (default): picks are keycaps `0`–`9`, the chosen one `autoFocus`, Tab + Enter for the rest. |
| 4 | Terminal border title lands on the border row | `PANEL_TITLE.terminal = 'border'` (default). Off by one cell: adjust `top`/`left` in `panel()` and record it. |
| 4 | Terminal border title misplaced | `PANEL_TITLE.terminal = 'row'`: the title row inside the frame. |
| 4 | Desktop confirmed | `PANEL_TITLE.desktop = 'border'`. Unconfirmed or broken: `'row'` (default). |
| 5 | A reads ` d  done` and presses | `KEYCAP_FACE = 'overlay'` (default). |
| 5 | A misdraws | `KEYCAP_FACE = 'accent'`: the engine's own `d: done`. Every test asserts Button props, never the face, so no test changes. |

---

## Stage 1: Kit and phase strip

**Goal.** Every later stage draws with one kit (spec §4.1) and one two-way phase strip (spec §5). Stage 1 builds both. It moves the review pane's one-way `f` cycle onto the strip and adds a `/tm-kit` gallery (demo source only), where the user judges every primitive by eye. Band, Session tab, review list, handovers and restore come in Stages 2–5 and use only what this stage produces.

**Verified before writing.** This stage's code was replayed task by task on a copy of both mods at `feat/database-native-foundation` 35cccf8. After every task, `claude plugin test`, `validate --strict` and `tsc` were green. Test counts below are the baseline plus N, where the baseline is Task 0.1's count (247 for taskmaster-mods and 24 for rr-tui at 35cccf8).

**Test-plugin rule found while replaying.** A `Plugin` given to `test(…, { plugins: [...] })` (the RR stub) runs its `register` in its own environment. It may not use anything imported into the fixture file, or every render fails with `X is not defined`. Keep the stub self-contained. Kit unit tests do not need the stub: they draw directly with `tests/fixtures/kit-ui.ts`.

**What Stage 1 produces for Stages 2–5** (every signature is final; the code is in the tasks):

`hooks/kit.tsx`:

```ts
// types/index.d.ts

```

The counts per pane:

| Pane | `count` | `closed` | `allCount` (passed to the strip) | no task tab |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| review | in-review tasks the scope lists (waiting: with a `human_action`) | 0 | `snapshot.queueTotal` | never |
| handovers | open handovers linked through the phase's tasks (`handover_tasks`), each counted once per phase | closed ones, likewise. `withClosed` adds them while `◐ closed` is on | the open handovers' total (`snapshot.handoversTotal`; Stage 4 adds closed) | `NO_TASK_SQL` row: handovers that link no task |
| restore | 0 from the query. Stage 5 recounts with `withCounts(phases, counts)` from the scan (session → bound task → phase) | 0 | sessions in scope | `withCounts` adds it when `counts` has `NO_TASK` |

Done and archived phases date by `completed`, others by `start_date` (`YYYY-MM-DD`, `''` when absent).

### Task 1.1: rr-tui `badgeProps` and `tint`, wired through to taskmaster-mods

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/rr-tui/types/index.d.ts` (add `RrTextProps`; `Rr` gains `badgeProps`, `tint`)
- Modify: `mods/rr-tui/hooks/kit.ts` (append `badgeProps`, `tint`)
- Modify: `mods/rr-tui/hooks/register.tsx` (noun refusals and two `rr.*` hooks)
- Modify: `mods/rr-tui/hooks/gallery.ts` (a `badges` section)
- Modify: `mods/rr-tui/tests/kit.test.ts`, `mods/rr-tui/tests/fixtures/consumer.ts`, `mods/rr-tui/tests/noun.test.ts`
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/rr.ts` (derived types), `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (`rrOf`), `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/fixtures/rr-stub.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the existing `$.rr` contract (`RrTokens`, `tint12`, `tint24`, `surface`, `border.strong`).
- Produces: `$.rr.badgeProps({ tone, strength?, on? }) => Promise<RrTextProps>` (default strength 24, default ground page; fill = `tint{strength}[on][tone]` with ink `fg.bold`, bold; survivalist fill = `border.strong` at 24, else one surface step up). `$.rr.tint({ fg, bg, alpha }) => Promise<string>` (#rrggbb blend, alpha clamped to 0..1, throws on a non-hex). `RrTextProps = { color: string; backgroundColor: string; bold: boolean }`. In taskmaster-mods `hooks/rr.ts`: `RrGround`, `RrStrength`, `RrSignalKind`, `RrTextProps`.

The two mods change together: the worktree's `taskmaster-mods/.claude-plugin/types/rr-tui/index.d.ts` is a symlink to `rr-tui/types/index.d.ts`, so the moment the contract grows, `rrOf` and the stub must grow too, or taskmaster-mods' `tsc` fails.

Append to `mods/rr-tui/tests/kit.test.ts`:

```ts
describe('badges and tints', () => {
  const ON: readonly RrGround[] = ['page', 'raised', 'overlay']

  test('a badge is the tone tint on its ground (24 by default, 12 quiet) with bold foreground-bold ink; survivalist steps a surface', () => {
    for (const p of POLARITIES) {
      const t = tokensFor(p)
      for (const tone of TONES) {
        for (const on of ON) {
          const strong = kit.badgeProps(t, { tone, on })
          const quiet = kit.badgeProps(t, { tone, on, strength: 12 })
          expect(strong.color).toBe(t.fg.bold)
          expect(strong.bold).toBe(true)
          if (p === 'survivalist') {
            expect(strong.backgroundColor).toBe(t.border.strong)
            expect(quiet.backgroundColor).not.toBe(t.border.strong)
          } else {
            expect(strong.backgroundColor).toBe(t.tint24[on][tone])
            expect(quiet.backgroundColor).toBe(t.tint12[on][tone])
          }
        }
      }
    }
    expect(kit.badgeProps(tokensFor('dark'), { tone: 'info' })).toEqual(kit.badgeProps(tokensFor('dark'), { tone: 'info', on: 'page', strength: 24 }))
  })

  test('badge ink reads at 4.5:1 or better on its fill in every polarity, tone, ground and strength', () => {
    for (const p of POLARITIES) {
      const t = tokensFor(p)
      for (const tone of TONES) {
        for (const on of ON) {
          for (const strength of [12, 24] as const) {
            const b = kit.badgeProps(t, { tone, on, strength })
            expect(contrast(b.color, b.backgroundColor) >= 4.5, `${p} ${tone} on ${on} at ${strength}`).toBe(true)
          }
        }
      }
    }
  })

  test('tint lays fg over bg at alpha, clamps alpha to 0..1, and refuses anything but #rrggbb', () => {
    expect(kit.tint({ fg: '#ffffff', bg: '#000000', alpha: 0.5 })).toBe('#808080')
    expect(kit.tint({ fg: '#ff0000', bg: '#0000ff', alpha: 0.25 })).toBe('#4000bf')
    expect(kit.tint({ fg: '#ffffff', bg: '#000000', alpha: 2 })).toBe('#ffffff')
    expect(kit.tint({ fg: '#ffffff', bg: '#000000', alpha: -1 })).toBe('#000000')
    let refused = ''
    try {
      kit.tint({ fg: 'red', bg: '#000000', alpha: 0.5 })
    } catch (error) {
      refused = String(error)
    }
    expect(refused.includes('#rrggbb')).toBe(true)
  })
})
```

In `mods/rr-tui/hooks/kit.ts`, replace:


with:

```ts
import type { RrBoxProps, RrButtonProps, RrGround, RrLevel, RrSignalKind, RrStrength, RrTextProps, RrTokens, RrTone, RrTreatment } from '../types'
```

Append to `mods/rr-tui/hooks/kit.ts`:

```ts
export function badgeProps(t: RrTokens, a: { tone: RrTone; strength?: RrStrength; on?: RrGround }): RrTextProps {
  const on = a.on ?? 'page'
  const strong = (a.strength ?? 24) === 24
  if (t.polarity === 'survivalist') return { backgroundColor: strong ? t.border.strong : t.surface[NEXT_STEP[on]], color: t.fg.bold, bold: true }
  return { backgroundColor: (strong ? t.tint24 : t.tint12)[on][a.tone], color: t.fg.bold, bold: true }
}

const HEX = /^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/

export function tint(a: { fg: string; bg: string; alpha: number }): string {
  if (!HEX.test(a.fg) || !HEX.test(a.bg)) throw new Error(`rr-tui: tint takes two #rrggbb colours, got ${a.fg} and ${a.bg}`)
  const alpha = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, a.alpha))
  const channel = (hex: string, i: number): number => Number.parseInt(hex.slice(1 + 2 * i, 3 + 2 * i), 16)
  const mixed = [0, 1, 2].map(i => Math.round(channel(a.fg, i) * alpha + channel(a.bg, i) * (1 - alpha)))
  return `#${mixed.map(v => v.toString(16).padStart(2, '0')).join('')}`
}
```

### Task 1.2: The kit's base: keycaps, badges, labels, signals, rules, footers, confirm rows

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/kit.tsx`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/fixtures/tokens.ts`, `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/fixtures/kit-ui.ts`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/kit.test.ts`
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/draw.tsx` (lines 4, 29–55: `Ui`, `Piece`, `fit` now come from the kit), `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (import `Ui` from the kit)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1.1's `RrGround`, `RrStrength`, `RrSignalKind`, `RrTextProps`, `$.rr.badgeProps`; `truncate` from `hooks/model.ts`; `widthOf`, `isEl`, `Drawn` from `tests/fixtures/measure.ts`.
- Produces: `Ui`, `Piece`, `BadgeTable`, `Kit`, `KitRr`, `KEYCAP_FACE`, `PANEL_TITLE`, `TM_TAG`, `HOTKEY`, `kitOf`, `stepUp`, `fit`, `row`, `label`, `signal`, `KeycapSpec`, `keycap`, `checkbox`, `badge`, `rule`, `helpFooter`, `confirmRow` (signatures above). Test fixtures: `STUB_TOKENS`, `TONE`, `STRONG`, `stubBadge`, `FAKE_UI`, `testKit`, `walk`, `byKey`, `textsOf`, `hoversOf`.

Design notes the code carries:
- The keycap is one plain Button (`key` = id, `hotkey` = key) in a keyed Box `<id>-box`. On the terminal, with `KEYCAP_FACE = 'overlay'`, RR's filled ` k ` glyph sits in an absolute Box over the engine's `k: ` prefix, so it reads as a key cap followed by the label, and the width stays `3 + label`. On the desktop the glyph sits beside the native button.
- `dim` means present but inert: a press toasts the reason through `Kit.say`. `faded` dims but acts (empty or done phase tabs). `selected` is the signature 24% fill.
- Hover changes only the fill, one surface step up (`stepUp`). Nothing moves, no rail, no shadow.
- `kitOf` fetches tokens once per render and rr-tui's badge recipe once per polarity (30 calls, cached for the module's life).

```ts
// User intent: the stand-in RR tokens the kit's direct tests draw with — the values rr-stub.ts holds inline (a test plugin
// cannot import) — plain and predictable, so tests assert words, keys and recipes, not real colours.
import type { RrGround, RrStrength, RrTextProps, RrTokens, RrTone } from '../../hooks/rr'

export const TONE = { success: '#3a9a5b', warning: '#c4881d', critical: '#d14343', info: '#5b8fc7', signature: '#8a9eeb' } as const
export const STRONG = { success: '#1f5f35', warning: '#7a5410', critical: '#7d2525', info: '#2f557d', signature: '#4a5590' } as const
const KEYCAP = { bg: '#8a9eeb', ink: '#0d0d0c' }

export const STUB_TOKENS: RrTokens = {
  polarity: 'dark',
  surface: { page: '#141413', raised: '#1d1d1b', overlay: '#272725', recessed: '#0d0d0c' },
  fg: { bold: '#f5f3ed', default: '#d2cfc8', subtle: '#a09c95', disabled: '#66645f' },
  border: { default: '#343331', subtle: '#302f2d', strong: '#4d4c48', focus: '#5e79e6' },
  signatureText: '#8a9eeb',
  tone: TONE,
  tint12: { page: TONE, raised: TONE, overlay: TONE },
  tint24: { page: STRONG, raised: STRONG, overlay: STRONG },
  keycap: { success: KEYCAP, warning: KEYCAP, critical: KEYCAP, info: KEYCAP, signature: KEYCAP },
}

/** rr-tui's badge recipe over STUB_TOKENS. */
export const stubBadge = (a: { tone: RrTone; strength?: RrStrength; on?: RrGround }): RrTextProps => ({
  backgroundColor: ((a.strength ?? 24) === 24 ? STUB_TOKENS.tint24 : STUB_TOKENS.tint12)[a.on ?? 'page'][a.tone],
  color: STUB_TOKENS.fg.bold,
  bold: true,
})
```

`mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/fixtures/kit-ui.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: draw kit primitives in a test without a session — the engine's own plain-data trees (h with a tag name),
// a Kit over the stand-in tokens, and small finders — so each primitive is checked on its own.
import type { RenderSurface } from 'claude-code'

import type { Kit, Ui } from '../../hooks/kit'
import type { RrGround, RrTextProps, RrTone } from '../../hooks/rr'
import { isEl, type Drawn } from './measure'
import { STUB_TOKENS, stubBadge } from './tokens'

const tag = (type: 'Box' | 'Text' | 'Button' | 'Input' | 'Select') => (props: Record<string, unknown>) => {
  const { children, ...rest } = props
  const kids = children === undefined ? [] : Array.isArray(children) ? children : [children]
  return h(type as 'Box', rest, ...kids)
}

export const FAKE_UI = { Box: tag('Box'), Text: tag('Text'), Button: tag('Button'), Input: tag('Input'), Select: tag('Select') } as unknown as Ui

const GROUNDS: readonly RrGround[] = ['page', 'raised', 'overlay']
const TONES: readonly RrTone[] = ['success', 'warning', 'critical', 'info', 'signature']
const table = (strength: 12 | 24): Record<RrGround, Record<RrTone, RrTextProps>> =>
  Object.fromEntries(GROUNDS.map(on => [on, Object.fromEntries(TONES.map(tone => [tone, stubBadge({ tone, strength, on })]))])) as Record<
    RrGround,
    Record<RrTone, RrTextProps>
  >

/** A Kit over STUB_TOKENS; `said` collects what a dim keycap would toast. */
export function testKit(surface: RenderSurface = 'terminal', said: string[] = []): Kit {
  return { ui: FAKE_UI, t: STUB_TOKENS, surface, badges: { 12: table(12), 24: table(24) }, say: text => void said.push(text) }
}

/** Every element of a tree, outermost first, including what a mapped list nested. */
export function walk(n: unknown): Drawn[] {
  if (Array.isArray(n)) return n.flatMap(walk)
  if (!isEl(n)) return []
  return [n, ...(n.children ?? []).flatMap(walk)]
}

export const byKey = (n: unknown, key: string): Drawn | undefined => walk(n).find(e => e.props?.key === key)

/** The text of every Text element, in order. */
export const textsOf = (n: unknown): string[] =>
  walk(n)
    .filter(e => e.type === 'Text')
    .map(e => (e.children ?? []).filter((c): c is string => typeof c === 'string').join(''))

/** Every `hover` the tree sets (hoisted out of props by the engine). */
export const hoversOf = (n: unknown): Record<string, unknown>[] =>
  walk(n).flatMap(e => {
    const hover = (e as Drawn & { hover?: Record<string, unknown> }).hover
    return hover === undefined ? [] : [hover]
  })
```

`mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/kit.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: pin the kit every surface is rebuilt on (spec §4.1) — keycaps that press by key, tinted badges, panels with
// their title in the border, stepped cards, fixed toolbar slots that dim — and the user's hard rules: no rails, no motion
// on hover, no shadows.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { badge, checkbox, confirmRow, fit, helpFooter, KEYCAP_FACE, keycap, label, rule, signal } from '../hooks/kit'
import { byKey, testKit, textsOf, walk } from './fixtures/kit-ui'
import { widthOf } from './fixtures/measure'
import { STRONG, STUB_TOKENS as T, TONE } from './fixtures/tokens'

const noop = (): void => undefined

describe('keycap', () => {
  test('one plain Button keyed by id with its hotkey and label, in a keyed Box; the RR glyph sits over the engine prefix', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const cap = keycap(k, { id: 'done', key: 'd', label: 'done', tone: 'success', onPress: noop })
    const box = byKey(cap.el, 'done-box')
    expect(box?.type).toBe('Box')
    const button = byKey(cap.el, 'done')
    expect(button).toMatchObject({ type: 'Button', props: { key: 'done', hotkey: 'd', label: 'done', plain: true }, hover: { bold: true, color: T.fg.bold } })
    expect(cap.width).toBe(3 + 'done'.length)
    expect(widthOf(cap.el)).toBe(cap.width)
    const glyph = walk(cap.el).find(e => e.type === 'Text' && (e.children ?? []).includes(' d '))
    if (KEYCAP_FACE === 'overlay') {
      expect(glyph?.props).toMatchObject({ backgroundColor: T.keycap.success.bg, color: T.keycap.success.ink, bold: true })
      expect(walk(cap.el).some(e => e.props?.position === 'absolute')).toBe(true)
    } else {
      expect(glyph).toBeUndefined()
    }
  })

  test('a hotkey the engine would refuse throws, naming the keycap', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    for (const bad of ['[', '/', 'R', ' ', 'ab', '']) {
      let refused = ''
      try {
        keycap(k, { id: 'x', key: bad, label: 'x', onPress: noop })
      } catch (error) {
        refused = String(error)
      }
      expect(refused.includes('keycap x') && refused.includes('one digit or one lowercase letter'), JSON.stringify(bad)).toBe(true)
    }
  })

  test('no key: the label alone, no glyph, no hotkey', () => {
    const cap = keycap(testKit(), { id: 'all', label: 'all', onPress: noop })
    expect(byKey(cap.el, 'all')?.props?.hotkey).toBeUndefined()
    expect(textsOf(cap.el)).toEqual([])
    expect(cap.width).toBe(3)
  })

  test('dim: the label dims and the glyph greys out; faded dims the label only; selected fills the signature 24% tint', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const dim = keycap(k, { id: 'prev', key: 'p', label: '‹', dim: 'Already on the first page', onPress: noop })
    expect(byKey(dim.el, 'prev')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
    if (KEYCAP_FACE === 'overlay') {
      expect(walk(dim.el).find(e => e.type === 'Text')?.props).toMatchObject({ backgroundColor: T.surface.overlay, color: T.fg.disabled })
    }
    const faded = keycap(k, { id: 'tab', key: '3', label: 'patch-1-8 ✓ 0', faded: true, onPress: noop })
    expect(byKey(faded.el, 'tab')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
    const on = keycap(k, { id: 'sel', key: '1', label: 'All 31', selected: true, onPress: noop })
    expect(byKey(on.el, 'sel-box')?.props?.backgroundColor).toBe(STRONG.signature)
    expect(byKey(keycap(k, { id: 'plain', key: '2', label: 'x', onPress: noop }).el, 'plain-box')?.props?.backgroundColor).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('on the desktop the glyph sits beside the native button, never laid over it', () => {
    const cap = keycap(testKit('desktop'), { id: 'copy', key: 'c', label: 'copy', onPress: noop })
    expect(walk(cap.el).some(e => e.props?.position === 'absolute')).toBe(false)
    expect(textsOf(cap.el)).toEqual([' c '])
    expect(byKey(cap.el, 'copy')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'c', label: 'copy' })
  })

  test('a checkbox is □ / ■ on a keycap', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    expect(byKey(checkbox(k, { id: 'sel:a', selected: false, onPress: noop }).el, 'sel:a')?.props?.label).toBe('□')
    expect(byKey(checkbox(k, { id: 'sel:a', key: 's', selected: true, onPress: noop }).el, 'sel:a')?.props).toMatchObject({ label: '■', hotkey: 's' })
  })
})

describe('badge, label, signal, rule, footer, fit', () => {
  test('a badge is its word upper-cased and padded on the tone tint with bold ink; dim greys it', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const b = badge(k, 'in review', 'info')
    expect(textsOf(b.el)).toEqual([' IN REVIEW '])
    expect(walk(b.el)[0]?.props).toMatchObject({ backgroundColor: STRONG.info, color: T.fg.bold, bold: true })
    expect(b.width).toBe(' IN REVIEW '.length)
    expect(walk(badge(k, 'wip', 'warning', { strength: 12 }).el)[0]?.props?.backgroundColor).toBe(TONE.warning)
    expect(walk(badge(k, '0 waiting', 'warning', { dim: true }).el)[0]?.props).toMatchObject({ color: T.fg.disabled, bold: false })
  })

  test('label is upper-cased signature text; signal is glyph, bold word and subtle detail', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    expect(textsOf(label(k, 'review').el)).toEqual(['REVIEW'])
    const s = signal(k, 'critical', 'offline', 'tm')
    expect(textsOf(s.el)).toEqual(['◆', ' offline', '  tm'])
    expect(s.width).toBe('◆ offline  tm'.length)
  })

  test('rule is dashed in the default border colour; the footer joins hints with · and cuts to width', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    expect(textsOf(rule(k, 5))).toEqual(['┄┄┄┄┄'])
    expect(walk(rule(k, 5))[0]?.props?.color).toBe(T.border.default)
    expect(textsOf(helpFooter(k, ['h l phase', 'f phases'], 80))).toEqual(['h l phase · f phases'])
    expect(textsOf(helpFooter(k, ['h l phase', 'f phases'], 8))).toEqual(['h l pha…'])
  })

  test('fit keeps pieces in order while they fit; the first always stays', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const a = label(k, 'aaaa')
    const b = label(k, 'bbbb')
    expect(fit([a, null, b], 9, 1)).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(fit([a, b], 8, 1)).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(fit([a, b], 2, 1)).toHaveLength(1)
  })
})

describe('confirm row', () => {
  test('question, then y yes and n no (no focused) on one row when it fits; stacked when narrow', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const wide = confirmRow(k, { id: 'confirm', question: 'done tm-audit-031?', onYes: noop, onNo: noop, width: 60 })
    expect(wide).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(byKey(wide, 'confirm-yes')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'y', label: 'yes' })
    expect(byKey(wide, 'confirm-no')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'n', label: 'no', autoFocus: true })
    expect(textsOf(wide)[0]).toBe(' done tm-audit-031? ')
    const narrow = confirmRow(k, { id: 'confirm', question: 'done tm-audit-031?', onYes: noop, onNo: noop, width: 24 })
    expect(narrow).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(byKey(narrow[1], 'confirm-no')).toBeDefined()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the kit's base**

`mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/kit.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: one interactive drawing kit for every taskmaster-mods surface (spec §4.1, §5) — keycaps, badges, panels,
// cards, toolbars, pagers, selection bars, confirm rows and the phase strip — in Reality Reprojection, synchronous after
// one fetch per render. JSX compiles to h(...): never name a variable `h` in this file (it would shadow the factory).
import type { Elements, RenderNode, RenderSurface, UiPressArgument } from 'claude-code'

import { truncate } from './model'
import type { Rr, RrGround, RrSignalKind, RrStrength, RrTextProps, RrTokens, RrTone } from './rr'

export type Ui = Pick<Elements['terminal'], 'Box' | 'Text' | 'Button' | 'Input' | 'Select'>
/** A drawn piece and the cells it takes across, so a row can be fitted to the room before it is laid out. */
export type Piece = { readonly el: RenderNode; readonly width: number }
export type BadgeTable = Readonly<Record<RrStrength, Readonly<Record<RrGround, Readonly<Record<RrTone, RrTextProps>>>>>>
/** What every kit function draws with: fetched once per render (kitOf), then every call is synchronous. */
export type Kit = {
  readonly ui: Ui
  readonly t: RrTokens
  readonly surface: RenderSurface
  /** rr-tui's badge recipe for every strength, ground and tone. */
  readonly badges: BadgeTable
  /** Toasts a line: what a dim keycap says when pressed, instead of acting. */
  readonly say: (text: string) => void
}
/** The $.rr calls a kit needs. */
export type KitRr = Pick<Rr, 'tokens' | 'badgeProps'>

/**
 * What a keycap draws on the terminal (spec §14 item 5, Task 0.2): `overlay` lays RR's filled keycap over the engine's
 * `k: ` prefix, so it reads ` k  label`; `accent` leaves the engine's own accent-coloured `k: label`.
 */
export const KEYCAP_FACE: 'overlay' | 'accent' = 'overlay'
/** Where a panel's title sits, per surface (spec §14 item 4, Task 0.2): set into the top border, or a first row inside it. */
export const PANEL_TITLE: Readonly<Record<'terminal' | 'desktop', 'border' | 'row'>> = { terminal: 'border', desktop: 'row' }
/** The tag every Taskmaster panel title starts with. */
export const TM_TAG = '◆ TASKMASTER'
/** The only hotkeys the engine takes (probed 2026-10-11: `[`, `/`, `R` and space fail the render). */
export const HOTKEY = /^[a-z0-9]$/

const SIGNAL_GLYPH: Readonly<Record<RrSignalKind, string>> = { success: '●', warning: '▲', critical: '◆', info: 'ⓘ' }
const GROUNDS: readonly RrGround[] = ['page', 'raised', 'overlay']
const TONES: readonly RrTone[] = ['success', 'warning', 'critical', 'info', 'signature']
const STRENGTHS: readonly RrStrength[] = [12, 24]
const RUNG: Readonly<Record<RrGround, number>> = { page: 0, raised: 1, overlay: 2 }

// rr-tui's badge recipe, fetched once per polarity and reused by every later render (a reload starts afresh).
const badgeCache = new Map<string, BadgeTable>()

export async function kitOf(ui: Ui, rr: KitRr, surface: RenderSurface, say: (text: string) => void): Promise<Kit> {
  const t = await rr.tokens()
  let badges = badgeCache.get(t.polarity)
  if (badges === undefined) {
    const cells = await Promise.all(
      STRENGTHS.flatMap(strength =>
        GROUNDS.flatMap(on => TONES.map(async tone => ({ strength, on, tone, props: await rr.badgeProps({ tone, strength, on }) }))),
      ),
    )
    const table = Object.fromEntries(STRENGTHS.map(strength => [strength, Object.fromEntries(GROUNDS.map(on => [on, {}]))])) as unknown as Record<
      RrStrength,
      Record<RrGround, Record<RrTone, RrTextProps>>
    >
    for (const cell of cells) table[cell.strength][cell.on][cell.tone] = cell.props
    badges = table
    badgeCache.set(t.polarity, badges)
  }
  return { ui, t, surface, badges, say }
}

/** The ladder a card and a hover climb: page, raised, overlay, then the signature tints above overlay (surface stepping). */
function rung(t: RrTokens, at: number): string {
  const ladder = [t.surface.page, t.surface.raised, t.surface.overlay, t.tint12.overlay.signature, t.tint24.overlay.signature]
  return ladder[Math.min(ladder.length - 1, Math.max(0, at))] ?? t.surface.overlay
}

/** One surface step up from a ground: every hover fill (colour only, never motion). */
export const stepUp = (t: RrTokens, on: RrGround): string => rung(t, RUNG[on] + 1)

/** Keeps the pieces in order while they fit `width` with `gap` between them; the first piece always stays. */
export function fit(pieces: readonly (Piece | null)[], width: number, gap: number): RenderNode[] {
  const kept: RenderNode[] = []
  let used = 0
  for (const piece of pieces) {
    if (piece === null) continue
    const need = kept.length === 0 ? piece.width : used + gap + piece.width
    if (kept.length > 0 && need > width) continue
    kept.push(piece.el)
    used = need
  }
  return kept
}

/** Pieces side by side, top-aligned so a taller piece never stretches its neighbours. */
export function row(k: Kit, pieces: readonly Piece[], gap = 1): Piece {
  const { Box } = k.ui
  return {
    el: (
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={gap} alignItems="flex-start">
        {pieces.map(p => p.el)}
      </Box>
    ),
    width: pieces.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.width, 0) + gap * Math.max(0, pieces.length - 1),
  }
}

/** RR's section label: the word upper-cased in signature text (bold in survivalist, which has no hue). */
export function label(k: Kit, text: string): Piece {
  const { Text } = k.ui
  const word = text.toUpperCase()
  return {
    el: (
      <Text color={k.t.signatureText} bold={k.t.polarity === 'survivalist'}>
        {word}
      </Text>
    ),
    width: word.length,
  }
}

/** RR's signal: the glyph in the tone, the word bold, an optional subtle detail; colour never travels without its shape. */
export function signal(k: Kit, kind: RrSignalKind, word: string, detail = ''): Piece {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  return {
    el: (
      <Box flexDirection="row">
        <Text color={k.t.tone[kind]}>{SIGNAL_GLYPH[kind]}</Text>
        <Text color={k.t.fg.bold} bold>
          {` ${word}`}
        </Text>
        {detail === '' ? null : <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{`  ${detail}`}</Text>}
      </Box>
    ),
    width: 2 + word.length + (detail === '' ? 0 : 2 + detail.length),
  }
}

export type KeycapSpec = {
  /** The Button's key (its `ui.press` element); the wrapper Box is `<id>-box`. */
  readonly id: string
  /** One digit or one lowercase letter (anything else throws); absent: no hotkey and no glyph, the label alone. */
  readonly key?: string
  readonly label: string
  /** The glyph's fill; default signature. */
  readonly tone?: RrTone
  readonly onPress: (press: UiPressArgument) => void
  /** Present: drawn dim and inert — a press toasts this reason instead of acting (a fixed slot dims, never vanishes). */
  readonly dim?: string
  /** Drawn dim but still acting: an empty or done phase tab, selectable all the same. */
  readonly faded?: boolean
  /** Chosen (a phase tab, an on toggle): the signature 24% tint behind the whole keycap. */
  readonly selected?: boolean
  readonly autoFocus?: boolean
  /** The ground it sits on (hover and selected fills); default page. */
  readonly on?: RrGround
}

/** A keycap: RR's filled key glyph and a plain label, one Button that the key, a click or Enter presses. */
export function keycap(k: Kit, c: KeycapSpec): Piece {
  const { Box, Text, Button } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  if (c.key !== undefined && !HOTKEY.test(c.key)) {
    throw new Error(`taskmaster-mods: keycap ${c.id}: hotkey "${c.key}" is not one digit or one lowercase letter`)
  }
  const on = c.on ?? 'page'
  const why = c.dim
  const cap = why === undefined ? t.keycap[c.tone ?? 'signature'] : { bg: t.surface.overlay, ink: t.fg.disabled }
  const fill = c.selected === true ? t.tint24[on].signature : undefined
  const glyph =
    c.key === undefined ? null : (
      <Text backgroundColor={cap.bg} color={cap.ink} bold>
        {` ${c.key} `}
      </Text>
    )
  const button = (
    <Button
      key={c.id}
      plain
      {...(c.key === undefined ? {} : { hotkey: c.key })}
      label={c.label}
      {...(why !== undefined || c.faded === true ? { dimColor: true } : {})}
      {...(c.autoFocus === true ? { autoFocus: true as const } : {})}
      hover={{ bold: true, color: t.fg.bold }}
      onPress={why === undefined ? c.onPress : () => k.say(why)}
    />
  )
  const width = (c.key === undefined ? 0 : 3) + c.label.length
  const ground = fill === undefined ? {} : { backgroundColor: fill }
  if (k.surface !== 'terminal') {
    // A desktop draws its own native button: the RR glyph sits beside it, never over it.
    return {
      el: (
        <Box key={`${c.id}-box`} flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} flexShrink={0} {...ground}>
          {glyph}
          {button}
        </Box>
      ),
      width: width + (glyph === null ? 0 : 1),
    }
  }
  return {
    el: (
      <Box key={`${c.id}-box`} flexDirection="row" flexShrink={0} {...ground} hover={{ backgroundColor: fill ?? stepUp(t, on) }}>
        {button}
        {glyph !== null && KEYCAP_FACE === 'overlay' ? (
          <Box position="absolute" left={0} top={0}>
            {glyph}
          </Box>
        ) : null}
      </Box>
    ),
    width,
  }
}

/** A card's select box: `□` / `■` as a keycap (the focused card's carries `s`). */
export function checkbox(
  k: Kit,
  a: { readonly id: string; readonly selected: boolean; readonly onPress: () => void; readonly key?: string; readonly dim?: string; readonly on?: RrGround },
): Piece {
  return keycap(k, { ...a, label: a.selected ? '■' : '□' })
}

/** A status badge: the word upper-cased on its tone's tint in bold ink (never a rail); dim for a zero count. */
export function badge(k: Kit, text: string, tone: RrTone, a: { readonly strength?: RrStrength; readonly on?: RrGround; readonly dim?: boolean } = {}): Piece {
  const { Text } = k.ui
  const word = ` ${text.toUpperCase()} `
  const on = a.on ?? 'page'
  const props: RrTextProps = a.dim === true ? { color: k.t.fg.disabled, backgroundColor: stepUp(k.t, on), bold: false } : k.badges[a.strength ?? 24][on][tone]
  return { el: <Text {...props}>{word}</Text>, width: word.length }
}

/** A subtle dashed separator across `width` cells. */
export function rule(k: Kit, width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Text } = k.ui
  return <Text color={k.t.border.default}>{'┄'.repeat(Math.max(0, Math.floor(width)))}</Text>
}

/** One subtle line of key hints, cut to `width`. */
export function helpFooter(k: Kit, items: readonly string[], width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Text } = k.ui
  return (
    <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle} wrap="truncate-end">
      {truncate(items.join(' · '), Math.max(1, width))}
    </Text>
  )
}

/**
 * The y/n confirm: the question on the warning tint, then `y yes` and `n no` (no is the focused safe default; Enter cancels).
 * One row when it fits, else the question above the pair: never y without n. Keys `<id>-yes` / `<id>-no`; drawn after the
 * pager, its `n` wins the clash (later wins).
 */
export function confirmRow(
  k: Kit,
  a: { readonly id: string; readonly question: string; readonly onYes: () => void; readonly onNo: () => void; readonly width: number; readonly on?: RrGround },
): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const on = a.on ?? 'page'
  const yes = keycap(k, { id: `${a.id}-yes`, key: 'y', label: 'yes', tone: 'success', onPress: a.onYes, on })
  const no = keycap(k, { id: `${a.id}-no`, key: 'n', label: 'no', autoFocus: true, onPress: a.onNo, on })
  const pair = row(k, [yes, no])
  const text = truncate(a.question, Math.max(4, a.width - 2))
  const question = <Text {...k.badges[24][on].warning}>{` ${text} `}</Text>
  if (text.length + 2 + 1 + pair.width > a.width) return [question, pair.el]
  return [
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} alignItems="flex-start">
      {question}
      {pair.el}
    </Box>,
  ]
}
```

### Task 1.3: The kit's containers: panel, card, toolbar, pager, selection bar

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/kit.tsx` (append after `confirmRow`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/kit.test.ts` (imports, a `STYLE_ONLY` set, one new `describe`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1.2's `Kit`, `keycap`, `label`, `signal`, `fit`, `TM_TAG`, `PANEL_TITLE`, the internal `rung` / `RUNG` ladder.
- Produces: `PanelSpec`, `panelTitle`, `panel`, `CardSpec`, `card`, `toolbar`, `pager`, `BulkAction`, `Ghost`, `selectionBar` (signatures above).

Design notes:
- The panel's title sits in the top border by absolute placement (`top: -1`) on the raised fill. `PANEL_TITLE` switches it to a first row: the default on the desktop, and the outcome-driven fallback from Task 0.2.
- A card is one surface step above its ground and one more when selected. Hover lifts the fill one more step: colour only. Only the focused card draws its full-perimeter `border.focus` border and its expanded body. The unfocused card pads one cell more, so text lines up.
- The pager's `p`/`n` and every inert slot dim and toast why; they never vanish.

```ts
describe('panel, card, toolbar, pager, selection bar', () => {
  test('a terminal panel: round full border, raised fill, the title in signature text set into the top border, health top right', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const p = panel(k, { title: panelTitle('session'), health: { kind: 'success', word: 'tm ok' }, width: 60 }, [label(k, 'body').el])
    const root = walk(p)[0]
    expect(root?.props).toMatchObject({ borderStyle: 'round', borderColor: T.border.strong, backgroundColor: T.surface.raised, width: 60 })
    const placed = walk(p).filter(e => e.props?.position === 'absolute')
    if (PANEL_TITLE.terminal === 'border') {
      expect(placed.map(e => e.props?.top)).toEqual([-1, -1])
      expect(textsOf(placed[0])).toEqual([' ◆ TASKMASTER · SESSION '])
      expect(walk(placed[0]).find(e => e.type === 'Text')?.props?.color).toBe(T.signatureText)
    }
    expect(textsOf(p)).toContain(' tm ok')
    expect(textsOf(p)).toContain('BODY')
  })

  test('the desktop panel puts its title on a first row; collapse drops the body; a long title is cut', () => {
    const desk = panel(testKit('desktop'), { title: panelTitle('restore'), width: 40 }, [])
    if (PANEL_TITLE.desktop === 'row') {
      expect(walk(desk).some(e => e.props?.position === 'absolute')).toBe(false)
      expect(textsOf(desk)[0]).toBe('◆ TASKMASTER · RESTORE')
    }
    const k = testKit()
    expect(textsOf(panel(k, { title: 'T', collapse: true, width: 30 }, [label(k, 'hidden').el]))).not.toContain('HIDDEN')
    expect(textsOf(panel(k, { title: panelTitle('a very long section name indeed'), width: 20 }, []))[0]?.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(16)
  })

  test('a card steps one surface above its ground, selected one more, hover one more; focus is a full border and the body', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const head = label(k, 'head').el
    const body = [label(k, 'body').el]
    const rest = walk(card(k, { id: 'a', selected: false, focused: false }, head, body))[0]
    expect(rest).toMatchObject({ props: { key: 'card:a', backgroundColor: T.surface.raised, paddingX: 2 }, hover: { backgroundColor: T.surface.overlay } })
    expect(rest?.props?.borderStyle).toBeUndefined()
    expect(textsOf(rest)).toEqual(['HEAD'])
    const chosen = walk(card(k, { id: 'b', selected: true, focused: true }, head, body))[0]
    expect(chosen).toMatchObject({ props: { backgroundColor: T.surface.overlay, borderStyle: 'round', borderColor: T.border.focus, paddingX: 1 } })
    expect(textsOf(chosen)).toEqual(['HEAD', 'BODY'])
    const onRaised = walk(card(k, { id: 'c', selected: true, focused: false, on: 'raised' }, head, body))[0]
    expect(onRaised?.props?.backgroundColor).toBe(TONE.signature)
  })

  test('toolbar: title at the left, slots at the right after a spacer; the title gives way first', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const slot = keycap(k, { id: 'refresh', key: 'u', label: 'refresh', onPress: noop })
    const bar = toolbar(k, { title: 'handovers', slots: [slot], width: 60 })
    expect(textsOf(bar)[0]).toBe('HANDOVERS')
    expect(walk(bar).some(e => e.props?.flexGrow === 1)).toBe(true)
    expect(textsOf(toolbar(k, { title: 'handovers', slots: [slot], width: 16 }))[0]).toBe('HAN…')
  })

  test('pager: p ‹ and n › always drawn, dim at the ends, and the n / total count', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const first = pager(k, { page: 0, pages: 4, onTurn: noop })
    expect(byKey(first.el, 'page-prev')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'p', label: '‹', dimColor: true })
    expect(byKey(first.el, 'page-next')?.props?.dimColor).toBeUndefined()
    expect(textsOf(first.el)).toContain('1 / 4')
    const last = pager(k, { page: 9, pages: 4, onTurn: noop })
    expect(byKey(last.el, 'page-next')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
    expect(textsOf(last.el)).toContain('4 / 4')
    expect(textsOf(pager(k, { page: 0, pages: 0, onTurn: noop }).el)).toContain('1 / 1')
  })

  test('selection bar: `<verb> <count>` keycaps (dim at 0), quiet ghost buttons with no hotkey at the right', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const bar = selectionBar(k, {
      actions: [
        { id: 'bulk-done', key: 'b', verb: 'Done', count: 2, tone: 'success', onPress: noop },
        { id: 'bulk-agent', verb: 'To agent', count: 0, onPress: noop },
      ],
      ghosts: [
        { id: 'sel-all', label: 'all', onPress: noop },
        { id: 'sel-none', label: 'none', onPress: noop },
      ],
      width: 70,
    })
    expect(byKey(bar, 'bulk-done')?.props).toMatchObject({ label: 'Done 2', hotkey: 'b' })
    expect(byKey(bar, 'bulk-agent')?.props).toMatchObject({ label: 'To agent 0', dimColor: true })
    expect(byKey(bar, 'sel-all')?.props).toMatchObject({ label: 'all', dimColor: true, plain: true })
    expect(byKey(bar, 'sel-all')?.props?.hotkey).toBeUndefined()
    expect(textsOf(bar)).toContain('·')
  })

  test('hard rules: every hover only restyles (no offsets), and nothing draws a one-sided border or a shadow', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const trees = [
      keycap(k, { id: 'a', key: 'a', label: 'a', onPress: noop }).el,
      card(k, { id: 'c', selected: true, focused: true }, label(k, 'x').el, []),
      panel(k, { title: 'p', width: 30 }, []),
      pager(k, { page: 1, pages: 3, onTurn: noop }).el,
      selectionBar(k, { actions: [{ id: 'x', verb: 'Go', count: 1, onPress: noop }], ghosts: [{ id: 'g', label: 'all', onPress: noop }], width: 40 }),
    ]
    for (const tree of trees) {
      for (const hover of hoversOf(tree)) for (const key of Object.keys(hover)) expect(STYLE_ONLY.has(key), key).toBe(true)
      for (const e of walk(tree)) {
        for (const key of Object.keys(e.props ?? {})) expect(/^border(Left|Right|Top|Bottom)|shadow/i.test(key), key).toBe(false)
      }
    }
  })
})
```

Append to `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/kit.tsx`:

```tsx
export type PanelSpec = {
  /** The whole title (panelTitle builds `◆ TASKMASTER · SESSION`). */
  readonly title: string
  /** The signal at the top right: `● tm ok`, `◆ offline`. */
  readonly health?: { readonly kind: RrSignalKind; readonly word: string }
  /** True: the body is not drawn; the panel is its frame and title alone. */
  readonly collapse?: boolean
  /** Cells across, the border included. */
  readonly width: number
}

export const panelTitle = (section: string): string => `${TM_TAG} · ${section.toUpperCase()}`

/**
 * A panel: full-perimeter round border, the raised fill across the whole box, the title in signature text set into the top
 * border (or on a first row: PANEL_TITLE), the health signal at the top right.
 */
export function panel(k: Kit, a: PanelSpec, children: readonly RenderNode[]): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const health = a.health === undefined ? null : signal(k, a.health.kind, a.health.word)
  const title = truncate(a.title, Math.max(1, a.width - 6 - (health === null ? 0 : health.width + 3)))
  const body = a.collapse === true ? [] : [...children]
  const frame = {
    flexDirection: 'column' as const,
    borderStyle: 'round',
    borderColor: t.border.strong,
    backgroundColor: t.surface.raised,
    paddingX: 1,
    width: a.width,
  }
  if (PANEL_TITLE[k.surface === 'desktop' ? 'desktop' : 'terminal'] === 'row') {
    return (
      <Box {...frame}>
        <Box flexDirection="row">
          <Text color={t.signatureText} bold>
            {title}
          </Text>
          <Box flexGrow={1} />
          {health === null ? null : health.el}
        </Box>
        {body}
      </Box>
    )
  }
  return (
    <Box {...frame}>
      <Box position="absolute" top={-1} left={1}>
        <Text color={t.signatureText} backgroundColor={t.surface.raised} bold>
          {` ${title} `}
        </Text>
      </Box>
      {health === null ? null : (
        <Box position="absolute" top={-1} right={1} backgroundColor={t.surface.raised} paddingX={1}>
          {health.el}
        </Box>
      )}
      {body}
    </Box>
  )
}

export type CardSpec = {
  /** The card's hover scope: its Box is keyed `card:<id>`. */
  readonly id: string
  readonly selected: boolean
  /** The focused card draws its full-perimeter focus border and its expanded body. */
  readonly focused: boolean
  /** The ground beneath the card; default page (a card sits one step up, a selected one two). */
  readonly on?: RrGround
}

/** A card: a surface step above its ground (selected one more), one step up again on hover, colour only; never a rail. */
export function card(k: Kit, a: CardSpec, collapsedRow: RenderNode, expandedBody: readonly RenderNode[]): RenderNode {
  const { Box } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const at = RUNG[a.on ?? 'page'] + 1 + (a.selected ? 1 : 0)
  // The unfocused card pads one more cell, so its text sits where the bordered (focused) card's does.
  const edge = a.focused ? { borderStyle: 'round', borderColor: t.border.focus, paddingX: 1 } : { paddingX: 2 }
  return (
    <Box key={`card:${a.id}`} flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={rung(t, at)} hover={{ backgroundColor: rung(t, at + 1) }} {...edge}>
      {collapsedRow}
      {a.focused ? [...expandedBody] : null}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** A pane's top row: its title, a spacer, then fixed slots (pager, toggles, refresh); inert slots dim, never vanish. */
export function toolbar(k: Kit, a: { readonly title: string; readonly slots: readonly Piece[]; readonly width: number }): RenderNode {
  const { Box } = k.ui
  const used = a.slots.reduce((sum, p) => sum + p.width + 2, 0)
  const title = label(k, truncate(a.title, Math.max(1, a.width - used)))
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row" alignItems="flex-start">
      {title.el}
      <Box flexGrow={1} />
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2} alignItems="flex-start">
        {a.slots.map(p => p.el)}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  )
}

/** `p ‹`  `n ›`  `1 / 4`: both keycaps always drawn, dim at the ends (a press there says why); `onTurn` gets the 0-based page. */
export function pager(
  k: Kit,
  a: { readonly page: number; readonly pages: number; readonly onTurn: (page: number) => void; readonly on?: RrGround },
): Piece {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const pages = Math.max(1, a.pages)
  const at = Math.min(Math.max(0, a.page), pages - 1)
  const on = a.on ?? 'page'
  const prev = keycap(k, { id: 'page-prev', key: 'p', label: '‹', on, onPress: () => a.onTurn(at - 1), ...(at === 0 ? { dim: 'Already on the first page' } : {}) })
  const next = keycap(k, { id: 'page-next', key: 'n', label: '›', on, onPress: () => a.onTurn(at + 1), ...(at === pages - 1 ? { dim: 'Already on the last page' } : {}) })
  const count = `${at + 1} / ${pages}`
  return {
    el: (
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} alignItems="flex-start">
        {prev.el}
        {next.el}
        <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{count}</Text>
      </Box>
    ),
    width: prev.width + next.width + count.length + 2,
  }
}

/** A bulk action: `<verb> <count>` as a keycap; dim (with why) while nothing is selected. */
export type BulkAction = {
  readonly id: string
  readonly key?: string
  readonly verb: string
  readonly count: number
  readonly tone?: RrTone
  readonly onPress: () => void
}
/** A ghost: a quiet text button (all · none · unfinished). */
export type Ghost = { readonly id: string; readonly label: string; readonly onPress: () => void }

/** The selection bar: primary bulk actions with counts at the left, ghost selectors at the right. */
export function selectionBar(
  k: Kit,
  a: { readonly actions: readonly BulkAction[]; readonly ghosts: readonly Ghost[]; readonly width: number; readonly on?: RrGround },
): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Button, Text } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const on = a.on ?? 'page'
  const actions = a.actions.map(x =>
    keycap(k, {
      id: x.id,
      ...(x.key === undefined ? {} : { key: x.key }),
      label: `${x.verb} ${x.count}`,
      tone: x.tone ?? 'signature',
      on,
      onPress: x.onPress,
      ...(x.count === 0 ? { dim: `Nothing selected to ${x.verb.toLowerCase()}` } : {}),
    }),
  )
  const ghostWidth = a.ghosts.reduce((sum, g) => sum + g.label.length, 0) + 3 * Math.max(0, a.ghosts.length - 1)
  const ghosts = a.ghosts.flatMap((g, i) => [
    ...(i === 0 ? [] : [<Text color={t.fg.subtle}>·</Text>]),
    <Box key={`${g.id}-box`} flexDirection="row">
      <Button key={g.id} plain dimColor label={g.label} hover={{ bold: true, color: t.fg.bold }} onPress={g.onPress} />
    </Box>,
  ])
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row" alignItems="flex-start">
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} alignItems="flex-start">
        {fit(actions, Math.max(1, a.width - ghostWidth - 2), 1)}
      </Box>
      <Box flexGrow={1} />
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1}>
        {ghosts}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  )
}
```

### Task 1.4: The phase strip's model (`phases.ts`) and the per-pane store key

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/phases.ts`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/phases.test.ts`
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/types/index.d.ts` (`TmPhase` gains `date?`, `closed?`; new `TmPhasePane`; `TmPhaseView` gains `loaded`, `showDone`, `overlay`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (the `PHASE_VIEW` default takes the new fields; the atom becomes per pane in Task 1.7)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `phaseName`, `truncate` from `hooks/model.ts`; `parsePhases` and `tests/fixtures/phases.ts` `PHASES_WAITING` (test only).
- Produces: everything in `hooks/phases.ts` listed above.

Rules the model encodes (spec §5):
- **Order:** All, then a remembered choice the list lacks (its bare id), the active phase, planned phases by `order`, done and archived phases newest first (by date, then order), then no task.
- **Done toggle:** `◐ done` defaults to hidden once more than 4 done phases exist. The chosen tab always shows.
- **Steps:** ‹ › step over the shown tabs and stop at the ends; there is no wrap.
- **What fits:** the chosen tab always fits; then come All and the rest in order, until one does not fit or ten are drawn.
- **Digits:** 0 is All; 1–9 are the visible tabs after All.
- **Fallback:** only once the list is read, a lost choice falls back to All.
- **Store:** review's legacy `phase:<root>` moves to `phase:review:<root>` on first read.

```ts
// User intent: pin the phase strip's model (spec §5) without drawing — order, both-way steps that stop at the ends, digits,
// what fits, the done toggle, the fallback when a remembered phase is gone, and the store key that moved per pane.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { parsePhases } from '../hooks/parse'
import {
  ALL,
  atEnd,
  emptyLine,
  FRESH_PHASE_VIEW,
  fallback,
  isDoneShown,
  jump,
  legacyPhaseKey,
  loadChoice,
  MAX_VISIBLE,
  NO_TASK,
  orderPhases,
  overlayLine,
  type PhaseStore,
  phaseKey,
  saveChoice,
  shownTabs,
  step,
  storedPhase,
  tabCells,
  tabLabel,
  visibleTabs,
  withClosed,
  withCounts,
} from '../hooks/phases'
import type { TmPhase } from '../types'
import { PHASES_WAITING } from './fixtures/phases'

const phase = (id: string, status: string, order: number, count: number, extra: Partial<TmPhase> = {}): TmPhase => ({
  id,
  status,
  order,
  count,
  name: `${id.toUpperCase()} — scope`,
  ...extra,
})
const REAL = ((): TmPhase[] => {
  const parsed = parsePhases(PHASES_WAITING)
  if (!parsed.ok) throw new Error(parsed.reason)
  return parsed.value
})()
const ids = (tabs: readonly { id: string }[]): string[] => tabs.map(t => t.id)

function memoryStore(seed: Record<string, unknown> = {}): PhaseStore & { map: Map<string, unknown> } {
  const map = new Map(Object.entries(seed))
  return {
    map,
    get: async key => map.get(key),
    set: async (key, value) => {
      map.set(key, value)
    },
    delete: async key => {
      map.delete(key)
    },
  }
}

describe('order', () => {
  test('All, the active phase, planned by order, done newest first, then no task; names are the short names', () => {
    const tabs = orderPhases(REAL, 31)
    expect(tabs[0]).toEqual({ id: ALL, name: 'All', kind: 'all', count: 31, date: '', dim: false })
    expect(ids(tabs).slice(0, 12)).toEqual([
      ALL,
      '2-0-4',
      'patch-1-9-5',
      'alpha-followup',
      'engine-polish-post',
      'p2',
      'patch-1-9-8',
      'p3',
      'studio',
      'research',
      '2-0-6',
      '2-1-0',
    ])
    // done and archived phases, no dates in this table: highest order first; ties keep the server's order
    expect(ids(tabs).slice(12)).toEqual(['2-0-3', '2-0-2', '2-0-1', 'patch-1-9-3', 'patch-1-9-6', 'patch-1-9-7', 'patch-1-9-4', 'engine-polish', 'p1'])
    expect(tabLabel(tabs[1]!)).toBe('Patch 2.0.4 · active 249')
    expect(tabLabel(tabs[2]!)).toBe('Patch 1.9.5 47')
    expect(tabs.find(t => t.id === 'alpha-followup')).toMatchObject({ name: 'Alpha Patches', dim: true })
    expect(tabLabel(tabs.find(t => t.id === '2-0-3')!)).toBe('Patch 2.0.3 ✓ 1')
  })

  test('done phases with dates sort newest first; no task comes last; a choice the list lacks shows as its bare id after All', () => {
    const tabs = orderPhases(
      [
        phase('old', 'done', 5, 0, { date: '2026-09-01' }),
        phase('new', 'done', 1, 2, { date: '2026-10-01' }),
        { id: NO_TASK, status: 'none', order: 0, count: 3, name: 'no task' },
        phase('a', 'active', 3, 1),
      ],
      6,
      'gone',
    )
    expect(ids(tabs)).toEqual([ALL, 'gone', 'a', 'new', 'old', NO_TASK])
    expect(tabs[1]).toMatchObject({ name: 'gone', dim: true, count: 0 })
    expect(tabLabel(tabs[5]!)).toBe('no task 3')
    expect(ids(orderPhases([], 0, NO_TASK))).toEqual([ALL, NO_TASK])
    expect(orderPhases([], 0, NO_TASK)[1]?.name).toBe('no task')
  })
})

describe('done toggle and visibility', () => {
  test('done shows by default up to four done phases; the toggle wins; a chosen done phase stays shown', () => {
    const real = orderPhases(REAL, 31)
    expect(isDoneShown(real, null)).toBe(false)
    expect(isDoneShown(real, true)).toBe(true)
    const few = orderPhases([phase('d1', 'done', 1, 0), phase('a', 'active', 2, 1)], 1)
    expect(isDoneShown(few, null)).toBe(true)
    expect(isDoneShown(few, false)).toBe(false)
    expect(ids(shownTabs(real, ALL, false))).not.toContain('2-0-3')
    expect(ids(shownTabs(real, '2-0-3', false))).toContain('2-0-3')
    expect(shownTabs(real, ALL, true)).toHaveLength(real.length)
  })

  test('the chosen tab always fits; then tabs in order, All first, until one does not fit, ten at most', () => {
    const shown = shownTabs(orderPhases(REAL, 31), '2-0-6', false)
    const tight = visibleTabs(shown, '2-0-6', 0, false)
    expect(ids(tight.tabs)).toEqual(['2-0-6'])
    expect(tight.hidden).toHaveLength(shown.length - 1)
    const room = tabCells(shown[0]!, true) + tabCells(shown.find(t => t.id === '2-0-6')!, true) + tabCells(shown[1]!, true)
    expect(ids(visibleTabs(shown, '2-0-6', room, true).tabs)).toEqual([ALL, '2-0-4', '2-0-6'])
    expect(visibleTabs(shown, ALL, 10_000, true).tabs).toHaveLength(MAX_VISIBLE)
    expect(tabCells(shown[1]!, true) - tabCells(shown[1]!, false)).toBe(3)
  })

  test('digit 0 is All; digit n is the n-th visible tab after All; a digit past the drawn tabs is nothing', () => {
    const fitted = visibleTabs(shownTabs(orderPhases(REAL, 31), ALL, false), ALL, 10_000, true)
    expect(jump(fitted, 0)).toBe(ALL)
    expect(jump(fitted, 1)).toBe('2-0-4')
    expect(jump(fitted, 2)).toBe('patch-1-9-5')
    expect(jump(visibleTabs(orderPhases([], 0), ALL, 100, true), 3)).toBeNull()
    expect(jump(visibleTabs(shownTabs(orderPhases(REAL, 31), '2-0-6', false), '2-0-6', 0, true), 0)).toBe(ALL)
  })
})

describe('stepping', () => {
  test('‹ and › walk the shown tabs both ways and stop at the ends; an unknown choice steps to All', () => {
    const shown = shownTabs(orderPhases(REAL, 31), ALL, false)
    expect(step(shown, ALL, 1)).toBe('2-0-4')
    expect(step(shown, '2-0-4', 1)).toBe('patch-1-9-5')
    expect(step(shown, 'patch-1-9-5', -1)).toBe('2-0-4')
    expect(step(shown, ALL, -1)).toBe(ALL)
    expect(atEnd(shown, ALL, -1)).toBe(true)
    expect(atEnd(shown, ALL, 1)).toBe(false)
    expect(step(shown, '2-1-0', 1)).toBe('2-1-0')
    expect(atEnd(shown, '2-1-0', 1)).toBe(true)
    expect(step(shown, 'nowhere', 1)).toBe(ALL)
    expect(atEnd(shown, 'nowhere', -1)).toBe(true)
  })
})

describe('fallback and words', () => {
  test('a remembered phase the read list lacks falls back to All, naming it; before the list is read nothing falls back', () => {
    const loaded = { ...FRESH_PHASE_VIEW, loaded: true, phases: REAL }
    expect(fallback({ ...loaded, choice: '2-0-6' })).toEqual({ choice: '2-0-6', lost: '' })
    expect(fallback({ ...loaded, choice: 'gone' })).toEqual({ choice: ALL, lost: 'gone' })
    expect(fallback({ ...loaded, choice: ALL })).toEqual({ choice: ALL, lost: '' })
    expect(fallback({ ...FRESH_PHASE_VIEW, choice: 'gone' })).toEqual({ choice: 'gone', lost: '' })
  })

  test('the empty line names the phase and, for a done one, its date; the overlay line lists name, status, count, date', () => {
    const [done] = orderPhases([phase('p18', 'done', 1, 0, { date: '2026-10-05', name: 'Patch 1.8 — Demo scope' })], 0).slice(1)
    expect(emptyLine('Nothing waiting', done)).toBe('Nothing waiting in Patch 1.8 · done 2026-10-05')
    expect(emptyLine('Nothing waiting', orderPhases([], 4)[0])).toBe('Nothing waiting')
    expect(emptyLine('No sessions', undefined)).toBe('No sessions')
    expect(overlayLine(done!, 80)).toBe('Patch 1.8 · done · 0 · 2026-10-05')
    expect(overlayLine(orderPhases([], 9)[0]!, 80)).toBe('All · every phase · 9')
    expect(overlayLine(done!, 10)).toBe('Patch 1.8…')
  })

  test('restore recounts the phases from its own counts and adds no task; handovers add closed when ◐ closed is on', () => {
    const phases = [phase('a', 'active', 2, 0), phase('b', 'planned', 3, 0)]
    expect(withCounts(phases, new Map([['a', 4], [NO_TASK, 2]]))).toEqual([
      { ...phases[0]!, count: 4 },
      { ...phases[1]!, count: 0 },
      { id: NO_TASK, status: 'none', order: 0, count: 2, name: 'no task' },
    ])
    expect(withCounts(phases, new Map()).map(p => p.id)).toEqual(['a', 'b'])
    expect(withClosed([phase('a', 'active', 1, 2, { closed: 3 }), phase('b', 'planned', 2, 1)]).map(p => p.count)).toEqual([5, 1])
  })
})

describe('the stored choice', () => {
  test('a stored choice is a string, empty included; anything else is no choice', () => {
    expect(storedPhase({ phase: 'r15' })).toBe('r15')
    expect(storedPhase({ phase: '' })).toBe('')
    expect(storedPhase(undefined)).toBeNull()
    expect(storedPhase({ phase: 3 })).toBeNull()
    expect(storedPhase('r15')).toBeNull()
  })

  test('each pane keeps its own key; review moves the legacy phase:<root> to phase:review:<root> on first read', async () => {
    const root = 'C:\\work\\proj'
    expect(phaseKey('handovers', root)).toBe('phase:handovers:C:\\work\\proj')
    const store = memoryStore({ [legacyPhaseKey(root)]: { phase: '2-0-6' } })
    expect(await loadChoice(store, 'handovers', root)).toBeNull()
    expect(store.map.has(legacyPhaseKey(root))).toBe(true)
    expect(await loadChoice(store, 'review', root)).toBe('2-0-6')
    expect(store.map.get(phaseKey('review', root))).toEqual({ phase: '2-0-6' })
    expect(store.map.has(legacyPhaseKey(root))).toBe(false)
    await saveChoice(store, 'restore', root, NO_TASK)
    expect(await loadChoice(store, 'restore', root)).toBe(NO_TASK)
    const both = memoryStore({ [legacyPhaseKey(root)]: { phase: 'old' }, [phaseKey('review', root)]: { phase: '' } })
    expect(await loadChoice(both, 'review', root)).toBe('')
    expect(both.map.has(legacyPhaseKey(root))).toBe(true)
  })
})
```
