- [ ] **Step 3: Write the model and extend the types**

`mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/phases.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: the phase strip's behaviour as plain functions shared by review, handovers and restore (spec §5) — which tabs,
// in what order, what fits, where ‹ › and the digits land, what a remembered choice falls back to and where it is kept — so
// the strip moves both ways and is tested without drawing.
import type { TmPhase, TmPhasePane, TmPhaseView } from '../types'
import { phaseName, truncate } from './model'

/** The All tab's id (and the stored "every phase" choice). */
export const ALL = ''
/** The trailing "no task" tab's id (handovers, restore): what links no task. */
export const NO_TASK = '~none'
/** `◐ done` is off by default once more done phases than this exist. */
export const DONE_HIDE_OVER = 4
/** All plus nine: the digits 0–9. */
export const MAX_VISIBLE = 10
export const PHASE_PANES: readonly TmPhasePane[] = ['review', 'handovers', 'restore']
export const FRESH_PHASE_VIEW: TmPhaseView = { choice: ALL, phases: [], loaded: false, showDone: null, overlay: false }
export const FRESH_PHASE_VIEWS: Readonly<Record<TmPhasePane, TmPhaseView>> = {
  review: FRESH_PHASE_VIEW,
  handovers: FRESH_PHASE_VIEW,
  restore: FRESH_PHASE_VIEW,
}

export type PhaseTabKind = 'all' | 'active' | 'planned' | 'done' | 'none'
/** One tab of the strip: `dim` for an empty or done phase (drawn dim, still selectable). */
export type PhaseTab = {
  readonly id: string
  readonly name: string
  readonly kind: PhaseTabKind
  readonly count: number
  readonly date: string
  readonly dim: boolean
}
/** The tabs the strip draws, in order, and the shown ones that did not fit (they live behind `+N ▾`). */
export type StripFit = { readonly tabs: readonly PhaseTab[]; readonly hidden: readonly PhaseTab[] }

function tabOf(p: TmPhase): PhaseTab {
  const kind: PhaseTabKind =
    p.id === NO_TASK ? 'none' : p.status === 'active' ? 'active' : p.status === 'done' || p.status === 'archived' ? 'done' : 'planned'
  return { id: p.id, name: kind === 'none' ? 'no task' : phaseName(p), kind, count: p.count, date: p.date ?? '', dim: kind === 'done' || p.count === 0 }
}

/**
 * The strip's order: All, a choice the list does not hold (its bare id, until the list says), the active phase, planned
 * phases by `order`, done phases newest first (by date, then order), then no task.
 */
export function orderPhases(phases: readonly TmPhase[], allCount: number, choice: string = ALL): PhaseTab[] {
  const tabs = phases.map(tabOf)
  const order = new Map(phases.map(p => [p.id, p.order]))
  const ord = (tab: PhaseTab): number => order.get(tab.id) ?? 0
  const of = (kind: PhaseTabKind): PhaseTab[] => tabs.filter(tab => tab.kind === kind)
  const planned = of('planned').sort((a, b) => ord(a) - ord(b))
  const done = of('done').sort((a, b) => (a.date === b.date ? ord(b) - ord(a) : a.date < b.date ? 1 : -1))
  const all: PhaseTab = { id: ALL, name: 'All', kind: 'all', count: allCount, date: '', dim: false }
  const unknown: PhaseTab[] =
    choice === ALL || tabs.some(tab => tab.id === choice)
      ? []
      : [{ id: choice, name: choice === NO_TASK ? 'no task' : choice, kind: choice === NO_TASK ? 'none' : 'planned', count: 0, date: '', dim: true }]
  return [all, ...unknown, ...of('active'), ...planned, ...done, ...of('none')]
}

/** `◐ done`: the person's toggle, else on unless more than DONE_HIDE_OVER done phases exist. */
export function isDoneShown(tabs: readonly PhaseTab[], toggle: boolean | null): boolean {
  return toggle ?? tabs.filter(tab => tab.kind === 'done').length <= DONE_HIDE_OVER
}

/** The tabs ‹ › walk: every tab, less the done ones while `◐ done` is off; the chosen tab always stays. */
export function shownTabs(tabs: readonly PhaseTab[], choice: string, showDone: boolean): PhaseTab[] {
  return tabs.filter(tab => showDone || tab.kind !== 'done' || tab.id === choice)
}

/** A tab's words: `All 31`, `Patch 1.9 · active 12`, `Release 2.0 4`, `Patch 1.8 ✓ 0`, `no task 3`. */
export function tabLabel(tab: PhaseTab): string {
  const name = tab.kind === 'active' ? `${tab.name} · active` : tab.kind === 'done' ? `${tab.name} ✓` : tab.name
  return `${name} ${tab.count}`
}

/** Cells a tab takes in the strip: its digit glyph when shown, its label, and the ` │ ` before it. */
export function tabCells(tab: PhaseTab, digit: boolean): number {
  return (digit ? 3 : 0) + tabLabel(tab).length + 3
}

/**
 * What fits in `room`: the chosen tab always, then the others in order (All first) until one does not fit or ten are
 * drawn; the rest go behind `+N ▾`.
 */
export function visibleTabs(shown: readonly PhaseTab[], choice: string, room: number, digits: boolean): StripFit {
  const keep = new Set<string>()
  let used = 0
  const chosen = shown.find(tab => tab.id === choice)
  if (chosen !== undefined) {
    keep.add(chosen.id)
    used += tabCells(chosen, digits)
  }
  for (const tab of shown) {
    if (keep.has(tab.id)) continue
    const need = tabCells(tab, digits)
    if (used + need > room || keep.size >= MAX_VISIBLE) break
    keep.add(tab.id)
    used += need
  }
  return { tabs: shown.filter(tab => keep.has(tab.id)), hidden: shown.filter(tab => !keep.has(tab.id)) }
}

/** ‹ (-1) or › (+1) from the chosen tab over the shown tabs; no wrap (the end stays put); an unknown choice steps to All. */
export function step(shown: readonly PhaseTab[], choice: string, dir: -1 | 1): string {
  const at = shown.findIndex(tab => tab.id === choice)
  if (at < 0) return ALL
  return shown[at + dir]?.id ?? choice
}

/** True when ‹ or › has nowhere to go (its keycap dims). */
export function atEnd(shown: readonly PhaseTab[], choice: string, dir: -1 | 1): boolean {
  const at = shown.findIndex(tab => tab.id === choice)
  return at < 0 ? dir === -1 : shown[at + dir] === undefined
}

/** Digit `n`: 0 is All; 1–9 the visible tabs after All, in order; null when no tab carries it. */
export function jump(visible: StripFit, n: number): string | null {
  if (n === 0) return ALL
  return visible.tabs.filter(tab => tab.id !== ALL)[n - 1]?.id ?? null
}

/** A remembered choice the read list no longer holds falls back to All; `lost` names it ('' when kept). */
export function fallback(view: TmPhaseView): { readonly choice: string; readonly lost: string } {
  if (!view.loaded || view.choice === ALL || view.phases.some(p => p.id === view.choice)) return { choice: view.choice, lost: '' }
  return { choice: ALL, lost: view.choice }
}

/** An empty selection's line: `Nothing waiting in Patch 1.8 · done 2026-10-05`; just `<empty>` under All. */
export function emptyLine(empty: string, tab: PhaseTab | undefined): string {
  if (tab === undefined || tab.kind === 'all') return empty
  return `${empty} in ${tab.name}${tab.kind === 'done' && tab.date !== '' ? ` · done ${tab.date}` : ''}`
}

const KIND_WORD: Readonly<Record<PhaseTabKind, string>> = {
  all: 'every phase',
  active: 'active',
  planned: 'planned',
  done: 'done',
  none: 'no linked task',
}

/** The overlay list's line: `Patch 1.8 · done · 0 · 2026-10-05`, cut to `width`. */
export function overlayLine(tab: PhaseTab, width: number): string {
  return truncate([tab.name, KIND_WORD[tab.kind], String(tab.count), ...(tab.date === '' ? [] : [tab.date])].join(' · '), width)
}

/** Restore's phases: the list's phases with the pane's own counts (absent: 0), and a no task tab when the counts name one. */
export function withCounts(phases: readonly TmPhase[], counts: ReadonlyMap<string, number>): TmPhase[] {
  const counted = phases.filter(p => p.id !== NO_TASK).map(p => ({ ...p, count: counts.get(p.id) ?? 0 }))
  const none = counts.get(NO_TASK)
  return none === undefined ? counted : [...counted, { id: NO_TASK, status: 'none', order: 0, count: none, name: 'no task' }]
}

/** Handovers with `◐ closed` on: each tab counts its open and closed handovers. */
export function withClosed(phases: readonly TmPhase[]): TmPhase[] {
  return phases.map(p => ({ ...p, count: p.count + (p.closed ?? 0) }))
}

// ── Where a choice is kept: `$.store` `phase:<pane>:<repo root>`, machine-wide, one per pane and project ────────────────

export const phaseKey = (pane: TmPhasePane, root: string): string => `phase:${pane}:${root}`
/** Before the strip, review kept its filter at `phase:<repo root>`; the first read moves it. */
export const legacyPhaseKey = (root: string): string => `phase:${root}`

/** A stored choice: its id ('' All, kept as an explicit choice), or null for none / unreadable. */
export function storedPhase(value: unknown): string | null {
  const phase = typeof value === 'object' && value !== null ? (value as { phase?: unknown }).phase : undefined
  return typeof phase === 'string' ? phase : null
}

export type PhaseStore = {
  readonly get: (key: string) => Promise<unknown>
  readonly set: (key: string, value: unknown) => Promise<void>
  readonly delete: (key: string) => Promise<void>
}

export const phaseStoreOf = (host: {
  readonly storeGet: (key: string) => Promise<unknown>
  readonly storeSet: (key: string, value: unknown) => Promise<void>
  readonly storeDelete: (key: string) => Promise<void>
}): PhaseStore => ({ get: key => host.storeGet(key), set: (key, value) => host.storeSet(key, value), delete: key => host.storeDelete(key) })

/** A pane's remembered choice, or null; review's legacy key moves to `phase:review:<root>` on this first read. */
export async function loadChoice(store: PhaseStore, pane: TmPhasePane, root: string): Promise<string | null> {
  const stored = storedPhase(await store.get(phaseKey(pane, root)))
  if (stored !== null || pane !== 'review') return stored
  const legacy = storedPhase(await store.get(legacyPhaseKey(root)))
  if (legacy === null) return null
  await store.set(phaseKey('review', root), { phase: legacy })
  await store.delete(legacyPhaseKey(root))
  return legacy
}

export async function saveChoice(store: PhaseStore, pane: TmPhasePane, root: string, choice: string): Promise<void> {
  await store.set(phaseKey(pane, root), { phase: choice })
}
```

```ts
// User intent: the phase strip's data contract per pane (spec §5) — one backlog_query a pane, the exact SQL, 6-cell rows
// with closed and date read back, the handovers' no task row read on its own — tested against the server's table shape.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import type { TmHost } from '../hooks/host'
import { parsePhases } from '../hooks/parse'
import { NO_TASK } from '../hooks/phases'
import { NO_TASK_SQL, phaseSql, readPhases } from '../hooks/tm'
import { NO_TASK_ROW, PHASES_DATED, PHASES_WAITING } from './fixtures/phases'

const DATE =
  "CASE WHEN p.status IN ('done','archived') THEN substr(coalesce(json_extract(p.doc,'$.completed'),''),1,10) ELSE substr(coalesce(json_extract(p.doc,'$.start_date'),''),1,10) END"
const TAIL = " AS phase, json_extract(p.doc,'$.name') AS name FROM entities p WHERE p.kind='phase' AND p.deleted=0 ORDER BY json_extract(p.doc,'$.order') DESC"

/** A TmHost that answers backlog_query from `answer` and keeps what it was asked and logged; nothing else is used. */
function hostOf(answer: (sql: string) => { text: string; isError?: boolean } | 'throw'): { host: TmHost; asked: string[]; logs: string[] } {
  const asked: string[] = []
  const logs: string[] = []
  const host = {
    sleep: (_ms: number, signal?: AbortSignal) =>
      new Promise<void>((_resolve, reject) => signal?.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(new Error('aborted')))),
    call: async (_tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      const sql = String(args.sql)
      asked.push(sql)
      const a = answer(sql)
      if (a === 'throw') throw new Error('tm: Connection closed')
      return { text: a.text, isError: a.isError === true }
    },
    log: (text: string) => void logs.push(text),
  } as unknown as TmHost
  return { host, asked, logs }
}

describe('phase queries per pane', () => {
  test('review counts its scope, with closed 0 and the date; restore counts nothing; handovers count linked open and closed', () => {
    expect(phaseSql('review', true)).toBe(
      `SELECT json_array(p.id, p.status, json_extract(p.doc,'$.order'), (SELECT count(*) FROM entities t WHERE t.kind='task' AND t.deleted=0 AND t.status='in-review' AND json_extract(t.doc,'$.phase')=p.id AND coalesce(json_extract(t.doc,'$.human_action'),'')<>''), 0, ${DATE})${TAIL}`,
    )
    expect(phaseSql('review', false)).not.toContain('human_action')
    expect(phaseSql('restore', true)).toBe(`SELECT json_array(p.id, p.status, json_extract(p.doc,'$.order'), 0, 0, ${DATE})${TAIL}`)
    const handovers = phaseSql('handovers', true)
    expect(handovers).toContain("FROM handover_tasks ht JOIN entities t ON t.kind='task' AND t.id=ht.task_id AND t.deleted=0")
    expect(handovers).toContain("ho.status='open' WHERE json_extract(t.doc,'$.phase')=p.id)")
    expect(handovers).toContain("ho.status='closed' WHERE json_extract(t.doc,'$.phase')=p.id)")
    expect(handovers.endsWith(TAIL)).toBe(true)
    expect(handovers).not.toContain('UNION')
    expect(NO_TASK_SQL).toContain(`json_array('${NO_TASK}', 'none', 0,`)
    expect(NO_TASK_SQL).toContain('NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM handover_tasks ht WHERE ht.handover_id=ho.id)')
  })

  test('6-cell rows read closed and date; the older 4-cell rows read without them', () => {
    const dated = parsePhases(PHASES_DATED)
    if (!dated.ok) throw new Error(dated.reason)
    expect(dated.value[0]).toEqual({ id: '2-0-4', status: 'active', order: 14, count: 249, closed: 0, date: '2026-10-01', name: 'Patch 2.0.4 — Alpha Scope & Polish' })
    expect(dated.value[1]).toMatchObject({ id: '2-0-3', closed: 2, date: '2026-09-28' })
    expect(dated.value[2]).toMatchObject({ id: '2-1-0', date: '' })
    const old = parsePhases(PHASES_WAITING)
    if (!old.ok) throw new Error(old.reason)
    expect('date' in (old.value[0] ?? {})).toBe(false)
    expect('closed' in (old.value[0] ?? {})).toBe(false)
  })
})

describe('readPhases', () => {
  test('review and restore ask once with their own query', async () => {
    for (const pane of ['review', 'restore'] as const) {
      const { host, asked } = hostOf(() => ({ text: PHASES_DATED }))
      expect((await readPhases(host, pane, true))?.map(p => p.id)).toEqual(['2-0-4', '2-0-3', '2-1-0'])
      expect(asked).toEqual([phaseSql(pane, true)])
    }
  })

  test('handovers read the phases, then the no task row, and append it', async () => {
    const { host, asked } = hostOf(sql => ({ text: sql === NO_TASK_SQL ? NO_TASK_ROW : PHASES_DATED }))
    const phases = await readPhases(host, 'handovers', true)
    expect(asked).toEqual([phaseSql('handovers', true), NO_TASK_SQL])
    expect(phases?.at(-1)).toEqual({ id: NO_TASK, status: 'none', order: 0, count: 3, closed: 5, date: '', name: 'no task' })
  })

  test('a refused or failing no task read keeps the phases without that tab, and logs', async () => {
    for (const fail of ['refused', 'throw'] as const) {
      const { host, logs } = hostOf(sql =>
        sql === NO_TASK_SQL ? (fail === 'throw' ? 'throw' : { text: 'Error: NOT EXISTS is not allowed' }) : { text: PHASES_DATED },
      )
      expect((await readPhases(host, 'handovers', true))?.map(p => p.id), fail).toEqual(['2-0-4', '2-0-3', '2-1-0'])
      expect(logs.length, fail).toBe(1)
    }
  })

  test('an unreadable or failing phase read is null and logs', async () => {
    for (const answer of [() => ({ text: 'not a table' }), () => 'throw' as const, () => ({ text: 'Error: no such table', isError: true })]) {
      const { host, logs } = hostOf(answer)
      expect(await readPhases(host, 'review', true)).toBeNull()
      expect(logs.length).toBe(1)
    }
  })
})
```

In `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/tm.ts`, replace everything from the doc comment `/** The phase list query, newest phase first, …` (the old `phaseSql` and `readPhases`) to the end of the file with:

```ts
const PHASE_DATE =
  "CASE WHEN p.status IN ('done','archived') THEN substr(coalesce(json_extract(p.doc,'$.completed'),''),1,10) ELSE substr(coalesce(json_extract(p.doc,'$.start_date'),''),1,10) END"
const linkedHandovers = (status: 'open' | 'closed'): string =>
  `(SELECT count(DISTINCT ht.handover_id) FROM handover_tasks ht JOIN entities t ON t.kind='task' AND t.id=ht.task_id AND t.deleted=0 JOIN entities ho ON ho.kind='handover' AND ho.id=ht.handover_id AND ho.deleted=0 AND ho.status='${status}' WHERE json_extract(t.doc,'$.phase')=p.id)`
const unlinkedHandovers = (status: 'open' | 'closed'): string =>
  `(SELECT count(*) FROM entities ho WHERE ho.kind='handover' AND ho.deleted=0 AND ho.status='${status}' AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM handover_tasks ht WHERE ht.handover_id=ho.id))`

/**
 * One pane's phase list query (spec §5): a row a phase, `json_array(id, status, order, count, closed, date)` and the name,
 * newest first (each cell well under the server's 80-character cut). Review counts the in-review tasks its scope lists
 * (naming a human action when `waitingOnly`); handovers the open and closed handovers linked through each phase's tasks;
 * restore counts nothing here (its counts come from the scan, withCounts).
 */
export function phaseSql(pane: TmPhasePane, waitingOnly: boolean): string {
  const row = (count: string, closed: string): string =>
    `SELECT json_array(p.id, p.status, json_extract(p.doc,'$.order'), ${count}, ${closed}, ${PHASE_DATE}) AS phase, json_extract(p.doc,'$.name') AS name FROM entities p WHERE p.kind='phase' AND p.deleted=0 ORDER BY json_extract(p.doc,'$.order') DESC`
  if (pane === 'handovers') return row(linkedHandovers('open'), linkedHandovers('closed'))
  if (pane === 'restore') return row('0', '0')
  const waiting = waitingOnly ? " AND coalesce(json_extract(t.doc,'$.human_action'),'')<>''" : ''
  return row(
    `(SELECT count(*) FROM entities t WHERE t.kind='task' AND t.deleted=0 AND t.status='in-review' AND json_extract(t.doc,'$.phase')=p.id${waiting})`,
    '0',
  )
}

/** The handovers strip's `no task` row, read on its own: open and closed handovers that link no task. */
export const NO_TASK_SQL = `SELECT json_array('${NO_TASK}', 'none', 0, ${unlinkedHandovers('open')}, ${unlinkedHandovers('closed')}, '') AS phase, 'no task' AS name`

/**
 * One pane's phase list, from one backlog_query (handovers: a second for its no task row). Null when the server refuses, is
 * unreachable or answers unreadably (the reason goes to the debug log), so the strip stays where it was; a handovers list
 * whose no task read fails keeps its phases without that tab.
 */
export async function readPhases(host: TmHost, pane: TmPhasePane, waitingOnly: boolean): Promise<readonly TmPhase[] | null> {
  const read = async (sql: string): Promise<TmPhase[] | null> => {
    const text = (await callTm(host, 'backlog_query', { sql, limit: 200 })).text
    const parsed = parsePhases(text)
    if (parsed.ok) return parsed.value
    host.log(`taskmaster-mods: unreadable backlog_query reply: ${text.slice(0, 2000)}`)
    return null
  }
  try {
    const phases = await read(phaseSql(pane, waitingOnly))
    if (phases === null || pane !== 'handovers') return phases
    try {
      const none = await read(NO_TASK_SQL)
      return none === null ? phases : [...phases, ...none.filter(p => p.id === NO_TASK)]
    } catch (error) {
      host.log(`taskmaster-mods: no task phase row not read: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`)
      return phases
    }
  } catch (error) {
    host.log(`taskmaster-mods: phases not read: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`)
    return null
  }
}
```

### Task 1.6: Drawing the strip: tabs, ‹ ›, `+N ▾`, `◐ done`, the overlay, the empty line

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/kit.tsx` (imports; append the strip section)
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/strip.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1.4's model (`orderPhases`, `isDoneShown`, `shownTabs`, `visibleTabs`, `tabCells`, `tabLabel`, `step`, `atEnd`, `emptyLine`, `overlayLine`, `ALL`); Task 1.2's `keycap`, `label`, `helpFooter`.
- Produces: `StripView`, `StripHandlers`, `OVERLAY_PLACEMENT`, `OVERLAY_LIST`, `phaseStrip`, `phaseOverlay`, `phaseEmpty`, `phaseSection` (signatures above).

Behaviour:
- **Fixed slots:** ‹ › and `+N ▾` always stay, and dim instead of vanishing. `◐ done` gives way first when room is short, then All does, then a long chosen label is cut, so the strip never runs past its width.
- **Digits:** shown only while the pane holds the keyboard. Withheld while the overlay is open, or while the pane passes `digits: false` (review until Stage 3 moves the ticks to `t` + digit).
- **Overlay:** lists every phase, done ones included. A pick chooses and closes. `f` toggles it.

```ts
// User intent: pin how the phase strip draws (spec §5) — real buttons for tabs, ‹ › that dim at the ends, digits only while
// the pane is focused, `+N ▾` for what does not fit, `◐ done`, the overlay list and the empty line with `0 All`.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { OVERLAY_LIST, OVERLAY_PLACEMENT, phaseEmpty, phaseOverlay, phaseSection, phaseStrip, type StripView } from '../hooks/kit'
import { parsePhases } from '../hooks/parse'
import { ALL, FRESH_PHASE_VIEW } from '../hooks/phases'
import type { TmPhase } from '../types'
import { byKey, testKit, textsOf, walk } from './fixtures/kit-ui'
import { widthOf } from './fixtures/measure'
import { PHASES_WAITING } from './fixtures/phases'
import { STRONG } from './fixtures/tokens'

const REAL = ((): TmPhase[] => {
  const parsed = parsePhases(PHASES_WAITING)
  if (!parsed.ok) throw new Error(parsed.reason)
  return parsed.value
})()
const handlers = { choose: (): void => undefined, toggleDone: (): void => undefined, overlay: (): void => undefined }
const strip = (over: Partial<StripView['view']> = {}, rest: Partial<StripView> = {}): StripView => ({
  pane: 'review',
  view: { ...FRESH_PHASE_VIEW, phases: REAL, loaded: true, ...over },
  allCount: 31,
  focused: true,
  digits: true,
  empty: 'Nothing waiting',
  ...rest,
})
const tabKeys = (tree: unknown): string[] =>
  walk(tree)
    .map(e => e.props?.key)
    .filter((key): key is string => typeof key === 'string' && key.startsWith('phase-tab:') && !key.endsWith('-box'))

describe('phase strip', () => {
  test('tabs are keyed buttons with digits while focused; All is 0; the chosen tab carries the signature fill', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const tree = phaseStrip(k, strip({ choice: '2-0-4' }), handlers, 200)
    expect(tabKeys(tree).slice(0, 3)).toEqual(['phase-tab:', 'phase-tab:2-0-4', 'phase-tab:patch-1-9-5'])
    expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-tab:')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '0', label: 'All 31' })
    expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-tab:2-0-4')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '1', label: 'Patch 2.0.4 · active 249' })
    expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-tab:2-0-4-box')?.props?.backgroundColor).toBe(STRONG.signature)
    expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-tab:alpha-followup')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
    expect(textsOf(tree)).toContain('│')
  })

  test('no digits while the pane is unfocused, while digits belong to another row, or while the overlay is open', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    for (const s of [strip({}, { focused: false }), strip({}, { digits: false }), strip({ overlay: true })]) {
      expect(byKey(phaseStrip(k, s, handlers, 200), 'phase-tab:')?.props?.hotkey).toBeUndefined()
    }
  })

  test('‹ h and › l always drawn; ‹ dims on All, › dims on the last shown tab', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const first = phaseStrip(k, strip(), handlers, 200)
    expect(byKey(first, 'phase-prev')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'h', label: '‹', dimColor: true })
    expect(byKey(first, 'phase-next')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'l', label: '›' })
    expect(byKey(first, 'phase-next')?.props?.dimColor).toBeUndefined()
    const last = phaseStrip(k, strip({ choice: '2-1-0' }), handlers, 200)
    expect(byKey(last, 'phase-next')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
  })

  test('what does not fit goes behind `+N ▾` (f); done tabs hide over four done phases until ◐ done; the chosen tab always shows', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const narrow = phaseStrip(k, strip({ choice: '2-0-6' }), handlers, 60)
    expect(tabKeys(narrow)).toEqual(['phase-tab:', 'phase-tab:2-0-6'])
    expect(byKey(narrow, 'phase-more')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'f', label: `+${REAL.length - 1} ▾` })
    const wide = phaseStrip(k, strip(), handlers, 400)
    expect(tabKeys(wide)).not.toContain('phase-tab:2-0-3')
    expect(byKey(wide, 'phase-done')?.props?.label).toBe('○ done')
    expect(tabKeys(phaseStrip(k, strip({ showDone: true }), handlers, 400))).toHaveLength(10)
    expect(byKey(phaseStrip(k, strip({ showDone: true }), handlers, 400), 'phase-done')?.props?.label).toBe('◐ done')
    expect(tabKeys(phaseStrip(k, strip({ choice: 'p1' }), handlers, 400))).toContain('phase-tab:p1')
  })

  test('narrow: ◐ done gives way, then All; a long chosen tab is cut; the strip never runs past its width', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    for (const width of [72, 48, 36, 28]) {
      for (const choice of [ALL, '2-0-4']) {
        for (const focused of [true, false]) {
          const tree = phaseStrip(k, strip({ choice }, { focused }), handlers, width)
          expect(widthOf(tree), `${width} ${choice} ${focused}`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(width)
        }
      }
    }
    expect(byKey(phaseStrip(k, strip(), handlers, 200), 'phase-done')).toBeDefined()
    const tight = phaseStrip(k, strip({ choice: '2-0-4' }), handlers, 36)
    expect(byKey(tight, 'phase-done')).toBeUndefined()
    expect(byKey(tight, 'phase-tab:2-0-4')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '1' })
    expect(String(byKey(tight, 'phase-tab:2-0-4')?.props?.label)).toMatch(/…$/)
  })

  test('nothing read yet: All alone, plus the remembered choice as its bare id; `▾` still opens the overlay', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const tree = phaseStrip(k, strip({ phases: [], loaded: false, choice: '2-0-6' }), handlers, 200)
    expect(tabKeys(tree)).toEqual(['phase-tab:', 'phase-tab:2-0-6'])
    expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-tab:2-0-6')?.props?.label).toBe('2-0-6 0')
    expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-more')?.props?.label).toBe('▾')
  })
})

describe('overlay and empty line', () => {
  test('the overlay lists every phase, done ones included, with status, count and date; the chosen one is focused', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const tree = phaseOverlay(k, strip({ choice: '2-0-6', overlay: true }), handlers, 80)
    expect(textsOf(tree)).toContain('PHASES')
    expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-close')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'f', label: 'close' })
    if (OVERLAY_LIST === 'buttons') {
      expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-pick:')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '0', label: 'All · every phase · 31' })
      expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-pick:2-0-3')?.props?.label).toBe('Patch 2.0.3 · done · 1')
      expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-pick:2-0-6')?.props?.autoFocus).toBe(true)
    } else {
      expect(byKey(tree, 'phase-select')?.props?.value).toBe('2-0-6')
    }
  })

  test('phaseSection draws strip then body; while the overlay is open it draws the overlay per OVERLAY_PLACEMENT', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const body = [phaseEmpty(k, strip(), handlers)]
    expect(phaseSection(k, strip(), handlers, 120, body)).toHaveLength(2)
    const open = phaseSection(k, strip({ overlay: true }), handlers, 120, body)
    if (OVERLAY_PLACEMENT === 'swap') {
      expect(open).toHaveLength(2)
      expect(textsOf(open[1])).toContain('PHASES')
    } else {
      expect(walk(open).some(e => e.props?.position === 'absolute')).toBe(true)
    }
  })

  test('an empty selection names the phase and offers 0 All; under All it is the bare words', () => {
    const k = testKit()
    const filtered = phaseEmpty(k, strip({ choice: '2-0-6' }), handlers)
    expect(textsOf(filtered)).toContain('Nothing waiting in Patch 2.0.6')
    expect(byKey(filtered, 'phase-all')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '0', label: 'All' })
    const all = phaseEmpty(k, strip({ choice: ALL }), handlers)
    expect(textsOf(all)).toEqual(['Nothing waiting'])
    expect(byKey(all, 'phase-all')).toBeUndefined()
  })
})
```

```tsx
import type { TmPhasePane, TmPhaseView } from '../types'
import { truncate } from './model'
import { ALL, atEnd, emptyLine, isDoneShown, orderPhases, overlayLine, shownTabs, step, tabCells, tabLabel, visibleTabs } from './phases'
```

Append to `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/kit.tsx`:

```tsx
// ── The phase strip (spec §5): shared by review, handovers and restore ──────────────────────────────────────────────

/** One pane's strip as it draws: its phase view, the All count, and whether the digits are its own right now. */
export type StripView = {
  readonly pane: TmPhasePane
  readonly view: TmPhaseView
  /** The All tab's count: the pane's own total (review waiting, handovers open, restore sessions in scope). */
  readonly allCount: number
  /** The pane holds the keyboard: tabs carry 0–9 and show them. */
  readonly focused: boolean
  /** False while another row owns the digits (review's `t` + digit): tabs carry no digit. */
  readonly digits: boolean
  /** The pane's words for an empty selection: `Nothing waiting`, `No open handovers`, `No sessions`. */
  readonly empty: string
  /** The ground the strip sits on; default page. */
  readonly on?: RrGround
}

export type StripHandlers = {
  /** A tab, ‹ ›, a digit, an overlay pick or `0 All` chose this phase id. */
  readonly choose: (phaseId: string) => void
  readonly toggleDone: () => void
  readonly overlay: (open: boolean) => void
}

/**
 * Where the overlay list draws (spec §14 item 2, Task 0.2): `swap` replaces the pane body below the strip while open (nothing
 * hides behind it); `absolute` lays it over the body.
 */
export const OVERLAY_PLACEMENT: 'swap' | 'absolute' = 'swap'
/** The overlay's list: keycap rows (0–9, Tab + Enter) or one Select (↑↓ + Enter), per Task 0.2. */
export const OVERLAY_LIST: 'buttons' | 'select' = 'buttons'
const SELECT_ALL = '*'
const moreLabel = (hidden: number): string => (hidden > 0 ? `+${hidden} ▾` : '▾')

/**
 * The strip: `‹ h` · tabs (digits while focused: 0 All, then 1–9 in order) · `+N ▾ f` · `› l`, then `◐ done` at the right.
 * Tabs are real buttons; ‹ › dim at the ends (no wrap); empty and done tabs draw dim but choose all the same; the chosen tab
 * carries the signature fill.
 */
export function phaseStrip(k: Kit, s: StripView, on: StripHandlers, width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const ground = s.on ?? 'page'
  const choice = s.view.choice
  const tabs = orderPhases(s.view.phases, s.allCount, choice)
  const showDone = isDoneShown(tabs, s.view.showDone)
  const shown = shownTabs(tabs, choice, showDone)
  const digits = s.focused && s.digits && !s.view.overlay
  const prev = keycap(k, {
    id: 'phase-prev',
    key: 'h',
    label: '‹',
    on: ground,
    onPress: () => on.choose(step(shown, choice, -1)),
    ...(atEnd(shown, choice, -1) ? { dim: 'Already on the first phase' } : {}),
  })
  const next = keycap(k, {
    id: 'phase-next',
    key: 'l',
    label: '›',
    on: ground,
    onPress: () => on.choose(step(shown, choice, 1)),
    ...(atEnd(shown, choice, 1) ? { dim: 'Already on the last phase' } : {}),
  })
  const done = keycap(k, { id: 'phase-done', label: `${showDone ? '◐' : '○'} done`, selected: showDone, on: ground, onPress: on.toggleDone })
  // ‹ › and `+N ▾` always stay; `◐ done` gives way when the chosen tab and All would not fit beside it; then All gives way;
  // a tab longer than the room left is cut, so the strip never runs past its width.
  const all = tabs[0]
  const chosen = shown.find(tab => tab.id === choice)
  const base = prev.width + next.width + 3 + moreLabel(tabs.length).length + 3
  const want = (chosen === undefined ? 0 : tabCells(chosen, digits)) + (choice === ALL || all === undefined ? 0 : tabCells(all, digits))
  const withDone = width - base - done.width - 1 >= want
  const room = Math.max(0, width - base - (withDone ? done.width + 1 : 0))
  const fitted = visibleTabs(shown, choice, room, digits)
  const keptAll = choice !== ALL && all !== undefined && fitted.tabs.some(tab => tab.id === ALL)
  const labelRoom = Math.max(3, room - (keptAll && all !== undefined ? tabCells(all, digits) : 0) - (digits ? 3 : 0) - 3)
  const others = fitted.tabs.filter(tab => tab.id !== ALL)
  const more = keycap(k, {
    id: 'phase-more',
    key: 'f',
    label: moreLabel(tabs.length - fitted.tabs.length),
    selected: s.view.overlay,
    on: ground,
    onPress: () => on.overlay(!s.view.overlay),
  })
  const cells = fitted.tabs.map(tab =>
    keycap(k, {
      id: `phase-tab:${tab.id}`,
      ...(digits ? { key: tab.id === ALL ? '0' : String(others.indexOf(tab) + 1) } : {}),
      label: truncate(tabLabel(tab), labelRoom),
      selected: tab.id === choice,
      faded: tab.dim,
      on: ground,
      onPress: () => on.choose(tab.id),
    }),
  )
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} alignItems="flex-start">
      {prev.el}
      {cells.flatMap((cell, i) => (i === 0 ? [cell.el] : [<Text color={k.t.border.default}>│</Text>, cell.el]))}
      {more.el}
      {next.el}
      <Box flexGrow={1} />
      {withDone ? done.el : null}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** The `+N ▾` / `f` overlay: every phase (done ones included) with status, count and date; a pick chooses and closes it. */
export function phaseOverlay(k: Kit, s: StripView, on: StripHandlers, width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Select } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const tabs = orderPhases(s.view.phases, s.allCount, s.view.choice)
  const room = Math.max(10, width - 4)
  const close = keycap(k, { id: 'phase-close', key: 'f', label: 'close', on: 'overlay', onPress: () => on.overlay(false) })
  const list =
    OVERLAY_LIST === 'select'
      ? [
          <Select
            key="phase-select"
            label="phase"
            options={tabs.map(tab => ({ value: tab.id === ALL ? SELECT_ALL : tab.id, label: overlayLine(tab, room) }))}
            value={s.view.choice === ALL ? SELECT_ALL : s.view.choice}
            autoFocus
            onSelect={value => on.choose(value === SELECT_ALL ? ALL : value)}
          />,
        ]
      : tabs.map(
          (tab, i) =>
            keycap(k, {
              id: `phase-pick:${tab.id}`,
              ...(i <= 9 ? { key: String(i) } : {}),
              label: overlayLine(tab, room - 3),
              selected: tab.id === s.view.choice,
              faded: tab.dim,
              on: 'overlay',
              ...(tab.id === s.view.choice ? { autoFocus: true } : {}),
              onPress: () => on.choose(tab.id),
            }).el,
        )
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={t.surface.overlay} borderStyle="round" borderColor={t.border.strong} paddingX={1}>
      <Box flexDirection="row">
        {label(k, 'phases').el}
        <Box flexGrow={1} />
        {close.el}
      </Box>
      {list}
      {helpFooter(k, ['Tab moves', 'Enter picks', '0-9 pick', 'f closes'], room)}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** An empty selection: `Nothing waiting in <phase> · done <date>` and, under a phase, the `0 All` keycap back. */
export function phaseEmpty(k: Kit, s: StripView, on: StripHandlers): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const tab = orderPhases(s.view.phases, s.allCount, s.view.choice).find(x => x.id === s.view.choice)
  const all = keycap(k, { id: 'phase-all', key: '0', label: 'All', on: s.on ?? 'page', onPress: () => on.choose(ALL) })
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2} alignItems="flex-start">
      <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{emptyLine(s.empty, tab)}</Text>
      {s.view.choice === ALL ? null : all.el}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** A pane's strip and body: the strip, then the body — or, while the overlay is open, the overlay per OVERLAY_PLACEMENT. */
export function phaseSection(k: Kit, s: StripView, on: StripHandlers, width: number, body: readonly RenderNode[]): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box } = k.ui
  const strip = phaseStrip(k, s, on, width)
  if (!s.view.overlay) return [strip, ...body]
  const overlay = phaseOverlay(k, s, on, width)
  if (OVERLAY_PLACEMENT === 'swap') return [strip, overlay]
  return [
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      {strip}
      <Box flexDirection="column">
        {[...body]}
        <Box position="absolute" top={0} left={2}>
          {overlay}
        </Box>
      </Box>
    </Box>,
  ]
}
```

### Task 1.7: The review pane on the strip; per-pane phase views; demo phases

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/types/index.d.ts` (`PluginState`: `phaseView` → `phaseViews`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/model.ts` (remove `PHASE_PREFIX`, `storedPhase`, `phaseLabel`, `nextPhase`: phases.ts owns them now)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/flows.ts` (`loadPhases(pane)`, `choosePhase`, `toggleDone`, `setOverlay` replace `cyclePhase`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (atom `PHASE_VIEWS`, writer, flows deps, `restorePhases`, demo seed, review render)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/draw.tsx` (`ReviewView`/`ReviewHandlers`; the strip where the filter line and the `f` chip were)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/demo.ts` (`DEMO_SEED = 4`, dated `DEMO_PHASES`, `demoPhases`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/model.test.ts`, `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/card.test.ts`
- Replace: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/phase.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything from Tasks 1.2–1.6.
- Produces: `TmFlows.loadPhases`, `choosePhase`, `toggleDone`, `setOverlay`; `TmWriter.phaseView(pane, change)`; `TmFlowDeps.readPhases(pane)` and `phaseChosen(pane, id)`; `PluginState.phaseViews`; `demoPhases(pane)`; `ReviewView.strip` / `surface` / `say`; `ReviewHandlers.strip`. Stage 3 builds the review list on these; Stage 4 calls `loadPhases('handovers')` from `openHandovers` and draws `phaseSection` with `pane: 'handovers'`.

What the person sees:
- The review pane's `phase: all` line and `f: phase` chip are replaced by the strip.
- `h` / `l` step both ways, `f` opens the overlay, and tabs are pressable.
- The strip carries no digits until Stage 3, because today's check items own 1–9.
- The choice is kept at `phase:review:<root>`, moved from `phase:<root>` on first read. A phase that no longer exists falls back to All with a toast.

```ts
// User intent: pick the review queue's phase from inside the review pane with a two-way strip (spec §5) on a big backlog —
// tabs, h / l both ways, the overlay for what does not fit, done phases on demand — the choice kept per pane and project.
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { OVERLAY_PLACEMENT } from '../hooks/kit'
import { command, pane, PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import * as R from './fixtures/replies'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { type World, worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const TM = { plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const REVIEW_PANE = pane('tm-review')
const WIDE_PANE = { ...REVIEW_PANE, props: { ...REVIEW_PANE.props, bodyColumns: 400 } }
const PHASE_SQL =
  "SELECT json_array(p.id, p.status, json_extract(p.doc,'$.order'), (SELECT count(*) FROM entities t WHERE t.kind='task' AND t.deleted=0 AND t.status='in-review' AND json_extract(t.doc,'$.phase')=p.id AND coalesce(json_extract(t.doc,'$.human_action'),'')<>''), 0, CASE WHEN p.status IN ('done','archived') THEN substr(coalesce(json_extract(p.doc,'$.completed'),''),1,10) ELSE substr(coalesce(json_extract(p.doc,'$.start_date'),''),1,10) END) AS phase, json_extract(p.doc,'$.name') AS name FROM entities p WHERE p.kind='phase' AND p.deleted=0 ORDER BY json_extract(p.doc,'$.order') DESC"
const STRONG_SIGNATURE = '#4a5590' // rr-stub's 24% signature tint
const listArgs = (world: World) => world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_list_tasks').map(c => c.args)
const queries = (world: World) => world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_query')
const reviewKey = (world: World) => `phase:review:${world.root}`

describe('review phase strip', () => {
  test('opening the pane reads the phase list once with the exact query; the strip shows All and the active phase', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await clock.settle()
    expect(queries(world)).toEqual([])
    await $.command.run(command('tm-review'))
    await clock.settle()
    expect(queries(world).map(c => c.args)).toEqual([{ sql: PHASE_SQL, limit: 200 }])
    await $.turn.complete({ answer: '', reason: 'end_turn' } as never)
    await clock.settle()
    expect(queries(world)).toHaveLength(1)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'phase-tab:' }))?.props.label).toMatch(/^All \d+$/)
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'phase-tab:2-0-4' }))?.props.label).toBe('Patch 2.0.4 · active 249')
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: 'phase-tab:-box' }))?.props.backgroundColor).toBe(STRONG_SIGNATURE)
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'phase-more' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'f' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'phase' })).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('l and h step both ways and stop at All; each step is kept and the list_tasks phase arg follows', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-review'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    const steps = [
      ['phase-next', '2-0-4'],
      ['phase-next', 'patch-1-9-5'],
      ['phase-prev', '2-0-4'],
      ['phase-prev', ''],
    ] as const
    for (const [key, phase] of steps) {
      await ui.press({ key })
      await clock.settle()
      await ui.redraw()
      expect(world.store.get(reviewKey(world)), `${key} → ${phase}`).toEqual({ phase })
      expect(listArgs(world).at(-1)?.phase, `${key} → ${phase}`).toBe(phase === '' ? undefined : phase)
    }
    const calls = listArgs(world).length
    await ui.press({ key: 'phase-prev' })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(world.toasts).toContain('Already on the first phase')
    expect(listArgs(world)).toHaveLength(calls)
  })

  test('a tab press chooses that phase and fills it; the cursor goes back to the first card', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-review'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'skip' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'tm-audit-031' })).toBeDefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'phase-tab:p2' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(world.store.get(reviewKey(world))).toEqual({ phase: 'p2' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: 'phase-tab:p2-box' }))?.props.backgroundColor).toBe(STRONG_SIGNATURE)
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'unified-chat-022' })).toBeDefined()
  })

  test('done phases stay hidden over four until ◐ done; a chosen done phase stays drawn when they hide again', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-review'))

      refused: () => ({ text: 'Error: unknown tool backlog_query', isError: true }),
      error: () => ({ text: 'Error: no such table: entities' }),
      garbage: () => ({ text: 'not a table' }),
      offline: () => 'offline',
    }
    let mode = 'refused'
    world.mcp = (tool, args) => (tool === 'backlog_query' ? (answers[mode]?.() ?? 'offline') : R.backlog(tool, args))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-review'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'phase-tab:2-0-6' }))?.props.label).toBe('2-0-6 0')
    for (const next of ['refused', 'error', 'garbage', 'offline']) {
      mode = next
      const before = world.logs.length
      await ui.press({ key: 'phase-more' })
      await clock.settle()
      await ui.redraw()
      expect(world.logs.length, next).toBeGreaterThan(before)
      expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'phase-tab:2-0-6' }))?.props.label, next).toBe('2-0-6 0')
      expect(world.store.get(reviewKey(world)), next).toEqual({ phase: '2-0-6' })
      await ui.press({ key: 'phase-close' })
      await ui.redraw()
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'unified-chat-022' }), next).toBeDefined()
    }
    expect(world.logs.every(line => line.to === 'debug')).toBe(true)
  })
```

```ts
/** The phase filter's `$.store` key is this plus the repo root: one choice per project, machine-wide. */
export const PHASE_PREFIX = 'phase:'
export const PAGE_SIZE = 5

/** A stored phase choice: its id ('' every phase, kept as an explicit choice), or null for none / unreadable. */
export function storedPhase(value: unknown): string | null {
  const phase = typeof value === 'object' && value !== null ? (value as { phase?: unknown }).phase : undefined
  return typeof phase === 'string' ? phase : null
}
```

In `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/model.ts`, delete:

```ts
/** The review pane's filter line: `phase: all`, or `phase: <name> (<count>)`; the bare id while the phase list is not known. */
export function phaseLabel(choice: string, phases: readonly TmPhase[]): string {
  if (choice === '') return 'phase: all'
  const known = phases.find(p => p.id === choice)
  return known === undefined ? `phase: ${choice}` : `phase: ${phaseName(known)} (${known.count})`
}

/**
 * The phase `f` moves to: all, the active phase (offered even with nothing waiting: the board's marker can lag, but it is
 * the usual start), then every other phase with waiting tasks, newest (highest order) first, then all again. A current
 * phase the cycle does not offer goes to all.
 */
export function nextPhase(current: string, phases: readonly TmPhase[]): string {
  const active = phases.find(p => p.status === 'active')
  const others = phases.filter(p => p.id !== active?.id && p.count > 0).sort((a, b) => b.order - a.order)
  const cycle = ['', ...(active === undefined ? [] : [active.id]), ...others.map(p => p.id)]
  const at = cycle.indexOf(current)
  return at < 0 ? '' : (cycle[(at + 1) % cycle.length] ?? '')
}
```

```ts
  { id: 'patch-1-9', status: 'active', order: 2, count: 3, name: 'Patch 1.9 — Demo scope' },
```

```ts
/** Demo's phases (review counts); the demo queue itself is not filtered by them. */
export const DEMO_PHASES: readonly TmPhase[] = [
  { id: 'release-2', status: 'planned', order: 3, count: 2, name: 'Release 2.0 — Demo scope', date: '' },
  { id: 'patch-1-9', status: 'active', order: 2, count: 3, name: 'Patch 1.9 — Demo scope', date: '2026-10-06' },
  { id: 'patch-1-8', status: 'done', order: 1, count: 0, name: 'Patch 1.8 — Demo scope', date: '2026-10-05' },
]
/** Open demo handovers per phase, through their tasks; three link no task. */
const DEMO_HANDOVER_COUNTS: Readonly<Record<string, number>> = { 'patch-1-9': 2 }

/** Each pane's demo phases: review's counts; handovers' open handovers plus a no task tab; restore's are counted later (0). */
export function demoPhases(pane: TmPhasePane): readonly TmPhase[] {
  if (pane === 'review') return DEMO_PHASES
  const counted = DEMO_PHASES.map(p => ({ ...p, count: pane === 'handovers' ? (DEMO_HANDOVER_COUNTS[p.id] ?? 0) : 0, closed: 0 }))
  return [...counted, { id: NO_TASK, status: 'none', order: 0, count: pane === 'handovers' ? 3 : 0, closed: 0, name: 'no task', date: '' }]
}
```

```ts
  const keepChoice = async (pane: TmPhasePane, phaseId: string): Promise<void> => {
    if (d.source !== 'demo') await saveChoice(phaseStoreOf(d.host), pane, await d.host.repoRoot(), phaseId)
  }
  // A pane's phases, read fresh: a remembered choice the list no longer holds falls back to All, with a toast (spec §5).
  const loadPhases = async (pane: TmPhasePane): Promise<readonly TmPhase[] | null> => {
    const phases = (await d.readPhases?.(pane)) ?? null
    if (phases === null) return null
    let lost = ''
    await d.write.phaseView(pane, v => {
      const kept = fallback({ ...v, phases, loaded: true })
      lost = kept.lost
      return { ...v, phases, loaded: true, choice: kept.choice }
    })
    if (lost !== '') {
      await keepChoice(pane, ALL)
      d.host.toast(`taskmaster-mods: phase ${lost} no longer exists; showing All`)
      d.phaseChosen?.(pane, ALL)
    }
    return phases
  }
  // A tab, ‹ ›, a digit, an overlay pick or 0 All: kept per pane and project (not in demo), the overlay closes, and the pane
  // starts over at its top (review's cursor: skips forgotten, or a skipped task of the new phase would hide).
  const choosePhase = async (pane: TmPhasePane, phaseId: string): Promise<void> => {
    let changed = false
    await d.write.phaseView(pane, v => {
      changed = v.choice !== phaseId
      return { ...v, choice: phaseId, overlay: false }
    })
    if (!changed) return
    await keepChoice(pane, phaseId)
    if (pane === 'review') await d.write.cursor(c => ({ ...c, mode: 'card', refusal: '', currentId: '', skipped: [] }))
    if (pane === 'handovers') {
      await d.write.handoverPage(0)
      await pick('')
    }
    d.phaseChosen?.(pane, phaseId)
  }
```

In `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx`, replace:

```tsx
/**
 * The phase filter this project last chose (`$.store` `phase:<repo root>`, an explicit all included) replaces the reviewPhase
 * option's default, once per instance and before the first refresh asks the server; the atom mirrors it for the drawings.
 */
async function restorePhase($: EngineInterface, host: TmHost): Promise<void> {
  try {
    const stored = storedPhase(await host.storeGet(`${PHASE_PREFIX}${await host.repoRoot()}`))
    if (stored !== null) mod.scope = { ...mod.scope, phase: stored }
  } catch {
    // an unreadable store keeps the option's default
  }
  const choice = mod.scope.phase
  await update($, PHASE_VIEW, v => (v.choice === choice ? v : { ...v, choice }))
}
```

with:

```tsx
/**
 * Each pane's remembered phase (`$.store` `phase:<pane>:<repo root>`, an explicit All included; review's legacy
 * `phase:<repo root>` moves there on this read), once per instance and before the first refresh asks the server. Review's
 * replaces the reviewPhase option's default and is the scope; the atom mirrors every pane's for the drawings.
 */
async function restorePhases($: EngineInterface, host: TmHost): Promise<void> {
  const chosen: Partial<Record<TmPhasePane, string>> = {}
  try {
    const root = await host.repoRoot()
    const store = phaseStoreOf(host)
    for (const pane of PHASE_PANES) {
      const stored = await loadChoice(store, pane, root)
      if (stored !== null) chosen[pane] = stored
    }
  } catch {
    // an unreadable store keeps the option's default (review) and All (the others)
  }
  if (chosen.review !== undefined) mod.scope = { ...mod.scope, phase: chosen.review }
  const review = mod.scope.phase
  await update($, PHASE_VIEWS, all => ({
    review: all.review.choice === review ? all.review : { ...all.review, choice: review },
    handovers: { ...all.handovers, choice: chosen.handovers ?? all.handovers.choice },
    restore: { ...all.restore, choice: chosen.restore ?? all.restore.choice },
  }))
}
```

```ts
// User intent: /tm-kit shows every kit primitive in demo mode so the kit can be judged by eye — it opens, draws on the
// terminal and the desktop, a dim keycap says why instead of acting, and the strip on it really moves; tm mode never opens it.
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { command, pane, PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const DEMO = { options: { source: 'demo' }, plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const KIT_PANE = { ...pane('tm-kit'), props: { ...pane('tm-kit').props, bodyColumns: 120 } }

describe('/tm-kit', () => {
  test('demo: /tm-kit opens the gallery, which draws on the terminal and the desktop', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    expect(await $.command.run(command('tm-kit'))).toEqual({ text: 'Kit gallery opened.' })
    expect(world.opened).toEqual(['tm-kit'])
    for (const surface of ['terminal', 'desktop'] as const) {
      const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...KIT_PANE, surface })
      for (const key of ['page-prev', 'g-refresh', 'g-success', 'g-dim', 'phase-next', 'sel:tm-audit-031', 'g-bulk-done', 'g-confirm-no']) {
        expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key }), `${surface} ${key}`).toBeDefined()
      }
      await ui.unmount()
    }
  })

  test('a dim keycap toasts its reason; a live one toasts that it was pressed', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...KIT_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'g-dim' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'g-success' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'page-prev' })
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual(['Nothing to do here', 'pressed success', 'Already on the first page'])
  })

  test('the strip on the gallery moves the review phase, in demo only (no store, no tm)', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...KIT_PANE })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'phase-tab:patch-1-9' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '1', label: 'Patch 1.9 · active 3' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'phase-next' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: 'phase-tab:patch-1-9-box' }))?.props.backgroundColor).toBe('#4a5590')
    expect(world.calls).toEqual([])
  })

  test('tm mode: /tm-kit only says the gallery is a demo surface', { plugins: [RR_STUB] }, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    expect((await $.command.run(command('tm-kit'))).text).toMatch(/demo mode/)
    expect(world.opened).toEqual([])
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement the gallery and wire it**

`mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/kit-gallery.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: /tm-kit — every taskmaster-mods kit primitive on one screen with sample data (demo source only), so the kit
// is judged by eye on the terminal and the desktop before the band and panes are rebuilt on it.
// JSX compiles to h(...): never name a variable `h` in this file (it would shadow the factory).
import type { RenderNode } from 'claude-code'

import {
  badge,
  card,
  checkbox,
  confirmRow,
  helpFooter,
  keycap,
  type Kit,
  label,
  pager,
  panel,
  panelTitle,
  phaseStrip,
  row,
  rule,
  selectionBar,
  signal,
  type StripHandlers,
  type StripView,
  toolbar,
} from './kit'
import type { RrTone } from './rr'

export const KIT = 'tm-kit'
const TONE_KEYS: readonly { tone: RrTone; key: string }[] = [
  { tone: 'success', key: 'r' },
  { tone: 'warning', key: 'w' },
  { tone: 'critical', key: 'x' },
  { tone: 'info', key: 'i' },
  { tone: 'signature', key: 'e' },
]

/** `press(id)` is what every sample does (a toast naming it); the strip acts for real on the review pane's demo phases. */
export function kitGalleryTree(k: Kit, strip: StripView, onStrip: StripHandlers, press: (id: string) => void, width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const w = Math.max(30, width - 2)
  const tap = (id: string) => () => press(id)
  const section = (name: string, ...rows: RenderNode[]): RenderNode => (
    <Box flexDirection="column" marginTop={1}>
      {label(k, name).el}
      {rows}
    </Box>
  )
  const head = (id: string, selected: boolean) =>
    row(k, [checkbox(k, { id: `sel:${id}`, selected, onPress: tap(`select ${id}`) }), badge(k, 'blocked', 'critical'), label(k, id)]).el
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column">
      {toolbar(k, {
        title: 'kit',
        slots: [pager(k, { page: 0, pages: 3, onTurn: n => press(`turn ${n}`) }), keycap(k, { id: 'g-refresh', key: 'u', label: 'refresh', onPress: tap('refresh') })],
        width: w,
      })}
      {section('phase strip', phaseStrip(k, strip, onStrip, w))}
      {section(
        'keycaps',
        row(k, TONE_KEYS.map(x => keycap(k, { id: `g-${x.tone}`, key: x.key, label: x.tone, tone: x.tone, onPress: tap(x.tone) }))).el,
        row(k, [
          keycap(k, { id: 'g-dim', key: 'z', label: 'dim', dim: 'Nothing to do here', onPress: tap('dim') }),
          keycap(k, { id: 'g-faded', key: 'q', label: 'faded', faded: true, onPress: tap('faded') }),
          keycap(k, { id: 'g-selected', key: 'v', label: 'selected', selected: true, onPress: tap('selected') }),
          keycap(k, { id: 'g-plain', label: 'no key', onPress: tap('plain') }),
        ]).el,
      )}
      {section(
        'badges',
        row(k, TONE_KEYS.map(x => badge(k, x.tone, x.tone))).el,
        row(k, [...TONE_KEYS.map(x => badge(k, x.tone, x.tone, { strength: 12 })), badge(k, '0 waiting', 'warning', { dim: true })]).el,
        row(k, [signal(k, 'success', 'tm ok'), signal(k, 'critical', 'offline', 'tm')], 2).el,
      )}
      {section(
        'panel',
        panel(k, { title: panelTitle('session'), health: { kind: 'success', word: 'tm ok' }, width: w }, [
          <Text color={k.t.fg.default}>A panel body line.</Text>,
          rule(k, w - 4),
          <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>No task bound to this session</Text>,
        ]),
        panel(k, { title: panelTitle('restore'), collapse: true, width: w }, []),
      )}
      {section(
        'cards',
        card(k, { id: 'g1', selected: false, focused: true }, head('tm-audit-031', false), [
          <Text color={k.t.fg.default}>human: verify prod row counts</Text>,
        ]),
        card(k, { id: 'g2', selected: true, focused: false }, head('tm-audit-044', true), []),
        card(k, { id: 'g3', selected: false, focused: false }, head('docs-012', false), []),
      )}
      {section(
        'selection',
        selectionBar(k, {
          actions: [
            { id: 'g-bulk-done', verb: 'Done', count: 2, tone: 'success', onPress: tap('bulk done') },
            { id: 'g-bulk-agent', verb: 'To agent', count: 0, tone: 'warning', onPress: tap('bulk agent') },
          ],
          ghosts: [
            { id: 'g-all', label: 'all', onPress: tap('all') },
            { id: 'g-none', label: 'none', onPress: tap('none') },
          ],
          width: w,
        }),
      )}
      {section('confirm', ...confirmRow(k, { id: 'g-confirm', question: 'done tm-audit-031?', onYes: tap('yes'), onNo: tap('no'), width: w }))}
      {helpFooter(k, ['h l phase', 'f phases', '0-9 jump', 'p n page', 'u refresh'], w)}
    </Box>
  )
}
```

## Stage 2: Band and Session tab

**Goal.** The band stops reading like the end of Claude's reply (spec §1.1). It becomes at most two lines on its own full-width raised ground, under a signature-tinted `◆ TASKMASTER` chip, with one blank row above it. Line 1 is this session's task; line 2 is this session's handover (spec D5) with `c copy`; keycaps `1` `2` `3` `4` open the panes. The full detail moves into a new **Session tab** (`3`, `/tm-session`, spec §7). This stage also owns the shared new-tab launcher (ported from cm-restore), the "resumed from" record (spec §10.5) and the D5 resolution that Stages 4 and 5 build on.

**Verified before writing.** Every task below was replayed on a copy of both mods with Stage 0–1 applied (`scratchpad/mods`). After each task `claude plugin test`, `claude plugin validate --strict` and `tsc` were green, and the load smoke answered `ok`. Test counts are given as Stage 1's final count plus N; in the replay Stage 1 ended at **287** taskmaster-mods tests, and Stage 2 ends at **328**. rr-tui does not change in this stage.

**Order and files.** Apply the tasks in order: later anchors (for example Task 2.5's "the final `await resolveHandover($)`") name lines an earlier task added. Existing files use CRLF line endings; new files may be LF. An independent agent executed Tasks 2.1–2.6 from this text alone on a fresh copy and reached the same counts and the same code.

**Load timeouts.** With other sessions running tests at the same time, a test that normally takes 1–2 s can fail with `Error: timed out after 5000 ms`. That happened during the replay on tests this stage does not touch, and they passed on a re-run. A failure that says only `timed out after 5000 ms` is re-run once before anyone investigates it. Any other failure is real.

**Key map (from the Stage 0 table; hotkeys are `/^[a-z0-9]$/` only).**

| Surface | Keys |
|---|---|
| Band | `1` review, `2` handovers, `3` session, `4` restore (only while a burst is pending), `c` copy the handover, `d` done (only when the bound task is ready), `x` hide the restore keycap; `y` / `n` while a confirm is up |
| Session tab | `d` done, `a` to agent, `c` copy, `h` open in handovers, `w` new tab, `i` decisions · blockers, `1` `2` `4`; `y` / `n` while a confirm is up |

**Decisions this stage makes where the spec leaves room** (each is coded and tested below):

- **When the band shows at all.** It shows when there is a task, a D5 handover, a pending restore burst, or anything counted. With tm offline and none of those, it stays hidden as today, so a project without Taskmaster gets no band. The faults-only status line still says `◆ tm offline`.
- **`d` on the band** shows only when the task is bound by this session (never inferred) and **ready for done**: in review, or with a pipeline that has no gate outstanding (`isReady`). An inferred task shows a subtle `inferred` and no `d`.
- **`a` (to agent) leaves the band.** It lives in the Session tab. There it reuses the existing flow `bandSendBack`, which opens the review pane at its `note` Input on that task (`openReview(id, 'note')`).
- **Copy no longer clears the handover.** The old notice row vanished after `3: copy`. The band handover is now D5 and stays: copying is repeatable.
- **Counts on the band:** `1 N waiting on you` → `1 N waiting` → `1 N` as the room shrinks; `2 N handovers` → `2 N`; `4 N restore` → `4 N`. A zero count fades but still opens its pane. Offline counts read `–`.
- **Where the counts sit:** on line 2 after the handover when there is a task and a second line; otherwise on line 1, before `3 session ›`.
- **The confirm and a refusal take line 2.** `d` puts ` done <id>? ` with `y yes` and `n no` (focused) on line 2, keeping the counts at the right. A refusal shows ` REFUSED ` and the server's words there until the next `d`.
- **The D5 handover tag on the band:** `written here ·`, `resumed from ·`, `for this task ·`. The Session tab shows the same words as a badge.
- **Restore hide** (`x`): writes `$.store` `restore-hidden:<epoch>` (not in demo) and the `restoreHidden` atom, so the keycap goes at once and stays hidden for that burst in every later session.
- **A handover read by id.** When the session was resumed from a handover the open list does not hold, D5 reads it once with `backlog_handover_get` (slim view). The same reader backs `copyHandover` by id.

**What Stage 2 produces for Stages 3–5** (final signatures; the code is in the tasks):

### Task 2.1: The new-tab launcher (`hooks/launch.ts`), ported from cm-restore

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/launch.ts`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/launch.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing from the engine directly. All I/O comes in through `LaunchIo`, so the module is pure and testable without a session. Task 2.4 binds it to `TmHost`.
- Produces: everything listed under `hooks/launch.ts` in "What Stage 2 produces". Its behaviour:
  - **Windows:** `wt.exe -w 0 nt [-d <cwd>] --title <title> pwsh -NoExit -EncodedCommand <utf16le base64 of psScript>`. No argument carries a raw `;`. The title has `;` and `"` blanked and is cut to 40. `-d` is left out when the cwd holds a `;`.
  - **macOS:** the own terminal by `TERM_PROGRAM` (iTerm2, WezTerm), then Terminal.app.
  - **Linux:** no launcher.
  - **Commands:** a fresh spec runs `claude '<prompt>'`; a resume spec runs `claude --resume <id>` (plus the quoted `/compact …` prompt when `compact`). `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR` is set only when the person set it.
  - **Spacing and fallback:** launches are 700 ms apart. Whatever did not open is copied as one block, one command per line. Whatever could not be copied either is named in `failed`. So is a spec with no folder, or a session id that is not `/^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9-]*$/`.

What is ported, and what changed: cm-restore's `utf16leBase64`, the three quoters, `resumeScript` → `psScript`, `posixScript`, `macTerminalFor`, `macArgv` and `wtArgv`, with its quoting tests. A `LaunchSpec` replaces cm-restore's session record, so one launcher serves fresh and resume launches. The platform check and the process runner are injected (`LaunchIo`) instead of reading `$`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/launch.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: prove the new-tab launcher builds a safe command on every platform (ported from cm-restore's quoting tests)
// and that a launch that cannot open a tab still leaves the command on the clipboard.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import {
  COMPACT_PROMPT,
  LAUNCH_STAGGER_MS,
  launchAll,
  launchCommand,
  type LaunchEnv,
  type LaunchIo,
  type LaunchSpec,
  launchSummary,
  macArgv,
  macTerminalFor,
  posixScript,
  psScript,
  resumePrompt,
  utf16leBase64,
  wtArgv,
} from '../hooks/launch'

const RESUME: LaunchSpec = { cwd: "C:\\Work\\O'Brien", title: 'Contract; Phase C', mode: 'resume', sessionId: 'b1c2-d3' }
const FRESH: LaunchSpec = {
  cwd: 'C:\\work\\proj',
  title: 'handover 2026-10-05-shipped',
  mode: 'fresh',
  prompt: resumePrompt('2026-10-05-shipped', "C:\\work\\proj\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\it's.md"),
}
const MAC: LaunchSpec = { ...RESUME, cwd: "/Users/me/O'Brien \"x\"" }

/** A LaunchIo that answers from `exits` (argv[0] → exit code; absent: could not start) and records what it ran and copied. */
function ioOf(env: LaunchEnv, exits: Record<string, number>, copies = true) {
  const runs: string[][] = []
  const copied: string[] = []
  const slept: number[] = []
  const io: LaunchIo = {
    env: async () => env,
    run: async argv => {
      runs.push([...argv])
      const code = exits[argv[0] ?? '']
      return code === undefined ? null : { exitCode: code, stdout: argv[0] === 'uname' ? 'Darwin\n' : '' }
    },
    copy: async text => {
      copied.push(text)
      return copies
    },
    sleep: async ms => {
      slept.push(ms)
    },
  }
  return { io, runs, copied, slept }
}

describe('commands', () => {
  test('the fresh prompt names the handover and its path', () => {
    expect(resumePrompt('H-0412', 'C:\\p\\H-0412.md')).toBe('Resume from handover H-0412 (C:\\p\\H-0412.md)')
  })

  test('base64 matches the UTF-16LE encoding pwsh -EncodedCommand expects', () => {
    // "hi" -> 68 00 69 00
    expect(utf16leBase64('hi')).toBe('aABpAA==')
    expect(utf16leBase64('a')).toBe('YQA=')
  })

  test('wt argv carries no raw semicolons and quotes the path for PowerShell', () => {
    const argv = wtArgv(RESUME, 'C:\\cfg')
    expect(argv.some(a => a.includes(';'))).toBe(false)
    expect(argv.slice(0, 6)).toEqual(['wt.exe', '-w', '0', 'nt', '-d', "C:\\Work\\O'Brien"])
    expect(argv).toContain('Contract  Phase C')
    expect(psScript(RESUME, 'C:\\cfg')).toBe("$env:CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR = 'C:\\cfg'; Set-Location -LiteralPath 'C:\\Work\\O''Brien'; claude --resume b1c2-d3")
    expect(wtArgv({ ...RESUME, cwd: 'C:\\a;b' }, undefined).some(a => a.includes(';'))).toBe(false)
  })

  test('a fresh launch quotes its prompt; compact applies to --resume only', () => {
    expect(psScript(FRESH, undefined)).toBe(
      "Set-Location -LiteralPath 'C:\\work\\proj'; claude 'Resume from handover 2026-10-05-shipped (C:\\work\\proj\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\it''s.md)'",
    )
    expect(psScript({ ...FRESH, compact: true }, undefined)).not.toContain('/compact')
    expect(psScript({ ...RESUME, compact: true }, undefined)).toContain(`claude --resume b1c2-d3 '${COMPACT_PROMPT}'`)
    expect(psScript({ ...FRESH, prompt: '' }, undefined)).toBe("Set-Location -LiteralPath 'C:\\work\\proj'; claude")
  })

  test('posix commands quote for zsh/bash and only set a config dir that was chosen', () => {
    expect(posixScript(MAC, undefined)).toBe(String.raw`cd '/Users/me/O'\''Brien "x"' && claude --resume b1c2-d3`)
    expect(posixScript({ ...MAC, compact: true }, '/Users/me/.claude-work')).toBe(
      String.raw`cd '/Users/me/O'\''Brien "x"' && CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR='/Users/me/.claude-work' claude --resume b1c2-d3 ` + `'${COMPACT_PROMPT}'`,
    )
    expect(psScript(MAC, undefined)).not.toContain('CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR')
    expect(launchCommand('mac', MAC, undefined)).toBe(posixScript(MAC, undefined))
    expect(launchCommand('linux', MAC, undefined)).toBe(posixScript(MAC, undefined))
    expect(launchCommand('windows', MAC, undefined)).toBe(psScript(MAC, undefined))
  })

  test('mac launchers: own terminal by TERM_PROGRAM, AppleScript strings escaped', () => {
    expect(macTerminalFor('iTerm.app')).toBe('iterm')
    expect(macTerminalFor('WezTerm')).toBe('wezterm')
    expect(macTerminalFor('Apple_Terminal')).toBe('terminal')
    expect(macTerminalFor(undefined)).toBe('terminal')
    const terminal = macArgv('terminal', MAC, undefined)
    expect(terminal[0]).toBe('osascript')
    expect(terminal).toContain(String.raw`do script "cd '/Users/me/O'\\''Brien \"x\"' && claude --resume b1c2-d3"`)
    const iterm = macArgv('iterm', MAC, undefined)
    expect(iterm).toContain('tell current window to create tab with default profile')
    expect(iterm.filter(a => a === '-e').length).toBe((iterm.length - 1) / 2)
    expect(macArgv('wezterm', MAC, undefined, '/bin/bash')).toEqual(['wezterm', 'cli', 'spawn', '--cwd', MAC.cwd, '--', '/bin/bash', '-lc', posixScript(MAC, undefined)])
  })
})

describe('launchAll', () => {
  test('Windows: one wt.exe per spec, 700 ms apart; nothing copied', async () => {
    const { io, runs, copied, slept } = ioOf({ os: 'Windows_NT' }, { 'wt.exe': 0 })
    const r = await launchAll(io, [FRESH, RESUME])
    expect(r).toEqual({ opened: 2, copied: null, failed: [] })
    expect(runs.map(a => a[0])).toEqual(['wt.exe', 'wt.exe'])
    expect(slept).toEqual([LAUNCH_STAGGER_MS])
    expect(copied).toEqual([])
    expect(launchSummary(r)).toBe('Opened 2 tabs')
  })

  test('mac: the own terminal first, then Terminal.app; uname decides the platform', async () => {
    const { io, runs } = ioOf({ termProgram: 'iTerm.app' }, { uname: 0, osascript: 1 })
    const r = await launchAll(io, [MAC])
    expect(runs.map(a => a[0])).toEqual(['uname', 'osascript', 'osascript'])
    expect(runs[1]).toContain('tell application "iTerm"')
    expect(runs[2]).toContain('tell application "Terminal"')
    expect(r.opened).toBe(0)
    expect(r.copied).toBe(posixScript(MAC, undefined))
  })

  test('no terminal opens: every command is copied as one block, one a line, and the toast says to paste', async () => {
    const { io, copied } = ioOf({ os: 'Windows_NT', configDir: 'C:\\cfg' }, {})
    const r = await launchAll(io, [FRESH, RESUME])
    expect(copied).toEqual([`${psScript(FRESH, 'C:\\cfg')}\n${psScript(RESUME, 'C:\\cfg')}`])
    expect(launchSummary(r)).toBe('Copied 2 commands — paste each into a new terminal')
    expect(launchSummary({ opened: 0, copied: psScript(FRESH, undefined), failed: [] })).toBe('Copied — paste into a new terminal')
  })

  test('linux has no launcher: the command is copied; with no clipboard either, the titles are named', async () => {
    const { io } = ioOf({}, { uname: 0 }, false)
    const r = await launchAll(io, [{ ...MAC, title: 'Pricing' }])
    expect(r).toEqual({ opened: 0, copied: null, failed: ['Pricing'] })
    expect(launchSummary(r)).toBe('Could not open: Pricing')
  })

  test('a spec with no folder or no usable session id is refused before anything runs', async () => {
    const { io, runs } = ioOf({ os: 'Windows_NT' }, { 'wt.exe': 0 })
    const r = await launchAll(io, [
      { ...RESUME, title: 'A', sessionId: 'x; rm -rf' },
      { ...FRESH, title: 'B', cwd: '' },
      FRESH,
    ])
    expect(r).toEqual({ opened: 1, copied: null, failed: ['A (no session id)', 'B (no folder)'] })
    expect(runs).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(launchSummary(r)).toBe('Opened 1 tab · Could not open: A (no session id), B (no folder)')
    expect(await launchAll(io, [])).toEqual({ opened: 0, copied: null, failed: [] })
  })
})
```
