- [ ] **Step 3: Write the launcher**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/launch.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: open a Claude Code session in a new terminal tab from any taskmaster-mods surface (Session `w`, handovers `w`,
// restore) — fresh with a "Resume from handover" prompt, or `--resume <id>` — ported from cm-restore's launcher; when no
// terminal can be opened, the command goes on the clipboard instead, never lost.

/** Session ids are uuids: anything else never reaches a command line. */
const SESSION_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9][A-Za-z0-9-]*$/
export const LAUNCH_STAGGER_MS = 700
export const COMPACT_PROMPT = '/compact Keep where the work stopped, open decisions, branches and worktrees, and the next step.'

export type LaunchSpec = {
  readonly cwd: string
  readonly title: string
  readonly mode: 'fresh' | 'resume'
  /** fresh: the session's first prompt. */
  readonly prompt?: string
  /** resume: the session to reopen. */
  readonly sessionId?: string
  /** resume: open with /compact as the first prompt. */
  readonly compact?: boolean
}
/** `copied`: the commands put on the clipboard for what could not be opened; `failed`: neither opened nor copied. */
export type LaunchResult = { readonly opened: number; readonly copied: string | null; readonly failed: readonly string[] }
export type Platform = 'windows' | 'mac' | 'linux'
/** The environment a launch reads, each from its own literal `$.env.get`. */
export type LaunchEnv = { readonly os?: string; readonly termProgram?: string; readonly shell?: string; readonly configDir?: string }
export type LaunchIo = {
  readonly env: () => Promise<LaunchEnv>
  /** A command by its argv; null when it could not start. */
  readonly run: (argv: readonly string[]) => Promise<{ readonly exitCode: number; readonly stdout: string } | null>
  readonly copy: (text: string) => Promise<boolean>
  readonly sleep: (ms: number) => Promise<void>
}

/** The fresh session's first prompt; RESUME_PROMPT_RE (resumed.ts) reads the id back out of it. */
export function resumePrompt(handoverId: string, path: string): string {
  return `Resume from handover ${handoverId} (${path})`
}

const B64 = 'ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/'

/** What `pwsh -EncodedCommand` takes: the script as UTF-16LE, base64. */
export function utf16leBase64(text: string): string {
  const bytes: number[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < text.length; i++) {
    const c = text.charCodeAt(i)
    bytes.push(c & 0xff, c >> 8)
  }
  let out = ''
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 3) {
    const n = ((bytes[i] ?? 0) << 16) | ((bytes[i + 1] ?? 0) << 8) | (bytes[i + 2] ?? 0)
    out += B64.charAt((n >> 18) & 63) + B64.charAt((n >> 12) & 63)
    out += i + 1 < bytes.length ? B64.charAt((n >> 6) & 63) : '='
    out += i + 2 < bytes.length ? B64.charAt(n & 63) : '='
  }
  return out
}

export const psQuote = (s: string): string => `'${s.replace(/'/g, "''")}'`
export const shQuote = (s: string): string => `'${s.replace(/'/g, `'\\''`)}'`
export const asQuote = (s: string): string => `"${s.replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/"/g, '\\"')}"`

/** `claude` and what follows it: `--resume <id>` (with /compact when asked), or the fresh prompt quoted. */
function claudeLine(spec: LaunchSpec, quote: (s: string) => string): string {
  if (spec.mode === 'resume') return `claude --resume ${spec.sessionId ?? ''}${spec.compact === true ? ` ${quote(COMPACT_PROMPT)}` : ''}`
  return spec.prompt === undefined || spec.prompt === '' ? 'claude' : `claude ${quote(spec.prompt)}`
}

/** PowerShell: the Windows Terminal tab, and the Windows clipboard fallback. Only a config dir the person chose is set. */
export function psScript(spec: LaunchSpec, dir: string | undefined): string {
  return [...(dir ? [`$env:CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR = ${psQuote(dir)}`] : []), `Set-Location -LiteralPath ${psQuote(spec.cwd)}`, claudeLine(spec, psQuote)].join('; ')
}

/** zsh / bash: macOS and Linux. */
export function posixScript(spec: LaunchSpec, dir: string | undefined): string {
  const env = dir ? `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR=${shQuote(dir)} ` : ''
  return `cd ${shQuote(spec.cwd)} && ${env}${claudeLine(spec, shQuote)}`
}

export function launchCommand(p: Platform, spec: LaunchSpec, dir: string | undefined): string {
  return p === 'windows' ? psScript(spec, dir) : posixScript(spec, dir)
}

/** wt.exe splits its own command line on ';', so the script travels base64-encoded and no argument carries a raw ';'. */
export function wtArgv(spec: LaunchSpec, dir: string | undefined): string[] {
  const title = spec.title.replace(/[;"]/g, ' ').slice(0, 40)
  const at = spec.cwd.includes(';') ? [] : ['-d', spec.cwd]
  return ['wt.exe', '-w', '0', 'nt', ...at, '--title', title, 'pwsh', '-NoExit', '-EncodedCommand', utf16leBase64(psScript(spec, dir))]
}

export type MacTerminal = 'iterm' | 'wezterm' | 'terminal'

export function macTerminalFor(termProgram: string | undefined): MacTerminal {
  if (termProgram === 'iTerm.app') return 'iterm'
  if (termProgram === 'WezTerm') return 'wezterm'
  return 'terminal'
}

/** argv that opens the command in a new tab (iTerm2, WezTerm) or window (Terminal.app). */
export function macArgv(term: MacTerminal, spec: LaunchSpec, dir: string | undefined, shell = '/bin/zsh'): string[] {
  const cmd = posixScript(spec, dir)
  if (term === 'wezterm') return ['wezterm', 'cli', 'spawn', '--cwd', spec.cwd, '--', shell, '-lc', cmd]
  const script =
    term === 'iterm'
      ? [
          'tell application "iTerm"',
          'activate',
          'if (count of windows) = 0 then',
          'create window with default profile',
          'else',
          'tell current window to create tab with default profile',
          'end if',
          `tell current session of current window to write text ${asQuote(cmd)}`,
          'end tell',
        ]
      : ['tell application "Terminal"', 'activate', `do script ${asQuote(cmd)}`, 'end tell']
  return ['osascript', ...script.flatMap(line => ['-e', line])]
}

export async function platformOf(env: LaunchEnv, run: LaunchIo['run']): Promise<Platform> {
  if (env.os === 'Windows_NT') return 'windows'
  return (await run(['uname', '-s']))?.stdout.trim() === 'Darwin' ? 'mac' : 'linux'
}

/** Why a spec cannot be launched at all ('' when it can). */
function refusal(spec: LaunchSpec): string {
  if (spec.cwd === '') return 'no folder'
  if (spec.mode === 'resume' && !SESSION_ID.test(spec.sessionId ?? '')) return 'no session id'
  return ''
}

async function openOne(io: LaunchIo, p: Platform, env: LaunchEnv, spec: LaunchSpec, dir: string | undefined): Promise<boolean> {
  const ran = async (argv: readonly string[]) => (await io.run(argv))?.exitCode === 0
  if (p === 'windows') return ran(wtArgv(spec, dir))
  if (p === 'mac') {
    // The person's own terminal first; Terminal.app is always there. First use asks for Automation permission.
    const preferred = macTerminalFor(env.termProgram)
    for (const term of preferred === 'terminal' ? (['terminal'] as const) : ([preferred, 'terminal'] as const)) {
      if (await ran(macArgv(term, spec, dir, env.shell || '/bin/zsh'))) return true
    }
  }
  return false
}

/**
 * Opens each spec in a new tab, 700 ms apart. What could not be opened is copied as one block of commands (one a line);
 * what could not be copied either is named in `failed`, as is a spec with no folder or session id.
 */
export async function launchAll(io: LaunchIo, specs: readonly LaunchSpec[]): Promise<LaunchResult> {
  if (specs.length === 0) return { opened: 0, copied: null, failed: [] }
  const env = await io.env()
  const p = await platformOf(env, io.run)
  const dir = env.configDir || undefined
  let opened = 0
  let tried = 0
  const left: LaunchSpec[] = []
  const failed: string[] = []
  for (const spec of specs) {
    const why = refusal(spec)
    if (why !== '') {
      failed.push(`${spec.title} (${why})`)
      continue
    }
    if (tried > 0) await io.sleep(LAUNCH_STAGGER_MS)
    tried += 1
    if (await openOne(io, p, env, spec, dir)) opened += 1
    else left.push(spec)
  }
  if (left.length === 0) return { opened, copied: null, failed }
  const text = left.map(spec => launchCommand(p, spec, dir)).join('\n')
  if (await io.copy(text)) return { opened, copied: text, failed }
  return { opened, copied: null, failed: [...failed, ...left.map(spec => spec.title)] }
}

/** The one toast a launch ends with. */
export function launchSummary(r: LaunchResult): string {
  const parts: string[] = []
  if (r.opened > 0) parts.push(r.opened === 1 ? 'Opened 1 tab' : `Opened ${r.opened} tabs`)
  if (r.copied !== null) {
    const n = r.copied.split('\n').length
    parts.push(n === 1 ? 'Copied — paste into a new terminal' : `Copied ${n} commands — paste each into a new terminal`)
  }
  if (r.failed.length > 0) parts.push(`Could not open: ${r.failed.join(', ')}`)
  return parts.length === 0 ? 'Nothing to open' : parts.join(' · ')
}
```

### Task 2.2: "Resumed from" records (`hooks/resumed.ts`)

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/resumed.ts`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/resumed.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `PhaseStore` (Stage 1, `hooks/phases.ts`) as the store seam; `resumePrompt` (Task 2.1) in the test.
- Produces: `RESUME_PROMPT_RE`, `resumedKey`, `resumedIdOf`, `recordResumedFrom`, `readResumedFrom`.
  - The store value at `resumed-from:<session id>` is the handover id as a plain string.
  - An empty session id, or a handover id that is not `/^[A-Za-z0-9][\w.-]*$/`, is never written.
  - Anything unreadable reads back as `null`.

The entries are tiny, one per session that resumed from a handover, and nothing prunes them. Pruning them would need a timestamp, and the contract signature carries none.

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/resumed.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: a session resumed from a handover can always say which one — the prompt the launcher writes is read back
// exactly, and the store entry round-trips per session id and tolerates junk.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { resumePrompt } from '../hooks/launch'
import type { PhaseStore } from '../hooks/phases'
import { readResumedFrom, recordResumedFrom, RESUME_PROMPT_RE, resumedIdOf, resumedKey } from '../hooks/resumed'

function memoryStore(seed: Record<string, unknown> = {}): { store: PhaseStore; values: Map<string, unknown> } {
  const values = new Map(Object.entries(seed))
  return {
    values,
    store: {
      get: async key => values.get(key),
      set: async (key, value) => {
        values.set(key, value)
      },
      delete: async key => {
        values.delete(key)
      },
    },
  }
}

describe('resumed from', () => {
  test('the launcher prompt reads back to its handover id; other prompts do not match', () => {
    const prompt = resumePrompt('2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022', 'C:\\p\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022.md')
    expect(RESUME_PROMPT_RE.test(prompt)).toBe(true)
    expect(resumedIdOf(prompt)).toBe('2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022')
    expect(resumedIdOf('  Resume from handover H-0412 (x)')).toBe('H-0412')
    expect(resumedIdOf('Please Resume from handover H-0412')).toBeNull()
    expect(resumedIdOf('Resume from handover')).toBeNull()
  })

  test('recorded per session id under resumed-from:<id>, read back; junk and empty ids read as none', async () => {
    const { store, values } = memoryStore({ [resumedKey('junk')]: { handoverId: 3 } })
    expect(resumedKey('s-1')).toBe('resumed-from:s-1')
    await recordResumedFrom(store, 's-1', 'H-0412')
    expect(values.get('resumed-from:s-1')).toBe('H-0412')
    expect(await readResumedFrom(store, 's-1')).toBe('H-0412')
    expect(await readResumedFrom(store, 's-2')).toBeNull()
    expect(await readResumedFrom(store, 'junk')).toBeNull()
    await recordResumedFrom(store, '', 'H-1')
    await recordResumedFrom(store, 's-3', 'bad id; rm')
    expect([...values.keys()].sort()).toEqual(['resumed-from:junk', 'resumed-from:s-1'])
    expect(await readResumedFrom(store, '')).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the module**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/resumed.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: remember which handover a session was resumed from (spec §10.5) — written before a `--resume` launch, by a
// fresh session's "Resume from handover" first prompt, or by handovers `r` — so the band and the Session tab can say it.
import type { PhaseStore } from './phases'

/** Matches resumePrompt's text (launch.ts) at the start of a prompt; group 1 is the handover id. */
export const RESUME_PROMPT_RE = /^\s*Resume from handover ([A-Za-z0-9][\w.-]*)/

export const resumedKey = (sessionId: string): string => `resumed-from:${sessionId}`

const HANDOVER_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9][\w.-]*$/

/** The handover id a prompt resumes from, or null. */
export function resumedIdOf(prompt: string): string | null {
  return RESUME_PROMPT_RE.exec(prompt)?.[1] ?? null
}

export async function recordResumedFrom(store: PhaseStore, sessionId: string, handoverId: string): Promise<void> {
  if (sessionId === '' || !HANDOVER_ID.test(handoverId)) return
  await store.set(resumedKey(sessionId), handoverId)
}

/** The handover this session was resumed from; null when none was recorded or the entry is unreadable. */
export async function readResumedFrom(store: PhaseStore, sessionId: string): Promise<string | null> {
  if (sessionId === '') return null
  const stored = await store.get(resumedKey(sessionId))
  return typeof stored === 'string' && HANDOVER_ID.test(stored) ? stored : null
}
```

### Task 2.3: D5, the session's handover — resolution, resumed-from at start, the resume prompt, a handover read by id

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/types/index.d.ts` (`TmSessionHandover`; PluginState `sessionHandover`, `resumedFrom`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/model.ts` (`sessionHandoverOf`, `withNotice`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/parse.ts` (`parseHandoverSlim`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/tm.ts` (`readHandover`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/flows.ts` (`TmWriter.resumedFrom`, `TmFlowDeps.resolve`, `recordResumed`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (atoms, `resolveHandover`, `loadResumed`, the `prompt.submit` hook, the call sites)
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/session-handover.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `recordResumedFrom`, `readResumedFrom`, `RESUME_PROMPT_RE`, `resumedIdOf` (Task 2.2); `phaseStoreOf` (Stage 1); `callTm`, `handoverPath`, the existing NOTICE atom (also written by handover-guard.ts, which this stage never edits).
- Produces: `TmSessionHandover`; `sessionHandoverOf`; `withNotice`; `parseHandoverSlim`; `readHandover`; `TmFlows.recordResumed`; the `sessionHandover` and `resumedFrom` state. D5 is resolved:
  - at `session.start`, after the binding and the resumed-from record are read;
  - at every `classic.SessionStart` (clear / resume / fork), from the new id's record;
  - after every good refresh;
  - after this session writes a handover;
  - after `recordResumed`.

  Drawings call `withNotice`, so a notice the handover guard writes directly is shown at once.

How a resumed-from handover is found: first in the open list. When a reachable snapshot's open list does not hold it, it is read once by id, off the caller, with `backlog_handover_get` (no `sections`, the slim view). D5 then runs again. A resumed-from handover that cannot be found falls through to the task's handover.

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/session-handover.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: the band and the Session tab always name the handover that matters to this session (spec D5) — the one it
// wrote, else the one it was resumed from (recorded by a resume launch or a "Resume from handover" first prompt), else the
// newest open one for its task — and a handover the open list no longer holds is still found by id.
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'
import type { On } from 'claude-code'

import { resumePrompt } from '../hooks/launch'
import { sessionHandoverOf, withNotice } from '../hooks/model'
import { parseHandoverSlim } from '../hooks/parse'
import type { TmHandover, TmHandoverNotice, TmSessionHandover, TmSnapshot } from '../types'
import { PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import * as R from './fixtures/replies'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { stateOf, worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const TM = { plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const T = 'mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__'
const BOUND = { 'binding:sess-A': { taskId: 'tm-audit-030', at: 0 } }
const AUDIT = '2026-10-04-audit-fixes-landed'
const SHIPPED = '2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022'
const OLD = '2026-09-20-closed-one'
const SLIM_OLD = [
  `## Handover: ${OLD}`,
  '',
  '**tldr:** Store migration verified, rows match',
  '**next_action:** Sign off tm-audit-031',
  "**task_ids:** ['tm-audit-031', 'tm-audit-029']",
  '**status:** closed',
  '**created:** 2026-09-20T16:31:00',
].join('\n')
const SH = `${PLUGIN}.sessionHandover`

const ho = (id: string, created: string, taskIds: readonly string[], nextAction = ''): TmHandover => ({
  id,
  created,
  tldr: `tldr of ${id}`,
  nextAction,
  path: `/p/${id}.md`,
  branch: '',
  taskIds,
  thread: '',
})
const snap = (handovers: readonly TmHandover[], boundId: string | null): TmSnapshot => ({
  reachable: true,
  reason: '',
  fetchedAt: 0,
  queue: [],
  queueTotal: 0,
  handovers,
  handoversTotal: handovers.length,
  bound: boundId === null ? null : { taskId: boundId, inferred: false, detail: null, pipeline: null },
})
const NOTICE: TmHandoverNotice = { id: 'h-new', tldr: 'Wrote it here', path: '/p/h-new.md', text: 'block' }

function tmWorld(on: On, store: Record<string, unknown> = BOUND) {
  const clock = mock.clock(on)
  const world = worldOf(on, clock, store)
  world.mcp = (tool, args) => (tool === 'backlog_handover_get' ? { text: args.handover_id === OLD ? SLIM_OLD : `Handover not found: ${String(args.handover_id)}` } : R.backlog(tool, args))
  on('prompt.submit', ($, e) => ({ text: e.text }))
  return { clock, world, state: stateOf(on) }
}

describe('D5, purely', () => {
  const list = [ho('h-2', '2026-10-05T09:00', ['t-1'], 'next 2'), ho('h-1', '2026-10-04T09:00', ['t-1', 't-2'])]

  test('written here beats resumed from, which beats the newest open handover of the bound task', () => {
    const s = snap(list, 't-2')
    expect(sessionHandoverOf({ notice: NOTICE, resumedId: 'h-2', resumed: null, snapshot: s })).toEqual({
      id: 'h-new',
      tag: 'written here',
      tldr: 'Wrote it here',
      path: '/p/h-new.md',
      created: '',
    })
    expect(sessionHandoverOf({ notice: null, resumedId: 'h-2', resumed: null, snapshot: s })).toEqual({
      id: 'h-2',
      tag: 'resumed from',
      tldr: 'tldr of h-2',
      path: '/p/h-2.md',
      created: '2026-10-05T09:00',
      next: 'next 2',
    })
    expect(sessionHandoverOf({ notice: null, resumedId: '', resumed: null, snapshot: s })?.id).toBe('h-1')
    expect(sessionHandoverOf({ notice: null, resumedId: '', resumed: null, snapshot: snap(list, 't-1') })?.id).toBe('h-2')
  })

  test('a resumed-from handover outside the list comes from its read; unknown, it falls through to the task; nothing is null', () => {
    const away = ho('h-9', '2026-09-01T10:00', [])
    expect(sessionHandoverOf({ notice: null, resumedId: 'h-9', resumed: away, snapshot: snap(list, 't-1') })?.tag).toBe('resumed from')
    expect(sessionHandoverOf({ notice: null, resumedId: 'h-9', resumed: null, snapshot: snap(list, 't-1') })).toMatchObject({ id: 'h-2', tag: 'task' })
    expect(sessionHandoverOf({ notice: null, resumedId: '', resumed: null, snapshot: snap(list, null) })).toBeNull()
    expect(sessionHandoverOf({ notice: null, resumedId: '', resumed: null, snapshot: null })).toBeNull()
  })

  test('a drawing lets a newer notice win over what was resolved', () => {
    const resolved: TmSessionHandover = { id: 'h-2', tag: 'task', tldr: 't', path: '/p', created: '' }
    expect(withNotice(resolved, null, null)).toBe(resolved)
    expect(withNotice(resolved, NOTICE, snap(list, null))).toMatchObject({ id: 'h-new', tag: 'written here' })
    const written: TmSessionHandover = { id: 'h-new', tag: 'written here', tldr: 'Wrote it here', path: '/p/h-new.md', created: '' }
    expect(withNotice(written, NOTICE, null)).toBe(written)
  })

  test("the slim backlog_handover_get reply reads into a handover; not found is null; anything else is unreadable", () => {
    const read = parseHandoverSlim(SLIM_OLD, 'C:\\work\\proj')
    expect(read).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: {
        id: OLD,
        created: '2026-09-20T16:31:00',
        tldr: 'Store migration verified, rows match',
        nextAction: 'Sign off tm-audit-031',
        path: `C:\\work\\proj\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\${OLD}.md`,
        branch: '',
        taskIds: ['tm-audit-031', 'tm-audit-029'],
        thread: '',
      },
    })
    expect(parseHandoverSlim('Handover not found: x', '/r')).toEqual({ ok: true, value: null })
    expect(parseHandoverSlim('No backlog found.', '/r').ok).toBe(false)
  })
})

describe('D5, live', () => {
  test('with no record the band handover is the newest open one for the bound task', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const { clock, state } = tmWorld(on)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)

    expect(state.get(SH)).toMatchObject({ id: OLD, tag: 'resumed from', tldr: 'Store migration verified, rows match' })
    await $.turn.complete({ answer: '', reason: 'end_turn' } as never)

    expect(world.store.get('resumed-from:sess-A')).toBe(SHIPPED)
  })

  test('a handover written here wins over the record; /resume reads the resumed id\'s record', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const { clock, world, state } = tmWorld(on, { ...BOUND, 'resumed-from:sess-A': SHIPPED, 'resumed-from:sess-R': AUDIT })
    on('tool.call', ($, e) => ({ result: { content: [{ type: 'text', text: R.HANDOVER_WRITTEN }] }, text: R.HANDOVER_WRITTEN }) as never)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await clock.settle()
    await $.tool.call({ tool: `${T}backlog_handover_create`, tldr: 'Live data wired into the band', next_action: 'Run it' } as never)
    await clock.settle()
    expect(state.get(SH)).toMatchObject({ id: '2026-10-06-live-data-wired', tag: 'written here', tldr: 'Live data wired into the band' })

    await $.classic.SessionStart({ source: 'resume', session_id: 'sess-R' } as never)
    await clock.settle()
    expect(state.get(`${PLUGIN}.resumedFrom`)).toBe(AUDIT)
  })
})
```

```ts
// ── The review card (redesigned 2026-10-06, spec §6.1)
```

with:

```ts
const fromHandover = (h: TmHandover, tag: TmSessionHandover['tag']): TmSessionHandover => ({
  id: h.id,
  tag,
  tldr: h.tldr,
  path: h.path,
  created: h.created,
  ...(h.nextAction === '' ? {} : { next: h.nextAction }),
})

/**
 * D5: the handover this session shows — the one it wrote (the notice), else the one it was resumed from (from the open list,
 * or `resumed` read by id), else the newest open handover linked to its bound task; null when none applies.
 */
export function sessionHandoverOf(a: {
  readonly notice: TmHandoverNotice | null
  readonly resumedId: string
  readonly resumed: TmHandover | null
  readonly snapshot: TmSnapshot | null
}): TmSessionHandover | null {
  const listed = (id: string): TmHandover | undefined => a.snapshot?.handovers.find(h => h.id === id)
  if (a.notice !== null) {
    const h = listed(a.notice.id)
    return { id: a.notice.id, tag: 'written here', tldr: a.notice.tldr, path: a.notice.path, created: h?.created ?? '', ...(h && h.nextAction !== '' ? { next: h.nextAction } : {}) }
  }
  if (a.resumedId !== '') {
    const h = listed(a.resumedId) ?? (a.resumed?.id === a.resumedId ? a.resumed : undefined)
    if (h !== undefined) return fromHandover(h, 'resumed from')
  }
  const taskId = a.snapshot?.bound?.taskId
  const linked = taskId === undefined ? undefined : a.snapshot?.handovers.find(h => h.taskIds.includes(taskId))
  return linked === undefined ? null : fromHandover(linked, 'task')
}

/** What a surface draws: the resolved handover, unless a handover notice arrived since (the guard writes one directly). */
export function withNotice(resolved: TmSessionHandover | null, notice: TmHandoverNotice | null, snapshot: TmSnapshot | null): TmSessionHandover | null {
  if (notice === null || (resolved?.id === notice.id && resolved.tag === 'written here')) return resolved
  return sessionHandoverOf({ notice, resumedId: '', resumed: null, snapshot })
}
```

In `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/parse.ts`, replace:

```ts
export type HandoverReceipt = {
```

with:

```ts
/**
 * backlog_handover_get's slim view (no sections): `## Handover: <id>`, then `**field:** value` lines — tldr, next_action,
 * task_ids (a list), status, created. "Handover not found" reads as null; anything else without the header is unreadable.
 */
export function parseHandoverSlim(raw: string, root: string): Parsed<TmHandover | null> {
  const text = raw.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').trim()
  if (/^Handover not found/.test(text)) return ok(null)
  const head = /^## Handover: (\S+)/.exec(text)
  if (head === null) return fail(`handover_get: ${firstParagraph(text) || 'empty reply'}`)
  const fields = new Map<string, string>()
  for (const line of text.split('\n').slice(1)) {
    const m = /^\*\*([a-z_]+):\*\* ?(.*)$/.exec(line)
    if (m !== null) fields.set(m[1] ?? '', (m[2] ?? '').trim())
  }
  const get = (key: string): string => {
    const v = fields.get(key) ?? ''
    return v === 'None' ? '' : v
  }
  const id = head[1] ?? ''
  return ok({
    id,
    created: get('created'),
    tldr: get('tldr'),
    nextAction: get('next_action'),
    path: handoverPath(root, id),
    branch: '',
    taskIds: [...get('task_ids').matchAll(/'([^']+)'|"([^"]+)"/g)].map(m => m[1] ?? m[2] ?? ''),
    thread: '',
  })
}
```

```ts
/**
 * One handover by id (backlog_handover_get, slim): the band's "resumed from" when the open list does not hold it. Null when
 * the server does not know it, refuses, is unreachable or answers unreadably (that text goes to the debug log).
 */
export async function readHandover(host: TmHost, handoverId: string): Promise<TmHandover | null> {
  try {
    const text = (await callTm(host, 'backlog_handover_get', { handover_id: handoverId })).text
    const parsed = parseHandoverSlim(text, await host.repoRoot())
    if (parsed.ok) return parsed.value
    host.log(`taskmaster-mods: unreadable backlog_handover_get reply: ${text.slice(0, 2000)}`)
    return null
  } catch (error) {
    host.log(`taskmaster-mods: handover ${handoverId} not read: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`)
    return null
  }
}
```

8. Replace the doc line `/** The handover this session's main agent just wrote, from the call's input and the server's receipt: the band's notice. */` (above `noteHandover`) with:

```ts
/**
 * D5 (spec §3): the handover this session shows — written here (the notice) > resumed from (`resumedFrom`) > the newest open
 * one linked to the bound task. A resumed-from handover the open list does not hold is read by id once (tm), off the caller;
 * D5 runs again when it lands.
 */
async function resolveHandover($: EngineInterface): Promise<void> {
  const { snapshot } = await dataOf($)
  const resumedId = await read($, RESUMED_FROM)
  const host = mod.host
  // Read by id only once a reachable snapshot's open list has been looked at and does not hold it.
  const unlisted = snapshot !== null && snapshot.reachable && !snapshot.handovers.some(h => h.id === resumedId)
  if (resumedId !== '' && unlisted && mod.source === 'tm' && host !== null && !mod.resumedAsked.has(resumedId)) {
    mod.resumedAsked.add(resumedId)
    host.after(0, () => {
      void readHandover(host, resumedId).then(async found => {
        mod.resumedCache.set(resumedId, found)
        await mod.resolve?.()
      })
    })
  }
  const next = sessionHandoverOf({ notice: await read($, NOTICE), resumedId, resumed: mod.resumedCache.get(resumedId) ?? null, snapshot })
  await update($, SESSION_HANDOVER, prev => (JSON.stringify(prev) === JSON.stringify(next) ? prev : next))
}

/** This session's resumed-from record (`$.store` `resumed-from:<session id>`) into `resumedFrom`; unreadable reads as none. */
async function loadResumed($: EngineInterface, host: TmHost, sessionId: string): Promise<void> {
  let id = ''
  try {
    id = (await readResumedFrom(phaseStoreOf(host), sessionId)) ?? ''
  } catch {
    // a store that cannot be read now: no resumed-from line this time
  }
  await update($, RESUMED_FROM, () => id)
}

/** The handover this session's main agent just wrote, from the call's input and the server's receipt: the band's notice. */
```

**Interfaces:**

- Produces:

- Behaviour:
  - **`openHandover`** opens tm-handovers on the page that holds the handover, with that handover picked and `ho:<id>` focused. One not in the open list opens page 1 and toasts. Stage 4 adds selecting its phase.
  - **`copyHandover`** takes this session's notice block first (the receipt's own), else the open list's `handoverCopyText`, else one read by id. It toasts `Copied handover <id>` or the clipboard fallback with the path.
  - **`launch`** in demo runs nothing and toasts `Demo: would open <titles>`.

`$.env.get` only takes a string literal (d.ts), so `launchEnv` reads the four names one by one; a name never travels as a variable.

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/session-flows.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: the keys the band and the Session tab share — open a pane, open one handover where it sits, copy any handover
// by id, launch a fresh session in a new tab — behave the same from every surface, and demo never opens a real terminal.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { createFlows, type TmWriter } from '../hooks/flows'
import type { TmHost } from '../hooks/host'
import { psScript, resumePrompt } from '../hooks/launch'
import type { TmHandover, TmHandoverNotice, TmSnapshot } from '../types'

const ho = (n: number): TmHandover => ({
  id: `h-${n}`,
  created: `2026-10-0${n % 9}T10:00`,
  tldr: `tldr ${n}`,
  nextAction: `next ${n}`,
  path: `C:\\p\\h-${n}.md`,
  branch: '',
  taskIds: [],
  thread: `thread-${n}`,
})
const SNAP: TmSnapshot = {
  reachable: true,
  reason: '',
  fetchedAt: 0,
  queue: [],
  queueTotal: 0,
  handovers: [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 6, 7].map(ho),
  handoversTotal: 7,
  bound: null,
}
const NOTICE: TmHandoverNotice = { id: 'h-new', tldr: 'Wrote it', path: 'C:\\p\\h-new.md', text: 'Wrote it\nC:\\p\\h-new.md\nResume: x — y' }

/** Flows over a recording host and an in-memory writer; `exits` answers each command by argv[0] (absent: cannot start). */
function rig(a: { source?: 'tm' | 'demo'; notice?: TmHandoverNotice | null; exits?: Record<string, number>; copies?: boolean; read?: TmHandover | null } = {}) {
  const seen = { opened: [] as string[], toasts: [] as string[], copies: [] as string[], runs: [] as string[][], focus: [] as string[], page: -1, pick: '' }
  let notice = a.notice ?? null
  let snapshot: TmSnapshot | null = SNAP
  const host = new Proxy(
    {
      openPane: async (id: string) => {
        seen.opened.push(id)
        return true
      },
      toast: (text: string) => {
        seen.toasts.push(text)
      },
      copy: async (text: string) => {
        seen.copies.push(text)
        return a.copies === false ? { isCopied: false, reason: 'no-clipboard' } : { isCopied: true, reason: '' }
      },
      focus: async (requestId: string, key: string) => {
        seen.focus.push(`${requestId}/${key}`)
      },
      launchEnv: async () => ({ os: 'Windows_NT' }),
      run: async (argv: readonly string[]) => {
        seen.runs.push([...argv])
        const code = (a.exits ?? { 'wt.exe': 0 })[argv[0] ?? '']
        return code === undefined ? null : { exitCode: code, stdout: '' }
      },
      sleep: async () => undefined,
      after: () => undefined,
    },
    { get: (target, key) => (key in target ? target[key as keyof typeof target] : async () => undefined) },
  ) as unknown as TmHost
  const held = {
    notice: async (change: (n: TmHandoverNotice | null) => TmHandoverNotice | null) => {
      notice = change(notice)
    },
    snapshot: async (change: (s: TmSnapshot | null) => TmSnapshot | null) => {
      snapshot = change(snapshot)
    },
    handoverPage: async (page: number) => {
      seen.page = page
    },
    pick: async (id: string) => {
      seen.pick = id
    },
  }
  const write = new Proxy(held, { get: (target, key) => (key in target ? target[key as keyof typeof held] : async () => undefined) }) as unknown as TmWriter
  const flows = createFlows({
    host,
    write,
    actions: { done: async () => ({ ok: true }), backToAgent: async () => ({ ok: true }) },
    afterWrite: () => undefined,
    boundId: async () => null,
    source: a.source ?? 'tm',
    ...(a.read === undefined ? {} : { readHandover: async () => a.read ?? null }),
  })
  return { flows, seen }
}

describe('opening panes', () => {
  test('1 2 3 4 open review, handovers, session and restore', async () => {
    const { flows, seen } = rig()
    for (const pane of ['review', 'handovers', 'session', 'restore'] as const) await flows.openPane(pane)
    expect(seen.opened).toEqual(['tm-review', 'tm-handovers', 'tm-session', 'tm-restore'])
  })

  test('one handover opens the pane on its page, picked and focused; one not listed opens page 1 and says so', async () => {
    const { flows, seen } = rig()
    await flows.openHandover('h-7')
    expect([seen.page, seen.pick, seen.opened, seen.focus]).toEqual([1, 'h-7', ['tm-handovers'], ['tm-handovers/ho:h-7']])
    await flows.openHandover('h-99')
    expect([seen.page, seen.pick]).toEqual([0, ''])
    expect(seen.toasts).toEqual(['taskmaster-mods: handover h-99 is not in the open list'])
  })
})

describe('copy by id', () => {
  test("this session's notice copies its own block; a listed handover its Telegram block", async () => {
    const { flows, seen } = rig({ notice: NOTICE })
    await flows.copyHandover('h-new')
    await flows.copyHandover('h-2')
    expect(seen.copies).toEqual([NOTICE.text, 'tldr 2\nC:\\p\\h-2.md\nResume: thread-2 — next 2'])
    expect(seen.toasts).toEqual(['Copied handover h-new', 'Copied handover h-2'])
  })

  test('a handover outside the list is read by id; unknown says not found; no clipboard names the path', async () => {
    const away = { ...ho(9), id: 'h-away', path: 'C:\\p\\h-away.md' }
    const { flows, seen } = rig({ read: away, copies: false })
    await flows.copyHandover('h-away')
    expect(seen.copies).toEqual(['tldr 9\nC:\\p\\h-away.md\nResume: thread-9 — next 9'])
    expect(seen.toasts).toEqual(['Clipboard unavailable (no-clipboard): copy by hand from C:\\p\\h-away.md'])
    const none = rig({ read: null })
    await none.flows.copyHandover('h-gone')
    expect(none.seen.toasts).toEqual(['taskmaster-mods: handover h-gone not found'])
  })
})

describe('launch', () => {
  const spec = { cwd: 'C:\\work\\proj', title: 'handover h-2', mode: 'fresh' as const, prompt: resumePrompt('h-2', 'C:\\p\\h-2.md') }

  test('opens a Windows Terminal tab with the fresh prompt and toasts once', async () => {
    const { flows, seen } = rig()
    expect(await flows.launch([spec])).toEqual({ opened: 1, copied: null, failed: [] })
    expect(seen.runs[0]?.slice(0, 4)).toEqual(['wt.exe', '-w', '0', 'nt'])
    expect(seen.toasts).toEqual(['Opened 1 tab'])
  })

  test('no terminal: the command is copied and the toast says to paste it', async () => {
    const { flows, seen } = rig({ exits: {} })
    await flows.launch([spec])
    expect(seen.copies).toEqual([psScript(spec, undefined)])
    expect(seen.toasts).toEqual(['Copied — paste into a new terminal'])
  })

  test('demo runs nothing and says what it would open', async () => {
    const { flows, seen } = rig({ source: 'demo' })
    expect(await flows.launch([spec])).toEqual({ opened: 0, copied: null, failed: [] })
    expect(seen.runs).toEqual([])
    expect(seen.toasts).toEqual(['Demo: would open handover h-2'])
  })
})
```

```ts
  const fill = async (text: string): Promise<void> => {
    if (!(await d.host.fill(text))) d.host.toast(PROMPT_REFUSED)
  }
  // A handover's Telegram-ready block by id: this session's notice (its block is the receipt's own), else the open list,
  // else (tm) one read by id. Read through the writers, which hand back the current state unchanged.
  const handoverText = async (id: string): Promise<{ readonly text: string; readonly path: string } | null> => {
    const seen: { notice: TmHandoverNotice | null; listed: TmHandover | undefined } = { notice: null, listed: undefined }
    await d.write.notice(n => {
      seen.notice = n
      return n
    })
    if (seen.notice !== null && seen.notice.id === id) return { text: seen.notice.text, path: seen.notice.path }
    await d.write.snapshot(s => {
      seen.listed = s?.handovers.find(h => h.id === id)
      return s
    })
    const found = seen.listed ?? (await d.readHandover?.(id)) ?? null
    return found === null ? null : { text: handoverCopyText(found), path: found.path }
  }
  const openHandovers = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await d.write.pick('')
    await d.write.summaryOpen(() => '')
    await d.write.handoverPage(0)
    await open(HANDOVERS, 'Handovers')
  }
```

```ts
  return {
    openReview,
    openHandovers,
    /** Band and Session `1`–`4`: the pane, opened as its own command opens it. */
    openPane: async (pane: TmPane): Promise<void> => {
      if (pane === 'review') return openReview()
      if (pane === 'handovers') return openHandovers()
      return pane === 'session' ? open(SESSION, 'Session') : open(RESTORE, 'Restore')
    },
    /** Session `h`: the handovers pane on the page that holds this handover, picked and focused (Stage 4 adds its phase). */
    openHandover: async (handoverId: string): Promise<void> => {
      const at = { index: -1 }
      await d.write.snapshot(s => {
        at.index = s?.handovers.findIndex(h => h.id === handoverId) ?? -1
        return s
      })
      await d.write.summaryOpen(() => '')
      await d.write.handoverPage(at.index < 0 ? 0 : Math.floor(at.index / PAGE_SIZE))
      await d.write.pick(at.index < 0 ? '' : handoverId)
      await open(HANDOVERS, 'Handovers')
      if (at.index < 0) d.host.toast(`taskmaster-mods: handover ${handoverId} is not in the open list`)
      else await d.host.focus(HANDOVERS, `ho:${handoverId}`)
    },
    /** New terminal tabs, 700 ms apart (launch.ts), then one toast; demo opens nothing and says what it would open. */
    launch: async (specs: readonly LaunchSpec[], surface?: RenderSurface): Promise<LaunchResult> => {
      if (d.source === 'demo') {
        d.host.toast(`Demo: would open ${specs.map(s => s.title).join(', ')}`)
        return { opened: 0, copied: null, failed: [] }
      }
      const r = await launchAll(
        {
          env: () => d.host.launchEnv(),
          run: argv => d.host.run(argv, LAUNCH_TIMEOUT_MS),
          copy: async text => (await d.host.copy(text, surface)).isCopied,
          sleep: ms => d.host.sleep(ms),
        },
        specs,
      )
      d.host.toast(launchSummary(r))
      return r
    },
    loadPhases,
```

### Task 2.5: The band — two lines on their own raised ground under `◆ TASKMASTER`

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/band.tsx` (the band tree moves out of draw.tsx)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/draw.tsx` (remove `BandHandlers` and `bandTree`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/model.ts` (new `BandTask` / `BandModel` / `bandModel`; `statusWord`, `statusTone`, `isReady`, `restoreHiddenKey`; `bandRows` goes)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/types/index.d.ts` (`TmTaskDetail.phase`; `TmRestorePending`, `TmSessionBurst`; PluginState `restorePending`, `restoreHidden`, `sessionBurst`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/parse.ts` (`parseGetTask` reads `phase`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/flows.ts` (`hideRestore`, `TmWriter.restoreHidden`; `copyNotice` goes)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/demo.ts` (`DEMO_SEED = 5`, `DEMO_RESUMED_FROM`, a phase on each demo task)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (atoms, `restoreShown`, the AbovePrompt render, the demo resumed-from seed)
- Replace: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/band.test.ts`
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/model.test.ts`, `tests/actions.test.ts`, `tests/live.test.ts`, `tests/parse.test.ts`, `tests/refresh.test.ts`, `tests/reload.test.ts`, `tests/session.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From the kit (Stage 1): `kitOf`, `badge`, `keycap` (`on: 'raised'`, `faded`, `autoFocus`), `label`, `signal`, `TM_TAG`, `Kit.badges`.
  - From Tasks 2.3 and 2.4: `withNotice`, `TmFlows.openPane`, `copyHandover(id, surface)`, `TmPane`.
  - Existing: `bandAskDone`, `bandConfirmDone`, `bandCancel`.
- Produces: `bandTree`, `BandHandlers`, `HANDOVER_TAG`; the new `bandModel`; `statusWord`, `statusTone`, `isReady`; `restoreHiddenKey`; `TmFlows.hideRestore`; `TmRestorePending`, `TmSessionBurst` and their state; `TmTaskDetail.phase`; `DEMO_SEED = 5`, `DEMO_RESUMED_FROM`.

What the person sees (spec §6; the user's main complaint is the band blending into the transcript):

```
                                                                                       (one blank row)
 ◆ TASKMASTER   IN REVIEW   unified-chat-022  Unified chat pre-build gets the full…   d done   3 session ›
 ↳ HANDOVER  resumed from ·  Audit fixes landed on the native branch    c copy   1 5 waiting on you   2 6 handovers
```

- Both lines sit on `surface.raised` across the full width, with `paddingX={1}`. Above them is one blank row, when the prompt area gives three rows or more.
- `◆ TASKMASTER` is a signature-24 badge; the status is a 12 badge in its tone; the id is subtle; the title is bold.
- Each line squeezes its words through five levels before its text drops under 12 cells:
  - the counts first (`waiting on you` → `waiting` → bare numbers);
  - then the handover tag;
  - then `session ›` → `session` → key only, with `◆ TASKMASTER` → `◆ TM`;
  - at the tightest the status badge goes, the id is cut to 8 characters, and offline shows its glyph alone.
- Keys drop only when even that does not fit.
- `d` asks on line 2 (` done <id>? `, `y yes`, `n no` focused). A refusal shows ` REFUSED ` and the server's words on line 2.
- Offline: a critical `offline` signal on line 1, and the counts read `–` and fade.
- `4 N restore` and `x hide` appear only while Stage 5's `restorePending` is set and not hidden.

Replace `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/band.test.ts` with:

```ts
// User intent: the band as a person sees it (spec §6) — two lines at most on their own raised ground under `◆ TASKMASTER`,
// never mistaken for transcript text: the task with `d` and `3 session`, the session's handover with `c`, keys `1` `2` `4`
// to the panes, sized to the room on both surfaces, and its `d` asking before it signs anything off.
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { DEMO_DETAILS, DEMO_REASON } from '../hooks/demo'
import type { TmSnapshot } from '../types'
import { BAND, PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import { type Drawn, isEl, widthOf } from './fixtures/measure'
import * as R from './fixtures/replies'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { stateOf, worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const DEMO = { options: { source: 'demo' }, plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const TM = { plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const SNAP = `${PLUGIN}.snapshot`
const AUDIT = '2026-10-04-audit-fixes-landed'
const AUDIT_BLOCK = `Audit fixes landed on the native branch\nC:\\Users\\demo\\project\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\${AUDIT}.md\nResume: tm-audit — Record the merge for tm-audit-030`
const BURST = { epoch: 1_760_000_000, unfinished: 3 }

/** Our band in a mounted AbovePrompt: its blank-row wrapper, the raised ground inside it, and that ground's lines. */
async function bandOf(ui: { drawn: () => Promise<unknown> }): Promise<{ outer: Drawn; ground: Drawn; lines: Drawn[] }> {
  const drawn = (await ui.drawn()) as Drawn
  const outer = drawn.children?.[0]
  const ground = isEl(outer) ? outer.children?.[0] : undefined
  if (!isEl(outer) || !isEl(ground)) throw new Error(`no band drawn: ${JSON.stringify(drawn)}`)
  return { outer, ground, lines: (ground.children ?? []).filter(isEl) }
}

describe('band', () => {
  test('draws on its own raised ground, one blank row above, above whatever the other mods drew', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    const { outer, ground, lines } = await bandOf(ui)
    expect(outer.props?.paddingTop).toBe(1)
    expect(ground.props).toMatchObject({ backgroundColor: '#1d1d1b', paddingX: 1 })
    expect(lines).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' ◆ TASKMASTER ' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' IN REVIEW ' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'unified-chat-022' })).toBeDefined()
    const tree = JSON.stringify(await ui.drawn())
    expect(tree.indexOf('◆ TASKMASTER')).toBeLessThan(tree.indexOf('BELOW'))
  })

  test('yields entirely to a survey', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const tree = JSON.stringify(await $.ui.render({ ...BAND, props: { ...BAND.props, hasSurvey: true } }))
    expect(tree).not.toContain('TASKMASTER')
    expect(tree).toContain('BELOW')
  })

  test('hidden when nothing is bound, no handover and nothing counted', TM, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    expect(await $.ui.render(BAND)).toEqual({ type: 'Text', children: ['BELOW'] })
  })

  test('line 1: the task with d done and 3 session; line 2: the handover it was resumed from, c copy and the counts', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    for (const [key, hotkey, label] of [
      ['band-done', 'd', 'done'],
      ['band-session', '3', 'session ›'],
      ['band-copy', 'c', 'copy'],
      ['band-review', '1', '5 waiting on you'],
      ['band-handovers', '2', '6 handovers'],
    ] as const) {
      expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key }))?.props, key).toMatchObject({ hotkey, label, plain: true })
    }
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: '↳ HANDOVER' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'resumed from ·' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'Audit fixes landed on the native branch' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-restore' })).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('1 2 3 open review, handovers and session; c copies the handover block', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    for (const key of ['band-review', 'band-handovers', 'band-session', 'band-copy']) await ui.press({ key })
    expect(world.opened).toEqual(['tm-review', 'tm-handovers', 'tm-session'])
    expect(world.copies).toEqual([AUDIT_BLOCK])
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual([`Copied handover ${AUDIT}`])
  })

  test('no handover: the counts move up and the band is one line; no task: the chip and the counts', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    const state = stateOf(on)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    state.set(`${PLUGIN}.sessionHandover`, null)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    expect((await bandOf(ui)).lines).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-review' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-done' })).toBeDefined()
    state.set(SNAP, { ...(state.get(SNAP) as TmSnapshot), bound: null })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect((await bandOf(ui)).lines).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'unified-chat-022' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-review' }))?.props.label).toBe('5 waiting on you')
  })

  test('every row fits from 30 to 120 columns on both surfaces; words shrink to key and count before any key goes', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    for (const surface of ['terminal', 'desktop'] as const) {
      for (const columns of [30, 44, 60, 90, 120]) {
        const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND, surface, props: { ...BAND.props, bodyColumns: columns } })
        const { ground, lines } = await bandOf(ui)
        // Cells are the terminal's measure; the desktop lays out its own native buttons, so there only the keys are checked.
        if (surface === 'terminal') {
          expect(widthOf(ground), `${columns}`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(columns)
          for (const line of lines) expect(widthOf(line), `${columns}: ${JSON.stringify(line)}`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(columns - 2)
        }
        for (const key of ['band-done', 'band-session', 'band-copy', 'band-review', 'band-handovers']) {
          expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key }), `${surface} ${columns} ${key}`).toBeDefined()
        }
        await ui.unmount()
      }
    }
    const narrow = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND, props: { ...BAND.props, bodyColumns: 30 } })
    expect((await narrow.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-review' }))?.props.label).toBe('5')
    expect(await narrow.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' ◆ TM ' })).toBeDefined()
  })

  test('two rows: no blank row above; one row: line 1 alone, or the y/n while a confirm is up', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const two = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND, props: { ...BAND.props, maxRows: 2 } })
    expect((await bandOf(two)).outer.props?.paddingTop).toBe(0)
    expect((await bandOf(two)).lines).toHaveLength(2)
    await two.unmount()
    const one = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND, props: { ...BAND.props, maxRows: 1 } })
    expect((await bandOf(one)).lines).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(await one.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-done' })).toBeDefined()
    await one.press({ key: 'band-done' })
    expect(await one.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-yes' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await one.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-no' })).toBeDefined()
  })

  test('d asks first, n cancels, y signs the bound task off and the band moves on', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-done' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' done unified-chat-022? ' })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-no' }))?.props.autoFocus).toBe(true)
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-no' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-yes' })).toBeUndefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-done' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-yes' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'unified-chat-022' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-review' }))?.props.label).toBe('4 waiting on you')
  })

  test('a narrow confirm never shows y without n, and its question names the task', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    for (const columns of [44, 30, 24]) {
      const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND, props: { ...BAND.props, bodyColumns: columns, maxRows: 4 } })
      await ui.press({ key: 'band-done' })
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-yes' }), `${columns}`).toBeDefined()
      expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-no' }))?.props.autoFocus, `${columns}`).toBe(true)
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: / (done )?unified-[-a-z0-9]*…?\? / }), `${columns}`).toBeDefined()
      for (const line of (await bandOf(ui)).lines) expect(widthOf(line), `${columns}: ${JSON.stringify(line)}`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(columns - 2)
      await ui.press({ key: 'band-no' })
      await ui.unmount()
    }
  })

  test('a band refusal shows on line 2, only on the task it was given for', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    const state = stateOf(on)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const demo = state.get(SNAP) as TmSnapshot
    const bind = (id: string) => state.set(SNAP, { ...demo, bound: { taskId: id, inferred: false, detail: DEMO_DETAILS[id] ?? null, pipeline: null } })
    bind('tm-audit-031')
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-done' })
    await ui.redraw()
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-yes' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' REFUSED ' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /outstanding gates/ })).toBeDefined()
    bind('docs-012')
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' REFUSED ' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /outstanding gates/ })).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('a refresh between d and y never retargets y (Review Focus 5)', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    const state = stateOf(on)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const demo = state.get(SNAP) as TmSnapshot
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-done' })
    await ui.redraw()
    state.set(SNAP, { ...demo, queue: [...demo.queue].reverse() })
    await ui.redraw()
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-yes' })
    const after = state.get(SNAP) as TmSnapshot
    expect(after.queue.map(i => i.id)).toEqual(['DEC-4', 'ISS-7', 'docs-012', 'tm-audit-031'])
    expect(after.bound).toBeNull()
    state.set(SNAP, { ...demo, bound: { taskId: 'tm-audit-031', inferred: false, detail: DEMO_DETAILS['tm-audit-031'] ?? null, pipeline: null } })
    await ui.redraw()
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-done' })
    await ui.redraw()
    state.set(SNAP, { ...demo })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-yes' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-done' })).toBeDefined()
    expect((state.get(SNAP) as TmSnapshot).queue).toHaveLength(5)
  })

  test('d shows only for a bound task that is ready: not for an in-progress one with gates outstanding', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    const state = stateOf(on)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const demo = state.get(SNAP) as TmSnapshot
    const detail = { ...DEMO_DETAILS['tm-audit-031']!, status: 'in-progress' }
    state.set(SNAP, { ...demo, reason: DEMO_REASON, bound: { taskId: 'tm-audit-031', inferred: false, detail, pipeline: { laneless: false, lane: 'full', gateState: '', outstanding: ['review-gate'] } } })
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' IN PROGRESS ' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-done' })).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('4 restore shows while a burst is pending; x hides it for that burst, here and in every later session', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock, { 'binding:sess-A': { taskId: 'tm-audit-030', at: 0 } })
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    const state = stateOf(on, { [`${PLUGIN}.restorePending`]: BURST })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-restore' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '4', label: '3 restore' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-restore-hide' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'x', label: 'hide' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-restore' })
    expect(world.opened).toEqual(['tm-restore'])
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-restore-hide' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(world.store.get(`restore-hidden:${BURST.epoch}`)).toBe(true)
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-restore' })).toBeUndefined()
    // Another session: nothing hidden in its own state, the store still hides that burst; a newer burst shows again.
    state.set(`${PLUGIN}.restoreHidden`, null)
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-restore' })).toBeUndefined()
    state.set(`${PLUGIN}.restorePending`, { epoch: BURST.epoch + 600, unfinished: 2 })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-restore' }))?.props.label).toBe('2 restore')
  })

  test('offline: the offline signal on line 1 and the counts faded, still pressable', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock, { 'binding:sess-A': { taskId: 'tm-audit-030', at: 0 } })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...BAND })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' offline' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'tm-audit-030' })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'band-review' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ label: '– waiting on you', dimColor: true })
    await ui.press({ key: 'band-review' })
    expect(world.opened).toEqual(['tm-review'])
  })
})
```

In `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/model.test.ts`:
- In the `../hooks/model` import, remove `bandRows,`; add `isReady,` before `isStaleTicks,`, and `statusTone,` and `statusWord,` after `stageLine,`.
- Then replace the whole `describe('band', () => { … })` block (from `describe('band', () => {` up to, not including, `describe('text', () => {`) with:

```ts
describe('band', () => {
  const NONE = { handover: null, restore: null, connecting: false }
  const HO = { id: 'h1', tag: 'task' as const, tldr: 'Wired live data', path: '/p/h1.md', created: '' }
  const bound = (detail: TmTaskDetail | null, pipeline = PIPELINE, inferred = false) => ({ taskId: 'tm-audit-030', inferred, detail, pipeline })

  test('hidden entirely with no task, no handover, no restore burst and nothing counted', () => {
    expect(bandModel(null, NONE)).toBeNull()
    expect(bandModel(snap({}), NONE)).toBeNull()
    expect(bandModel(snap({ reachable: false, reason: 'down' }), NONE)).toBeNull()
  })

  test('a bound task carries its title and status; d is offered only when it is ready and bound here', () => {
    const m = bandModel(snap({ bound: bound(DETAIL) }), NONE)
    expect(m?.task).toEqual({ id: 'tm-audit-030', title: 'Audit fixes', status: 'in-progress', inferred: false, ready: false })
    expect(bandModel(snap({ bound: bound(DETAIL, { ...PIPELINE, outstanding: [] }) }), NONE)?.task?.ready).toBe(true)
    expect(bandModel(snap({ bound: bound({ ...DETAIL, status: 'in-review' }) }), NONE)?.task?.ready).toBe(true)
    expect(bandModel(snap({ bound: bound({ ...DETAIL, status: 'in-review' }, PIPELINE, true) }), NONE)?.task?.ready).toBe(false)
  })

  test('offline keeps the locally bound id with no status, counts nothing and says offline', () => {
    const m = bandModel(snap({ reachable: false, reason: 'down', queueTotal: 9, bound: bound(null, PIPELINE) }), NONE)
    expect(m).toEqual({
      task: { id: 'tm-audit-030', title: '', status: '', inferred: false, ready: false },
      handover: null,
      restore: null,
      fault: 'offline',
      waiting: 0,
      waitingCapped: false,
      handovers: 0,
    })
  })

  test('a handover or a restore burst shows the band on its own; connecting with no good snapshot says so', () => {
    expect(bandModel(null, { ...NONE, handover: HO })).toMatchObject({ task: null, handover: HO, fault: 'none' })
    expect(bandModel(snap({}), { ...NONE, restore: 3 })).toMatchObject({ task: null, restore: 3 })
    expect(bandModel(null, { ...NONE, connecting: true })).toMatchObject({ task: null, fault: 'connecting' })
    expect(bandModel(snap({ queueTotal: 2 }), { ...NONE, connecting: true })?.fault).toBe('none')
  })

  test("the counts are the server's totals; a waiting count cut at the window is marked capped", () => {
    expect(bandModel(snap({ queue: [task('a-001', 'high')], queueTotal: 355, handoversTotal: 7 }), NONE)).toMatchObject({ waiting: 355, waitingCapped: false, handovers: 7 })
    expect(bandModel(snap({ queueTotal: 50, queueCapped: true }), NONE)).toMatchObject({ waiting: 50, waitingCapped: true })
    expect(bandModel(snap({ handoversTotal: 2 }), NONE)).toMatchObject({ task: null, waiting: 0, handovers: 2 })
  })

  test('status words and tones; ready for done', () => {
    expect(statusWord('in-review')).toBe('IN REVIEW')
    expect([statusTone('in-review'), statusTone('blocked'), statusTone('done'), statusTone('in-progress')]).toEqual(['info', 'critical', 'success', 'signature'])
    expect(isReady({ ...DETAIL, status: 'done' }, { ...PIPELINE, outstanding: [] })).toBe(false)
    expect(isReady(DETAIL, { laneless: true, lane: '', gateState: '', outstanding: [] })).toBe(false)
    expect(isReady(DETAIL, null)).toBe(false)
  })

  test('stage line wording', () => {
    expect(stageLine(DETAIL, { ...PIPELINE, outstanding: [] })).toBe('IN PROGRESS → ready for done')
    expect(stageLine(DETAIL, { laneless: true, lane: '', gateState: '', outstanding: [] })).toBe('IN PROGRESS → no pipeline')
    expect(stageLine(DETAIL, null)).toBe('IN PROGRESS')
  })
})
```

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/band.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: the band above the prompt (spec §6) — at most two lines on their own raised ground under a `◆ TASKMASTER`
// chip, so it is never mistaken for the end of Claude's reply: this session's task, its handover, and a key to every pane.
// JSX compiles to h(...): never name a variable `h` in this file (it would shadow the factory).
import type { RenderNode, RenderSurface } from 'claude-code'

import type { TmBandMode, TmSessionHandover } from '../types'
import type { TmPane } from './flows'
import { badge, keycap, type Kit, label, type Piece, signal, TM_TAG } from './kit'
import { type BandModel, CONNECTING, oneLine, statusTone, statusWord, truncate } from './model'

export type BandHandlers = {
  readonly open: (pane: TmPane) => void
  readonly askDone: (taskId: string) => void
  readonly confirmDone: (taskId: string) => void
  readonly cancel: () => void
  readonly copy: (handoverId: string, surface: RenderSurface) => void
  readonly hideRestore: () => void
}

/** The cells the flexible text (the title, the tldr, a refusal) keeps before the fixed pieces start to shrink. */
const MIN_TEXT = 12
/** The fewest characters of a task id the band shows (line 1 at its tightest, a confirm question). */
const MIN_ID = 8
const GAP = 2
/** Squeeze levels: 0 every word; 4 keys, counts and a cut id only (the status badge goes last). */
const LEVELS = [0, 1, 2, 3, 4] as const
type Level = (typeof LEVELS)[number]

/** How the band names a D5 handover. */
export const HANDOVER_TAG: Readonly<Record<TmSessionHandover['tag'], string>> = {
  'written here': 'written here',
  'resumed from': 'resumed from',
  task: 'for this task',
}

type Flex = { readonly text: string; readonly color: string; readonly bold: boolean }
type Line = { readonly left: readonly Piece[]; readonly flex: Flex | null; readonly right: readonly Piece[] }

function text(k: Kit, value: string, color: string, bold = false): Piece {
  const { Text } = k.ui
  return {
    el: (
      <Text color={color} bold={bold} wrap="truncate-end">
        {value}
      </Text>
    ),
    width: value.length,
  }
}

const sum = (pieces: readonly Piece[]): number => pieces.reduce((n, p) => n + p.width, 0)

/** The pieces, in order, while they fit `room` with GAP between them (none at all when the first does not). */
function keep(pieces: readonly Piece[], room: number): RenderNode[] {
  const kept: RenderNode[] = []
  let used = -GAP
  for (const p of pieces) {
    if (used + GAP + p.width > room) break
    used += GAP + p.width
    kept.push(p.el)
  }
  return kept
}

/** The room a line leaves its flexible text: the inner width less every fixed piece and the gaps between them all. */
function roomOf(line: Line, inner: number): number {
  const items = line.left.length + line.right.length + (line.flex === null ? 0 : 1)
  return inner - sum(line.left) - sum(line.right) - GAP * Math.max(0, items - 1)
}

/** The first squeeze level at which the flexible text keeps MIN_TEXT cells (or all of itself); else the tightest. */
function settle(build: (level: Level) => Line, inner: number): Line {
  for (const level of LEVELS) {
    const line = build(level)
    const want = line.flex === null ? 0 : Math.min(MIN_TEXT, line.flex.text.length)
    if (roomOf(line, inner) >= want) return line
  }
  return build(4)
}

/** A line laid out: the fixed left pieces, the flexible text cut to its room, the right group pushed to the far end. */
function draw(k: Kit, line: Line, inner: number): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const room = roomOf(line, inner)
  const flex = line.flex === null || room < 1 ? null : truncate(line.flex.text, room)
  // Too narrow even for the keys: the left pieces stay, then as many right-hand keys as still fit, in order.
  const used = sum(line.left) + GAP * Math.max(0, line.left.length - 1) + (flex === null || flex === '' ? 0 : GAP + flex.length)
  const right = room < 0 ? keep(line.right, inner - used - GAP) : line.right.map(p => p.el)
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={GAP} alignItems="flex-start">
      {line.left.map(p => p.el)}
      {flex === null || flex === '' || line.flex === null ? null : (
        <Text color={line.flex.color} bold={line.flex.bold} wrap="truncate-end">
          {flex}
        </Text>
      )}
      {right.length === 0 ? null : (
        <Box flexGrow={1} flexDirection="row" justifyContent="flex-end" columnGap={GAP}>
          {right}
        </Box>
      )}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** `1 waiting`, `2 handovers`, `4 restore` (+ `x hide`): zero and offline counts fade but still open their pane. */
function counts(k: Kit, m: BandModel, level: Level, on: BandHandlers): Piece[] {
  const offline = m.fault !== 'none'
  const n = (count: number, capped = false): string => (offline ? '–' : `${count}${capped ? '+' : ''}`)
  const waiting = [`${n(m.waiting, m.waitingCapped)} waiting on you`, `${n(m.waiting, m.waitingCapped)} waiting`][level] ?? n(m.waiting, m.waitingCapped)
  const handovers = level === 0 ? `${n(m.handovers)} handovers` : n(m.handovers)
  const pieces = [
    keycap(k, { id: 'band-review', key: '1', label: waiting, on: 'raised', faded: offline || m.waiting === 0, onPress: () => on.open('review') }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'band-handovers', key: '2', label: handovers, on: 'raised', faded: offline || m.handovers === 0, onPress: () => on.open('handovers') }),
  ]
  if (m.restore !== null) {
    pieces.push(
      keycap(k, { id: 'band-restore', key: '4', label: level <= 1 ? `${m.restore} restore` : `${m.restore}`, on: 'raised', onPress: () => on.open('restore') }),
      keycap(k, { id: 'band-restore-hide', key: 'x', label: level <= 2 ? 'hide' : '', on: 'raised', onPress: on.hideRestore }),
    )
  }
  return pieces
}

const tagChip = (k: Kit, level: Level): Piece => badge(k, level <= 2 ? TM_TAG : '◆ TM', 'signature', { on: 'raised' })
const sessionKey = (k: Kit, level: Level, on: BandHandlers): Piece =>
  keycap(k, { id: 'band-session', key: '3', label: level <= 2 ? 'session ›' : level === 3 ? 'session' : '', on: 'raised', onPress: () => on.open('session') })

/** `done <id>?`, then `<id>?`, the id cut to `room`, never under MIN_ID characters: null when even that does not fit. */
function question(id: string, room: number): string | null {
  for (const ask of [`done ${id}?`, `${id}?`]) if (ask.length <= room) return ask
  const least = Math.min(MIN_ID, id.length)
  return room - 1 >= least ? `${truncate(id, room - 1)}?` : null
}

/**
 * The band (spec §6). Line 1: the chip, the task (status badge, id, bold title), `d done` when it is ready, `3 session ›`.
 * Line 2: the D5 handover (`c copy`), or a y/n confirm, or a refusal; the counts sit on line 2 when there is one under a
 * task, else on line 1. Each line squeezes its words before its text drops under MIN_TEXT; one blank row above when room.
 */
export function bandTree(k: Kit, m: BandModel, mode: TmBandMode, width: number, maxRows: number, on: BandHandlers): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const inner = Math.max(1, width - 2)
  const task = m.task
  const confirming = task !== null && mode.confirmingId === task.id
  const refused = task !== null && mode.refusal !== '' && mode.refusalId === task.id
  const second = task !== null ? confirming || refused || m.handover !== null : m.handover !== null
  const countsBelow = task !== null && second
  const lines: RenderNode[] = []

  if (m.fault === 'connecting' && task === null) {
    lines.push(draw(k, { left: [tagChip(k, 0)], flex: { text: CONNECTING, color: t.fg.subtle, bold: false }, right: [] }, inner))
  } else {
    lines.push(
      draw(
        k,
        settle(level => {
          const left: Piece[] = [tagChip(k, level)]
          let flex: Flex | null = null
          // Offline at the tightest is its glyph alone: the status line still says `◆ tm offline`.
          if (m.fault === 'offline') left.push(signal(k, 'critical', level <= 3 ? 'offline' : ''))
          if (task !== null) {
            if (m.fault !== 'offline' && task.status !== '' && level <= 3) left.push(badge(k, statusWord(task.status), statusTone(task.status), { on: 'raised', strength: 12 }))
            left.push(text(k, level <= 3 ? task.id : truncate(task.id, MIN_ID), t.fg.subtle))
            const title = oneLine(task.title)
            flex = title === '' ? null : { text: title, color: task.inferred ? t.fg.subtle : t.fg.bold, bold: !task.inferred }
          }
          // A task only inferred from the branch says so, and never offers d (ruling F1: d acts on this session's binding).
          const right: Piece[] = task !== null && task.inferred ? [text(k, 'inferred', t.fg.subtle)] : []
          if (task !== null && task.ready) {
            right.push(keycap(k, { id: 'band-done', key: 'd', label: level <= 3 ? 'done' : '', tone: 'success', on: 'raised', onPress: () => on.askDone(task.id) }))
          }
          if (!countsBelow) right.push(...counts(k, m, level, on))
          right.push(sessionKey(k, level, on))
          return { left, flex, right }
        }, inner),
        inner,
      ),
    )
  }

  if (second && task !== null && confirming) {
    const yes = keycap(k, { id: 'band-yes', key: 'y', label: 'yes', tone: 'success', on: 'raised', onPress: () => on.confirmDone(task.id) })
    const no = keycap(k, { id: 'band-no', key: 'n', label: 'no', autoFocus: true, on: 'raised', onPress: on.cancel })
    const ask = question(task.id, inner - yes.width - no.width - 2 * GAP - 2)
    const props = k.badges[24].raised.warning
    if (ask === null) {
      // Too narrow for one row: the question above the pair (never y without n).
      lines.push(<Text {...props}>{` ${truncate(`${task.id}?`, inner - 2)} `}</Text>)
      lines.push(draw(k, { left: [yes, no], flex: null, right: [] }, inner))
    } else {
      const asked: Piece = { el: <Text {...props}>{` ${ask} `}</Text>, width: ask.length + 2 }
      lines.push(draw(k, { left: [asked, yes, no], flex: null, right: counts(k, m, 2, on) }, inner))
    }
  } else if (second && task !== null && refused) {
    lines.push(
      draw(
        k,
        settle(level => ({
          left: [badge(k, 'refused', 'critical', { on: 'raised' })],
          flex: { text: oneLine(mode.refusal), color: t.fg.default, bold: false },
          right: counts(k, m, level, on),
        }), inner),
        inner,
      ),
    )
  } else if (second && m.handover !== null) {
    const ho = m.handover
    lines.push(
      draw(
        k,
        settle(level => {
          const left: Piece[] = [label(k, level <= 2 ? '↳ handover' : '↳')]
          if (level <= 1) left.push(text(k, `${HANDOVER_TAG[ho.tag]} ·`, t.fg.subtle))
          const right: Piece[] = [keycap(k, { id: 'band-copy', key: 'c', label: level <= 3 ? 'copy' : '', on: 'raised', onPress: press => on.copy(ho.id, press.surface) })]
          if (countsBelow) right.push(...counts(k, m, level, on))
          return { left, flex: { text: oneLine(ho.tldr) || ho.id, color: t.fg.default, bold: false }, right }
        }, inner),
        inner,
      ),
    )
  }

  // The rows the prompt area gives: a blank row above only with room to spare; a confirm keeps its y/n over line 1.
  const blank = maxRows >= lines.length + 1 && maxRows >= 3
  const room = Math.max(1, maxRows - (blank ? 1 : 0))
  const kept = lines.length <= room ? lines : confirming ? lines.slice(-room) : lines.slice(0, room)
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" paddingTop={blank ? 1 : 0}>
      <Box flexDirection="column" backgroundColor={t.surface.raised} paddingX={1}>
        {kept}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  )
}
```

2. Replace everything from `export type BandTask = {` up to, not including, `export function ageLabel(`. That span covers the old `BandTask`, `CONNECTING`, `BandModel`, `BandRow`, `bandModel` and `bandRows`. The replacement:

```ts
/** Status words as the surfaces show them: `in-review` → `IN REVIEW`. */
export const statusWord = (status: string): string => status.toUpperCase().replace(/-/g, ' ')

/** A status badge's tone: in review is information, blocked is critical, done is success, anything else the signature. */
export function statusTone(status: string): 'success' | 'critical' | 'info' | 'signature' {
  if (status === 'in-review') return 'info'
  if (status === 'blocked') return 'critical'
  if (status === 'done' || status === 'archived') return 'success'
  return 'signature'
}

/** Ready for done: not done yet, and in review, or with a pipeline that has no gate outstanding. */
export function isReady(d: TmTaskDetail, p: TmPipeline | null): boolean {
  if (d.status === 'done' || d.status === 'archived') return false
  return d.status === 'in-review' || (p !== null && !p.laneless && p.outstanding.length === 0)
}

export type BandTask = {
  readonly id: string
  readonly title: string
  /** '' while Taskmaster's detail is not known (offline, or not read yet): no status badge. */
  readonly status: string
  readonly inferred: boolean
  /** `d done` shows: bound by this session (never inferred), live, and ready for done. */
  readonly ready: boolean
}
/** The neutral note while a transient failure is retried and there is no good snapshot to show. */
export const CONNECTING = 'Connecting to Taskmaster…'

/**
 * What the band draws (spec §6). `waitingCapped`: the count was cut at the window with no server total (drawn `N+`);
 * `restore`: the pending burst's unfinished sessions (null: no `4`); `fault`: offline fades the counts, connecting says so.
 */
export type BandModel = {
  readonly task: BandTask | null
  readonly handover: TmSessionHandover | null
  readonly waiting: number
  readonly waitingCapped: boolean
  readonly handovers: number
  readonly restore: number | null
  readonly fault: 'none' | 'offline' | 'connecting'
}

/**
 * The band's model; null (no band) when there is nothing to show: no task, no handover, no restore burst and, offline or
 * not, nothing counted. While a transient failure is retried with no good snapshot, the band says it is connecting.
 */
export function bandModel(
  s: TmSnapshot | null,
  a: { readonly handover: TmSessionHandover | null; readonly restore: number | null; readonly connecting: boolean },
): BandModel | null {
  const quiet = { waiting: 0, waitingCapped: false, handovers: 0, handover: a.handover, restore: a.restore }
  if (a.connecting && (s === null || !s.reachable)) return { task: null, fault: 'connecting', ...quiet }
  if (s === null) return a.handover === null && a.restore === null ? null : { task: null, fault: 'none', ...quiet }
  const b = s.bound
  const detail = b?.detail ?? null
  const task: BandTask | null =
    b === null
      ? null
      : {
          id: b.taskId,
          title: detail?.title ?? '',
          status: s.reachable ? (detail?.status ?? '') : '',
          inferred: b.inferred,
          ready: s.reachable && !b.inferred && detail !== null && isReady(detail, b.pipeline),
        }
  const m: BandModel = s.reachable
    ? { ...quiet, task, fault: 'none', waiting: s.queueTotal, waitingCapped: s.queueTotal > 0 && s.queueCapped === true, handovers: s.handoversTotal }
    : { ...quiet, task, fault: 'offline' }
  if (task === null && a.handover === null && a.restore === null && m.waiting === 0 && m.handovers === 0) return null
  return m
}
```

5. Before `/** A card's ticks: the $.state mirror once a toggle wrote it`, add:

```ts
/** The restore burst the band offers (`4`): pending (the scan wrote it) and hidden neither here nor, for that burst, anywhere. */
async function restoreShown($: EngineInterface): Promise<TmRestorePending | null> {
  const pending = await read($, RESTORE_PENDING)
  if (pending === null || (await read($, RESTORE_HIDDEN)) === pending.epoch) return null
  return (await $.store.get(restoreHiddenKey(pending.epoch))) === true ? null : pending
}
```

### Task 2.6: The Session tab (`3`, `/tm-session`)

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/session.tsx`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/session-tab.test.ts`
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/flows.ts` (`toggleSummary` takes `{ id }`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (header, `/tm-session` command, its pane render)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - From the kit: `panel`, `panelTitle`, `badge`, `keycap` (`dim`, `faded`, `selected`), `label`, `row`, `rule`, `confirmRow`.
  - From earlier tasks: `HANDOVER_TAG` (Task 2.5); `withNotice`, `isReady`, `statusWord`, `statusTone` (Tasks 2.3 and 2.5); `TmFlows.openPane`, `openHandover`, `copyHandover`, `launch` (Task 2.4); `resumePrompt` (Task 2.1).
  - Existing: `bandAskDone`, `bandConfirmDone`, `bandCancel`, `bandSendBack`, `toggleSummary`, `askSummary`, and the summaries state.
- Produces: `sessionTree`, `SessionView`, `SessionHandlers`; the `tm-session` pane and the `/tm-session` command.

What the person sees (spec §7). It is one panel, titled `◆ TASKMASTER · SESSION`, with the health signal (`tm ok` / `offline` / `connecting` / `loading` / `demo`) at the top right. Three sections sit between dashed rules, each with its label in a 10-cell column:

- **TASK:**
  - the status badge, the id (subtle) and the bold title;
  - `lane · phase · gate ✓ · ready for done | next: <gate> | no pipeline · inferred from the branch`;
  - `human: <check>`, with `d done` and `a agent` at the right.
  - `d` asks with ` done <id>? ` `y yes` `n no` in place of that row. `a` opens the review note on the task.
  - When they cannot act, both dim and say why on a press: no task, offline, inferred, or not ready.
  - A band refusal shows here too.
- **HANDOVER:**
  - the tag badge (`WRITTEN HERE` / `RESUMED FROM` / `FOR THIS TASK`), the id and its age;
  - the tldr, and `Next: …`;
  - `c copy`, `h in handovers`, `w new tab`, `i decisions·blockers`. `i` shows the handover's decisions and blockers, read as the handovers pane reads them.
- **SESSION:**
  - `resumed from <id>` or `not resumed from a handover`, plus `part of the <at> burst (<n> others open)` once Stage 5 writes `sessionBurst`;
  - then `1 N waiting on you`, `2 N handovers`, and `4 N unfinished · restore` while a burst is pending.
- An empty section says so in subtle text: "No task bound to this session", "No handover for this session yet".
