- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/session.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: the Session tab (spec §7; band `3`, /tm-session) — everything about this session in one ◆ TASKMASTER panel:
// its task with `d` done and `a` to agent, its handover with copy / open / new tab / decisions, and where it came from.
// JSX compiles to h(...): never name a variable `h` in this file (it would shadow the factory).
import type { RenderNode, RenderSurface } from 'claude-code'

import type { TmBandMode, TmHandoverSummary, TmSessionBurst, TmSessionHandover, TmSnapshot } from '../types'
import { HANDOVER_TAG } from './band'
import type { TmPane } from './flows'
import { badge, confirmRow, keycap, type Kit, label, panel, panelTitle, type Piece, row, rule } from './kit'
import { ageLabel, gateSignal, isReady, oneLine, statusTone, statusWord, truncate, wrapText } from './model'

export type SessionView = {
  readonly snapshot: TmSnapshot | null
  /** D5, with a newer notice already let in (withNotice). */
  readonly handover: TmSessionHandover | null
  readonly resumedFrom: string
  readonly burst: TmSessionBurst | null
  /** The pending restore burst's unfinished sessions (null: no `4`). */
  readonly restore: number | null
  readonly band: TmBandMode
  /** The handover's decisions and blockers when read; shown while `summaryOpen`. */
  readonly summary: TmHandoverSummary | undefined
  readonly summaryOpen: boolean
  readonly connecting: boolean
  readonly demo: boolean
  readonly now: number
}

export type SessionHandlers = {
  readonly open: (pane: TmPane) => void
  readonly askDone: (taskId: string) => void
  readonly confirmDone: (taskId: string) => void
  readonly cancel: () => void
  readonly sendBack: (taskId: string) => void
  readonly copy: (handoverId: string, surface: RenderSurface) => void
  readonly openHandover: (handoverId: string) => void
  readonly launch: (handover: TmSessionHandover, surface: RenderSurface) => void
  readonly toggleSummary: (handoverId: string, open: boolean) => void
}

/** The section label column: `HANDOVER` and two cells. */
const LABEL_W = 10

/** A section's first row: its label in the label column, then the pieces. */
function head(k: Kit, word: string, pieces: readonly RenderNode[]): RenderNode {
  const { Box } = k.ui
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row" alignItems="flex-start">
      <Box width={LABEL_W} flexShrink={0}>
        {label(k, word).el}
      </Box>
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2} alignItems="flex-start">
        {pieces}
      </Box>
    </Box>
  )
}

/** A row under a section's first, in the content column. */
function under(k: Kit, children: readonly RenderNode[]): RenderNode {
  const { Box } = k.ui
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="column" paddingLeft={LABEL_W}>
      {children}
    </Box>
  )
}

function line(k: Kit, text: string, color: string, width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Text } = k.ui
  return (
    <Text color={color} wrap="truncate-end">
      {truncate(text, Math.max(1, width))}
    </Text>
  )
}

/** `full · patch-1-9 · review-gate pass ✓ · ready for done`: lane, phase, gate and where the pipeline stands. */
function metaOf(snapshot: TmSnapshot): string {
  const b = snapshot.bound
  const d = b?.detail ?? null
  if (b === null || d === null) return ''
  const gate = gateSignal(d.gateState)
  const p = b.pipeline
  const stage = p === null ? '' : p.laneless ? 'no pipeline' : p.outstanding.length === 0 ? 'ready for done' : `next: ${p.outstanding[0]}`
  return [d.lane || 'no lane', d.phase ?? '', gate === null ? '' : `${gate.word}${gate.kind === 'success' ? ' ✓' : ''}`, stage, b.inferred ? 'inferred from the branch' : '']
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' · ')
}

/** Why `d` (and `a`) cannot act on the bound task, or '' when they can. */
function whyNot(snapshot: TmSnapshot, forDone: boolean): string {
  const b = snapshot.bound
  if (b === null) return 'No task bound to this session'
  if (!snapshot.reachable || b.detail === null) return 'Taskmaster is offline: try again once it answers'
  if (b.inferred) return `${b.taskId} is only inferred from the branch: pick it to bind it to this session`
  if (!forDone || isReady(b.detail, b.pipeline)) return ''
  const p = b.pipeline
  return p !== null && !p.laneless && p.outstanding.length > 0 ? `Not ready for done: ${p.outstanding[0]} is outstanding` : 'Not ready for done yet'
}

function taskSection(k: Kit, v: SessionView, on: SessionHandlers, inner: number): RenderNode[] {
  const { t } = k
  const room = inner - LABEL_W
  const s = v.snapshot
  const b = s?.bound ?? null
  if (s === null || b === null) return [head(k, 'task', [line(k, 'No task bound to this session', t.fg.subtle, room)])]
  const d = b.detail
  const first: Piece[] = []
  if (s.reachable && d !== null) first.push(badge(k, statusWord(d.status), statusTone(d.status), { on: 'raised', strength: 12 }))
  first.push({ el: line(k, b.taskId, t.fg.subtle, b.taskId.length), width: b.taskId.length })
  const used = first.reduce((n, p) => n + p.width + 2, 0)
  const title = d === null ? '' : oneLine(d.title)
  const rows: RenderNode[] = [
    head(k, 'task', [...first.map(p => p.el), ...(title === '' || room - used < 4 ? [] : [line(k, title, b.inferred ? t.fg.subtle : t.fg.bold, room - used)])]),
  ]
  const meta = metaOf(s)
  if (meta !== '') rows.push(under(k, [line(k, meta, t.fg.subtle, room)]))
  if (v.band.confirmingId === b.taskId) {
    rows.push(under(k, confirmRow(k, { id: 'session', question: `done ${b.taskId}?`, onYes: () => on.confirmDone(b.taskId), onNo: on.cancel, width: room, on: 'raised' })))
    return rows
  }
  const noDone = whyNot(s, true)
  const noAgent = whyNot(s, false)
  const keys = row(k, [
    keycap(k, { id: 'session-done', key: 'd', label: 'done', tone: 'success', on: 'raised', onPress: () => on.askDone(b.taskId), ...(noDone === '' ? {} : { dim: noDone }) }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'session-agent', key: 'a', label: 'agent', tone: 'warning', on: 'raised', onPress: () => on.sendBack(b.taskId), ...(noAgent === '' ? {} : { dim: noAgent }) }),
  ], 2)
  const human = d === null ? '' : oneLine(d.humanAction)
  const { Box } = k.ui
  rows.push(
    under(k, [
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2} alignItems="flex-start">
        {human === '' ? null : line(k, `human: ${human}`, t.fg.default, room - keys.width - 2)}
        <Box flexGrow={1} />
        {keys.el}
      </Box>,
    ]),
  )
  if (v.band.refusal !== '' && v.band.refusalId === b.taskId) {
    rows.push(under(k, [badge(k, 'refused', 'critical', { on: 'raised' }).el, ...wrapText(v.band.refusal, room).map(text => line(k, text, t.fg.default, room))]))
  }
  return rows
}

function handoverSection(k: Kit, v: SessionView, on: SessionHandlers, inner: number): RenderNode[] {
  const { t } = k
  const room = inner - LABEL_W
  const ho = v.handover
  if (ho === null) return [head(k, 'handover', [line(k, 'No handover for this session yet', t.fg.subtle, room)])]
  const age = ageLabel(ho.created, v.now)
  const tag = badge(k, HANDOVER_TAG[ho.tag], 'signature', { on: 'raised', strength: 12 })
  const id = `${ho.id}${age === '' ? '' : ` · ${age} ago`}`
  const rows: RenderNode[] = [
    head(k, 'handover', [tag.el, line(k, id, t.fg.subtle, room - tag.width - 2)]),
    under(k, [line(k, oneLine(ho.tldr) || ho.id, t.fg.default, room)]),
  ]
  if (ho.next !== undefined && ho.next !== '') rows.push(under(k, [line(k, `Next: ${oneLine(ho.next)}`, t.fg.subtle, room)]))
  rows.push(
    under(k, [
      row(k, [
        keycap(k, { id: 'session-copy', key: 'c', label: 'copy', on: 'raised', onPress: press => on.copy(ho.id, press.surface) }),
        keycap(k, { id: 'session-open', key: 'h', label: 'in handovers', on: 'raised', onPress: () => on.openHandover(ho.id) }),
        keycap(k, { id: 'session-launch', key: 'w', label: 'new tab', on: 'raised', onPress: press => on.launch(ho, press.surface) }),
        keycap(k, { id: 'session-summary', key: 'i', label: 'decisions·blockers', on: 'raised', selected: v.summaryOpen, onPress: () => on.toggleSummary(ho.id, !v.summaryOpen) }),
      ], 2).el,
    ]),
  )
  if (v.summaryOpen) {
    const sum = v.summary
    if (sum === undefined) rows.push(under(k, [line(k, 'loading decisions and blockers…', t.fg.subtle, room)]))
    else if (sum.unavailable === true) rows.push(under(k, [line(k, 'Decisions and blockers unavailable: i asks again', t.fg.subtle, room)]))
    else {
      for (const [word, items] of [['decisions', sum.decisions], ['blockers', sum.blockers]] as const) {
        rows.push(under(k, [label(k, word).el, ...(items.length === 0 ? [line(k, 'none', t.fg.subtle, room)] : items.map(item => line(k, `· ${oneLine(item)}`, t.fg.default, room)))]))
      }
    }
  }
  return rows
}

function sessionSection(k: Kit, v: SessionView, on: SessionHandlers, inner: number): RenderNode[] {
  const { t } = k
  const room = inner - LABEL_W
  const where = [
    v.resumedFrom === '' ? 'not resumed from a handover' : `resumed from ${v.resumedFrom}`,
    v.burst === null ? '' : `part of the ${v.burst.at} burst (${v.burst.others} others open)`,
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' · ')
  const s = v.snapshot
  const live = s !== null && s.reachable
  const n = (count: number): string => (live ? `${count}` : '–')
  const counts: Piece[] = [
    keycap(k, { id: 'session-review', key: '1', label: `${n(s?.queueTotal ?? 0)}${s?.queueCapped === true ? '+' : ''} waiting on you`, on: 'raised', faded: !live || s.queueTotal === 0, onPress: () => on.open('review') }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'session-handovers', key: '2', label: `${n(s?.handoversTotal ?? 0)} handovers`, on: 'raised', faded: !live || s.handoversTotal === 0, onPress: () => on.open('handovers') }),
  ]
  if (v.restore !== null) counts.push(keycap(k, { id: 'session-restore', key: '4', label: `${v.restore} unfinished · restore`, on: 'raised', onPress: () => on.open('restore') }))
  return [head(k, 'session', [line(k, where, v.resumedFrom === '' && v.burst === null ? t.fg.subtle : t.fg.default, room)]), row(k, counts, 3).el]
}

/** The Session tab's panel (spec §7): TASK, HANDOVER and SESSION between dashed rules, the health at the top right. */
export function sessionTree(k: Kit, v: SessionView, on: SessionHandlers, width: number): RenderNode {
  const inner = Math.max(LABEL_W + 8, width - 4)
  const s = v.snapshot
  const health =
    v.connecting && (s === null || !s.reachable)
      ? { kind: 'info' as const, word: 'connecting' }
      : s === null
        ? { kind: 'info' as const, word: 'loading' }
        : !s.reachable
          ? { kind: 'critical' as const, word: 'offline' }
          : v.demo
            ? { kind: 'info' as const, word: 'demo' }
            : { kind: 'success' as const, word: 'tm ok' }
  return panel(k, { title: panelTitle('session'), health, width }, [
    ...taskSection(k, v, on, inner),
    rule(k, inner),
    ...handoverSection(k, v, on, inner),
    rule(k, inner),
    ...sessionSection(k, v, on, inner),
  ])
}
```

In `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/flows.ts`, replace `    toggleSummary: async (h: TmHandover, open: boolean): Promise<void> => {` with `    toggleSummary: async (h: { readonly id: string }, open: boolean): Promise<void> => {`. It only ever reads `h.id`, and the Session tab holds a `TmSessionHandover`.

```tsx
  on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: SESSION }, async ($, e) => {
    const { Text } = $.ui.resolve(e)
    if (e.surface === 'mobile') return <Text>Open the Session tab in the terminal or the desktop app.</Text>
    await $.state.get(RR_POLARITY)
    const { snapshot, summaries } = await dataOf($)
    const handover = withNotice(await read($, SESSION_HANDOVER), await read($, NOTICE), snapshot)
    const open = handover !== null && (await read($, SUMMARY_OPEN)) === handover.id
    const held = handover === null ? undefined : summaries[handover.id]
    // Read when open and missing, or stale after a refresh (the stale one stays drawn meanwhile), as the handovers pane does.
    if (mod.source === 'tm' && handover !== null && open && (held === undefined || held.stale === true)) askSummary(handover.id)
    const pending = await restoreShown($)
    const cwd = await $.session.cwd()
    const ui = $.ui.resolve(e) as unknown as Ui
    const k = await kitOf(ui, rrOf($), e.surface, text => mod.host?.toast(text))
    const tree = sessionTree(
      k,
      {
        snapshot,
        handover,
        resumedFrom: await read($, RESUMED_FROM),
        burst: await read($, SESSION_BURST),
        restore: pending?.unfinished ?? null,
        band: await read($, BAND),
        summary: held,
        summaryOpen: open,
        connecting: mod.source === 'tm' && (await read($, FAULT)) === 'connecting',
        demo: mod.source === 'demo',
        now: await $.clock.now(),
      },
      {
        open: pane => act(f => f.openPane(pane)),
        askDone: id => act(f => f.bandAskDone(id)),
        confirmDone: id => act(f => f.bandConfirmDone(id)),
        cancel: () => act(f => f.bandCancel()),
        sendBack: id => act(f => f.bandSendBack(id)),
        copy: (id, surface) => act(f => f.copyHandover(id, surface)),
        openHandover: id => act(f => f.openHandover(id)),
        launch: (h, surface) =>
          act(async f => {
            await f.launch([{ cwd, title: `handover ${h.id}`, mode: 'fresh', prompt: resumePrompt(h.id, h.path) }], surface)
          }),
        toggleSummary: (id, show) => act(f => f.toggleSummary({ id }, show)),
      },
      e.props.bodyColumns,
    )
    const ground = await $.rr.surfaceProps({ level: 'page' })
    const { Box } = ui
    return (
      <Box {...ground} flexGrow={1}>
        {tree}
      </Box>
    )
  })
```

## Stage 3: Review list

**Goal.** The review pane becomes a paged card list (spec §8, §13 stage 3). Cards are sorted BLOCKED, then ACTION, then IN REVIEW, newest first within each kind. Five go to a page, and only the focused card is open. `j`/`k` move the focus and `p`/`n` turn the page. `s` selects a card. Ticking a check takes two keystrokes, `t` then the check's digit. The bulk row offers `Done N` and `To agent N` behind the same y/n and the same server-worded refusals as a single card. The stepper's `s` skip goes. The whole review tree moves out of `draw.tsx` into a new `hooks/review.tsx`, and its pure rules go into a new `hooks/review-list.ts`.

**Starting point.** This stage starts after Stage 1 Task 1.7, where the review pane already sits on the phase strip with `digits: false` and `f` opens the overlay. Stage 2 runs before this stage and also edits `register.tsx`, `flows.ts`, `types/index.d.ts`, `model.ts` and `draw.tsx`, where the band moves out to `band.tsx`. So every edit to a shared file below is anchored by a snippet in the review's own code, never by a line number. Import lists are edited by name ("add `X` to…", "drop `X` from…"), never by quoting the whole list. Stage 2's Session tab `a` reuses `bandSendBack`, which opens this pane at its `note` Input on the bound task. The compose page in Task 3.2 keeps that path (`openReview(id, 'note')` → `note`), and Stage 2's `session-tab.test.ts` and `band.test.ts` stay green on it.

**Verified before writing.** Every task below was replayed in order twice. The first replay was on the Stage 1 end state (287 → 296 → 305 → 312 → 320). The second was on Stage 2's end state (`scratchpad/s2/mods`), applying exactly the edits below (328 → 337 → 346 → 353 → 361, with Stage 2's `session-tab.test.ts` green throughout). After every task, `claude plugin test`, `validate --strict` and `tsc` were green, and a `claude -p` smoke loaded both mods (`ok`). Each task gives its count as "the count before this task + N". During the replay, a run on a busy machine sometimes failed a few unrelated tests with `timed out after 5000 ms`. Those tests passed when run again. If a failure is only that timeout, re-run once before debugging.

**Decisions this stage takes** (each one is open in the spec, and each is recorded here so the user can overrule it at the live check):

| Question | Decision | Why |
|---|---|---|
| What is BLOCKED? (§8 names it "critical") | A **critical-priority task or a P0 issue** is BLOCKED. An ACTION is anything else that asks the person: a task that names a `human_action`, a P1 issue, or an open decision. IN REVIEW is a task in review with no `human_action` (only the `reviewScope: all` option lists those). One function, `reviewKind`, holds the rule. | The queue lists only in-review tasks, P0/P1 issues and decisions, and none of them has a `blocked` status. Critical and P0 are the items that hold a release. |
| How does `t` + digit work? | `t` arms a **tick mode** (`reviewList.ticking`). While it is armed, the focused card's check keycaps carry `1`–`9` and the phase strip gives up its digits (`StripView.digits = !ticking`). Ticking a check disarms it. So do `t` again, `f`, `j`/`k`, the Tab ring moving to another card, `p`/`n`, a phase change, `d`, `a` and a bulk ask. Esc closes the pane, and opening the pane resets the mode. | The engine refuses any hotkey outside `[a-z0-9]`, so a key sequence can only be built from state. With this design, no digit is on two Buttons in any state, and the tests assert that. Probed with `claude plugin test`: the `t`/digit Buttons validate, and the tests assert exactly which Button carries each digit in every state (tick.test.ts). |
| The `n` clash between the pager and a y/n | Both y/n rows are drawn after the toolbar, so their `n` is the one the key presses. The engine documents this ("two clash, later wins"), and Stage 1's `confirmRow` already relies on it. A test asserts the draw order. | The pager's `p`/`n` are fixed slots that never vanish (spec §2). |
| Bulk keys | `b` for `Done N`, `g` for `To agent N`. These two keys are added to the brief's key map. | Without a key, bulk work would need Tab and Enter. Neither letter is used anywhere in the pane. |
| What does `all` select? | **This page's** tasks. | A sign-off should only reach cards the person has seen. A y/n still follows. |
| A note while other keys are drawn | A card's note, and the bulk note, each take the pane on their own: the target card or cards and the Input, with no other keyed Button. | Before this stage, the pane made ticks plain text while the note was up, so that a typed digit could never press anything. This keeps that safety for every key. |
| Issues and decisions in bulk | They take no selection: their box is dim, and pressing it says why. | Neither can be signed off or sent back with `backlog_complete_task` / `backlog_update_task`. |
| `DEMO_SEED` | **Not bumped.** No demo data changes shape (the new `reviewList` state is not part of the seed). 6 stays reserved for Stage 3 in case the live check asks for more demo cards. | Adding demo queue items would change the band counts that Stage 2's tests assert. The paging check runs on real data (Task 3.5). |

**What Stage 3 produces** (the code is in the tasks):

`hooks/review-list.ts` (pure):
```ts
export type ReviewKind = 'blocked' | 'action' | 'review'
export const KIND_WORD: Readonly<Record<ReviewKind, string>>          // blocked / action / in review
export const KIND_TONE: Readonly<Record<ReviewKind, 'critical' | 'warning' | 'info'>>
export const KINDS: readonly ReviewKind[]
export const FRESH_REVIEW_LIST: TmReviewList
export function reviewKind(item: TmQueueItem): ReviewKind
export function sortReview(items: readonly TmQueueItem[]): TmQueueItem[]
export function reviewItems(s: TmSnapshot, c: TmCursor, details: Readonly<Record<string, TmTaskDetail>>): TmQueueItem[]
export type ListView = { readonly entries: readonly TmQueueItem[]; readonly page: number; readonly pages: number; readonly focused: TmQueueItem | null; readonly index: number }
export function listView(items: readonly TmQueueItem[], currentId: string, page: number): ListView
export function neighbour(items: readonly TmQueueItem[], view: ListView, dir: -1 | 1): { readonly id: string; readonly page: number } | null
export function kindCounts(items: readonly TmQueueItem[]): Record<ReviewKind, number>
export function newestStamp(items: readonly TmQueueItem[]): string
export const isSelectable: (item: TmQueueItem) => boolean
export function selectedTasks(items: readonly TmQueueItem[], selected: readonly string[]): TmQueueItem[]
export const toggled: (selected: readonly string[], id: string) => string[]
export function doneQuestion(open: number, total: number, id: string, room: number): string
export type BulkResult = { readonly id: string; readonly refusal: string | null }
export function bulkSummary(verb: string, results: readonly BulkResult[]): string // `3 done · 1 refused: id (<server words>)`
```

`hooks/review.tsx`: `ReviewView`, `ReviewHandlers` and `reviewPaneTree(ui, rr, v, on, width)`. These move here from `draw.tsx`, which no longer exports them. Element keys: `card:<id>` (Box), `sel:<id>` (box keycap, `s` on the focused task), `focus-prev` (`k`), `focus-next` (`j`), `refresh` (`u`), `page-prev`/`page-next`, `done` (`d`), `back` (`a`), `tick` (`t`), `tick-<i>` (a digit only while armed), `open` (`o`), `viewer` (`v`), `copy` (`c`), `details` (`i`), `confirm-yes`/`confirm-no`, `note`/`note-cancel`, `bulk-done` (`b`), `bulk-agent` (`g`), `sel-all`/`sel-none`, `bulk-yes`/`bulk-no`, `bulk-note`/`bulk-cancel`.

`types/index.d.ts`: `TmReviewList = { page; selected; ticking; bulk: 'none' | 'done' | 'agent' | 'busy'; bulkIds }`, `PluginState['taskmaster-mods'].reviewList`. `TmCursor` loses `skipped`, and `currentId` is now the focused card.

`hooks/flows.ts`:
```ts
// TmWriter gains: reviewList: (change: (l: TmReviewList) => TmReviewList) => Promise<void>
// TmFlowDeps gains: refresh?: () => void        // tm: the refresher; absent in demo
// TmFlows gains:
focusCard: (id: string, page: number) => Promise<void>
turnReviewPage: (page: number) => Promise<void>
toggleSelect: (id: string) => Promise<void>
selectAll: (ids: readonly string[]) => Promise<void>
selectNone: () => Promise<void>
refresh: (pane: TmPhasePane) => Promise<void>   // u / Retry: d.refresh() (demo: a toast) + loadPhases(pane); Stage 4 can reuse it for its `u`
```

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/review-list.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: the review pane as a paged card list (spec §8) — which cards, in what order (BLOCKED, ACTION, IN REVIEW, newest
// first), which page and card hold the focus, what a bulk action may touch and how its outcome is told — as plain functions
// tested without drawing.
import type { TmCursor, TmQueueItem, TmReviewList, TmSnapshot, TmTaskDetail } from '../types'
import { PAGE_SIZE, reviewQueue, truncate } from './model'

export type ReviewKind = 'blocked' | 'action' | 'review'
export const KIND_WORD: Readonly<Record<ReviewKind, string>> = { blocked: 'blocked', action: 'action', review: 'in review' }
export const KIND_TONE: Readonly<Record<ReviewKind, 'critical' | 'warning' | 'info'>> = { blocked: 'critical', action: 'warning', review: 'info' }
export const KINDS: readonly ReviewKind[] = ['blocked', 'action', 'review']
export const FRESH_REVIEW_LIST: TmReviewList = { page: 0, selected: [], ticking: false, bulk: 'none', bulkIds: [] }

/**
 * A card's kind (spec §8): BLOCKED for what holds a release (a critical task, a P0 issue); ACTION for anything else that asks
 * the person (a task naming a human action, a P1 issue, an open decision); IN REVIEW for a task in review that names none.
 */
export function reviewKind(item: TmQueueItem): ReviewKind {
  if (item.kind === 'task') return item.priority === 'critical' ? 'blocked' : item.humanAction.trim() !== '' ? 'action' : 'review'
  if (item.kind === 'issue') return item.severity === 'P0' ? 'blocked' : 'action'
  return 'action'
}

const RANK: Readonly<Record<ReviewKind, number>> = { blocked: 0, action: 1, review: 2 }
// An unreadable or missing timestamp sorts after every dated card of its kind.
const stampOf = (stamp: string): number => {
  const ms = Date.parse(stamp)
  return Number.isNaN(ms) ? Number.NEGATIVE_INFINITY : ms
}

/** Review order: BLOCKED, ACTION, IN REVIEW; newest first within a kind; the server's order where that ties. */
export function sortReview(items: readonly TmQueueItem[]): TmQueueItem[] {
  return items
    .map((item, index) => ({ item, index, rank: RANK[reviewKind(item)], at: stampOf(item.timestamp) }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.rank - b.rank || (a.at === b.at ? 0 : b.at - a.at) || a.index - b.index)
    .map(entry => entry.item)
}

/** The cards the pane lists: the loaded window plus a pinned task (reviewQueue), in review order, less what this pass signed off. */
export function reviewItems(s: TmSnapshot, c: TmCursor, details: Readonly<Record<string, TmTaskDetail>>): TmQueueItem[] {
  return sortReview(reviewQueue(s, c, details).filter(item => !c.done.includes(item.id)))
}

/** One page of the list and its focused card: `index` is the focused card's place in the whole list (-1: none). */
export type ListView = {
  readonly entries: readonly TmQueueItem[]
  readonly page: number
  readonly pages: number
  readonly focused: TmQueueItem | null
  readonly index: number
}

/** The focused card's page when it is listed; else the stored page (clamped) and its first card. */
export function listView(items: readonly TmQueueItem[], currentId: string, page: number): ListView {
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(items.length / PAGE_SIZE))
  const at = currentId === '' ? -1 : items.findIndex(item => item.id === currentId)
  const shown = at >= 0 ? Math.floor(at / PAGE_SIZE) : Math.min(Math.max(0, page), pages - 1)
  const entries = items.slice(shown * PAGE_SIZE, (shown + 1) * PAGE_SIZE)
  const index = at >= 0 ? at : entries.length > 0 ? shown * PAGE_SIZE : -1
  return { entries, page: shown, pages, focused: items[index] ?? null, index }
}

/** `j` / `k`: the card `dir` away from the focused one, across pages, with its page; null at either end. */
export function neighbour(items: readonly TmQueueItem[], view: ListView, dir: -1 | 1): { readonly id: string; readonly page: number } | null {
  if (view.index < 0) return null
  const next = items[view.index + dir]
  return next === undefined ? null : { id: next.id, page: Math.floor((view.index + dir) / PAGE_SIZE) }
}

/** How many cards of each kind the list holds: the `2 BLOCKED  1 ACTION  9 IN REVIEW` row. */
export function kindCounts(items: readonly TmQueueItem[]): Record<ReviewKind, number> {
  const counts: Record<ReviewKind, number> = { blocked: 0, action: 0, review: 0 }
  for (const item of items) counts[reviewKind(item)] += 1
  return counts
}

/** The newest dated card's timestamp ('' when none is dated): the `newest 1d ago` of the summary line. */
export function newestStamp(items: readonly TmQueueItem[]): string {
  let best = ''
  for (const item of items) if (stampOf(item.timestamp) > stampOf(best)) best = item.timestamp
  return best
}

/** Only tasks are signed off or sent back from the list: issues and decisions take no selection. */
export const isSelectable = (item: TmQueueItem): boolean => item.kind === 'task'

/** The selected tasks still listed, in list order: what a bulk action may touch (a refresh can take some away). */
export function selectedTasks(items: readonly TmQueueItem[], selected: readonly string[]): TmQueueItem[] {
  return items.filter(item => isSelectable(item) && selected.includes(item.id))
}

export const toggled = (selected: readonly string[], id: string): string[] =>
  selected.includes(id) ? selected.filter(s => s !== id) : [...selected, id]

/** The pane's `done` question in `room` cells: the unchecked count while any is open, else the task by name. */
export function doneQuestion(open: number, total: number, id: string, room: number): string {
  const asks = open > 0 ? [`${open} of ${total} unchecked — done anyway?`, `${open}/${total} unchecked — done?`, `${open}/${total} unchecked?`] : [`done ${id}?`, `${id}?`]
  return asks.find(ask => ask.length <= room) ?? truncate(asks[asks.length - 1] ?? '', room)
}

/** One task's outcome in a bulk run: null when it went through, else the server's refusal as it worded it. */
export type BulkResult = { readonly id: string; readonly refusal: string | null }

/** One toast for a bulk run (spec §11): `3 done · 1 refused: tm-audit-031 (<server wording>)`. */
export function bulkSummary(verb: string, results: readonly BulkResult[]): string {
  const went = results.filter(r => r.refusal === null).length
  const refused = results.filter(r => r.refusal !== null)
  const head = `${went} ${verb}`
  return refused.length === 0 ? head : `${head} · ${refused.length} refused: ${refused.map(r => `${r.id} (${r.refusal})`).join('; ')}`
}
```

```ts
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual(['Already on the first card', 'Already on the last card'])
    await ui.press({ key: 'focus-prev' })
    expect(await focusedId(ui)).toBe('ISS-7')
  })

  test('five a page: n turns to the next page and focuses its first card; j and k cross pages', TM, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    stateOf(on, { [SNAP]: { ...REAL, queue: TWELVE, queueTotal: 12 } })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    const press = async (key: string) => {
      await ui.press({ key })
      await ui.redraw()
    }
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: '1 / 3' })).toBeDefined()
    await press('page-next')
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: '2 / 3' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await focusedId(ui)).toBe('t-006')
    await press('focus-prev')
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: '1 / 3' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await focusedId(ui)).toBe('t-005')
    await press('focus-next')
    await press('page-next')
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: '3 / 3' })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'page-next' }))?.props.dimColor).toBe(true)
  })

  test('a phase change starts over: page 1, its first card focused, nothing selected', TM, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))

    })

      await ui.press({ key })
      await ui.redraw()
    }
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: '1 / 3' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await focusedId(ui)).toBe('t-001')
    await ui.press({ key: 'page-next' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:t-006' }))?.props.label).toBe('□')
  })

  test('s selects the focused task; a press on any box selects that card; all takes the page, none clears', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))

    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:tm-audit-031' }))?.props.hotkey).toBeUndefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel:unified-chat-022' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:unified-chat-022' }))?.props.label).toBe('■')
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel:ISS-7' })
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual(['Issues and decisions are not signed off from the list'])
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel-all' })

      expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: `sel:${id}` }))?.props.label, id).toBe('■')
    }
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:ISS-7' }))?.props.label).toBe('□')
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel-none' })

  test('the Tab ring landing on a card box focuses that card', DEMO, async ($, on) => {

  test('o fills the prompt and keeps the pane open; an issue card says to look at it with the agent', DEMO, async ($, on) => {

    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'done' })).toBeUndefined()

  test('d asks first and the focused n cancels; y signs off and the next card takes the focus', DEMO, async ($, on) => {

  test('the y/n draws after the pager, so its n is the one the key presses (the later of two wins)', DEMO, async ($, on) => {

  test('moving off a card takes its y/n down: a y can never land on a card the person left', DEMO, async ($, on) => {

  })

  test('a refusal is shown on the card in the server words, and the card stays', DEMO, async ($, on) => {

  test('a takes a note on a page of its own, no other key drawn; then the pane closes and the prompt holds the note', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))

    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'unified-chat-022' })).toBeDefined()

  test('an empty queue says Queue clear with the pass tally', TM, async ($, on) => {

  })

  test('u asks tm for a fresh snapshot and the phases; demo says it has nothing newer', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    const reads = (tool: string) => world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === tool).length
    const [lists, queries] = [reads('backlog_list_tasks'), reads('backlog_query')]
    await ui.press({ key: 'refresh' })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(reads('backlog_list_tasks')).toBe(lists + 1)
    expect(reads('backlog_query')).toBe(queries + 1)
  })

  test('demo: u refreshes nothing and says so', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })

    expect(world.toasts).toEqual(['taskmaster-mods: demo data has nothing newer to read'])
    expect(world.calls).toEqual([])

  test('offline: the critical signal with the reason, and u retry', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)

    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' Taskmaster unreachable' })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'refresh' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'u', label: 'retry' })

  test('every mode validates on the terminal and the desktop', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))

      await ui.press({ key: 'confirm-no' })
      await ui.press({ key: 'sel:unified-chat-022' })
      await ui.press({ key: 'back' })
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Input', key: 'note' })).toBeDefined()
      await ui.press({ key: 'note-cancel' })
      await ui.press({ key: 'sel-none' })
      await ui.unmount()
    }
  })

  test('a narrow pane never shows y without n, and n keeps the focus', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    for (const columns of [72, 34, 28, 20]) {
      const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE, props: { ...REVIEW_PANE.props, bodyColumns: columns } })
      await ui.press({ key: 'done' })
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'confirm-yes' }), `${columns}`).toBeDefined()
      expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'confirm-no' }))?.props.autoFocus, `${columns}`).toBe(true)
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /unche/ }), `${columns}`).toBeDefined()
      await ui.press({ key: 'confirm-no' })
      await ui.unmount()
    }
  })

  test('tm mode: a sign-off the server cannot take is refused on the card, and the card stays', TM, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    stateOf(on, { [SNAP]: REAL, [`${PLUGIN}.details`]: DEMO_DETAILS })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'done' })
    await ui.redraw()
    await ui.press({ key: 'confirm-yes' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /^Taskmaster unreachable: backlog_complete_task: / })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await focusedId(ui)).toBe('unified-chat-022')
  })

  test('a refresh between d and y never retargets y (Review Focus 5)', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    const state = stateOf(on)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const demo = state.get(SNAP) as TmSnapshot
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'done' })
    await ui.redraw()
    // The refusing task turns critical: a y retargeted to the list's head would sign it off (and be refused).
    const [first, second, ...rest] = demo.queue
    state.set(SNAP, { ...demo, queue: [{ ...(second as TmQueueItem & { kind: 'task' }), priority: 'critical', timestamp: new Date(0).toISOString() }, first!, ...rest] })
    await ui.redraw()
    await ui.press({ key: 'confirm-yes' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /outstanding gates/ })).toBeUndefined()
    expect((state.get(SNAP) as TmSnapshot).queue.map(i => i.id)).toEqual(['tm-audit-031', 'docs-012', 'ISS-7', 'DEC-4'])
  })
})
```

Replace `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/card.test.ts` with:

```ts
// User intent: the focused review card (spec §8) — its one line, then lane and gate, the check as a tickable checklist kept
// only locally, details on demand, viewer and copy, and an action row that wraps rather than drops — every row in its room.
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { DEMO_DETAILS, demoSnapshot } from '../hooks/demo'
import { splitCheck } from '../hooks/model'
import type { TmSnapshot } from '../types'
import { pane, PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import { type Drawn, elementsOf, widthOf } from './fixtures/measure'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { stateOf, worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const DEMO = { options: { source: 'demo' }, plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const TM = { plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const ID = 'unified-chat-022'
const CHECK = DEMO_DETAILS[ID]!.humanAction
const [FIRST, SECOND] = splitCheck(CHECK).items as [string, string]
const ACTIONS = ['done', 'back', 'open', 'viewer', 'copy', 'details']
const wide = (columns: number) => ({ ...pane('tm-review'), props: { ...pane('tm-review').props, bodyColumns: columns } })
/** A tm-mode snapshot that is real data (not the demo marker), so tm-mode tests start from a state with a card. */
const REAL: TmSnapshot = { ...demoSnapshot(0), reason: '' }

describe('review card', () => {
  test('the focused card: box, kind, id, title and age; then lane and gate; then the labelled check with digit keys', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100) })
    for (const text of [' BLOCKED   ', ID, '1h', 'FULL', ' review-gate pass', 'LIVE CHECK ON DEV', 'needs unifiedChatGenerate + unifiedChatBuild granted']) {
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text }), text).toBeDefined()
    }
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /^Unified chat pre-build gets the full supervisor toolset/ })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-0' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '1', label: `☐ ${FIRST}` })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-1' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: '2', label: `☐ ${SECOND}` })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'details' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'i', label: '▸ more' })
  })

  test('the action row: d done, a agent, o open, v viewer, c copy check, i more; it wraps rather than drops', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    for (const columns of [100, 72, 48, 36, 28]) {
      const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(columns) })
      for (const [key, hotkey, label] of [
        ['done', 'd', 'done'],
        ['back', 'a', 'agent'],
        ['open', 'o', 'open'],
        ['viewer', 'v', 'viewer'],
        ['copy', 'c', 'copy check'],
      ] as const) {
        expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key }))?.props, `${columns} ${key}`).toMatchObject({ hotkey, label, plain: true })
      }
      const tree = (await ui.drawn()) as unknown as Drawn
      expect(widthOf(tree), `${columns}`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(columns)
      const keys = elementsOf(tree).flatMap(e => (e.type === 'Button' && ACTIONS.includes(String(e.props?.key)) ? [String(e.props?.key)] : []))
      expect(keys, `${columns}`).toEqual(ACTIONS)
      await ui.unmount()
    }
  })

  test('a narrow card gives way: the title, then the kind word to its initial; the id shortens last', TM, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    const long = { kind: 'task' as const, id: 'a-very-long-task-id-001', title: 'A title that fits a wide pane', priority: 'high' as const, humanAction: 'x', timestamp: '' }
    stateOf(on, { [`${PLUGIN}.snapshot`]: { ...REAL, queue: [long], queueTotal: 1 } })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const roomy = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100) })
    expect(await roomy.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'A title that fits a wide pane' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await roomy.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' ACTION    ' })).toBeDefined()
    await roomy.unmount()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(30) })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /^A title/ })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' A ' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'a-very-long-ta…' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(widthOf(await ui.drawn())).toBeLessThanOrEqual(30)
  })

  test('a tick toggles ☐/☑, lives in $.store ticks:<id> (read before write), never in Taskmaster', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on), { [`ticks:${ID}`]: { at: 0, items: [SECOND] } })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100) })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-1' }))?.props.label).toBe(`☑ ${SECOND}`)
    await ui.press({ key: 'tick-0' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-0' }))?.props.label).toBe(`☑ ${FIRST}`)
    expect(world.store.get(`ticks:${ID}`)).toMatchObject({ items: [SECOND, FIRST] })
    await ui.press({ key: 'tick-1' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-1' }))?.props.label).toBe(`☐ ${SECOND}`)
    expect(world.store.get(`ticks:${ID}`)).toMatchObject({ items: [FIRST] })
    expect(world.calls).toEqual([])
  })

  test('confirm names the task when all is ticked, and counts what is not', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100) })
    await ui.press({ key: 'done' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: / 2 of 2 unchecked — done anyway\? / })).toBeDefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'confirm-no' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'tick-0' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'done' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: / 1 of 2 unchecked — done anyway\? / })).toBeDefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'confirm-no' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'tick-1' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'done' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ` done ${ID}? ` })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'confirm-no' }))?.props.autoFocus).toBe(true)
  })

  test('under the y/n the ticks are plain text: no key to catch a digit pressed mid-confirm', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on), { [`ticks:${ID}`]: { at: 0, items: [SECOND] } })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100), surface: 'terminal' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'done' })
    for (const key of ['tick-0', 'tick-1', 'details', 'done']) expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key }), key).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: `☐ ${FIRST}` })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: `☑ ${SECOND}` })).toBeDefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'confirm-no' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-0' }))?.props.hotkey).toBe('1')
  })

  test('i shows notes, links and branch/PR under the check, and hides them again', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100) })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /pull\/412/ })).toBeUndefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'details' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'details' }))?.props.label).toBe('▾ more')
    for (const text of [/no branch/, /pull\/412/, /cookbook owns the step order/, /docs\/specs\/unified-chat\.md/]) {
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text }), String(text)).toBeDefined()
    }
    await ui.press({ key: 'details' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /pull\/412/ })).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('v in demo only announces the viewer; c copies the check, and says so when there is no clipboard', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100) })
    await ui.press({ key: 'viewer' })
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual([`would open ${ID} in the viewer`])
    expect(world.calls).toEqual([])
    await ui.press({ key: 'copy' })
    expect(world.copies).toEqual([CHECK])
    expect(world.toasts[1]).toBe(`Copied the check for ${ID}`)
    world.copyResult = { isCopied: false, reason: 'no-clipboard' }
    await ui.press({ key: 'copy' })
    expect(world.toasts[2]).toMatch(/^Clipboard unavailable \(no-clipboard\)/)
  })

  test('v in tm mode opens the viewer through the tm server and names the task; a failure says so', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    stateOf(on, { [`${PLUGIN}.snapshot`]: REAL, [`${PLUGIN}.details`]: DEMO_DETAILS })
    world.mcp = () => ({ text: 'Opened backlog viewer at http://127.0.0.1:4100/' })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100) })
    await ui.press({ key: 'viewer' })
    expect(world.calls).toEqual([{ tool: 'backlog_open_viewer', args: {} }])
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual([`Viewer opened: look for ${ID}`])
    world.mcp = () => 'offline'
    await ui.press({ key: 'viewer' })
    expect(world.toasts[1]).toMatch(/the viewer did not open/)
  })

  test('more than nine items: digit keys for the first nine, every item still drawn and tickable', TM, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    const action = Array.from({ length: 11 }, (_, i) => `- step ${i + 1}`).join('\n')
    const detail = { ...DEMO_DETAILS[ID]!, humanAction: action }
    stateOf(on, {
      [`${PLUGIN}.snapshot`]: { ...REAL, queue: [{ kind: 'task', id: ID, title: 't', priority: 'high', humanAction: action, timestamp: '' }], queueTotal: 1 },
      [`${PLUGIN}.details`]: { [ID]: detail },
    })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...wide(100) })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-8' }))?.props.hotkey).toBe('9')
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-9' }))?.props.hotkey).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-10' }))?.props.label).toBe('☐ step 11')
  })

  test('session start prunes ticks older than 30 days and unreadable ones, and nothing else', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const day = 86_400_000
    const clock = mock.clock(on, { now: 100 * day })
    const world = worldOf(on, clock, {
      'ticks:old-001': { at: 60 * day, items: ['x'] },
      'ticks:new-001': { at: 99 * day, items: ['y'] },
      'ticks:junk-001': 'junk',
      'binding:sess-A': { taskId: 'x', at: 1 },
    })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    expect([...world.store.keys()].sort()).toEqual(['binding:sess-A', 'ticks:new-001'])
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the pane**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/review.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: the review pane as a paged card list (spec §8) — the phase strip, what waits on the person by kind, five cards
// a page with only the focused one open, its check and actions, a selection for bulk work — drawn with the kit.
// JSX compiles to h(...): never name a variable `h` in this file (it would shadow the factory).
import type { RenderElement, RenderNode, RenderSurface } from 'claude-code'

import type { TmCursor, TmQueueItem, TmReviewList, TmSnapshot, TmTaskDetail } from '../types'
import {
  badge,
  card as cardBox,
  confirmRow,
  fit,
  helpFooter,
  type Kit,
  keycap,
  kitOf,
  label,
  pager,
  phaseEmpty,
  phaseSection,
  type Piece,
  row,
  selectionBar,
  signal,
  type StripHandlers,
  type StripView,
  toolbar,
  type Ui,
} from './kit'
import { ageLabel, cardMode, CONNECTING, gateSignal, oneLine, splitCheck, truncate, wrapText } from './model'
import { ALL } from './phases'
import {
  doneQuestion,
  isSelectable,
  KIND_TONE,
  KIND_WORD,
  KINDS,
  kindCounts,
  listView,
  neighbour,
  newestStamp,
  reviewItems,
  reviewKind,
} from './review-list'
import type { Rr, RrGround } from './rr'

export type ReviewView = {
  snapshot: TmSnapshot | null
  cursor: TmCursor
  list: TmReviewList
  details: Readonly<Record<string, TmTaskDetail>>
  now: number
  /** The ticked check items of a task (local UI state, never Taskmaster's). */
  ticks: (taskId: string) => Promise<readonly string[]>
  detailsOpen: boolean
  /** The phase strip (spec §5): this pane's phase view and counts. */
  strip: StripView
  /** The surface drawn on, for the kit (keycap face, panel title). */
  surface: RenderSurface
  /** Toasts a line: a dim keycap's why. */
  say: (text: string) => void
  /** tm is being retried after a transient failure: with no good snapshot the pane says "Connecting…". */
  connecting?: boolean
}

export type ReviewHandlers = {
  askDone: (id: string) => void
  confirmDone: (id: string) => void
  cancel: () => void
  askNote: (id: string) => void
  /** `humanAction`: the check the card shows, so the clear can tell an already-empty one from a failed clear. */
  sendBack: (id: string, note: string, humanAction: string) => void
  fill: (text: string) => void
  toggleTick: (id: string, item: string) => void
  toggleDetails: () => void
  openViewer: (id: string) => void
  copyCheck: (id: string, text: string, surface: RenderSurface) => void
  strip: StripHandlers
  /** j / k (or the Tab ring): this card takes the focus; `page` is its page. */
  focus: (id: string, page: number) => void
  /** p / n: show this page, its first card focused. */
  turnPage: (page: number) => void
  /** s, or a click on a card's box: select or unselect that task. */
  select: (id: string) => void
  /** `all`: select these tasks (this page's); `none`: clear the selection. */
  selectAll: (ids: readonly string[]) => void
  selectNone: () => void
  /** u: read the queue and the phases again (Retry while offline). */
  refresh: () => void
}

/** The id keeps at least this many cells before the title and age give way. */
const MIN_ID = 8
/** A card's cells inside its frame: the focused card's border and padding, or the unfocused card's wider padding. */
const CARD_FRAME = 4
/** Every kind badge is padded to the longest word, so the ids line up down the list. */
const KIND_CELLS = Math.max(...KINDS.map(kind => KIND_WORD[kind].length))

/** Wrapped lines in one colour, indented: a card never draws a line wider than its room. */
function lines(k: Kit, color: string, text: string, width: number, indent = 0): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  return wrapText(text, width - indent).map(line =>
    indent === 0 ? (
      <Text color={color}>{line}</Text>
    ) : (
      <Box paddingLeft={indent}>
        <Text color={color}>{line}</Text>
      </Box>
    ),
  )
}

/** Pieces in rows of `width`, wrapping rather than dropping. */
function wrapRows(k: Kit, pieces: readonly Piece[], width: number): RenderNode[] {
  const rows: Piece[][] = []
  let used = 0
  for (const piece of pieces) {
    const last = rows[rows.length - 1]
    if (last !== undefined && used + 1 + piece.width <= width) {
      last.push(piece)
      used += 1 + piece.width
    } else {
      rows.push([piece])
      used = piece.width
    }
  }
  return rows.map(r => row(k, r).el)
}

/** The pane's page ground, painted by the pane itself (the host's pane background is not RR's page). */
async function root(k: Kit, rr: Rr, children: readonly RenderNode[]): Promise<RenderElement> {
  const { Box } = k.ui
  const ground = await rr.surfaceProps({ level: 'page' })
  return (
    <Box {...ground} flexGrow={1}>
      {[...children]}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** The pane before it has data: Loading, Connecting (neutral), or unreachable with `u retry` (spec §8, §11). */
function status(k: Kit, s: TmSnapshot | null, connecting: boolean, on: ReviewHandlers): RenderNode[] {
  const { Text } = k.ui
  const title = label(k, 'review').el
  if (connecting) return [title, <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{CONNECTING}</Text>]
  if (s === null) return [title, <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>Loading…</Text>]
  const retry = keycap(k, { id: 'refresh', key: 'u', label: 'retry', onPress: on.refresh })
  return [title, signal(k, 'critical', 'Taskmaster unreachable', s.reason).el, retry.el]
}

type CardContext = {
  readonly item: TmQueueItem
  readonly focused: boolean
  readonly selected: boolean
  readonly room: number
  readonly v: ReviewView
  readonly on: ReviewHandlers
}

/** A card's one line: its box (`s` on the focused card), kind badge, id, title, and age at the right. */
function collapsedRow(k: Kit, c: CardContext, ground: RrGround): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const { item } = c
  const kind = reviewKind(item)
  const box = keycap(k, {
    id: `sel:${item.id}`,
    ...(c.focused && isSelectable(item) ? { key: 's' } : {}),
    label: c.selected ? '■' : '□',
    on: ground,
    onPress: () => c.on.select(item.id),
    ...(isSelectable(item) ? {} : { dim: 'Issues and decisions are not signed off from the list' }),
  })
  const least = Math.min(MIN_ID, item.id.length)
  // Gives way right to left: the title, then the age, then the kind word to its initial; the id shortens to MIN_ID, never away.
  const word = KIND_WORD[kind].padEnd(KIND_CELLS)
  const kindBadge = badge(k, box.width + 1 + word.length + 2 + 1 + least <= c.room ? word : word.slice(0, 1), KIND_TONE[kind], { on: ground })
  const age = ageLabel(item.timestamp, c.v.now)
  const fixed = box.width + 1 + kindBadge.width + 1
  const showAge = age !== '' && fixed + least + 2 + age.length <= c.room
  const idRoom = c.room - fixed - (showAge ? age.length + 2 : 0)
  const id = truncate(item.id, Math.max(least, idRoom))
  const titleRoom = idRoom - id.length - 1
  const title = titleRoom >= 4 ? truncate(oneLine(item.title), titleRoom) : ''
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} alignItems="flex-start">
      {box.el}
      {kindBadge.el}
      <Text color={k.t.fg.bold} bold>
        {id}
      </Text>
      {title === '' ? null : <Text color={k.t.fg.default}>{title}</Text>}
      {showAge ? <Box flexGrow={1} /> : null}
      {showAge ? <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{age}</Text> : null}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** The focused card's body: what it is, its check (tickable), details on demand, a refusal, then its actions or y/n. */
async function expandedBody(k: Kit, c: CardContext, ground: RrGround): Promise<RenderNode[]> {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const { item, v, on, room } = c
  const body: RenderNode[] = []
  if (item.kind !== 'task') {
    const what =
      item.kind === 'issue'
        ? `An open ${item.severity} issue: look at it with the agent.`
        : 'An open decision: resolve it with the decision skill (taskmaster:decision).'
    body.push(...lines(k, t.fg.default, what, room))
    const open = keycap(k, {
      id: 'open',
      key: 'o',
      label: 'open in prompt',
      on: ground,
      onPress: () => on.fill(item.kind === 'decision' ? `Resolve decision ${item.id} with the taskmaster:decision skill` : `Look at ${item.id}`),
    })
    body.push(open.el)
    return body
  }
  const detail = v.details[item.id]
  const action = item.humanAction || detail?.humanAction || ''
  const check = splitCheck(action)
  const ticked = await v.ticks(item.id)
  const mode = cardMode(v.cursor, item)

  const gate = detail === undefined ? null : gateSignal(detail.gateState)
  const lane = detail === undefined ? 'loading…' : detail.lane.toUpperCase() || 'NO LANE'
  body.push(
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1}>
      {fit([{ el: <Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{lane}</Text>, width: lane.length }, gate === null ? null : signal(k, gate.kind, gate.word)], room, 1)}
    </Box>,
  )

  // The check: a section label with its dim detail, then one tickable item per line; digits only while the card is armed.
  if (check.items.length > 0) {
    if (check.label !== '') {
      const head = label(k, truncate(check.label, room))
      const left = room - head.width - 2
      body.push(
        <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2}>
          {head.el}
          {check.detail !== '' && left >= 12 ? <Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{truncate(check.detail, left)}</Text> : null}
        </Box>,
      )
      if (check.detail !== '' && left < 12) body.push(...lines(k, t.fg.subtle, check.detail, room))
    } else if (check.detail !== '') {
      body.push(...lines(k, t.fg.subtle, check.detail, room))
    }
    // Ticks press only in card mode; under the y/n the items are plain text (marks still shown, same indent).
    const armed = mode === 'card'
    check.items.forEach((entry, i) => {
      const key = armed && i < 9 ? String(i + 1) : undefined
      const lead = (key === undefined ? 0 : 3) + 2
      const wrapped = wrapText(entry, room - lead)
      const first = `${ticked.includes(entry) ? '☑' : '☐'} ${wrapped[0] ?? ''}`
      body.push(
        <Box key={`tick-${i}-col`} flexDirection="column">
          {armed ? (
            keycap(k, { id: `tick-${i}`, ...(key === undefined ? {} : { key }), label: first, on: ground, onPress: () => on.toggleTick(item.id, entry) }).el
          ) : (
            <Text color={t.fg.default}>{first}</Text>
          )}
          {wrapped.slice(1).map(line => (
            <Box paddingLeft={lead}>
              <Text color={t.fg.default}>{line}</Text>
            </Box>
          ))}
        </Box>,
      )
    })
  } else {
    body.push(...lines(k, t.fg.subtle, '(no human_action recorded)', room))
  }

  if (v.detailsOpen) {
    if (detail === undefined) body.push(<Text color={t.fg.subtle}>loading details…</Text>)
    else {
      const facts = [
        `branch: ${detail.branch || 'no branch'}`,
        ...(detail.pr ? [`PR: ${detail.pr}`] : []),
        ...(detail.notes ?? []).map(note => `note: ${note}`),
        ...(detail.links ?? []).map(link => `link: ${link}`),
      ]
      for (const fact of facts) body.push(...lines(k, t.fg.subtle, fact, room, 2))
    }
  }

  if (v.cursor.refusal !== '' && v.cursor.currentId === item.id) {
    body.push(badge(k, 'refused', 'critical', { on: ground }).el, ...lines(k, t.fg.default, v.cursor.refusal, room))
  }

  if (mode === 'confirm') {
    const open = check.items.filter(entry => !ticked.includes(entry)).length
    const question = doneQuestion(open, check.items.length, item.id, room - 2)
    body.push(...confirmRow(k, { id: 'confirm', question, onYes: () => on.confirmDone(item.id), onNo: on.cancel, width: room, on: ground }))
    return body
  }
  const actions = [
    keycap(k, { id: 'done', key: 'd', label: 'done', tone: 'success', on: ground, onPress: () => on.askDone(item.id) }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'back', key: 'a', label: 'agent', tone: 'warning', on: ground, onPress: () => on.askNote(item.id) }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'open', key: 'o', label: 'open', on: ground, onPress: () => on.fill(`Look at ${item.id}`) }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'viewer', key: 'v', label: 'viewer', on: ground, onPress: () => on.openViewer(item.id) }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'copy', key: 'c', label: 'copy check', on: ground, onPress: press => on.copyCheck(item.id, action, press.surface) }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'details', key: 'i', label: `${v.detailsOpen ? '▾' : '▸'} more`, on: ground, onPress: on.toggleDetails }),
  ]
  body.push(...wrapRows(k, actions, room))
  return body
}

/**
 * The note for the agent, on its own: the card it answers and the Input. Nothing else with a key is drawn while it is up, so
 * a letter or digit typed into the note can never press anything.
 */
function composeNote(k: Kit, item: TmQueueItem, action: string, on: ReviewHandlers, inner: number): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box, Text, Input } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const cancel = keycap(k, { id: 'note-cancel', label: 'cancel', onPress: on.cancel })
  return [
    label(k, 'review · note for the agent').el,
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1}>
      <Text color={t.fg.bold} bold>
        {item.id}
      </Text>
      <Text color={t.fg.default}>{truncate(oneLine(item.title), Math.max(4, inner - item.id.length - 1))}</Text>
    </Box>,
    ...(action === '' ? [] : lines(k, t.fg.subtle, action, inner)),
    <Input
      key="note"
      label="note for the agent"
      placeholder="what should change"
      submitLabel="send back"
      autoFocus
      onSubmit={value => on.sendBack(item.id, value, action)}
    />,
    cancel.el,
  ]
}

export async function reviewPaneTree(ui: Ui, rr: Rr, v: ReviewView, on: ReviewHandlers, width: number): Promise<RenderElement> {
  const k = await kitOf(ui, rr, v.surface, v.say)
  const { Box, Text } = ui
  const { t } = k
  const s = v.snapshot
  if (s === null || !s.reachable) return root(k, rr, status(k, s, v.connecting === true, on))
  const inner = Math.max(10, width - 2)
  const items = reviewItems(s, v.cursor, v.details)
  const view = listView(items, v.cursor.currentId, v.list.page)
  const focused = view.focused

  if (focused !== null && focused.kind === 'task' && cardMode(v.cursor, focused) === 'note') {
    const action = focused.humanAction || v.details[focused.id]?.humanAction || ''
    return root(k, rr, composeNote(k, focused, action, on, inner))
  }

  // A narrow pane keeps the pager whole: refresh shortens to its glyph first.
  const bar = toolbar(k, {
    title: 'review',
    slots: [
      pager(k, { page: view.page, pages: view.pages, onTurn: page => on.turnPage(page) }),
      keycap(k, { id: 'refresh', key: 'u', label: inner >= 40 ? 'refresh' : '↻', onPress: on.refresh }),
    ],
    width: inner,
  })
  const done = v.cursor.done.length
  if (focused === null) {
    const clear = v.strip.view.choice === ALL ? signal(k, 'success', 'Queue clear', `${done} done this pass`).el : phaseEmpty(k, v.strip, on.strip)
    return root(k, rr, [bar, ...phaseSection(k, v.strip, on.strip, inner, [clear])])
  }

  const newest = ageLabel(newestStamp(items), v.now)
  const summary = [
    `${s.queueTotal} waiting on you`,
    ...(newest === '' ? [] : [`newest ${newest} ago`]),
    ...(s.queueTotal > items.length ? [`${items.length} listed`] : []),
    ...(done > 0 ? [`${done} done this pass`] : []),
  ].join(' · ')
  const counts = kindCounts(items)
  const kinds = KINDS.map(kind => badge(k, `${counts[kind]} ${KIND_WORD[kind]}`, KIND_TONE[kind], { dim: counts[kind] === 0 }))
  const pageTasks = view.entries.filter(isSelectable).map(item => item.id)
  const selection = selectionBar(k, {
    actions: [],
    ghosts: [
      { id: 'sel-all', label: 'all', onPress: () => on.selectAll(pageTasks) },
      { id: 'sel-none', label: 'none', onPress: on.selectNone },
    ],
    width: inner,
  })
  const room = Math.max(8, inner - CARD_FRAME)
  const cards: RenderNode[] = []
  for (const item of view.entries) {
    const c: CardContext = { item, focused: item.id === focused.id, selected: v.list.selected.includes(item.id), room, v, on }
    const ground: RrGround = c.selected ? 'overlay' : 'raised'
    cards.push(cardBox(k, { id: item.id, selected: c.selected, focused: c.focused }, collapsedRow(k, c, ground), c.focused ? await expandedBody(k, c, ground) : []))
  }
  const prev = neighbour(items, view, -1)
  const next = neighbour(items, view, 1)
  const up = keycap(k, {
    id: 'focus-prev',
    key: 'k',
    label: '↑',
    onPress: () => (prev === null ? undefined : on.focus(prev.id, prev.page)),
    ...(prev === null ? { dim: 'Already on the first card' } : {}),
  })
  const down = keycap(k, {
    id: 'focus-next',
    key: 'j',
    label: '↓',
    onPress: () => (next === null ? undefined : on.focus(next.id, next.page)),
    ...(next === null ? { dim: 'Already on the last card' } : {}),
  })
  const hints = ['s select', 'h/l phase', 'f phases', 'p/n page', '1-9 tick', 'u refresh']

  return root(k, rr, [
    bar,
    ...phaseSection(k, v.strip, on.strip, inner, [
      <Text color={t.fg.subtle} wrap="truncate-end">
        {truncate(summary, inner)}
      </Text>,
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2}>
        {fit(kinds, inner, 2)}
      </Box>,
      selection,
      ...cards,
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1}>
        {up.el}
        {down.el}
        {helpFooter(k, hints, inner - up.width - down.width - 2)}
      </Box>,
    ]),
  ])
}
```

```ts
export type CardPosition = { readonly item: TmQueueItem | null; readonly n: number; readonly total: number }

// queueTotal is the server's count of everything waiting; the queue is a window of its first rows (priority order).
export function cardPosition(queue: readonly TmQueueItem[], c: TmCursor, queueTotal: number): CardPosition {
  const visible = queue.filter(i => !c.done.includes(i.id))
  const remaining = visible.filter(i => !c.skipped.includes(i.id))
  const item = remaining.find(i => i.id === c.currentId) ?? remaining[0] ?? null
  return { item, n: c.done.length + c.skipped.length + 1, total: c.done.length + Math.max(visible.length, queueTotal) }
}
```

### Task 3.3: `t` + digit ticks a check

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/tick.test.ts`
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/card.test.ts` (a check's digit now needs `t`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/flows.ts` (`armTick`; every move, page turn, `f`, `d`, `a` and a tick disarm)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/review.tsx` (the `t` keycap, digits only while armed, the footer)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (strip digits follow tick mode; the Tab ring disarms)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3.2; Stage 1's `StripView.digits` (the strip draws `0`–`9` only while `focused && digits && !overlay`).
- Produces: `TmFlows.armTick()`, `ReviewHandlers.armTick`.

The mechanism (spec D8): the digits belong to the phase strip, as spec §5 wants. `t` (a keycap on the focused card, shown only when the card has a check) sets `reviewList.ticking`. On the next draw, the focused card's checks carry `1`–`9`, the strip draws no digits, and the footer reads `1-9 ticks a check · t or f cancels`. A press on a check ticks it and clears `ticking`. So does `t` again, `f` (the overlay's picks take the digits), `j`/`k`, a Tab-ring move to another card's box, `p`/`n`, a phase change, `d` and `a`. A check can still be ticked with Enter or a click, without `t`. The tests check, in every state, which Button carries each digit and that no digit is on two Buttons.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/tick.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: ticking a review check is two keystrokes, `t` then the check's digit (spec §8, D8) — the digits are the phase
// strip's the rest of the time, so a stray digit never ticks, and no digit is ever on two buttons at once.
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { DEMO_DETAILS } from '../hooks/demo'
import { splitCheck } from '../hooks/model'
import { pane, PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const DEMO = { options: { source: 'demo' }, plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const WIDE = { ...pane('tm-review'), props: { ...pane('tm-review').props, bodyColumns: 140 } }
const ID = 'unified-chat-022'
const [FIRST] = splitCheck(DEMO_DETAILS[ID]!.humanAction).items as [string, string]
type Ui = { findAll: (q: { type: 'Button' }) => Promise<{ props: Record<string, unknown> }[]> }
/** Every hotkey drawn, with the Buttons that carry it. */
const hotkeys = async (ui: Ui): Promise<Record<string, string[]>> => {
  const keys: Record<string, string[]> = {}
  for (const b of await ui.findAll({ type: 'Button' })) {
    if (typeof b.props.hotkey === 'string') (keys[b.props.hotkey] ??= []).push(String(b.props.key))
  }
  return keys
}
const shared = (keys: Record<string, string[]>): string[] => Object.keys(keys).filter(key => (keys[key] ?? []).length > 1)

describe('t + digit ticks a check', () => {
  test('unarmed, the checks carry no digit and the strip owns 0-9', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-0' }))?.props.hotkey).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 't', label: 'tick' })
    const keys = await hotkeys(ui)
    expect(keys['0']).toEqual(['phase-tab:'])
    expect(keys['1']).toEqual(['phase-tab:patch-1-9'])
    expect(shared(keys)).toEqual([])
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /t\+1-9 tick/ })).toBeDefined()
  })

  test('t arms: the checks own 1-9, the strip lets go of its digits, no digit is on two buttons; the footer says how out', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'tick' })
    const keys = await hotkeys(ui)
    expect(keys['1']).toEqual(['tick-0'])
    expect(keys['2']).toEqual(['tick-1'])
    expect(keys['0']).toBeUndefined()
    expect(shared(keys)).toEqual([])
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /1-9 ticks a check · t or f cancels/ })).toBeDefined()
  })

  test('the digit ticks once and disarms: the next digit is the strip again', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'tick' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'tick-0' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-0' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ label: `☑ ${FIRST}` })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'tick-0' }))?.props.hotkey).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await hotkeys(ui))['1']).toEqual(['phase-tab:patch-1-9'])
    expect(world.store.get(`ticks:${ID}`)).toMatchObject({ items: [FIRST] })
  })
```
