### Task 3.4: Bulk Done / To agent

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/bulk.test.ts`
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/flows.ts` (`claim`/`release` split out of `once`; `bulkAsk`, `bulkCancel`, `bulkDone`, `bulkSendBack`; a single y/n or note clears the bulk row)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/review.tsx` (the bar's actions, the bulk y/n, progress, the bulk note page)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (handlers)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 3.3; `TmActions.done` / `backToAgent` (actions.ts, unchanged); `afterSendBack`, `signedOff`, `disarm`, `fill` in flows; `bulkSummary`, `selectedTasks` (Task 3.1).
- Produces: `TmFlows.bulkAsk`, `bulkCancel`, `bulkDone`, `bulkSendBack`; `ReviewHandlers` gains the same four.

The safety kept from today's `actions.ts` and single-card flows:
- Every bulk write waits behind a y/n with `n` focused, or behind an explicit note.
- The ids are fixed when the bulk action is asked (`reviewList.bulkIds`). `y` sends exactly those ids, whatever a refresh or a selection change drew in between.
- Checking the row and moving it to `busy` is one state write, so a second `y` finds nothing armed.
- Each task's write is the same call a single card makes (`backlog_complete_task(task_id, done)`, or status → note → clear). The calls go one at a time, under the same per-task lock. A write that timed out keeps its task locked until it answers.
- A refusal is collected in the server's words and named in one toast: `1 done · 1 refused: tm-audit-031 (<server text>)`.
- Refused tasks stay selected. The tasks that went leave the list and the selection.
- To agent fills the prompt with `Back to <ids that went>: <note>`, so the note is never lost. The pane closes only when every task went.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/bulk.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: bulk Done / To agent on the selected review cards (spec §8, §11) with today's single-card safety — a y/n with
// n focused, exactly the asked tasks, one write per task at a time, one toast naming every refusal in the server's words.
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'
import type { On } from 'claude-code'
import type { Engine } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { SIGN_OFF } from '../hooks/actions'
import { DEMO_REFUSAL } from '../hooks/demo'
import { command, pane, PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import * as R from './fixtures/replies'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { type McpAnswer, type World, worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const DEMO = { options: { source: 'demo' }, plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const TM = { plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const REVIEW_PANE = pane('tm-review')
const READS = /^backlog_(list_tasks|issue_list|continuity_items|handover_list|get_task|task_pipeline|query)$/
const writes = (world: World) => world.calls.filter(c => !READS.test(c.tool))
const UC = 'unified-chat-022'
const TA = 'tm-audit-031'
const signed = (id: string) => ({ tool: 'backlog_complete_task', args: { task_id: id, done: SIGN_OFF } })
const status = (id: string) => ({ tool: 'backlog_update_task', args: { task_id: id, field: 'status', value: 'in-progress' } })
const noted = (id: string, note: string) => ({ tool: 'backlog_update_task', args: { task_id: id, next_step: `Back from review: ${note}` } })
const cleared = (id: string) => ({ tool: 'backlog_update_task', args: { task_id: id, field: 'human_action', value: '' } })
const backOk = (args: Record<string, unknown>): string =>
  args.next_step !== undefined ? R.KEYWORD_UPDATED : R.updated(String(args.task_id), String(args.field), String(args.value))

async function tmQueue($: Engine, on: On, over: Record<string, (args: Record<string, unknown>) => McpAnswer>) {
  const clock = mock.clock(on)
  const world = worldOf(on, clock)
  world.mcp = (tool, args) => over[tool]?.(args) ?? R.backlog(tool, args)
  await $.session.start(SESSION)
  await clock.settle()
  await $.command.run(command('tm-review'))
  await clock.settle()
  const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE, surface: 'terminal' })
  return { clock, world, ui }
}

describe('bulk done and to agent', () => {
  test('Done N and To agent N count the selected tasks; at 0 they dim and say why', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-done' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ label: 'Done 0', hotkey: 'b', dimColor: true })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-done' })
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual(['Nothing selected to done'])
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel:unified-chat-022' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel:docs-012' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-done' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ label: 'Done 2', hotkey: 'b' })
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-agent' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ label: 'To agent 2', hotkey: 'g' })
  })

  test('Done N asks y/n naming the tasks, n focused and drawn after the pager; n cancels', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${UC}` })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${TA}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-done' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ` Done 2: ${UC}, ${TA}? ` })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-no' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'n', autoFocus: true })
    const drawn = JSON.stringify(await ui.drawn())
    expect(drawn.indexOf('"bulk-no"')).toBeGreaterThan(drawn.indexOf('"page-next"'))
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-no' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-yes' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-done' }))?.props.label).toBe('Done 2')
  })

  test('y signs each off; one toast names every refusal in the server words; a refused card stays selected', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${UC}` })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${TA}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-done' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-yes' })
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual([`1 done · 1 refused: ${TA} (${DEMO_REFUSAL})`])
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: `card:${UC}` })).toBeUndefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: `sel:${TA}` }))?.props.label).toBe('■')
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-done' }))?.props.label).toBe('Done 1')
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /1 done this pass/ })).toBeDefined()
  })

  test('y signs off exactly the tasks asked: a selection changed before y changes nothing', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${UC}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-done' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel:docs-012' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-yes' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: `card:${UC}` })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: 'card:docs-012' })).toBeDefined()
  })

  test('tm: one backlog_complete_task per task, one at a time, in list order; while they run the row says so and no y is drawn', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const { clock, world, ui } = await tmQueue($, on, {
      backlog_complete_task: args => ({ hangMs: 1000, text: args.task_id === TA ? R.GATE_REFUSAL : R.COMPLETED }),
    })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${TA}` })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${UC}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-done' })
    void ui.press({ key: 'bulk-yes' })
    await clock.advance(500)
    expect(writes(world)).toEqual([signed(UC)])
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-yes' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' working' })).toBeDefined()
    await clock.advance(1000)
    await clock.advance(1000)
    await clock.settle()
    expect(writes(world)).toEqual([signed(UC), signed(TA)])
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual([`1 done · 1 refused: ${TA} (${R.GATE_REFUSAL.split('\n')[0]})`])
  })

  test('To agent N takes one note on a page of its own; each task goes back; the prompt holds the note and the pane closes', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE, surface: 'terminal' })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${UC}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel:docs-012' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-agent' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Input', key: 'bulk-note' })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.findAll({ type: 'Button' })).map(b => b.props.key)).toEqual(['bulk-cancel'])
    await ui.input({ key: 'bulk-note', text: 'tighten the copy' })
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual(['2 back to agent'])
    expect(world.fills).toEqual([`Back to ${UC}, docs-012: tighten the copy`])
    expect(world.closed).toEqual(['tm-review'])
  })

  test('tm: status, note, clear for each task in order; a refusal stops that task only, the pane stays, the prompt names who went', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const illegal = `Error: \`${TA}\`: illegal transition \`in-review\` → \`in-progress\`. Legal: done`
    const { clock, world, ui } = await tmQueue($, on, { backlog_update_task: args => ({ text: args.task_id === TA ? illegal : backOk(args) }) })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${UC}` })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${TA}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-agent' })
    await ui.input({ key: 'bulk-note', text: 'x' })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(writes(world)).toEqual([status(UC), noted(UC, 'x'), cleared(UC), status(TA)])
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual([`1 back to agent · 1 refused: ${TA} (status: ${illegal})`])
    expect(world.fills).toEqual([`Back to ${UC}: x`])
    expect(world.closed).toEqual([])
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: `sel:${TA}` }))?.props.label).toBe('■')
  })

  test('a card y/n and the bulk row never stand together', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...REVIEW_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: `sel:${UC}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-done' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'done' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-yes' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'confirm-yes' })).toBeDefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-done' })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'confirm-yes' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'bulk-yes' })).toBeDefined()
  })
})
```

with:

```ts
  const held = new Set<string>()
  const claim = (id: string): boolean => {
    if (busy.has(id)) return false
    busy.add(id)
    return true
  }
  const release = (id: string, pending: Promise<unknown> | undefined): void => {
    if (pending === undefined) {
      busy.delete(id)
      return
    }
    held.add(id)
    const free = () => {
      held.delete(id)
      busy.delete(id)
      d.afterWrite(id, 'failed')
    }
    void pending.then(free, free)
  }
  const once = async (id: string, work: () => Promise<Hold>): Promise<void> => {
    if (!claim(id)) {
      if (held.has(id)) {
        // Not left armed either: once the earlier call answers, a y must not fire off this press.
        await disarm(id)
        d.host.toast(`taskmaster-mods: the earlier write on ${id} has not answered yet; try again once it has`)
      }
      return
    }
    let pending: Promise<unknown> | undefined
    try {
      pending = (await work())?.pending
    } finally {
      release(id, pending)
    }
  }
  // The bulk row acts once, on exactly the ids it was asked for: the check and the move to `busy` are one write, so a second
  // y, or a y on a row drawn before a cancel, finds nothing armed and sends nothing.
  const bulkArmed = async (kind: 'done' | 'agent', ids: readonly string[]): Promise<boolean> => {
    let armed = false
    await d.write.reviewList(l => {
      armed = l.bulk === kind && l.bulkIds.length === ids.length && l.bulkIds.every((id, i) => id === ids[i])
      return armed ? { ...l, bulk: 'busy' } : l
    })
    return armed
  }
  // A single card's y/n or note and the bulk row never stand together; a running bulk is left to finish.
  const quietList = (l: TmReviewList): TmReviewList => ({ ...l, ticking: false, ...(l.bulk === 'busy' ? {} : { bulk: 'none' as const, bulkIds: [] }) })
```


with:

```ts
    selectNone: async (): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.reviewList(l => ({ ...l, selected: [] }))
    },
    /**
     * `Done N` / `To agent N`: the y/n (n focused) or the shared note, for exactly these tasks; a refresh before y changes
     * nothing. A card's own y/n goes down.
     */
    bulkAsk: async (kind: 'done' | 'agent', ids: readonly string[]): Promise<void> => {
      if (ids.length === 0) return
      await d.write.cursor(c => ({ ...c, mode: 'card', refusal: '' }))
      await d.write.reviewList(l => (l.bulk === 'busy' ? l : { ...l, bulk: kind, bulkIds: [...ids], ticking: false }))
      await d.host.focus(REVIEW, kind === 'done' ? 'bulk-no' : 'bulk-note')
    },
    bulkCancel: async (): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.reviewList(l => (l.bulk === 'busy' ? l : { ...l, bulk: 'none', bulkIds: [] }))
    },
    /**
     * y on `Done N`: each task signed off in turn with the card's own call (one write per task at a time, a timed-out one
     * held), then one toast naming every refusal in the server's words; refused tasks stay selected.
     */
    bulkDone: async (ids: readonly string[]): Promise<void> => {
      if (!(await bulkArmed('done', ids))) return
      const results: BulkResult[] = []
      try {
        for (const id of ids) {
          if (!claim(id)) {
            results.push({ id, refusal: 'an earlier write on it has not answered yet' })
            continue
          }
          let pending: Promise<unknown> | undefined
          try {
            const out = await d.actions.done(id)
            if (out.ok) {
              await d.write.cursor(c => ({ ...c, done: c.done.includes(id) ? c.done : [...c.done, id] }))
              await signedOff(id)
              results.push({ id, refusal: null })
            } else {
              await disarm(id)
              pending = out.pending
              results.push({ id, refusal: out.refusal })
            }
          } finally {
            release(id, pending)
          }
        }
      } finally {
        const went = results.filter(r => r.refusal === null).map(r => r.id)
        await d.write.reviewList(l => ({ ...l, bulk: 'none', bulkIds: [], selected: l.selected.filter(id => !went.includes(id)) }))
      }
      d.host.toast(bulkSummary('done', results))
    },
    /**
     * The note on `To agent N`: each task goes back in turn with the one note, as a card's `a` does (`actions`: the check each
     * card showed; a task no longer listed is not sent). One toast; the prompt names the tasks that went and holds the note
     * (with none gone, it still keeps the note); the pane closes only when every one went.
     */
    bulkSendBack: async (ids: readonly string[], note: string, actions: Readonly<Record<string, string>>): Promise<void> => {
      if (!(await bulkArmed('agent', ids))) return
      const results: BulkResult[] = []
      const moved: string[] = []
      try {
        for (const id of ids) {
          const humanAction = actions[id]
          if (humanAction === undefined) {
            results.push({ id, refusal: 'no longer listed: not sent back' })
            continue
          }
          if (!claim(id)) {
            results.push({ id, refusal: 'an earlier write on it has not answered yet' })
            continue
          }
          let pending: Promise<unknown> | undefined
          try {
            const out = await d.actions.backToAgent(id, note, humanAction)
            await disarm(id)
            if (out.ok || out.mayHaveMoved === true) {
              await d.write.snapshot(s => (s === null ? s : afterSendBack(s, id)))
              moved.push(id)
            }
            if (out.ok) {
              await d.write.band(b => (b.refusalId === id ? { confirmingId: b.confirmingId, refusal: '' } : b))
              d.afterWrite(id, 'sent-back')
            } else {
              pending = out.pending
              if (pending === undefined) d.afterWrite(id, 'failed')
            }
            results.push({ id, refusal: out.ok ? null : out.refusal })
          } finally {
            release(id, pending)
          }
        }
      } finally {
        await d.write.reviewList(l => ({ ...l, bulk: 'none', bulkIds: [], selected: l.selected.filter(id => !moved.includes(id)) }))
      }
      d.host.toast(bulkSummary('back to agent', results))
      const text = note.trim()
      const named = moved.length > 0 ? moved : text === '' ? [] : [...ids]
      if (named.length > 0) await fill(text === '' ? `Back to ${named.join(', ')}` : `Back to ${named.join(', ')}: ${text}`)
      if (moved.length === ids.length) await d.host.closePane(REVIEW)
    },
```

In `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/review.tsx`, replace:

```tsx
export async function reviewPaneTree(
```

with:

```tsx
/**
 * The shared note for `To agent N`, on its own like a card's note: the tasks it goes to and the Input; nothing else with a key.
 */
function composeBulkNote(k: Kit, items: readonly TmQueueItem[], v: ReviewView, on: ReviewHandlers, inner: number): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box, Text, Input } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const ids = v.list.bulkIds
  const actions: Record<string, string> = {}
  for (const item of items) if (item.kind === 'task' && ids.includes(item.id)) actions[item.id] = item.humanAction || v.details[item.id]?.humanAction || ''
  const cancel = keycap(k, { id: 'bulk-cancel', label: 'cancel', onPress: on.bulkCancel })
  return [
    label(k, `review · one note for ${ids.length} tasks`).el,
    ...ids.map(id => {
      const item = items.find(i => i.id === id)
      return (
        <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1}>
          <Text color={t.fg.bold} bold>
            {id}
          </Text>
          <Text color={t.fg.default}>{truncate(item === undefined ? '(no longer listed)' : oneLine(item.title), Math.max(4, inner - id.length - 1))}</Text>
        </Box>
      )
    }),
    <Input
      key="bulk-note"
      label={`note for the agent (${ids.length} tasks)`}
      placeholder="what should change"
      submitLabel="send back"
      autoFocus
      onSubmit={value => on.bulkSendBack(ids, value, actions)}
    />,
    cancel.el,
  ]
}
```

1. The layout. The toolbar reads `REVIEW`, then `p ‹` and `n ›` (both dim on one page), `1 / 1` and `u refresh`. Below it come the strip, then `5 waiting on you · newest 1h ago`, then `1 BLOCKED  4 ACTION  0 IN REVIEW` (IN REVIEW dim), then `Done 0`, `To agent 0`, `all · none`, then five cards, then the footer. The first card (`unified-chat-022`, BLOCKED) is open with a blue full border. The other cards are one line each: box, kind badge, id, title and age at the right. Check that nothing has a coloured left rail, nothing moves on hover (only the fill changes), and nothing has a shadow.
2. `j` and `k`. The focus moves card by card. The card left behind closes and the new one opens. At either end the key dims and a toast says why.
3. The Tab ring. Tab walks the boxes and the open card's keycaps. Landing on another card's box opens that card.
4. `t` then `1`. `t` fills its keycap. The checks show ` 1 `, ` 2 `, the strip tabs lose their digits, and the footer reads `1-9 ticks a check · t or f cancels`. `1` ticks the first check (`☑`) and everything goes back. Check that `t` then `t`, and `t` then `f`, leave without ticking. Check that a bare `1` (without `t`) jumps the strip to the first phase tab.
5. `d` shows the warning question with `y yes` and `n no`, with `n` focused. `n` cancels. On `tm-audit-031` (`j` once), `d` then `y` shows the `REFUSED` badge and the server's gate text on the card.

7. Bulk. Press `s` on `unified-chat-022`, then `j` `s` on `tm-audit-031`. The bar reads `Done 2` and `To agent 2`. Press `b`: the question names both ids, with `n` focused. `y` gives one toast: `1 done · 1 refused: tm-audit-031 (Cannot complete …)`. `unified-chat-022` is gone and `tm-audit-031` stays selected (■). Then select `docs-012` too and press `g`. The note page lists both tasks and draws no other key. Type a note and press Enter. Check the toast (`2 back to agent`), that the prompt holds `Back to tm-audit-031, docs-012: <note>`, and that the pane closes.

## Stage 4: Handovers

**Goal.** The handovers pane (spec §9) moves onto the Stage 1 strip and kit. It reads its own list (up to 100 open handovers, past the server's 30-entry index, plus up to 100 closed ones on `◐ closed`), groups handovers by phase through their linked tasks (`handover_tasks`) with a trailing **no task** tab, and draws them as paged cards. Only the focused card expands: tldr, NEXT, each linked task with its live status badge and phase, decisions and blockers on `i`, and the keys `r` resume here, `w` new tab, `c` copy (the Telegram block), `x` close (y/n). `s` selects, and `Close N` closes the selection after one y/n, one handover at a time, with one toast. `openHandover` (Stage 2, from the band and the Session tab) is extended to land on the handover's phase and page.

**Verified before writing.** This stage was replayed task by task on a copy of both mods with Stages 0–3 applied exactly as their plans write them (`scratchpad/s3f/mods`, 361 taskmaster-mods tests, green); the result is kept at `scratchpad/s4f/mods` for Stage 5. After writing each task's tests and before its implementation, the new tests failed as each Step 2 says; after each task, `claude plugin test`, `validate --strict` and `tsc` were green, and `claude -p` loaded both mods. Test counts below are relative: **N** is the count `claude plugin test` reported for taskmaster-mods at the end of Stage 3 (361 in the replay). Stage 4 ends at N + 40.

**Anchors.** Every edit below quotes the code as Stages 2 and 3 leave it (`register.tsx`, `flows.ts`, `demo.ts`, `types/index.d.ts`), so each "replace" finds exactly one match. Stage 2's `openHandovers` const (which `openPane('handovers')` calls), its `openHandover` entry and the lookup line in its `handoverText` are replaced or extended in place; Stage 3's `refresh(pane)` and `TmFlowDeps.refresh?` are used as they are. `hooks/draw.tsx`, which after Stage 3 holds only the old handovers tree, is deleted in Task 4.5. `hooks/handover-guard.ts` is never touched.

**Keys in the handovers pane** (all are `/^[a-z0-9]$/` hotkeys; the Stage 0 key map applies):

| Key | Where | Does |
|---|---|---|
| `h` / `l`, `f`, `0`–`9`, `◐ done` | strip (Stage 1) | step phases, overlay, jump, done phases |
| `p` / `n` | toolbar pager | page (5 a page) |
| `u` | toolbar | Stage 3's `refresh('handovers')`, then the pane's own list (`retry` when tm is down) |
| `◐ closed` | toolbar, no key (click / Tab + Enter) | closed handovers join, dimmed |
| `j` / `k` | footer | focus the next / previous card, across pages |
| `s` | focused card's box | select it (a click selects any card) |
| `r` `w` `c` `i` `x` | focused card | resume here (+ recorded) · new tab · copy · decisions·blockers · close |
| `y` / `n` | a close row | close / keep (`n` is focused; it wins over the pager's `n` while up) |
| `b` = `Close N`; `all` · `none` | selection bar (ghosts keyless) | bulk close (y/n), as review's `b` = Done N · select this page's open ones, as review's `all` · clear |

**What Stage 4 consumes.** From Stage 1: `kit.tsx` (`badge`, `card`, `checkbox`, `confirmRow`, `helpFooter`, `keycap`, `label`, `pager`, `phaseEmpty`, `phaseSection`, `row`, `selectionBar`, `signal`, `toolbar`, `kitOf`), `phases.ts` (`ALL`, `NO_TASK`, `withClosed`, `phaseKey`, `FRESH_PHASE_VIEW`), `tm.ts` (`phaseSql('handovers', …)`, `NO_TASK_SQL`, `readPhases`), `flows.ts` (`loadPhases`, `choosePhase`, `toggleDone`, `setOverlay`, `TmWriter.phaseView`), `demoPhases`. From Stage 2 (contracts): `LaunchSpec`, `resumePrompt` (`hooks/launch.ts`), `TmFlows.launch(specs, surface?)` (demo only toasts `Demo: would open <title>`), `copyHandover(handoverId, surface?)`, `openHandover(handoverId)`, `recordResumed(handoverId)` (Stage 2's `prompt.submit` hook also records a submitted resume line; calling it on `r` as well is harmless). From Stage 3: `TmFlows.refresh(pane)` (the generic `u`: a snapshot refresh and the pane's phases) and `TmFlowDeps.refresh?: () => void` (register already passes the refresher in tm mode); Stage 4 adds no refresh flow or dep of its own.

**What Stage 4 produces for Stage 5 and later** (final signatures; the code is in the tasks):

```ts

export function phaseHandovers(listed: readonly TmHandover[], choice: string, tasks: Readonly<Record<string, TmLinkedTask>>): readonly TmHandover[]

export function moveTarget(shown: readonly TmHandover[], page: number, pick: string, dir: -1 | 1): { readonly page: number; readonly id: string } | null
export function arrival(listed: readonly TmHandover[], id: string, tasks: Readonly<Record<string, TmLinkedTask>>, phases: readonly TmPhase[], choice: string): { readonly choice: string; readonly page: number } | null
export function closableSelection(selected: readonly string[], shown: readonly TmHandover[]): string[]
export function closeToast(closed: readonly string[], refused: readonly { readonly id: string; readonly refusal: string }[]): string
export function markClosed(list: TmHandoverList, ids: readonly string[]): TmHandoverList
export function withoutHandovers(s: TmSnapshot, ids: readonly string[]): TmSnapshot
// hooks/handovers.tsx (new; replaces draw.tsx, which Task 4.5 deletes)
export type HandoversView = { snapshot; list; marks; phaseView; pick; page; summaries; summaryOpen; now; focused; connecting? }
export type HandoversHandlers = { pick; move; turnPage; select; selectOnly; toggleSummary; resume; newTab; copy; askClose; confirmClose; cancelClose; toggleClosed; refresh; strip }
export function handoversPaneTree(k: Kit, v: HandoversView, on: HandoversHandlers, width: number): RenderNode[]
// hooks/flows.ts — TmWriter gains handoverList(change), handoverMarks(change);
// TmFlowDeps gains readHandoverList?(closed), closeHandover?: TmCloseHandover, findHandover?(id): Promise<TmHandover | null>
// (Stage 2's copyHandover looks there between the snapshot window and its tm read; snapshot refreshes use Stage 3's refresh? dep);
// TmFlows gains:

askClose(ask: TmCloseAsk), cancelClose, confirmClose, handoverLaunch(h): Promise<LaunchSpec>  // { cwd: session cwd, title: `handover <id>`, mode: 'fresh', prompt: resumePrompt(...) }, as Session `w`
resumeHandover: (h: TmHandover) => Promise<boolean>        // was Promise<void>: true when the prompt took the line
openHandovers (Stage 2's const, also behind openPane('handovers')) / openHandover(handoverId)  // now read the pane's phases and list; openHandover lands on phase + page, and one not listed leaves page 1 unpicked with Stage 2's toast
```

Stage 4 keeps the Button keys the live tests press: `ho:<id>` (a card's row), `summary` (`i`), `copy` (`c`), so `tests/live.test.ts` is unchanged.

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/types/index.d.ts` (`TmHandover.status`; `TmLinkedTask`, `TmHandoverList`, `TmCloseAsk`, `TmHandoverMarks`; two `PluginState` keys)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/parse.ts` (`parseHandovers` keeps `status`; `queryRows`, `parseLinkedTasks`)

- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/actions.ts` (append `TmCloseHandover`, `CLOSE_REASON`, `tmCloseHandover`, `demoCloseHandover`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/demo.ts` (`DEMO_SEED = 7`, handover counts per phase, two closed summaries, `DEMO_TASKS`, `demoHandoverList`)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `callTm`, `TmUnreachable`, `parseHandovers`, `handoverPath`, `NO_TASK`, `demoSnapshot`.
- Produces: the types, `parseLinkedTasks`, `HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT`, `linkedTasksSql`, `readHandoverList`, `tmCloseHandover`, `demoCloseHandover`, `CLOSE_REASON`, `DEMO_TASKS`, `demoHandoverList`, `DEMO_SEED = 7` (see the list above).

Why the pane reads its own list: the refresh keeps reading 30 open handovers every turn end (the band's counts need no more). The pane asks for 100 with `include_archived: true` only when it opens, on `u`, on `◐ closed` and after a close. Linked tasks come from one `backlog_query` over the listed task ids. Each row is `json_array(id, status, phase)`, under the server's 80-character cut. A row the server cut anyway does not read, and its task shows `?` "not read". The phase counts on the tabs stay Stage 1's `phaseSql('handovers')`, which joins through `handover_tasks` exactly as `phasesOf` (Task 4.2) does on the client.

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/handover-data.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: the handovers pane's own data (spec §9) — up to 100 open handovers past the 30-entry index, closed ones on
// demand, their linked tasks' live status and phase in one query, and the close write — read and written as the server words
// them, failing soft so the pane never loses what it already shows.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { CLOSE_REASON, demoCloseHandover, tmCloseHandover } from '../hooks/actions'
import { DEMO_SEED, DEMO_TASKS, demoHandoverList, demoPhases, demoSnapshot } from '../hooks/demo'
import type { TmHost } from '../hooks/host'
import { parseHandovers, parseLinkedTasks } from '../hooks/parse'
import { NO_TASK } from '../hooks/phases'
import { HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT, linkedTasksSql, readHandoverList, TmUnreachable } from '../hooks/tm'
import * as R from './fixtures/replies'

type Answer = { text: string; isError?: boolean } | 'throw'

/** A TmHost that answers from `answer` and keeps every call and log; nothing else is used. */
function hostOf(answer: (tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>) => Answer): {
  host: TmHost
  calls: { tool: string; args: Record<string, unknown> }[]
  logs: string[]
} {
  const calls: { tool: string; args: Record<string, unknown> }[] = []
  const logs: string[] = []
  const host = {
    sleep: (_ms: number, signal?: AbortSignal) =>
      new Promise<void>((_resolve, reject) => signal?.addEventListener('abort', () => reject(new Error('aborted')))),
    call: async (tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>) => {
      calls.push({ tool, args })
      const a = answer(tool, args)
      if (a === 'throw') throw new Error('tm: Connection closed')
      return { text: a.text, isError: a.isError === true }
    },
    log: (text: string) => void logs.push(text),
  } as unknown as TmHost
  return { host, calls, logs }
}

const server = (tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>): Answer => {
  if (tool === 'backlog_handover_list') return { text: args.status === 'closed' ? R.HANDOVERS_CLOSED : R.HANDOVERS_OPEN }
  if (tool === 'backlog_query') return { text: R.LINKED_TASKS }
  return { text: `Error: ${tool}` }
}
const listArgs = (status: string) => ({ format: 'json', status, limit: HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT, include_archived: true })

describe('handover list data', () => {
  test('a listed handover carries its status; anything but closed reads as open', () => {
    const open = parseHandovers(R.HANDOVERS_OPEN, '/proj')
    const closed = parseHandovers(R.HANDOVERS_CLOSED, '/proj')
    if (!open.ok || !closed.ok) throw new Error('unreadable fixture')
    expect(open.value.handovers.map(h => h.status)).toEqual(['open', 'open'])
    expect(closed.value.handovers.map(h => [h.id, h.status])).toEqual([['2026-10-02-row-counter-ported', 'closed']])
  })

  test('the linked-task table reads id, status, phase and title; a row whose cell was cut is left out', () => {
    const cut = `${R.LINKED_TASKS.split('\n').slice(0, -1).join('\n')}\n["a-very-long-task-id-that-the-server-cut","in-review","pha  Cut\n4 rows`
    const parsed = parseLinkedTasks(cut)
    if (!parsed.ok) throw new Error(parsed.reason)
    expect(parsed.value).toEqual([
      { id: 'unified-chat-022', status: 'in-review', phase: '2-0-4', title: 'Unified chat pre-build gets the full supervisor toolset; cookbook owns the order' },
      { id: 'tm-audit-030', status: 'in-progress', phase: '2-0-6', title: 'Agent tool-use audit fixes' },
      { id: 'tm-audit-029', status: 'done', phase: '2-0-3', title: 'Port the row counter' },
    ])
    expect(parseLinkedTasks('Error: not authorized: handover_tasks').ok).toBe(false)
    expect(parseLinkedTasks('task  title\n["a","todo",""]  A').ok).toBe(false)
  })

  test('the linked-task query names each id once, quoted, and reads status and phase in one cell', () => {
    expect(linkedTasksSql(['a-001', "it's-002"])).toBe(
      "SELECT json_array(t.id, t.status, coalesce(json_extract(t.doc,'$.phase'),'')) AS task, json_extract(t.doc,'$.title') AS title FROM entities t WHERE t.kind='task' AND t.deleted=0 AND t.id IN ('a-001','it''s-002')",
    )
  })

  test('open only: one list read past the index, then one task query for the linked ids', async () => {
    const { host, calls } = hostOf(server)
    const list = await readHandoverList(host, '/proj', false)
    expect(calls.map(c => c.tool)).toEqual(['backlog_handover_list', 'backlog_query'])
    expect(calls[0]?.args).toEqual(listArgs('open'))
    expect(calls[1]?.args).toEqual({ sql: linkedTasksSql(['unified-chat-022', 'tm-audit-030']), limit: 500 })
    expect(list?.closed).toBe(false)
    expect(list?.total).toBe(7)
    expect(list?.handovers.map(h => h.id)).toEqual(['2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022', '2026-10-04-audit-fixes-landed'])
    expect(list?.tasks['tm-audit-030']).toEqual({ id: 'tm-audit-030', status: 'in-progress', phase: '2-0-6', title: 'Agent tool-use audit fixes' })
  })

  test('with closed: the closed read follows, both merge newest first, and the total stays the open count', async () => {
    const { host, calls } = hostOf(server)
    const list = await readHandoverList(host, '/proj', true)
    expect(calls.map(c => c.args.status ?? 'query')).toEqual(['open', 'closed', 'query'])
    expect(calls[1]?.args).toEqual(listArgs('closed'))
    expect(list?.closed).toBe(true)
    expect(list?.total).toBe(7)
    expect(list?.handovers.map(h => h.status)).toEqual(['open', 'open', 'closed'])
    expect(Object.keys(list?.tasks ?? {}).sort()).toEqual(['tm-audit-029', 'tm-audit-030', 'unified-chat-022'])
  })

  test('no linked task asks no query', async () => {
    const { host, calls } = hostOf((tool, args) => (tool === 'backlog_handover_list' ? { text: R.handoversOpen(3) } : server(tool, args)))
    const list = await readHandoverList(host, '/proj', false)
    expect(calls.map(c => c.tool)).toEqual(['backlog_handover_list'])
    expect(list?.tasks).toEqual({})
  })

  test('a failing or unreadable list read is null and logs; a failing task read keeps the list with no tasks', async () => {
    for (const bad of [() => 'throw' as const, () => ({ text: 'not json' }), () => ({ text: '{"error":"No backlog found."}' })]) {
      const { host, logs } = hostOf((tool, args) => (tool === 'backlog_handover_list' ? bad() : server(tool, args)))
      expect(await readHandoverList(host, '/proj', false)).toBeNull()
      expect(logs).toHaveLength(1)
    }
    const { host, logs } = hostOf((tool, args) => (tool === 'backlog_query' ? { text: 'Error: no such table: handover_tasks' } : server(tool, args)))
    const list = await readHandoverList(host, '/proj', false)
    expect(list?.handovers).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(list?.tasks).toEqual({})
    expect(logs).toHaveLength(1)
  })
})

describe('closing a handover', () => {
  test('one update_status call to closed with the pane reason; the server success line is ok', async () => {
    const asked: { tool: string; args: Record<string, unknown> }[] = []
    const close = tmCloseHandover(async (tool, args) => {
      asked.push({ tool, args })
      return { text: `${R.handoverClosed(String(args.handover_id))} [seq 41]`, isError: false }
    })
    expect(await close('h-1')).toEqual({ ok: true })
    expect(asked).toEqual([{ tool: 'backlog_handover_update_status', args: { handover_id: 'h-1', status: 'closed', reason: CLOSE_REASON } }])
  })

  test('a refusal comes back as the server words it; a throw says unreachable; a timeout says it may have closed', async () => {
    expect(await tmCloseHandover(async () => ({ text: 'Handover not found: h-9', isError: false }))('h-9')).toEqual({ ok: false, refusal: 'Handover not found: h-9' })
    expect(await tmCloseHandover(async () => ({ text: 'Handover h-1 → status=closed (user-set).', isError: true }))('h-1')).toMatchObject({ ok: false })
    expect(
      await tmCloseHandover(async () => {
        throw new Error('tm: Connection closed')
      })('h-1'),
    ).toEqual({ ok: false, refusal: 'Taskmaster unreachable: tm: Connection closed' })
    const pending = Promise.resolve()
    const late = await tmCloseHandover(async () => {
      throw new TmUnreachable('backlog_handover_update_status: no reply within 15 s', true, pending)
    })('h-1')
    expect(late).toMatchObject({ ok: false, pending })
    expect(late.ok ? '' : late.refusal).toContain('it may still have been closed')
    expect(await demoCloseHandover()('anything')).toEqual({ ok: true })
  })
})

describe('demo handovers', () => {
  test('seed 7: the snapshot window plus two closed ones, every linked task known, and the strip counts agree', () => {
    expect(DEMO_SEED).toBe(7)
    const list = demoHandoverList(0)
    const open = demoSnapshot(0).handovers
    expect(list.handovers.filter(h => h.status === 'open').map(h => h.id)).toEqual(open.map(h => h.id))
    expect(list.handovers.filter(h => h.status === 'closed')).toHaveLength(2)
    expect(list.closed).toBe(true)
    for (const h of list.handovers) for (const id of h.taskIds) expect(DEMO_TASKS[id], id).toBeDefined()
    const count = (status: 'open' | 'closed', phase: string) =>
      list.handovers.filter(h => h.status === status && (phase === NO_TASK ? h.taskIds.length === 0 : h.taskIds.some(id => DEMO_TASKS[id]?.phase === phase))).length
    for (const p of demoPhases('handovers')) {
      expect(p.count, p.id).toBe(count('open', p.id))
      expect(p.closed, p.id).toBe(count('closed', p.id))
    }
  })
})
```

with:

```ts
const PHASE_ROW = /^(\[\S*\])(?:\s+(.*))?$/
const ROWS_FOOTER = /^\d+ rows?(?: \(capped\))?$/

/**
 * backlog_query's `<json array>  <text>` table: each row's cells and its trailing text. A line whose JSON cell does not
 * read (cut at 80 chars) is left out; a reply with no `N rows` footer is not a table the server finished.
 */
function queryRows(raw: string): Parsed<{ readonly cells: readonly unknown[]; readonly text: string }[]> {
  const lines = raw.trim().split('\n')
  if (isRefusal(raw)) return fail(`backlog_query: ${firstParagraph(raw)}`)
  if (!ROWS_FOOTER.test((lines[lines.length - 1] ?? '').trim())) return fail('backlog_query: no "N rows" footer')
  const rows: { cells: unknown[]; text: string }[] = []
  for (const line of lines.slice(1, -1)) {
    const row = PHASE_ROW.exec(line.trim())
    if (row === null) continue
    try {
      const cells: unknown = JSON.parse(row[1] ?? '')
      if (Array.isArray(cells)) rows.push({ cells, text: (row[2] ?? '').trim() })
    } catch {
      // a cell the server cut does not read: the row is left out
    }
  }
  return ok(rows)
}

/** The handovers pane's linked-task table (linkedTasksSql): `["id","status","phase"]  title` a task; a title cut stays cut. */
export function parseLinkedTasks(raw: string): Parsed<TmLinkedTask[]> {
  const rows = queryRows(raw)
  if (!rows.ok) return rows
  return ok(
    rows.value
      .filter(r => typeof r.cells[0] === 'string' && r.cells[0] !== '')
      .map(r => ({
        id: String(r.cells[0]),
        status: typeof r.cells[1] === 'string' ? r.cells[1] : '',
        phase: typeof r.cells[2] === 'string' ? r.cells[2] : '',
        title: r.text,
      })),
  )
}
```

```ts
/** Handovers the handovers pane reads per status (spec §9), the archive past the server's 30-entry index searched too. */
export const HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT = 100
/** backlog_query's row cap: the most linked tasks one read can answer. */
const LINKED_TASK_CAP = 500
const quoted = (id: string): string => `'${id.replace(/'/g, "''")}'`

/** The linked tasks' live status and phase: `json_array(id, status, phase)` and the title, one row a task (cells under 80). */
export function linkedTasksSql(ids: readonly string[]): string {
  return `SELECT json_array(t.id, t.status, coalesce(json_extract(t.doc,'$.phase'),'')) AS task, json_extract(t.doc,'$.title') AS title FROM entities t WHERE t.kind='task' AND t.deleted=0 AND t.id IN (${ids.map(quoted).join(',')})`
}

async function readLinkedTasks(host: TmHost, ids: readonly string[]): Promise<Readonly<Record<string, TmLinkedTask>>> {
  try {
    const text = (await callTm(host, 'backlog_query', { sql: linkedTasksSql(ids), limit: LINKED_TASK_CAP })).text
    const parsed = parseLinkedTasks(text)
    if (parsed.ok) return Object.fromEntries(parsed.value.map(t => [t.id, t]))
    host.log(`taskmaster-mods: unreadable backlog_query reply: ${text.slice(0, 2000)}`)
  } catch (error) {
    host.log(`taskmaster-mods: linked tasks not read: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`)
  }
  return {}
}

/**
 * The handovers pane's list: the open handovers (and, when `closed`, the closed ones), up to HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT each with
 * the archive searched, then one backlog_query for their linked tasks. Null when a list read fails or is unreadable (logged:
 * the pane keeps what it shows); a failed task read keeps the list with no tasks (every handover then shows under All only).
 */
export async function readHandoverList(host: TmHost, root: string, closed: boolean): Promise<TmHandoverList | null> {
  const listed = async (status: 'open' | 'closed') => {
    const args = { format: 'json', status, limit: HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT, include_archived: true }
    const text = (await callTm(host, 'backlog_handover_list', args)).text
    const parsed = parseHandovers(text, root)
    if (!parsed.ok) host.log(`taskmaster-mods: unreadable backlog_handover_list reply: ${text.slice(0, 2000)}`)
    return parsed.ok ? parsed.value : null
  }
  let open: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listed>>
  let shut: Awaited<ReturnType<typeof listed>> = { handovers: [], total: 0 }
  try {
    open = await listed('open')
    if (open !== null && closed) shut = await listed('closed')
  } catch (error) {
    host.log(`taskmaster-mods: handovers not read: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`)
    return null
  }
  if (open === null || shut === null) return null
  const handovers = [...open.handovers, ...shut.handovers].sort((a, b) => (a.created < b.created ? 1 : a.created > b.created ? -1 : 0))
  const ids = [...new Set(handovers.flatMap(h => h.taskIds))].slice(0, LINKED_TASK_CAP)
  const tasks = ids.length === 0 ? {} : await readLinkedTasks(host, ids)
  return { handovers, total: open.total, closed, tasks }
}
```

```ts
/** The handovers pane's close (spec §9): one handover moved to `closed`; a refusal comes back as the server words it. */
export type TmCloseHandover = (handoverId: string) => Promise<TmWriteOutcome>
/** The `status_reason` a close from the pane records. */
export const CLOSE_REASON = 'Closed in the handovers pane'
const CLOSED = /^Handover \S+ → status=closed\b/

export function tmCloseHandover(call: (tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>) => Promise<TmReply>): TmCloseHandover {
  return async handoverId => {
    let reply: TmReply
    try {
      reply = await call('backlog_handover_update_status', { handover_id: handoverId, status: 'closed', reason: CLOSE_REASON })
    } catch (error) {
      const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
      const pending = error instanceof TmUnreachable ? error.pending : undefined
      if (pending === undefined) return { ok: false, refusal: `Taskmaster unreachable: ${reason}` }
      return { ok: false, refusal: `Taskmaster unreachable: ${reason} — it may still have been closed: check before pressing again`, pending }
    }
    const head = firstParagraph(stripSeq(reply.text))
    if (!reply.isError && CLOSED.test(head)) return { ok: true }
    return { ok: false, refusal: head === '' ? 'Taskmaster refused without a reason' : head }
  }
}

/** Demo closes every handover (the pane marks it closed in $.state; tm is never reached). */
export function demoCloseHandover(): TmCloseHandover {
  return async () => ({ ok: true })
}
```

### Task 4.2: The pane's rules as plain functions (`hooks/handover-list.ts`)

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/handover-list.ts`
- Test: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/handover-list.test.ts` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `ageLabel`, `handoverPage`, `PAGE_SIZE` (model.ts); `ALL`, `NO_TASK` (phases.ts); Task 4.1's types and demo data (tests).
- Produces: `FRESH_MARKS`, `isClosed`, `phasesOf`, `listedHandovers`, `phaseHandovers`, `allCount`, `tallyLine`, `moveTarget`, `arrival`, `closableSelection`, `closeToast`, `markClosed`, `withoutHandovers`.

The rules in one place:
- A handover counts under each linked task's phase, once each. With no linked task it counts under **no task**. When its linked tasks were not read, it counts under All only.
- The pane lists its own read when there is one, else the snapshot's window. Closed handovers are listed only while `◐ closed` is on.
- `j` / `k` cross page boundaries and stop at the ends.
- A handover opened from elsewhere keeps the chosen phase if that phase holds it. Otherwise it moves to the first of its phases the strip knows (no task included), else to All.
- After closes there is one toast: `2 closed`, or `1 closed · 1 refused: <id> (<server wording>)` (spec §11).

```ts
// User intent: the handovers pane's rules without drawing (spec §9) — a handover counts under each of its linked tasks'
// phases or under no task, closed ones only on demand, j / k cross pages, a handover opened from elsewhere lands on its
// phase and page, and a close leaves one toast naming every refusal.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { DEMO_TASKS, demoHandoverList, demoSnapshot } from '../hooks/demo'
import {
  allCount,
  arrival,
  closableSelection,
  closeToast,
  listedHandovers,
  markClosed,
  moveTarget,
  phaseHandovers,
  phasesOf,
  tallyLine,
  withoutHandovers,
} from '../hooks/handover-list'
import { ALL, NO_TASK } from '../hooks/phases'
import type { TmHandover, TmPhase } from '../types'

const HOUR = 3_600_000
const NOW = 200 * HOUR
const LIST = demoHandoverList(NOW)
const SNAP = demoSnapshot(NOW)
const ids = (hs: readonly TmHandover[]) => hs.map(h => h.id)
const phase = (id: string): TmPhase => ({ id, status: 'planned', order: 1, count: 0, name: id })
const many = (n: number): TmHandover[] =>
  Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => ({ ...SNAP.handovers[0]!, id: `h-${i + 1}`, created: new Date(NOW - i * HOUR).toISOString(), taskIds: [] }))

describe('handover phases', () => {
  test('a handover counts under each linked task phase once, no task when it links none, nowhere when its tasks are unread', () => {
    const [shipped, audit, viewer, hang] = SNAP.handovers as readonly TmHandover[]
    expect(phasesOf(shipped!, DEMO_TASKS)).toEqual(['patch-1-9'])
    expect(phasesOf(audit!, DEMO_TASKS)).toEqual(['patch-1-9'])
    expect(phasesOf(viewer!, DEMO_TASKS)).toEqual([NO_TASK])
    expect(phasesOf(hang!, DEMO_TASKS)).toEqual(['release-2'])
    expect(phasesOf(hang!, {})).toEqual([])
    const { taskIds: _gone, ...old } = shipped!
    expect(phasesOf(old as TmHandover, DEMO_TASKS)).toEqual([NO_TASK])
  })

  test('the pane lists its own read, else the snapshot window; closed ones only while shown; a phase narrows it', () => {
    expect(ids(listedHandovers(LIST, SNAP, false))).toEqual(ids(SNAP.handovers))
    expect(listedHandovers(LIST, SNAP, true)).toHaveLength(7)
    expect(ids(listedHandovers(null, SNAP, true))).toEqual(ids(SNAP.handovers))
    expect(listedHandovers(null, null, false)).toEqual([])
    const all = listedHandovers(LIST, SNAP, true)
    expect(ids(phaseHandovers(all, 'patch-1-9', LIST.tasks))).toEqual(['2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022', '2026-10-04-audit-fixes-landed'])
    expect(ids(phaseHandovers(all, NO_TASK, LIST.tasks))).toEqual(['2026-10-03-viewer-reskin-2a', '2026-10-01-release-7-0-0', '2026-09-29-store-spike-notes'])
    expect(phaseHandovers(all, ALL, LIST.tasks)).toBe(all)
  })

  test('All counts the open total, plus the closed ones read while shown', () => {
    expect(allCount(LIST, SNAP, false)).toBe(SNAP.handoversTotal)
    expect(allCount(LIST, SNAP, true)).toBe(SNAP.handoversTotal + 2)
    expect(allCount(null, SNAP, true)).toBe(SNAP.handoversTotal)
    expect(allCount(null, null, false)).toBe(0)
  })

  test('the tally line: open, closed while listed, newest age; empty for nothing', () => {
    expect(tallyLine(listedHandovers(LIST, SNAP, false), NOW)).toBe('5 open · newest 2h ago')
    expect(tallyLine(listedHandovers(LIST, SNAP, true), NOW)).toBe('5 open · 2 closed · newest 2h ago')
    expect(tallyLine([], NOW)).toBe('')
  })
})

describe('moving and arriving', () => {
  const list = many(12)
  test('j and k cross pages and stop at the ends', () => {
    expect(moveTarget(list, 0, 'h-1', 1)).toEqual({ page: 0, id: 'h-2' })
    expect(moveTarget(list, 0, 'h-5', 1)).toEqual({ page: 1, id: 'h-6' })
    expect(moveTarget(list, 1, 'h-6', -1)).toEqual({ page: 0, id: 'h-5' })
    expect(moveTarget(list, 0, '', 1)).toEqual({ page: 0, id: 'h-2' })
    expect(moveTarget(list, 0, 'h-1', -1)).toBeNull()
    expect(moveTarget(list, 2, 'h-12', 1)).toBeNull()
    expect(moveTarget([], 0, '', 1)).toBeNull()
  })

  test('arriving keeps a phase that holds the handover, else picks its first known phase, else All; with its page', () => {
    const all = listedHandovers(LIST, SNAP, false)
    const phases = ['patch-1-9', 'release-2', NO_TASK].map(phase)
    expect(arrival(all, '2026-10-02-store-read-hang', LIST.tasks, phases, ALL)).toEqual({ choice: ALL, page: 0 })
    expect(arrival(all, '2026-10-02-store-read-hang', LIST.tasks, phases, 'patch-1-9')).toEqual({ choice: 'release-2', page: 0 })
    expect(arrival(all, '2026-10-04-audit-fixes-landed', LIST.tasks, phases, 'patch-1-9')).toEqual({ choice: 'patch-1-9', page: 0 })
    expect(arrival(all, '2026-10-03-viewer-reskin-2a', LIST.tasks, phases, 'release-2')).toEqual({ choice: NO_TASK, page: 0 })
    expect(arrival(all, '2026-10-02-store-read-hang', LIST.tasks, [], 'patch-1-9')).toEqual({ choice: ALL, page: 0 })
    expect(arrival(many(12), 'h-11', {}, [], ALL)).toEqual({ choice: ALL, page: 2 })
    expect(arrival(all, 'not-listed', LIST.tasks, phases, ALL)).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('closing', () => {
  test('Close N counts selected handovers that are open and shown', () => {
    const shown = listedHandovers(LIST, SNAP, true)
    expect(closableSelection(['2026-10-01-release-7-0-0', '2026-09-30-row-counter-ported', 'gone'], shown)).toEqual(['2026-10-01-release-7-0-0'])
  })

  test('one toast: the closed count, then every refusal with its server wording', () => {
    expect(closeToast(['a', 'b'], [])).toBe('2 closed')
    expect(closeToast(['a'], [{ id: 'b', refusal: 'Handover not found: b' }])).toBe('1 closed · 1 refused: b (Handover not found: b)')
    expect(closeToast([], [{ id: 'b', refusal: 'x' }, { id: 'c', refusal: 'y' }])).toBe('0 closed · 2 refused: b (x), c (y)')
  })

  test('closed handovers turn closed in the list and leave the snapshot window and its total', () => {
    const id = '2026-10-01-release-7-0-0'
    const list = markClosed(LIST, [id])
    expect(list.handovers.find(h => h.id === id)?.status).toBe('closed')
    expect(list.total).toBe(LIST.total - 1)
    const s = withoutHandovers(SNAP, [id])
    expect(ids(s.handovers)).not.toContain(id)
    expect(s.handoversTotal).toBe(SNAP.handoversTotal - 1)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/handover-list.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: what the handovers pane lists and where its keys land (spec §9) — which handovers a phase holds through their
// linked tasks, the `◐ closed` toggle, j / k across pages, where a handover opened from the band or Session tab sits, and
// the one toast a close leaves — as plain functions, tested without drawing.
import type { TmHandover, TmHandoverList, TmHandoverMarks, TmLinkedTask, TmPhase, TmSnapshot } from '../types'
import { ageLabel, handoverPage, PAGE_SIZE } from './model'
import { ALL, NO_TASK } from './phases'

export const FRESH_MARKS: TmHandoverMarks = { selected: [], ask: null, showClosed: false }

type Tasks = Readonly<Record<string, TmLinkedTask>>
const taskIdsOf = (h: TmHandover): readonly string[] => (Array.isArray(h.taskIds) ? h.taskIds : [])
export const isClosed = (h: TmHandover): boolean => h.status === 'closed'

/**
 * The phases a handover counts under, as the strip's query counts it: each linked task's phase (once each); NO_TASK when it
 * links no task. A linked task not read (or with no phase) adds nothing, so such a handover shows under All only.
 */
export function phasesOf(h: TmHandover, tasks: Tasks): string[] {
  const ids = taskIdsOf(h)
  if (ids.length === 0) return [NO_TASK]
  return [...new Set(ids.map(id => tasks[id]?.phase ?? '').filter(phase => phase !== ''))]
}

/** What the pane lists before the phase: its own read, else the snapshot's window; closed ones only while `◐ closed` is on. */
export function listedHandovers(list: TmHandoverList | null, snapshot: TmSnapshot | null, showClosed: boolean): readonly TmHandover[] {
  const all = list?.handovers ?? snapshot?.handovers ?? []
  return showClosed ? all : all.filter(h => !isClosed(h))
}

/** The handovers under the chosen phase ('' All: every one listed). */
export function phaseHandovers(listed: readonly TmHandover[], choice: string, tasks: Tasks): readonly TmHandover[] {
  return choice === ALL ? listed : listed.filter(h => phasesOf(h, tasks).includes(choice))
}

/** The strip's All count: the server's open total (the read's, else the snapshot's), plus the closed ones read while shown. */
export function allCount(list: TmHandoverList | null, snapshot: TmSnapshot | null, showClosed: boolean): number {
  const open = list?.total ?? snapshot?.handoversTotal ?? 0
  return open + (showClosed && list !== null ? list.handovers.filter(isClosed).length : 0)
}

/** The line under the strip: `9 open · newest 3h ago`, with ` · 2 closed` while closed ones are listed; '' when empty. */
export function tallyLine(shown: readonly TmHandover[], now: number): string {
  if (shown.length === 0) return ''
  const closed = shown.filter(isClosed).length
  const age = ageLabel(shown[0]?.created ?? '', now)
  return [`${shown.length - closed} open`, ...(closed > 0 ? [`${closed} closed`] : []), ...(age === '' ? [] : [`newest ${age} ago`])].join(' · ')
}

/** j (+1) / k (-1): the next or previous handover from the picked one, across pages; null at either end. */
export function moveTarget(shown: readonly TmHandover[], page: number, pick: string, dir: -1 | 1): { readonly page: number; readonly id: string } | null {
  const picked = handoverPage(shown, page, pick).picked
  const at = picked === undefined ? -1 : shown.indexOf(picked)
  const to = shown[at + dir]
  if (at < 0 || to === undefined) return null
  return { page: Math.floor((at + dir) / PAGE_SIZE), id: to.id }
}

/**
 * Where a handover opened from the band or the Session tab shows: the chosen phase when it already holds it, else the first
 * of its phases the strip knows (no task included), else All; and its page there. Null when the list does not hold it.
 */
export function arrival(
  listed: readonly TmHandover[],
  id: string,
  tasks: Tasks,
  phases: readonly TmPhase[],
  choice: string,
): { readonly choice: string; readonly page: number } | null {
  const h = listed.find(x => x.id === id)
  if (h === undefined) return null
  const own = phasesOf(h, tasks)
  const known = own.filter(p => phases.some(x => x.id === p))
  const chosen = choice === ALL || own.includes(choice) ? choice : (known[0] ?? ALL)
  const at = phaseHandovers(listed, chosen, tasks).findIndex(x => x.id === id)
  return { choice: chosen, page: Math.max(0, Math.floor(at / PAGE_SIZE)) }
}

/** Selected ids still open in what is shown: what `Close N` counts and closes. */
export function closableSelection(selected: readonly string[], shown: readonly TmHandover[]): string[] {
  return shown.filter(h => !isClosed(h) && selected.includes(h.id)).map(h => h.id)
}

/** The one toast after closes (spec §11): `2 closed`, `1 closed · 1 refused: <id> (<server wording>)`. */
export function closeToast(closed: readonly string[], refused: readonly { readonly id: string; readonly refusal: string }[]): string {
  const head = `${closed.length} closed`
  if (refused.length === 0) return head
  return `${head} · ${refused.length} refused: ${refused.map(r => `${r.id} (${r.refusal})`).join(', ')}`
}

/** After closes: the list marks them closed (shown dimmed under `◐ closed`, hidden otherwise). */
export function markClosed(list: TmHandoverList, ids: readonly string[]): TmHandoverList {
  return { ...list, total: Math.max(0, list.total - ids.length), handovers: list.handovers.map(h => (ids.includes(h.id) ? { ...h, status: 'closed' as const } : h)) }
}

/** After closes: the snapshot's open window loses them, and its total counts them out (the band's count follows). */
export function withoutHandovers(s: TmSnapshot, ids: readonly string[]): TmSnapshot {
  return { ...s, handovers: s.handovers.filter(h => !ids.includes(h.id)), handoversTotal: Math.max(0, s.handoversTotal - ids.length) }
}
```

### Task 4.3: The pane drawn with the kit (`hooks/handovers.tsx`)

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/handovers.tsx`
- Test: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/handovers-draw.test.ts` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Stage 1's kit and `withClosed`; Task 4.2's functions; `ageLabel`, `CONNECTING`, `dateOf`, `handoverPage`, `oneLine`, `PAGE_SIZE`, `phaseName`, `truncate`, `wrapText` (model.ts).
- Produces: `HandoversView`, `HandoversHandlers`, `handoversPaneTree(k, v, on, width): RenderNode[]`. The function is synchronous (the kit is). The caller lays the page ground around it, as `/tm-kit` does.

What the person sees, top to bottom:
- **Toolbar:** `HANDOVERS`, then `p ‹  n ›  1 / 2`, `○ closed`, `u refresh`. Inert slots dim; none vanish.
- **Strip:** Stage 1's strip, with a trailing `no task N` tab. While `◐ closed` is on, every tab counts open + closed (`withClosed`) and All adds the closed ones read.
- **Tally and bar:** `5 open · newest 2h ago` (` · 2 closed` while listed), then `b Close N` (warning tone, dim at 0) with `all · none` (`all` = this page's open ones). The bulk y/n row sits under the bar.
- **Cards:** one per handover, five a page. The collapsed row is `□` (`s` on the focused card only), `2h ago · tldr…`, and `branch · N tasks` at the right (`CLOSED` badge on a closed one; its row and box dim).
- **Focused card:** bordered and expanded. The tldr wraps whole, then the dim `id · branch` line, DECISIONS / BLOCKERS when `i` is open (before NEXT, as today), NEXT, the linked tasks (status badge padded to the card's widest, bold id, title, phase name at the right), then `r resume here  w new tab  c copy  i ▸ decisions·blockers  x close`. These wrap; none drop. `x` dims on a closed handover. While its close row is up, the y/n row replaces the keys.
- **Footer:** `j ↓  k ↑` keycaps (dim at the ends) and one subtle line of hints.
- **Status:** before data, while connecting, or with tm down, the pane shows the title, the state and a `u retry` keycap.

No coloured left rail anywhere: selection is a surface step (`card`), focus is the full border, status is a tinted badge.

```ts
// User intent: pin how the handovers pane draws (spec §9) — toolbar slots, the strip with its no task tab, the tally and the
// Close bar, one card per handover with only the focused one expanded (NEXT, linked tasks with status and phase, r w c i x),
// closed ones dimmed on demand, the y/n rows, and every row within the pane's width.

  turnPage: noop,
  select: noop,
  selectOnly: noop,
  toggleSummary: noop,
  resume: noop,
  newTab: noop,
  copy: noop,
  askClose: noop,
  confirmClose: noop,
  cancelClose: noop,
  toggleClosed: noop,

    expect(byKey(tree, 'ho:2026-09-30-row-counter-ported')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
    expect(textsOf(tree)).toContain(' CLOSED ')

  test('selection: a selected card steps up a surface, Close N counts it, the bulk y/n row asks under the bar', () => {

    expect(bg(0)).toBe(bg(1))
    expect(bg(0)).not.toBe(bg(2))

  test('x asks on the card itself: y/n replaces the keys; the bulk row is not drawn', () => {

    const focused = cards(tree)[0]

  test('before data, while connecting, and with tm down: the title and what is going on; u retries', () => {
    expect(textsOf(draw({ snapshot: null, list: null }))).toEqual(['HANDOVERS', 'Loading…'])
    expect(textsOf(draw({ snapshot: null, connecting: true }))).toEqual(['HANDOVERS', 'Connecting to Taskmaster…'])

  })

  test('before its own read the pane draws the snapshot window; every row fits the pane at 72 and 50 columns', () => {

        expect(widthOf({ type: 'Box', props: { flexDirection: 'column' }, children: draw(over, width) }), `${width}`).toBeLessThanOrEqual(width)
      }
    }
  })
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/handovers.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: the handovers pane (spec §9) drawn with the kit: toolbar (pager, `◐ closed`, refresh), the phase strip with its
// no task tab, a tally, the bulk Close bar, and one card per handover — the focused one expanded with NEXT, its linked tasks'
// live status and phase, decisions and blockers on `i`, and r / w / c / i / x — so a handover is found by phase and acted on.
// JSX compiles to h(...): never name a variable `h` in this file (it would shadow the factory).
import type { RenderNode, RenderSurface } from 'claude-code'

import type { TmCloseAsk, TmHandover, TmHandoverList, TmHandoverMarks, TmHandoverSummary, TmLinkedTask, TmPhase, TmPhaseView, TmSnapshot } from '../types'
import { allCount, closableSelection, isClosed, listedHandovers, moveTarget, phaseHandovers, tallyLine } from './handover-list'
import {
  badge,
  card,
  checkbox,
  confirmRow,
  helpFooter,
  keycap,
  type Kit,
  label,
  pager,
  phaseEmpty,
  phaseSection,
  type Piece,
  row,
  selectionBar,
  signal,
  type StripHandlers,
  type StripView,
  toolbar,
} from './kit'
import { ageLabel, CONNECTING, dateOf, handoverPage, oneLine, PAGE_SIZE, phaseName, truncate, wrapText } from './model'
import { withClosed } from './phases'
import type { RrGround, RrTone } from './rr'

export type HandoversView = {
  readonly snapshot: TmSnapshot | null
  /** The pane's own read (null: not yet; the snapshot's window is drawn meanwhile). */
  readonly list: TmHandoverList | null
  readonly marks: TmHandoverMarks
  readonly phaseView: TmPhaseView
  readonly pick: string
  readonly page: number
  readonly summaries: Readonly<Record<string, TmHandoverSummary>>
  readonly summaryOpen: string
  readonly now: number
  /** The pane holds the keyboard: the strip's tabs carry 0–9. */
  readonly focused: boolean
  /** tm is being retried after a transient failure: with no good snapshot the pane says so. */
  readonly connecting?: boolean
}

export type HandoversHandlers = {
  readonly pick: (id: string) => void
  /** j / k: show this page and focus this handover. */
  readonly move: (page: number, id: string) => void
  /** p / n: show this page and pick its first handover. */
  readonly turnPage: (page: number, firstId: string) => void
  readonly select: (id: string) => void
  /** `all` (this page's open ones) and `none` ([]). */
  readonly selectOnly: (ids: readonly string[]) => void
  readonly toggleSummary: (handover: TmHandover, open: boolean) => void
  readonly resume: (handover: TmHandover) => void
  readonly newTab: (handover: TmHandover, surface: RenderSurface) => void
  readonly copy: (handover: TmHandover, surface: RenderSurface) => void
  readonly askClose: (ask: TmCloseAsk) => void
  readonly confirmClose: () => void
  readonly cancelClose: () => void
  readonly toggleClosed: () => void
  readonly refresh: () => void
  readonly strip: StripHandlers
}

/** A linked task's badge tone by status; any other status (archived, unknown) draws dim. */
const TASK_TONE: Readonly<Record<string, RrTone>> = {
  done: 'success',
  'in-review': 'info',
  'in-progress': 'warning',
  blocked: 'critical',
  todo: 'signature',
}
const statusWord = (status: string): string => (status === '' ? '?' : status.replace(/-/g, ' '))
const PHASE_CELLS = 18
const MIN_LABEL = 12

/** Pieces laid in rows of `width`, wrapping, never dropping one (a card's keys are fixed slots). */
function packRows(k: Kit, pieces: readonly Piece[], width: number): RenderNode[] {
  const rows: Piece[][] = []
  let used = 0
  for (const piece of pieces) {
    const last = rows[rows.length - 1]
    if (last !== undefined && used + 1 + piece.width <= width) {
      last.push(piece)
      used += 1 + piece.width
    } else {
      rows.push([piece])
      used = piece.width
    }
  }
  return rows.map(r => row(k, r).el)
}

/** Wrapped lines in one colour, indented. */
function lines(k: Kit, color: string, text: string, width: number, indent = 0): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  return wrapText(text, width - indent).map(line => (
    <Box paddingLeft={indent}>
      <Text color={color}>{line}</Text>
    </Box>
  ))
}

/** One linked task: its status badge (padded to the card's widest), id, title, and its phase at the right. */
function taskRow(k: Kit, id: string, task: TmLinkedTask | undefined, phases: readonly TmPhase[], badgeCells: number, room: number, on: RrGround): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const known = task !== undefined
  const tone = known ? TASK_TONE[task.status] : undefined
  const mark = badge(k, statusWord(known ? task.status : ''), tone ?? 'signature', { on, ...(tone === undefined ? { dim: true } : {}) })
  const phase = known && task.phase !== '' ? truncate(phaseName(phases.find(p => p.id === task.phase) ?? { id: task.phase, status: '', order: 0, count: 0, name: '' }), PHASE_CELLS) : ''
  const titleRoom = Math.max(0, room - badgeCells - 1 - id.length - 1 - (phase === '' ? 0 : phase.length + 2))
  const title = known ? truncate(oneLine(task.title), titleRoom) : truncate('not read', titleRoom)
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row">
      <Box width={badgeCells} flexShrink={0}>
        {mark.el}
      </Box>
      <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} paddingLeft={1}>
        <Text color={t.fg.bold} bold>
          {id}
        </Text>
        {title === '' ? null : <Text color={known ? t.fg.default : t.fg.subtle}>{title}</Text>}
      </Box>
      <Box flexGrow={1} />
      {phase === '' ? null : <Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{phase}</Text>}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** The expanded summary: DECISIONS and BLOCKERS (read on `i`), or why they are not shown yet. */
function summaryNodes(k: Kit, summary: TmHandoverSummary | undefined, room: number): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box } = k.ui
  if (summary === undefined || summary.unavailable === true) {
    return [
      <Box flexDirection="column" marginTop={1}>
        {lines(k, k.t.fg.subtle, summary === undefined ? 'loading summary…' : 'summary unavailable', room)}
      </Box>,
    ]
  }
  const listOf = (items: readonly string[] | undefined): readonly string[] => (Array.isArray(items) ? items : [])
  return (
    [
      ['decisions', listOf(summary.decisions)],
      ['blockers', listOf(summary.blockers)],
    ] as const
  )
    .filter(([, items]) => items.length > 0)
    .map(([name, items]) => (
      <Box flexDirection="column" marginTop={1}>
        {label(k, name).el}
        {items.flatMap(item => lines(k, k.t.fg.default, item, room))}
      </Box>
    ))
}

type CardCtx = {
  readonly v: HandoversView
  readonly on: HandoversHandlers
  readonly tasks: Readonly<Record<string, TmLinkedTask>>
  readonly room: number
}

/** One handover: checkbox, age and tldr, refs; focused, the whole card (spec §9). */
function handoverCard(k: Kit, c: CardCtx, ho: TmHandover, focused: boolean): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const { v, on, room } = c
  const closed = isClosed(ho)
  const selected = v.marks.selected.includes(ho.id)
  const ground: RrGround = selected ? 'overlay' : 'raised'
  const box = checkbox(k, {
    id: `ho-sel:${ho.id}`,
    selected,
    on: ground,
    onPress: () => on.select(ho.id),
    ...(focused ? { key: 's' } : {}),
    ...(closed ? { dim: 'A closed handover is not selected for closing' } : {}),
  })
  const ids = Array.isArray(ho.taskIds) ? ho.taskIds : []
  const branch = typeof ho.branch === 'string' ? ho.branch : ''
  const refs = [branch, ids.length === 0 ? '' : `${ids.length} ${ids.length === 1 ? 'task' : 'tasks'}`].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')
  const closedMark = closed ? badge(k, 'closed', 'signature', { on: ground, dim: true }) : null
  const right = (refs === '' ? 0 : refs.length) + (closedMark === null ? 0 : closedMark.width + 1)
  const showRight = right > 0 && room - box.width - 1 - right - 2 >= MIN_LABEL
  const labelRoom = Math.max(1, room - box.width - 1 - (showRight ? right + 2 : 0))
  const age = ageLabel(ho.created, v.now)
  const text = `${age === '' ? dateOf(ho.created) : `${age} ago`} · ${oneLine(ho.tldr)}`
  const wrapped = focused ? wrapText(text, labelRoom) : [truncate(text, labelRoom)]
  const head = keycap(k, { id: `ho:${ho.id}`, label: wrapped[0] ?? '', faded: closed, on: ground, onPress: () => on.pick(ho.id) })
  const collapsed = (
    <Box flexDirection="row" alignItems="flex-start">
      {box.el}
      <Box paddingLeft={1}>{head.el}</Box>
      <Box flexGrow={1} />
      {showRight ? (
        <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} paddingLeft={2}>
          {refs === '' ? null : <Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{refs}</Text>}
          {closedMark === null ? null : closedMark.el}
        </Box>
      ) : null}
    </Box>
  )
  if (!focused) return card(k, { id: ho.id, selected, focused: false }, collapsed, [])

  const indent = box.width + 1
  const body: RenderNode[] = wrapped.slice(1).map(line => (
    <Box paddingLeft={indent}>
      <Text color={closed ? t.fg.subtle : t.fg.default}>{line}</Text>
    </Box>
  ))
  body.push(...lines(k, t.fg.subtle, [ho.id, branch].filter(Boolean).join(' · '), room, indent))
  const open = v.summaryOpen === ho.id
  if (open) body.push(...summaryNodes(k, v.summaries[ho.id], room))
  const next = oneLine(ho.nextAction) || '—'
  body.push(
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2} {...(open ? { marginTop: 1 } : {})}>
      {label(k, 'next').el}
      <Box flexDirection="column">{lines(k, t.fg.default, next, room - 'next'.length - 2)}</Box>
    </Box>,
  )
  if (ids.length > 0) {
    const words = ids.map(id => statusWord(c.tasks[id]?.status ?? ''))
    const badgeCells = Math.max(...words.map(w => w.length)) + 2
    body.push(
      <Box flexDirection="column" marginTop={1}>
        {ids.map(id => taskRow(k, id, c.tasks[id], v.phaseView.phases, badgeCells, room, ground))}
      </Box>,
    )
  }
  const ask = v.marks.ask
  if (ask !== null && ask.kind === 'one' && ask.ids[0] === ho.id) {
    body.push(
      ...confirmRow(k, {
        id: 'close',
        question: `Close ${ho.id}?`,
        onYes: on.confirmClose,
        onNo: on.cancelClose,
        width: room,
        on: ground,
      }),
    )
  } else {
    const keys: Piece[] = [
      keycap(k, { id: 'resume', key: 'r', label: 'resume here', on: ground, onPress: () => on.resume(ho) }),
      keycap(k, { id: 'new-tab', key: 'w', label: 'new tab', on: ground, onPress: press => on.newTab(ho, press.surface) }),
      keycap(k, { id: 'copy', key: 'c', label: 'copy', on: ground, onPress: press => on.copy(ho, press.surface) }),
      keycap(k, { id: 'summary', key: 'i', label: `${open ? '▾' : '▸'} decisions·blockers`, on: ground, onPress: () => on.toggleSummary(ho, !open) }),
      keycap(k, {
        id: 'close',
        key: 'x',
        label: 'close',
        tone: 'warning',
        on: ground,
        onPress: () => on.askClose({ kind: 'one', ids: [ho.id] }),
        ...(closed ? { dim: 'Already closed' } : {}),
      }),
    ]
    body.push(
      <Box flexDirection="column" marginTop={1}>
        {packRows(k, keys, room)}
      </Box>,
    )
  }
  return card(k, { id: ho.id, selected, focused: true }, collapsed, body)
}

/** Before data, while tm is retried, or with tm unreachable: the title, then what is going on (and `u retry` when down). */
function statusNodes(k: Kit, v: HandoversView, on: HandoversHandlers): RenderNode[] | null {
  const { Text } = k.ui
  const s = v.snapshot
  if (s !== null && s.reachable) return null
  const title = label(k, 'handovers').el
  if (v.connecting === true) return [title, <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{CONNECTING}</Text>]
  if (s === null) return [title, <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>Loading…</Text>]
  return [title, signal(k, 'critical', 'Taskmaster unreachable', s.reason).el, keycap(k, { id: 'refresh', key: 'u', label: 'retry', onPress: on.refresh }).el]
}

/** The handovers pane's children, drawn on the page ground the caller lays (spec §9). */
export function handoversPaneTree(k: Kit, v: HandoversView, on: HandoversHandlers, width: number): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const status = statusNodes(k, v, on)
  if (status !== null) return status
  const inner = Math.max(20, width - 2)
  const showClosed = v.marks.showClosed
  const tasks = v.list?.tasks ?? {}
  const listed = listedHandovers(v.list, v.snapshot, showClosed)
  const shown = phaseHandovers(listed, v.phaseView.choice, tasks)
  const pg = handoverPage(shown, v.page, v.pick)
  const picked = pg.picked
  const strip: StripView = {
    pane: 'handovers',
    view: showClosed ? { ...v.phaseView, phases: withClosed(v.phaseView.phases) } : v.phaseView,
    allCount: allCount(v.list, v.snapshot, showClosed),
    focused: v.focused,
    digits: true,
    empty: showClosed ? 'No handovers' : 'No open handovers',
  }
  const bar = toolbar(k, {
    title: 'handovers',
    slots: [
      pager(k, { page: pg.page, pages: pg.pages, onTurn: n => on.turnPage(n, shown[n * PAGE_SIZE]?.id ?? '') }),
      keycap(k, { id: 'show-closed', label: `${showClosed ? '◐' : '○'} closed`, selected: showClosed, onPress: on.toggleClosed }),
      keycap(k, { id: 'refresh', key: 'u', label: 'refresh', onPress: on.refresh }),
    ],
    width: inner,
  })
  const down = picked === undefined ? null : moveTarget(shown, pg.page, picked.id, 1)
  const up = picked === undefined ? null : moveTarget(shown, pg.page, picked.id, -1)
  const moves = row(k, [
    keycap(k, { id: 'move-down', key: 'j', label: '↓', onPress: () => down !== null && on.move(down.page, down.id), ...(down === null ? { dim: 'Already on the last handover' } : {}) }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'move-up', key: 'k', label: '↑', onPress: () => up !== null && on.move(up.page, up.id), ...(up === null ? { dim: 'Already on the first handover' } : {}) }),
  ])
  const footer = (
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2}>
      {moves.el}
      {helpFooter(k, ['s select', 'b close selected', 'h/l phase', '0 all', '1-9 jump', 'p/n page', 'u refresh', 'esc close'], Math.max(1, inner - moves.width - 2))}
    </Box>
  )
  if (picked === undefined) return [bar, ...phaseSection(k, strip, on.strip, inner, [phaseEmpty(k, strip, on.strip)]), footer]

  const closable = closableSelection(v.marks.selected, shown)
  // `all` selects this page's open handovers, as review's does its page.
  const openIds = pg.entries.filter(x => !isClosed(x)).map(x => x.id)
  const body: RenderNode[] = [
    <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{truncate(tallyLine(shown, v.now), inner)}</Text>,
    selectionBar(k, {
      actions: [{ id: 'close-selected', key: 'b', verb: 'Close', count: closable.length, tone: 'warning', onPress: () => on.askClose({ kind: 'bulk', ids: closable }) }],
      ghosts: [
        { id: 'select-all', label: 'all', onPress: () => on.selectOnly(openIds) },
        { id: 'select-none', label: 'none', onPress: () => on.selectOnly([]) },
      ],
      width: inner,
    }),
  ]
  const ask = v.marks.ask
  if (ask !== null && ask.kind === 'bulk') {
    const n = ask.ids.length
    body.push(...confirmRow(k, { id: 'bulk-close', question: `Close ${n} handover${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}?`, onYes: on.confirmClose, onNo: on.cancelClose, width: inner }))
  }
  const ctx: CardCtx = { v, on, tasks, room: inner - 4 }
  body.push(...pg.entries.map(ho => handoverCard(k, ctx, ho, ho.id === picked.id)))
  return [bar, ...phaseSection(k, strip, on.strip, inner, body), footer]
}
```
