### Task 4.4: The pane's flows: its own list, selection, close rows, bulk close, j/k, `w`, `openHandover` lands on phase and page

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/flows.ts` (writer, deps, `pick`, `choosePhase`, new consts and methods, `openHandovers`, `resumeHandover`, Stage 2's `openHandover`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (atoms `HANDOVER_LIST`, `HANDOVER_MARKS`; writer; flows deps; demo seed; the `resume` handler)
- Test: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/handover-flows.test.ts` (new)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 4.1–4.2; Stage 2's `resumePrompt`, `LaunchSpec`, `recordResumed`, the `openHandover` entry it added and the lookup line in its `copyHandover`; Stage 3's `TmFlowDeps.refresh?` (called after closes).
- Produces: `TmWriter.handoverList`, `TmWriter.handoverMarks`; `TmFlowDeps.readHandoverList`, `closeHandover`, `findHandover`; `TmFlows.closeHandovers` (the contract), `loadHandovers`, `toggleClosed`, `selectHandover`, `selectHandovers`, `moveTo`, `askClose`, `cancelClose`, `confirmClose`, `handoverLaunch`, and `resumeHandover(h): Promise<boolean>`. `openHandovers` and `openHandover` read the pane's phases, then its list, one call after the other.

Rules the flows keep:
- **Selection and the close row.** Any change of selection, a pick, a page turn, a `j`/`k`, a phase change or `◐ closed` takes the close row down. A phase change also clears the selection, so `Close N` never closes what the person cannot see.
- **Confirming.** `y` acts only while its row is up and takes the row down first, so a second `y` closes nothing. An id already being closed is skipped.
- **After closes.** Closed ids leave the selection; refused ones stay selected. The list marks them closed and the snapshot's window drops them, so the band's count follows at once. tm then asks for a snapshot refresh (Stage 3's `refresh?` dep) and re-reads phases and list. Demo changes only `$.state`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/handover-flows.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: what the handovers pane's keys do (spec §9, §11), checked on the flows alone — close one or many with one y/n
// and one toast, refusals kept selected, never a second close in flight, the selection and close row reset by any move, the
// closed toggle read once, and a handover opened from the band landing on its phase and page.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { demoActions, type TmCloseHandover } from '../hooks/actions'
import { demoHandoverList, demoPhases, demoSnapshot } from '../hooks/demo'
import { createFlows, type TmFlowDeps, type TmWriter } from '../hooks/flows'
import { FRESH_MARKS } from '../hooks/handover-list'
import type { TmHost } from '../hooks/host'
import { resumePrompt } from '../hooks/launch'
import { FRESH_PHASE_VIEWS, phaseKey } from '../hooks/phases'
import type { TmHandoverList, TmHandoverMarks, TmPhasePane, TmPhaseView, TmSnapshot } from '../types'

const HOUR = 3_600_000
const NOW = 200 * HOUR
const ROOT = 'C:\\work\\proj'
const CWD = 'C:\\work\\proj\\sub'
const [A, B, C] = demoSnapshot(NOW).handovers.map(x => x.id) as [string, string, string]

type Mem = {
  snapshot: TmSnapshot | null
  pick: string
  summaryOpen: string
  handoverPage: number
  handoverList: TmHandoverList | null
  handoverMarks: TmHandoverMarks
  phaseViews: Record<TmPhasePane, TmPhaseView>
}

/** Flows over memory: the writer keeps the handovers pane's state, the host records what was asked of the engine. */
function rig(over: Partial<TmFlowDeps> = {}, mem: Partial<Mem> = {}) {
  const state: Mem = {
    snapshot: demoSnapshot(NOW),
    pick: '',
    summaryOpen: '',
    handoverPage: 0,
    handoverList: demoHandoverList(NOW),
    handoverMarks: FRESH_MARKS,
    phaseViews: { ...FRESH_PHASE_VIEWS, handovers: { ...FRESH_PHASE_VIEWS.handovers, phases: demoPhases('handovers'), loaded: true } },
    ...mem,
  }
  const held: Partial<TmWriter> = {
    snapshot: async change => void (state.snapshot = change(state.snapshot)),
    pick: async id => void (state.pick = id),
    summaryOpen: async change => void (state.summaryOpen = change(state.summaryOpen)),
    handoverPage: async page => void (state.handoverPage = page),
    handoverList: async change => void (state.handoverList = change(state.handoverList)),
    handoverMarks: async change => void (state.handoverMarks = change(state.handoverMarks)),
    phaseView: async (pane, change) => void (state.phaseViews = { ...state.phaseViews, [pane]: change(state.phaseViews[pane]) }),
  }
  const write = new Proxy(held, { get: (target, key) => (key in target ? target[key as keyof TmWriter] : async () => undefined) }) as unknown as TmWriter
  const seen = { toasts: [] as string[], fills: [] as string[], focus: [] as string[], opened: [] as string[], later: [] as (() => void)[], store: new Map<string, unknown>() }
  const host = {
    toast: (text: string) => void seen.toasts.push(text),
    fill: async (text: string) => {
      seen.fills.push(text)
      return true
    },
    focus: async (_requestId: string, key: string) => void seen.focus.push(key),
    openPane: async (id: string) => {
      seen.opened.push(id)
      return true
    },
    after: (_ms: number, fn: () => void) => void seen.later.push(fn),
    repoRoot: async () => ROOT,
    cwd: async () => CWD,
    sessionId: async () => 'sess-A',
    storeGet: async (key: string) => seen.store.get(key),
    storeSet: async (key: string, value: unknown) => void seen.store.set(key, value),
    storeDelete: async (key: string) => void seen.store.delete(key),
  } as unknown as TmHost
  const flows = createFlows({ host, write, actions: demoActions(), afterWrite: () => undefined, boundId: async () => null, ...over })
  const settle = async () => {
    while (seen.later.length > 0) seen.later.shift()?.()
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i += 1) await Promise.resolve()
  }
  return { flows, state, seen, settle }
}

const closer = (refuse: Record<string, string> = {}) => {
  const asked: string[] = []
  const close: TmCloseHandover = async id => {
    asked.push(id)
    return refuse[id] === undefined ? { ok: true } : { ok: false, refusal: refuse[id] }
  }
  return { asked, close }
}

describe('closing handovers', () => {
  test('one call a handover, in order, then one toast; closed ones leave the selection, list and snapshot, refused stay selected', async () => {
    const { asked, close } = closer({ [B]: 'Handover not found: x' })
    let refreshes = 0
    const { flows, state, seen } = rig({ closeHandover: close, refresh: () => void (refreshes += 1) }, { handoverMarks: { ...FRESH_MARKS, selected: [A, B, C] } })
    await flows.closeHandovers([A, B, C])
    expect(asked).toEqual([A, B, C])
    expect(seen.toasts).toEqual([`2 closed · 1 refused: ${B} (Handover not found: x)`])
    expect(state.handoverMarks.selected).toEqual([B])
    expect(state.handoverList?.handovers.filter(x => x.status === 'closed').map(x => x.id)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([A, C]))
    expect(state.snapshot?.handovers.map(x => x.id)).not.toContain(A)
    expect(state.snapshot?.handoversTotal).toBe(demoSnapshot(NOW).handoversTotal - 2)
    expect(refreshes).toBe(1)
  })

  test('the y/n row acts only while it is up: y closes what it asked about once; n takes it down; asking focuses n', async () => {
    const { asked, close } = closer()
    const { flows, state, seen } = rig({ closeHandover: close })
    await flows.askClose({ kind: 'one', ids: [A] })
    expect(state.handoverMarks.ask).toEqual({ kind: 'one', ids: [A] })
    expect(seen.focus).toEqual(['close-no'])
    await flows.confirmClose()
    await flows.confirmClose()
    expect(asked).toEqual([A])
    await flows.askClose({ kind: 'bulk', ids: [B, C] })
    expect(seen.focus.at(-1)).toBe('bulk-close-no')
    await flows.cancelClose()
    await flows.confirmClose()
    expect(asked).toEqual([A])
    await flows.askClose({ kind: 'bulk', ids: [] })
    expect(state.handoverMarks.ask).toBeNull()
  })

  test('a handover being closed is never closed a second time in flight', async () => {
    let release = (): void => undefined
    const asked: string[] = []
    const close: TmCloseHandover = async id => {
      asked.push(id)
      await new Promise<void>(resolve => {
        release = resolve
      })
      return { ok: true }
    }
    const { flows, settle } = rig({ closeHandover: close })
    const first = flows.closeHandovers([A])
    const second = flows.closeHandovers([A])
    await settle()
    release()
    await Promise.all([first, second])
    expect(asked).toEqual([A])
  })

  test('with no close dep a close only toasts why; tm reads phases and list again after a close, demo does not', async () => {
    const none = rig()
    await none.flows.closeHandovers([A])
    expect(none.seen.toasts).toEqual(['taskmaster-mods: handovers cannot be closed from here'])
    const reads: string[] = []
    const deps = {
      closeHandover: closer().close,
      readPhases: async (pane: TmPhasePane) => {
        reads.push(`phases:${pane}`)
        return demoPhases(pane)
      },
      readHandoverList: async (closed: boolean) => {
        reads.push(`list:${closed}`)
        return demoHandoverList(NOW)
      },
    }
    await rig({ ...deps, source: 'demo' }).flows.closeHandovers([A])
    expect(reads).toEqual([])
    await rig({ ...deps, source: 'tm' }).flows.closeHandovers([A])
    expect(reads).toEqual(['phases:handovers', 'list:false'])
  })
})

describe('selection, moves and the closed toggle', () => {
  test('s toggles one, all / none set the selection; each, a pick and a phase change take the close row down', async () => {
    const { flows, state } = rig()
    await flows.selectHandover(A)
    await flows.selectHandover(B)
    await flows.selectHandover(A)
    expect(state.handoverMarks.selected).toEqual([B])
    await flows.askClose({ kind: 'bulk', ids: [B] })
    await flows.selectHandovers([A, B, C])
    expect(state.handoverMarks).toMatchObject({ selected: [A, B, C], ask: null })
    await flows.askClose({ kind: 'one', ids: [A] })
    await flows.pick(B)
    expect(state.handoverMarks.ask).toBeNull()
    await flows.choosePhase('handovers', 'patch-1-9')
    expect(state.handoverMarks.selected).toEqual([])
  })

  test('j / k show the page, pick the handover and move the focus to its row', async () => {
    const { flows, state, seen } = rig()
    await flows.moveTo(1, C)
    expect([state.handoverPage, state.pick]).toEqual([1, C])
    expect(seen.focus).toEqual([`ho:${C}`])
  })

  test('◐ closed flips, starts over at the top, and reads the closed ones only when the list holds none', async () => {
    const reads: boolean[] = []
    const readHandoverList = async (closed: boolean) => {
      reads.push(closed)
      return { ...demoHandoverList(NOW), closed }
    }
    const { flows, state } = rig({ readHandoverList }, { handoverList: { ...demoHandoverList(NOW), closed: false }, handoverPage: 1, pick: C })
    await flows.toggleClosed()
    expect(state.handoverMarks.showClosed).toBe(true)
    expect([state.handoverPage, state.pick]).toEqual([0, ''])
    expect(reads).toEqual([true])
    await flows.toggleClosed()
    await flows.toggleClosed()
    expect(reads).toEqual([true])
    expect(state.handoverMarks.showClosed).toBe(true)
  })
})

describe('opening the pane', () => {
  test('/tm-handovers starts over and reads the phases, then the list, off the open', async () => {
    const reads: string[] = []
    const { flows, state, seen, settle } = rig(
      {
        readPhases: async pane => {
          reads.push(`phases:${pane}`)
          return demoPhases(pane)
        },
        readHandoverList: async closed => {
          reads.push(`list:${closed}`)
          return demoHandoverList(NOW)
        },
      },
      { pick: B, handoverPage: 1, handoverMarks: { ...FRESH_MARKS, selected: [A], ask: { kind: 'one', ids: [A] } } },
    )
    await flows.openHandovers()
    expect(seen.opened).toEqual(['tm-handovers'])
    expect([state.pick, state.handoverPage, state.handoverMarks.selected, state.handoverMarks.ask]).toEqual(['', 0, [], null])
    expect(reads).toEqual([])
    await settle()
    expect(reads).toEqual(['phases:handovers', 'list:false'])
  })

  test('band or Session h: the handover lands under its phase (kept for the project) on its page, focused', async () => {
    const hang = '2026-10-02-store-read-hang'
    const { flows, state, seen } = rig({ readPhases: async pane => demoPhases(pane), source: 'tm' })
    state.phaseViews = { ...state.phaseViews, handovers: { ...state.phaseViews.handovers, choice: 'patch-1-9' } }
    await flows.openHandover(hang)
    expect(state.phaseViews.handovers.choice).toBe('release-2')
    expect(seen.store.get(phaseKey('handovers', ROOT))).toEqual({ phase: 'release-2' })
    expect([state.pick, state.handoverPage]).toEqual([hang, 0])
    expect(seen.focus.at(-1)).toBe(`ho:${hang}`)
    await flows.openHandover('not-in-the-list')
    expect([state.pick, state.handoverPage]).toEqual(['', 0])
    expect(seen.toasts).toEqual(['taskmaster-mods: handover not-in-the-list is not in the open list'])
    expect(state.phaseViews.handovers.choice).toBe('release-2')
  })
})

  // The handovers pane's own list (spec §9), read with `◐ closed`'s current setting; a failed read keeps what is drawn.
  const loadHandovers = async (): Promise<void> => {
    if (d.readHandoverList === undefined) return
    const held = { closed: false }
    await d.write.handoverMarks(m => {
      held.closed = m.showClosed
      return m
    })
    const list = await d.readHandoverList(held.closed)
    if (list !== null) await d.write.handoverList(() => list)
  }
  // The pane's phases, then its list: one call after another (the server answers one at a time).
  const reloadHandovers = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await loadPhases('handovers')
    await loadHandovers()
  }
  const unmark = async (): Promise<void> => {
    await d.write.handoverMarks(m => ({ ...m, selected: [], ask: null }))
  }
  // Ids being closed now: a second y (or a bulk row drawn before the first answered) never closes one twice.
  const closing = new Set<string>()
  /**
   * Closes handovers one at a time (backlog_handover_update_status), then one toast for all of them (spec §11): closed ones
   * leave the selection, turn closed in the list and leave the snapshot's window; refused ones stay selected.
   */
  const closeHandovers = async (ids: readonly string[]): Promise<void> => {
    const close = d.closeHandover
    if (close === undefined) {
      d.host.toast('taskmaster-mods: handovers cannot be closed from here')
      return
    }
    const todo = ids.filter(id => !closing.has(id))
    for (const id of todo) closing.add(id)
    const closed: string[] = []
    const refused: { id: string; refusal: string }[] = []
    try {
      for (const id of todo) {
        const out = await close(id)
        if (out.ok) closed.push(id)
        else refused.push({ id, refusal: out.refusal })
      }
    } finally {
      for (const id of todo) closing.delete(id)
    }
    if (todo.length === 0) return
    if (closed.length > 0) {
      await d.write.handoverMarks(m => ({ ...m, selected: m.selected.filter(id => !closed.includes(id)) }))
      await d.write.handoverList(l => (l === null ? l : markClosed(l, closed)))
      await d.write.snapshot(s => (s === null ? s : withoutHandovers(s, closed)))
    }
    d.host.toast(closeToast(closed, refused))
    if (closed.length === 0) return
    d.refresh?.()
    if (d.source !== 'demo') await reloadHandovers()
  }
  const moveTo = async (page: number, id: string): Promise<void> => {
    await d.write.handoverPage(page)
    await pick(id)
    await d.host.focus(HANDOVERS, `ho:${id}`)
  }
  // What the handovers pane draws from, read through the writers (each change hands its value back unchanged).
  const handoverState = async () => {
    const at: { list: TmHandoverList | null; snapshot: TmSnapshot | null; marks: TmHandoverMarks; view: TmPhaseView } = {
      list: null,
      snapshot: null,
      marks: FRESH_MARKS,
      view: FRESH_PHASE_VIEW,
    }
    await d.write.handoverList(l => {
      at.list = l
      return l
    })
    await d.write.snapshot(s => {
      at.snapshot = s
      return s
    })
    await d.write.handoverMarks(m => {
      at.marks = m
      return m
    })
    await d.write.phaseView('handovers', v => {
      at.view = v
      return v
    })
    return at
  }
```

    /** The pane's own list, read again (`u` runs it after Stage 3's `refresh('handovers')`). */
    loadHandovers,
    /** `◐ closed`: closed handovers join (read when the list holds none yet) or leave; the pane starts over at its top. */
    toggleClosed: async (): Promise<void> => {
      const held = { show: false, has: false }
      await d.write.handoverMarks(m => {
        held.show = !m.showClosed
        return { ...m, showClosed: held.show, ask: null }
      })
      await d.write.handoverPage(0)
      await pick('')
      await d.write.handoverList(l => {
        held.has = l?.closed === true
        return l
      })
      if (held.show && !held.has) await loadHandovers()
    },
    /** `s` or a click on a card's box; any change of selection takes the close row down. */
    selectHandover: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.handoverMarks(m => ({ ...m, ask: null, selected: m.selected.includes(id) ? m.selected.filter(x => x !== id) : [...m.selected, id] }))
    },
    /** `all` (this page's open ones) and `none` ([]). */
    selectHandovers: async (ids: readonly string[]): Promise<void> => {

    },
    /** j / k: show that page and focus that handover. */
    moveTo,
    /** `x` on the focused card, or `Close N`: the y/n row comes up, `n` (the safe default) focused. */
    askClose: async (ask: TmCloseAsk): Promise<void> => {
      if (ask.ids.length === 0) return
      await d.write.handoverMarks(m => ({ ...m, ask }))
      await d.host.focus(HANDOVERS, ask.kind === 'one' ? 'close-no' : 'bulk-close-no')
    },
    cancelClose: async (): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.handoverMarks(m => (m.ask === null ? m : { ...m, ask: null }))
    },
    /** `y`: closes what the row asked about, only while that row is up (a second `y` finds nothing armed). */
    confirmClose: async (): Promise<void> => {
      const held: { ask: TmCloseAsk | null } = { ask: null }
      await d.write.handoverMarks(m => {
        held.ask = m.ask
        return m.ask === null ? m : { ...m, ask: null }
      })
      if (held.ask !== null) await closeHandovers(held.ask.ids)
    },
    closeHandovers,
    /** `w`: a fresh session in a new tab, as the Session tab's `w` starts one: this cwd, `handover <id>`, the resume line. */
    handoverLaunch: async (h: TmHandover): Promise<LaunchSpec> => ({
      cwd: await d.host.cwd(),
      title: `handover ${h.id}`,
      mode: 'fresh',
      prompt: resumePrompt(h.id, h.path),
    }),
    /** n / p: turn to a page and pick its first handover. */

### Task 4.5: Wire the pane: `register.tsx` draws `handovers.tsx`; the old tree leaves `draw.tsx`

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/register.tsx` (the `tm-handovers` render hook, its imports, the `/tm-handovers` description, the focus hook)
- Delete: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/draw.tsx` (after Stages 2–3 it holds only the old handovers tree)
- Replace: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/handovers.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 4.1–4.4; Stage 2's `launch`, `copyHandover(id)`, `recordResumed`; Stage 3's `refresh(pane)` for `u`.
- Produces: the live pane. Handlers map one to one onto Task 4.4's flows, and `c` and `w` pass the press's surface on to Stage 2. `w` hands `launch` the spec `handoverLaunch` builds; in demo Stage 2's launch only toasts `Demo: would open handover <id>`. Focusing a card's row (`ho:<id>`) or its box (`ho-sel:<id>`) picks that card.

The Telegram block comes from Stage 2's `copyHandover(id, surface)`. It finds the handover in the snapshot's window, then (Task 4.4) in the pane's own list through `findHandover`, then with `backlog_handover_get`. The tests below assert the copy equals `handoverCopyText(h)` (the existing formatter: tldr, absolute path, `Resume: <thread> — <next action>`), including for a closed handover only the pane lists, so a different format or a lookup that misses the pane's list fails here.

```ts
// User intent: the handovers pane as a person uses it (spec §9) — opened by /tm-handovers, phases from linked tasks with a no
// task tab, the focused card's keys (r resume here and recorded, w new tab, c Telegram copy, i decisions·blockers, x close
// with y/n), bulk Close N, `◐ closed`, j / k and u — in demo and against the tm server's own words.
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { CLOSE_REASON } from '../hooks/actions'
import { demoHandoverList, demoSnapshot } from '../hooks/demo'
import { resumePrompt } from '../hooks/launch'
import { handoverCopyText, handoverPath } from '../hooks/model'
import { NO_TASK, phaseKey } from '../hooks/phases'
import { HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT, linkedTasksSql, NO_TASK_SQL, phaseSql } from '../hooks/tm'
import type { TmHandover } from '../types'
import { command, pane, PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import { elementsOf } from './fixtures/measure'
import * as R from './fixtures/replies'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { type World, worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const DEMO = { options: { source: 'demo' }, plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const TM = { plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const HANDOVERS_PANE = pane('tm-handovers')
const WIDE = { ...HANDOVERS_PANE, props: { ...HANDOVERS_PANE.props, bodyColumns: 140 } }
const SAMPLE = demoSnapshot(0).handovers
const [FIRST, SECOND, THIRD] = SAMPLE as readonly [TmHandover, TmHandover, TmHandover]
const OPEN_ID = '2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022'
const AUDIT_ID = '2026-10-04-audit-fixes-landed'
type Ui = { findAll: (q: { type: string }) => Promise<{ key?: string; props: Record<string, unknown> }[]> }
const cardKeys = async (ui: Ui) =>
  (await ui.findAll({ type: 'Box' })).map(b => b.key ?? '').filter(key => key.startsWith('card:'))
const writes = (world: World) => world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_handover_update_status')
const textsIn = (tree: unknown): string[] =>
  elementsOf(tree)
    .filter(e => e.type === 'Text')
    .map(e => (e.children ?? []).join(''))

describe('handovers pane, demo', () => {
  test('/tm-handovers opens it: five cards, the first focused, the strip with a no task tab, nothing asked of tm', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    expect(await $.command.run(command('tm-handovers'))).toEqual({ text: 'Handovers opened.' })
    expect(world.opened).toEqual(['tm-handovers'])
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    expect(await cardKeys(ui)).toEqual(SAMPLE.map(x => `card:${x.id}`))
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: `card:${FIRST.id}` }))?.props.borderStyle).toBe('round')
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: `phase-tab:${NO_TASK}` }))?.props.label).toBe('no task 2')
    expect(world.calls).toEqual([])
  })

  test('a phase tab narrows the cards to the handovers its tasks are in; 0 goes back to All', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'phase-tab:release-2' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await cardKeys(ui)).toEqual(['card:2026-10-02-store-read-hang'])
    await ui.press({ key: 'phase-tab:' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await cardKeys(ui)).toHaveLength(5)
  })

  test('c copies the Telegram block of the focused handover; r fills the resume line and keeps the pane', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...HANDOVERS_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: `ho:${SECOND.id}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'copy' })
    expect(world.copies).toEqual([handoverCopyText(SECOND)])
    await ui.press({ key: 'resume' })
    expect(world.fills).toEqual([resumePrompt(SECOND.id, SECOND.path)])
    expect(world.closed).toEqual([])
  })

  test('w hands Stage 2 a fresh launch, which demo only names', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...HANDOVERS_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'new-tab' })
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual([`Demo: would open handover ${FIRST.id}`])
  })

  test('c copies a handover only the pane lists (closed, past the snapshot window) in the same Telegram block', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'show-closed' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'page-next' })
    await ui.redraw()
    await ui.press({ key: 'copy' })
    const closed = demoHandoverList(0).handovers.find(x => x.id === '2026-09-30-row-counter-ported')!
    expect(world.copies).toEqual([handoverCopyText(closed)])
  })

  test('s selects the focused card, Close 2 asks y/n, y closes both with one toast; the cards leave', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: `ho-sel:${FIRST.id}` })
    await ui.press({ key: `ho:${SECOND.id}` })
    await ui.press({ key: `ho-sel:${SECOND.id}` })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'close-selected' }))?.props.label).toBe('Close 2')
    await ui.press({ key: 'close-selected' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' Close 2 handovers? ' })).toBeDefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-close-yes' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual(['2 closed'])
    expect(await cardKeys(ui)).toEqual([THIRD, ...SAMPLE.slice(3)].map(x => `card:${x.id}`))
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'close-selected' }))?.props.label).toBe('Close 0')
  })

  test('x asks on the card, n keeps it; ◐ closed shows closed ones dimmed with x dim on them', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'close' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'close-yes' })).toBeDefined()
    await ui.press({ key: 'close-no' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'close-yes' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(await cardKeys(ui)).toHaveLength(5)
    await ui.press({ key: 'show-closed' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'page-next' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await cardKeys(ui)).toEqual(['card:2026-09-30-row-counter-ported', 'card:2026-09-29-store-spike-notes'])
    expect(textsIn(await ui.drawn())).toContain(' CLOSED ')
    await ui.press({ key: 'close' })
    expect(world.toasts).toEqual(['Already closed'])
  })

  test('j moves the focus down a card and across the page; k back up', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'move-down' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: `card:${SECOND.id}` }))?.props.borderStyle).toBe('round')
    await ui.press({ key: 'move-up' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Box', key: `card:${FIRST.id}` }))?.props.borderStyle).toBe('round')
  })
})

describe('handovers pane, tm', () => {
  test('opening reads the phases, the no task row, the list past the index and the linked tasks, one call at a time', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await clock.settle()
    const before = world.calls.length
    await $.command.run(command('tm-handovers'))
    await clock.settle()
    expect(world.calls.slice(before).map(c => c.args)).toEqual([
      { sql: phaseSql('handovers', true), limit: 200 },
      { sql: NO_TASK_SQL, limit: 200 },
      { format: 'json', status: 'open', limit: HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT, include_archived: true },
      { sql: linkedTasksSql(['unified-chat-022', 'tm-audit-030']), limit: 500 },
    ])
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: `ho:${AUDIT_ID}` })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(textsIn(await ui.drawn())).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([' IN PROGRESS ', 'tm-audit-030', 'Agent tool-use audit fixes', 'Patch 2.0.6']))
  })

  test('r fills the resume line and records this session as resumed from it', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-handovers'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'resume' })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(world.fills).toEqual([resumePrompt(OPEN_ID, handoverPath(world.root, OPEN_ID))])
    expect(world.store.has(`resumed-from:${world.sessionId}`)).toBe(true)
  })

  test('x then y closes with backlog_handover_update_status, toasts, refreshes and reads the list again', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = (tool, args) => (tool === 'backlog_handover_update_status' ? { text: R.handoverClosed(String(args.handover_id)) } : R.backlog(tool, args))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-handovers'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    const lists = world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_list_tasks').length
    const reads = world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_handover_list').length
    await ui.press({ key: 'close' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'close-yes' })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(writes(world).map(c => c.args)).toEqual([{ handover_id: OPEN_ID, status: 'closed', reason: CLOSE_REASON }])
    expect(world.toasts).toContain('1 closed')
    expect(world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_list_tasks').length).toBeGreaterThan(lists)
    expect(world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_handover_list').length).toBeGreaterThan(reads + 1)
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'close-yes' })).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('a refused close keeps the card and the selection, and the toast carries the server wording', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = (tool, args) => (tool === 'backlog_handover_update_status' ? { text: `Handover not found: ${String(args.handover_id)}` } : R.backlog(tool, args))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-handovers'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: `ho-sel:${OPEN_ID}` })
    await ui.press({ key: 'close-selected' })
    await ui.press({ key: 'bulk-close-yes' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(world.toasts).toContain(`0 closed · 1 refused: ${OPEN_ID} (Handover not found: ${OPEN_ID})`)
    expect(await cardKeys(ui)).toContain(`card:${OPEN_ID}`)
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'close-selected' }))?.props.label).toBe('Close 1')
  })

  test('◐ closed reads the closed handovers once; u refreshes the snapshot and reads the pane again', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-handovers'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'show-closed' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_handover_list').map(c => c.args.status)).toEqual(['open', 'open', 'open', 'closed'])
    expect(await cardKeys(ui)).toContain('card:2026-10-02-row-counter-ported')
    const before = world.calls.length
    await ui.press({ key: 'refresh' })
    await clock.settle()
    const after = world.calls.slice(before)
    expect(after.some(c => c.tool === 'backlog_list_tasks')).toBe(true)
    expect(after.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_handover_list' && c.args.limit === HANDOVER_PANE_LIMIT).map(c => c.args.status)).toEqual(['open', 'closed'])
  })

  test('all selects the open handovers of this page only; b is Close N', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = (tool, args) => (tool === 'backlog_handover_list' ? { text: R.handoversOpen(7) } : R.backlog(tool, args))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-handovers'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'select-all' })
    await ui.redraw()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'close-selected' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'b', label: 'Close 5' })
  })

  test('a phase choice is kept per project for the handovers pane', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.mcp = R.backlog
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-handovers'))
    await clock.settle()
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...WIDE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'phase-next' })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(world.store.get(phaseKey('handovers', world.root))).toEqual({ phase: '2-0-4' })
  })
})
```

Replace the whole `on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: 'tm-handovers' }, …)` hook (from that line through its closing `  })`) with:

```tsx
  on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: 'tm-handovers' }, async ($, e) => {
    const { Box, Text } = $.ui.resolve(e)
    if (e.surface === 'mobile') return <Text>Open the handovers in the terminal or the desktop app.</Text>
    await $.state.get(RR_POLARITY)
    const { snapshot, summaries } = await dataOf($)
    const now = await $.clock.now()
    const stored = await read($, HANDOVER_LIST)
    const views = await read($, PHASE_VIEWS)
    const demo = mod.source === 'demo'
    // Demo draws its list and phases before the seed lands, as dataOf draws its snapshot (a render writes nothing).
    const view: HandoversView = {
      snapshot,
      list: demo && stored === null ? demoHandoverList(now) : stored,
      marks: await read($, HANDOVER_MARKS),
      phaseView: demo && !views.handovers.loaded ? { ...views.handovers, phases: demoPhases('handovers'), loaded: true } : views.handovers,
      pick: await read($, PICK),
      page: await read($, HANDOVER_PAGE),
      summaries,
      summaryOpen: await read($, SUMMARY_OPEN),
      now,
      focused: e.props.isFocused,
      connecting: mod.source === 'tm' && (await read($, FAULT)) === 'connecting',
    }
    // The open summary is read again when a refresh marked it stale (the toggle reads it the first time); the stale one
    // stays drawn meanwhile.
    if (mod.source === 'tm' && snapshot !== null && snapshot.reachable && view.summaryOpen !== '') {
      const shown = phaseHandovers(listedHandovers(view.list, snapshot, view.marks.showClosed), view.phaseView.choice, view.list?.tasks ?? {})
      const picked = handoverPage(shown, view.page, view.pick).picked
      const held = picked === undefined ? undefined : summaries[picked.id]
      if (picked !== undefined && picked.id === view.summaryOpen && (held === undefined || held.stale === true)) askSummary(picked.id)
    }
    const ui = $.ui.resolve(e) as unknown as Ui
    const k = await kitOf(ui, rrOf($), e.surface, text => mod.host?.toast(text))
    const ground = await $.rr.surfaceProps({ level: 'page' })
    const tree = handoversPaneTree(
      k,
      view,
      {
        pick: id => act(f => f.pick(id)),
        move: (page, id) => act(f => f.moveTo(page, id)),
        turnPage: (page, firstId) => act(f => f.turnPage(page, firstId)),
        select: id => act(f => f.selectHandover(id)),
        selectOnly: ids => act(f => f.selectHandovers(ids)),
        toggleSummary: (handover, open) => act(f => f.toggleSummary(handover, open)),
        resume: handover =>
          act(async f => {
            if (await f.resumeHandover(handover)) await f.recordResumed(handover.id)
          }),
        // Stage 2's launch only names what it would open in demo.
        newTab: (handover, surface) =>
          act(async f => {
            await f.launch([await f.handoverLaunch(handover)], surface)
          }),
        copy: (handover, surface) => act(f => f.copyHandover(handover.id, surface)),
        askClose: ask => act(f => f.askClose(ask)),
        confirmClose: () => act(f => f.confirmClose()),
        cancelClose: () => act(f => f.cancelClose()),
        toggleClosed: () => act(f => f.toggleClosed()),
        // u: Stage 3's refresh (snapshot and this pane's phases), then the pane's own list.
        refresh: () =>
          act(async f => {
            await f.refresh('handovers')
            await f.loadHandovers()
          }),
        strip: {
          choose: id => act(f => f.choosePhase('handovers', id)),
          toggleDone: () => act(f => f.toggleDone('handovers')),
          overlay: open => act(f => f.setOverlay('handovers', open)),
        },
      },
      e.props.bodyColumns,
    )
    return (
      <Box {...ground} flexGrow={1}>
        {tree}
      </Box>
    )
  })
```

With taskmaster-mods' Data source on `demo` (`/config`), run `/tm-handovers`, give the pane the keys (ctrl+x tab or a click), and check:

1. **Toolbar:** `HANDOVERS`, `p ‹` and `n ›` dim on one page, `1 / 1`, `○ closed`, `u refresh`.
2. **Strip:** `0 All 6`, `1 Patch 1.9 · active 2`, `2 Release 2.0 1`, `3 Patch 1.8 ✓ 0` (dim), `4 no task 2`. `2` shows only "Store read hang root cause found". `4` shows the two that link no task. `0` returns to All.
3. **Cards:** the first card is bordered and expanded: tldr, the dim `id · branch` line, NEXT, the linked task `IN REVIEW unified-chat-022 … Patch 1.9`, then `r w c i x`. `j` / `k` move the border down and up and dim at the ends. The second card lists `DONE tm-audit-030` and `IN REVIEW tm-audit-031`. Hover changes only the fill. Check for no rail, no shift and no shadow.
4. **Keys on the focused card:**
   - `c`: a toast, then paste somewhere and check the three-line Telegram block (tldr, absolute path, `Resume: …`).
   - `r`: the prompt fills with `Resume from handover <id> (<path>)` and the pane stays.
   - `w`: a toast `Demo: would open handover <id>` (Stage 2's demo launch).
   - `i`: DECISIONS / BLOCKERS open above NEXT; `i` again closes them.
   - `x`: a y/n row with `n` focused; `n` keeps the handover.
5. **Bulk close:** `s` on two cards (`■`, one surface step up), then `b` (`Close 2`) → `Close 2 handovers?` → `y`: one toast `2 closed`, and the cards go. The band's `[2]` count drops by two. (Demo's tab counts stay as seeded; tm re-reads them after a close.)
6. **Closed:** `○ closed` → `◐ closed`: tabs count open + closed (`no task 3`, `Patch 1.8 ✓ 1`), page 2 shows the two closed ones dimmed with `CLOSED`, and `x` on them toasts "Already closed".
7. **Narrow width:** narrow the terminal to about 70 columns. Rows truncate, the card's keys wrap onto a second row, and nothing runs past the edge.

1. `/tm-handovers`: the cards fill from the server. The no task tab and the phase tabs carry counts, and a handover with tasks in two phases appears under both. The focused card's linked tasks show live status and phase names.
2. `◐ closed` shows closed handovers. More than 30 open handovers page beyond page 6 (the list now reads past the 30-entry index).
3. `x` + `y` on a handover the user is happy to close (or skip it): one toast, the card goes, the band count follows. A real `w` opens a new Windows Terminal tab whose prompt is the resume line (Stage 2's launcher).
4. From the band or the Session tab, `h` on the session's handover opens the pane under that handover's phase, focused on its card. `c` on a closed handover (under `◐ closed`) copies its block too.
5. Optional: repeat in `C:\Users\gruku\Files\Work\CodeMaestro` for the big store (27+ open handovers, 388 archived). Note whether opening the pane is quick. If the list read times out at 3 s there, record it: the pane keeps the snapshot's 30 and logs "handovers not read". Raising the bound is a follow-up, not part of this stage.

## Stage 5: Restore pane

**Goal.** After a burst of sessions closes, `/tm-restore` (and the band's `4 N restore` keycap) shows which of this repo's sessions closed together, which were unfinished by Taskmaster's word, and reopens them in one press: fresh from their handover, or `--resume` (spec §10). It is cm-restore's core, rebuilt: the scanner reports transcript facts only, one `backlog_query` gives the status, tasks and phases, and the pane is drawn with the Stage 1 kit and phase strip.

**Verified before writing.** Every task below was replayed in order on a copy of both mods with Stages 0–4 applied and green (401 plugin tests, `DEMO_SEED` 7). Every anchor quotes that code. After each task's test step the new tests failed. After its implementation step, `claude plugin test`, `claude plugin validate --strict`, `tsc` and the scanner's pytest were green, and the replayed tree matched the verified one byte for byte. Test counts read "401 + N" at each task's end: Stage 5 adds 76 plugin tests (477 in all) and 10 pytest tests.

**Flaky timeouts are load, not bugs.** While several agents ran `claude plugin test` at once, unrelated old tests sometimes failed with `timed out after 5000 ms`. A rerun on a quiet machine was green each time. If a run shows only such timeouts, run it again before you debug.

**Shell, paths and checks** are Stage 0's: `WT=C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/taskmaster/.worktrees/mods-ux`, never `cd`, and the mod checks below after every implementation step. From Task 5.1 on, the checks include the scanner's pytest:

```bash
WT=C:/Users/gruku/Files/Claude/taskmaster/.worktrees/mods-ux
claude plugin test $WT/mods/taskmaster-mods
claude plugin validate --strict $WT/mods/taskmaster-mods
npx -y -p typescript@5.6.3 tsc -p $WT/mods/taskmaster-mods
python -m pytest $WT/mods/taskmaster-mods/scanner -q
```

**What Stage 5 consumes** (and nothing else):

- Stage 1: `kitOf`, `keycap`, `checkbox`, `badge`, `signal`, `row`, `card`, `panel`, `panelTitle`, `toolbar`, `pager`, `selectionBar`, `helpFooter`, `phaseSection`, `phaseEmpty`, `HOTKEY`, `KitRr`, `Ui`, `StripView`, `StripHandlers`, `BulkAction`, `Ghost` (kit.tsx); `NO_TASK`, `withCounts`, `FRESH_PHASE_VIEW(S)`, `PhaseStore` (phases.ts); `TmFlows.loadPhases`, `choosePhase`, `toggleDone` and `setOverlay` with pane `'restore'`; `demoPhases('restore')`; `phaseSql('restore')` (read by `loadPhases`).
- Stage 3: `TmFlows.refresh(pane: TmPhasePane)`. Task 5.7 gives it a first case for `'restore'`: the restore flows rescan, then the strip's phases are read again. Stage 3's bulk-key convention applies too: `b` is the primary bulk action, `g` the secondary, and `all` ticks the current page only.
- Stage 2: `LaunchSpec`, `LaunchResult`, `resumePrompt` (hooks/launch.ts); `RESTORE` (hooks/model.ts, `'tm-restore'`); `TmFlows.launch(specs, surface?)`, which in demo opens nothing and toasts `Demo: would open …`; `TmFlows.openPane('restore')` (band and Session `4`); the band's `restoreHidden` / `restore-hidden:<epoch>`, which it handles itself; `recordResumedFrom`, `readResumedFrom` (hooks/resumed.ts); the types `TmRestorePending` and `TmSessionBurst`, and the PluginState keys `restorePending` and `sessionBurst`. Stage 5 writes both keys; register.tsx's `RESTORE_PENDING` and `SESSION_BURST` atoms read them for the band and the Session tab.

**What Stage 5 produces** (every signature final; the code is in the tasks):

`scanner/scan.py`: `scan --config-dir DIR --root DIR [--days N] [--exclude ID] [--cache FILE]` prints `{"v": 1, "scannedAt", "root", "sessions": [TmScannedSession…]}`, newest close first. `digest --config-dir DIR --session ID [--max-chars N]` prints `{"first", "tail"}` or `{"error"}`.

`types/index.d.ts`: `TmScannedHandover`, `TmScannedSession`, `TmScan`, `TmRefTask`, `TmRefs`, `TmRestoreData`, `TmResumeMode`, `TmRestoreView`, and PluginState keys `restoreData` and `restoreView`.

`hooks/restore/model.ts` (pure):
```ts
export type RestoreStatus = 'interrupted' | 'wip' | 'unknown' | 'handed-off' | 'done'
export const STATUS_ORDER, STATUS_LABEL, STATUS_TONE, TASK_TONE
export const DEFAULT_DAYS = 4, SIGNAL_DAYS = 3, BURST_GAP_MIN = 10, RESTORE_PAGE_SIZE = 5
export const CTX_WARN = 400_000, CTX_HIGH = 700_000, PENDING_WITHIN_S = 48 * 3600
export const FRESH_RESTORE_DATA: TmRestoreData, FRESH_RESTORE_VIEW: TmRestoreView
export type RestoreRow = { s; status; why; boundId; task: TmRefTask | null; phase; handover: TmScannedHandover | null; handoverStatus }
export type RestoreBurst = { ids; from; to; open }
export type RestorePage = { ids; from; to; isBurst; burstSize; since }
export function scanOf(value: unknown): TmScan | null
export function refsSql(scan: TmScan): string | null
export function parseRefs(raw: string): Parsed<TmRefs>
export function classify(s: TmScannedSession, refs: TmRefs | null): { status: RestoreStatus; why: string }
export function rowOf(s, refs): RestoreRow;  rowsOf(scan, refs): RestoreRow[];  paneRows(data: TmRestoreData, self: string): RestoreRow[]
export function phaseCounts(rows): Map<string, number>          // feeds withCounts(phases, counts)
export function burstsOf(rows, gapMin?): RestoreBurst[];  defaultBurst(bursts): number
export function pagesOf(rows, bursts, choice, size?): RestorePage[];  pageRows(rows, page): RestoreRow[];  pageHeader(page, now): string
export function runningRows(rows): RestoreRow[];  blockedWhy(row): string;  inPhase(row, choice): boolean
export function isTicked(row, selected, opened): boolean;  resumeModeOf(row, modes): TmResumeMode;  detailOf(row): { label; text }
export function launchSpecOf(row, mode, compact, root): LaunchSpec;  copyTextOf(row, mode, root): string
export function briefKey(row): string;  briefPrompt(row, digest): string;  briefText(reply): string
export function restorePendingOf(bursts, now: number): TmRestorePending | null      // spec §10.4
export function sessionBurstOf(bursts, rows, sessionId, resumedFrom: string | null): TmSessionBurst | null
export function hhmm, dayLabel, when, spanLabel, ago, formatTokens, ctxTone, plain
```

`hooks/restore/scan.ts`: `RestoreHost`, `RestorePaths`, `RunOut`, `Digest`, `runScan(rh, { root, days, exclude })`, `runDigest(rh, sessionId)`, `resolveRefs(tm, scan): Promise<TmRefs | null>`, `PYTHONS`, `SCAN_TIMEOUT_MS`, `DIGEST_TIMEOUT_MS`.

`hooks/restore/flows.ts`: `createRestoreFlows(d: RestoreDeps)` returning `open(days): Promise<string>`, `rescan()`, `startScan()`, `turnPage(page)`, `focus(id)`, `toggle(id)`, `select(changes)`, `toggleMode(id)`, `toggleCompact()`, `resume(ids)`, `copy(id)`, `toggleBrief(id)`, `brief(ids)`, plus `RestoreDeps`, `RestoreWriter`, `RESOLVE_RETRIES`, `RESOLVE_RETRY_MS` and `SCANNING`.

`hooks/restore/draw.tsx`: `restorePaneTree(k: Kit, v: RestorePaneView, on: RestoreHandlers, width: number): RenderNode`, plus `RestorePaneView` and `RestoreHandlers`.

`hooks/restore/hooks.tsx`: `onRestore(on: On, options: PluginOptions, link: RestoreLink): void`, `RestoreLink = { flows: () => TmFlows | null }`, `refreshRestore(): Promise<void>` (wired into `TmFlowDeps.refreshRestore`, so `TmFlows.refresh('restore')` rescans) and `BRIEF_MODEL = 'claude-haiku-5-5'`. It registers `/tm-restore [days]` (requestId `RESTORE`), its pane, `ui.press` and `ui.focus` on that pane, and the interactive `session.start` scan that writes `restorePending` and `sessionBurst`.

`hooks/demo.ts`: `DEMO_REFS`, `DEMO_BRIEF`, `demoRestore(now)`, and `DEMO_SEED = 8`. userConfig `briefModel` (default `claude-haiku-5-5`). Test fixtures: `tests/fixtures/restore.ts` (`ROOT`, `T0`, `session`, `scanOf`, `NO_REFS`, `refsReply`, `fakeRestore`), and `world.answer` (a whole `$.process.run` answer) in `tests/fixtures/world.ts`, beside Stage 2's `runs`, `runExit` and `env`.

**File ownership.** The contract lists `hooks/restore/{model.ts, draw.tsx, scan.ts}` and `scanner/`. This stage also creates `hooks/restore/flows.ts`, `hooks/restore/hooks.tsx` and `tests/fixtures/restore.ts`, all in places only Stage 5 writes. Shared files get small anchored edits: `types/index.d.ts`, `hooks/tm.ts` (`callTm` takes a narrower host), `hooks/demo.ts`, `hooks/flows.ts` (`refresh`'s restore case; `TmFlowDeps.refreshRestore`), `hooks/register.tsx` (three lines), `.claude-plugin/plugin.json`, `tests/fixtures/world.ts` (`answer`), and `tests/handover-data.test.ts`, whose `DEMO_SEED` check now allows 8.

**Why a module of its own.** `claude plugin validate` refuses `$` passed across an import (probed: "$ is followed only into a function declared in this same file, never across an import"). So the restore hooks cannot borrow register.tsx's `ready($)` or `hostOf($)`. Like `handover-guard.ts`, `hooks/restore/hooks.tsx` holds its own atoms and builds its deps from its own `$`. Its `restorePending` and `sessionBurst` atoms sit beside register.tsx's `RESTORE_PENDING` and `SESSION_BURST` on the same keys, which validates. register.tsx lends it only `() => mod.flows`. The restore pane's presses still reach register.tsx's plugin-wide `ui.press` hook, which builds `mod.flows` first, so `launch` and the strip's phase flows are there when a key is pressed.

**Spec points this stage settles** (record them in the spec when Task 5.8 signs off):

1. **Facts in Python, judgement in TypeScript.** The scanner reports facts only. Bursts, classification, paging and ticks are pure TS (`restore/model.ts`), because classification needs tm (§10.2).
2. **Task ids** come from `task_id` and `task_ids` arguments of the main thread's `mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__*` calls, kept in last-touched order. The bound task is the last one. Sidechain calls are ignored. **Handover ids** come from `Handover written: <id>` in a successful `backlog_handover_create` result (plain, `{"result": …}`-wrapped, or a text block), with `- Path:` when given. Its `tldr` and `next_action` come from the call's input.
3. **End-session** means the `taskmaster:end-session` Skill call or the `/taskmaster:end-session` slash command. A prompt typed after it counts in `promptsAfterEnd`.
4. **Which handover counts:** the session's last one written. A `superseded` handover counts as settled, like `closed`.
5. **tm unreachable:** only the end-session rule can be told, so every other status reads `?`. For ticks and the band, `?` counts as unfinished: resume still works (§11). The session-start scan asks tm three more times, 5 s apart, before it settles for `?`.
6. **Scope:** sessions whose first working directory lies under the repo root. Worktrees under it are included. `claude -p` runs (`entrypoint` `sdk-*`) and sibling folders with the same prefix are excluded. This session is scanned, for its burst line, but never listed.
7. **Bursts** take running sessions that closed before at their last close (a `--resume` keeps the id), so a resumed session still knows its burst. A session that never closed is in no burst.
8. **Paging under a phase:** that phase's sessions, newest first, with no burst page. Choosing a phase returns to page 1.
9. **The fresh / --resume toggle** is offered on any session with a handover. `◐ compact` applies to `--resume` only. **Copy** puts `cd "<cwd>"; claude --resume <id>` (or `claude "<fresh prompt>"`) on the clipboard.
10. **A launch that opened fewer tabs than asked** marks none reopened, so the ticks stay for another try.
11. **Keys** (the Stage 0 set and Stage 3's bulk convention, plus these): card `s` tick, `r` resume, `m` fresh / --resume, `i` brief, `c` copy; bulk `b` Resume N (primary), `g` Brief N (secondary), with all · unfinished · none over the current page; `z` ◐ compact; `u` rescan (through `TmFlows.refresh('restore')`); `j` / `k` move; `p` / `n` page; and the strip's `h` / `l`, `f` and `0`–`9`. Only the focused card carries the card keys. Tab onto a card's box focuses it.

### Task 5.1: The scanner (`scanner/scan.py`) and its pytest

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/scanner/scan.py`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/scanner/test_scan.py`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/scanner/fixtures/{interrupted,ended,ended-then-more,handed-off,worktree,sdk-run,elsewhere}.jsonl`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Claude Code's transcripts (`<config>/projects/<encoded cwd>/<session id>.jsonl`) and its live-session registry (`<config>/sessions/<pid>.json`), read-only.
- Produces: the `scan` and `digest` subcommands above. The JSON keys are exactly `TmScannedSession`'s fields (Task 5.2 types them). The cache file is `{"v": 1, "files": {path: {"sig", "s"}}}`.

The fixture transcripts were written for these tests from the record shapes in real transcripts (types, fields, `{"result": …}`-wrapped tool results, `entrypoint`, `isSidechain`, cost-state). No real content was copied. `{{ROOT}}`, `{{WORKTREE}}` and `{{SIBLING}}` are replaced JSON-escaped by the test; `{{ROOT_IN_RESULT}}` sits inside a doubly encoded result, so it takes the root with forward slashes.

```python
# User intent: prove the restore scanner reads only this repo's sessions and reports the transcript facts the restore pane
# classifies by (interrupt, end-session with or without prompts after, tm task and handover ids, running, cache), using
# small transcripts authored for these tests, never real ones.
from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import subprocess
import sys
import time
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
sys.path.insert(0, str(HERE))
import scan  # noqa: E402

FIXTURES = HERE / "fixtures"
SCRIPT = HERE / "scan.py"
IN_REPO = ["interrupted", "ended", "ended-then-more", "handed-off", "sdk-run"]


class World:
    def __init__(self, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
        self.root = tmp_path / "repo"
        self.worktree = self.root / ".worktrees" / "delta"
        self.sibling = tmp_path / "repo-sibling"
        for d in (self.worktree, self.sibling):
            d.mkdir(parents=True)
        self.cfg = tmp_path / "cfg"
        self.cache = tmp_path / "tmp" / "tm-restore-scan-cache.json"
        for name in IN_REPO:
            self.lay(name, self.root)
        self.lay("worktree", self.worktree)
        self.lay("elsewhere", self.sibling)

    def lay(self, name: str, cwd: Path) -> Path:
        text = (FIXTURES / f"{name}.jsonl").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        for token, path in (("{{ROOT}}", self.root), ("{{WORKTREE}}", self.worktree), ("{{SIBLING}}", self.sibling)):
            text = text.replace(token, json.dumps(str(path))[1:-1])
        text = text.replace("{{ROOT_IN_RESULT}}", self.root.as_posix())
        folder = self.cfg / "projects" / scan.encode_dir(str(cwd))
        folder.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        out = folder / f"{name}.jsonl"
        out.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
        return out

    def transcript(self, name: str) -> Path:
        return next((self.cfg / "projects").glob(f"*/{name}.jsonl"))

    def scan(self, capsys, *extra: str) -> dict:
        assert scan.main(["scan", "--config-dir", str(self.cfg), "--root", str(self.root), *extra]) == 0
        return json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)

    def by_id(self, capsys, *extra: str) -> dict:
        return {s["id"]: s for s in self.scan(capsys, *extra)["sessions"]}


@pytest.fixture
def world(tmp_path: Path) -> World:
    return World(tmp_path)


def test_only_this_repos_interactive_sessions_are_read(world, capsys):
    # sdk-run is a `claude -p` run; elsewhere lives in a sibling folder whose name starts like the repo's.
    assert set(world.by_id(capsys)) == {"interrupted", "ended", "ended-then-more", "handed-off", "worktree"}


def test_an_interrupt_is_the_last_event(world, capsys):
    s = world.by_id(capsys)["interrupted"]
    assert s["lastEvent"] == "interrupt"
    assert s["interrupted"] is True
    assert s["taskIds"] == ["alpha-001"]
    assert s["ctx"] == 125012
    assert s["cost"] == 1.23
    assert s["closedEpoch"] == (1791622790000 + 20000) / 1000
    assert s["hasClosed"] is True
    assert (s["title"], s["branch"], s["prompts"]) == ("Port the row counter", "feat/rows", 1)
    assert s["lastPrompt"] == "Port the row counter to the new store"


def test_end_session_with_nothing_after_it(world, capsys):
    s = world.by_id(capsys)["ended"]
    assert (s["endedSession"], s["promptsAfterEnd"], s["interrupted"]) == (True, 0, False)
    assert s["lastEvent"] == "end_turn"
    # Last touched last: beta-002 was read again after beta-001 was updated, so it is the bound task.
    assert s["taskIds"] == ["beta-001", "beta-002"]
    assert s["title"] == "Sign off the migration"
    assert s["lastReply"] == "Session closed out: beta-002 is in review."


def test_end_session_by_command_then_another_prompt(world, capsys):
    s = world.by_id(capsys)["ended-then-more"]
    assert (s["endedSession"], s["promptsAfterEnd"]) == (True, 1)
    assert s["prompts"] == 2  # the slash command itself is not a prompt
    assert s["hasClosed"] is False
    assert s["closedEpoch"] == scan.iso_to_epoch("2026-10-10T11:02:05.000Z")
    assert s["title"] == "Write the README section"


def test_handover_ids_come_from_create_results(world, capsys):
    sessions = world.by_id(capsys)
    gamma = sessions["handed-off"]
    assert gamma["handovers"] == [
        {
            "id": "2026-10-10-gamma-wrap",
            "path": f"{world.root.as_posix()}/.taskmaster/handovers/2026-10-10-gamma-wrap.md",
            "tldr": "Gamma parser half done",
            "next": "Finish the error rows, then run the suite",
        }
    ]
    assert gamma["taskIds"] == ["gamma-003"]  # the sidechain's side-999 is not this session's
    assert gamma["ctx"] == 452003
    # A plain-text receipt without a path still names the id; an errored call names nothing.
    delta = sessions["worktree"]
    assert [(h["id"], h["path"]) for h in delta["handovers"]] == [("2026-10-10-delta-wait", "")]
    assert delta["cwd"] == str(world.worktree)


def test_running_comes_from_a_live_pid_file(world, capsys):
    dead = subprocess.Popen([sys.executable, "-c", "pass"])
    dead.wait()
    reg = world.cfg / "sessions"
    reg.mkdir(parents=True)
    (reg / f"{os.getpid()}.json").write_text(json.dumps({"pid": os.getpid(), "sessionId": "worktree"}), encoding="utf-8")
    (reg / f"{dead.pid}.json").write_text(json.dumps({"pid": dead.pid, "sessionId": "ended"}), encoding="utf-8")
    (reg / "junk.json").write_text("not json", encoding="utf-8")
    sessions = world.by_id(capsys)
    assert sessions["worktree"]["running"] is True
    assert sessions["ended"]["running"] is False


def test_the_days_window_and_exclude(world, capsys):
    old = time.time() - 10 * 86400
    os.utime(world.transcript("ended"), (old, old))
    ids = set(world.by_id(capsys, "--days", "4", "--exclude", "interrupted"))
    assert ids == {"ended-then-more", "handed-off", "worktree"}


def test_unchanged_files_come_from_the_cache(world, capsys, monkeypatch):
    calls: list[str] = []
    real = scan.scan_file

    def counting(path: Path) -> dict:
        calls.append(path.stem)
        return real(path)

    monkeypatch.setattr(scan, "scan_file", counting)
    first = world.by_id(capsys, "--cache", str(world.cache))
    assert len(calls) == 7  # every transcript in the repo's folders, kept or not
    calls.clear()
    assert world.by_id(capsys, "--cache", str(world.cache)) == first
    assert calls == []
    with world.transcript("ended").open("a", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        fh.write("\n")
    world.by_id(capsys, "--cache", str(world.cache))
    assert calls == ["ended"]
    world.cache.write_text(json.dumps({"v": scan.CACHE_VERSION + 1, "files": {}}), encoding="utf-8")
    calls.clear()
    world.by_id(capsys, "--cache", str(world.cache))
    assert len(calls) == 7


def test_digest_keeps_prompts_prose_and_collapsed_tools(world, capsys):
    assert scan.main(["digest", "--config-dir", str(world.cfg), "--session", "ended"]) == 0
    out = json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out)
    assert out["first"] == "USER: Pick up beta-002"
    assert out["tail"].splitlines() == [
        "USER: Pick up beta-002",
        "TOOL: mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__backlog_claim",
        "ASSISTANT: Session closed out: beta-002 is in review.",
    ]
    assert scan.main(["digest", "--config-dir", str(world.cfg), "--session", "nope"]) == 0
    assert json.loads(capsys.readouterr().out) == {"error": "session not found"}


def test_the_script_runs_isolated_and_needs_a_root(world):
    ok = subprocess.run(
        [sys.executable, "-I", str(SCRIPT), "scan", "--config-dir", str(world.cfg), "--root", str(world.root)],
        capture_output=True, text=True, encoding="utf-8", check=False,
    )
    assert ok.returncode == 0, ok.stderr
    assert len(json.loads(ok.stdout)["sessions"]) == 5
    refused = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-I", str(SCRIPT), "scan", "--config-dir", str(world.cfg)], capture_output=True, text=True, check=False)
    assert refused.returncode == 2


def test_a_truncated_last_line_and_bad_bytes_are_skipped(world, capsys):
    # A session killed mid-write leaves a cut last line; a bad byte must not sink the whole transcript.
    with world.transcript("interrupted").open("ab") as fh:
        fh.write(b'ÿþ not json
{"type":"user","message":{"role":"us')
    s = world.by_id(capsys)["interrupted"]
    assert s["lastEvent"] == "interrupt"
    assert s["prompts"] == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Write the scanner**

`mods/taskmaster-mods/scanner/scan.py`:

```python
# User intent: tell the restore pane what each of this repo's recent Claude Code sessions did — transcript facts only
# (prompts, last event, tm task ids and handover ids, end-session) — so Taskmaster, not a regex, says what is unfinished.
"""Session scanner for taskmaster-mods' restore pane (spec §10.1).

  scan.py scan   --config-dir DIR --root DIR [--days N] [--exclude ID] [--cache FILE]
  scan.py digest --config-dir DIR --session ID [--max-chars N]

Prints one JSON document on stdout. Transcripts are streamed line by line (they run past the mod runtime's 4 MiB read
cap) and per-file facts are cached by mtime + size. Only sessions whose working directory lies under --root are read.
"""
from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import json
import os
import re
import sys
import time
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path

CACHE_VERSION = 1
TM_TOOL = "mcp__plugin_taskmaster_tm__"
HANDOVER_TOOL = TM_TOOL + "backlog_handover_create"
END_SESSION_SKILL = "taskmaster:end-session"
END_SESSION_COMMAND = "<command-name>/taskmaster:end-session</command-name>"
INTERRUPT_MARK = "[Request interrupted by user"
HANDOVER_WRITTEN = re.compile(r"Handover written: (\S+)")
HANDOVER_PATH = re.compile(r"^- Path: (.+?)\s*$", re.MULTILINE)
MAX_TASK_IDS = 8
# A closed session's last event: anything but the agent's own finished reply means it was cut off mid-turn.
MID_TURN = ("interrupt", "prompt", "tool_use", "tool_result")


def iso_to_epoch(value: str | None) -> float | None:
    if not value:
        return None
    try:
        return datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00")).timestamp()
    except ValueError:
        return None


def clip(text: str | None, limit: int) -> str:
    if not text:
        return ""
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip()
    return text if len(text) <= limit else text[: limit - 1] + "…"


def text_of(content) -> str:
    if isinstance(content, str):
        return content
    if isinstance(content, list):
        return "\n".join(b.get("text", "") for b in content if isinstance(b, dict) and b.get("type") == "text")
    return ""


def result_text(content) -> str:
    """A tool result's text, a `{"result": "<text>"}` wrapper (the tm server's output schema) unwrapped."""
    text = content if isinstance(content, str) else text_of(content)
    stripped = text.strip()
    if stripped.startswith("{") and '"result"' in stripped:
        try:
            value = json.loads(stripped)
        except ValueError:
            return text
        if isinstance(value, dict) and isinstance(value.get("result"), str):
            return value["result"]
    return text


def encode_dir(path: str) -> str:
    """Claude Code's project folder name for a working directory: every non-alphanumeric character becomes `-`."""
    return re.sub(r"[^A-Za-z0-9]", "-", path)


def is_under(path: str | None, root: str) -> bool:
    if not path:
        return False
    p = os.path.normcase(os.path.normpath(path))
    r = os.path.normcase(os.path.normpath(root))
    return p == r or p.startswith(r.rstrip(os.sep) + os.sep)


def is_human_prompt(rec: dict) -> bool:
    if rec.get("type") != "user" or rec.get("isMeta") or rec.get("isSidechain"):
        return False
    if rec.get("toolUseResult") is not None or rec.get("sourceToolUseID"):
        return False
    content = (rec.get("message") or {}).get("content")
    if isinstance(content, list) and any(isinstance(b, dict) and b.get("type") == "tool_result" for b in content):
        return False
    text = text_of(content).strip()
    if not text:
        return False
    # Engine-injected wrappers: command echoes, reminders, teammate and task notifications, the Esc marker.
    return not text.startswith(("<", "Caveat:", "Another Claude session sent a message", INTERRUPT_MARK))


def touch(task_ids: list[str], value) -> None:
    """Keeps the ids in last-touched order: the last one is the session's bound task (spec §10.2)."""
    for tid in value if isinstance(value, list) else [value]:
        if isinstance(tid, str) and tid.strip():
            tid = tid.strip()
            if tid in task_ids:
                task_ids.remove(tid)
            task_ids.append(tid)


def scan_file(path: Path) -> dict:
    s: dict = {
        "id": path.stem, "cwd": None, "branch": None, "entrypoint": None, "aiTitle": None, "customTitle": None,
        "firstPrompt": None, "lastPrompt": None, "lastReply": None, "lastEvent": None, "prompts": 0,
        "ctx": 0, "cost": 0.0, "start": None, "end": None, "costEnd": None,
        "taskIds": [], "handovers": [], "endedSession": False, "promptsAfterEnd": 0,
    }
    pending: dict[str, dict] = {}
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as fh:
        for line in fh:
            try:
                rec = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if not isinstance(rec, dict):
                continue
            t = rec.get("type")
            if t == "ai-title":
                s["aiTitle"] = rec.get("aiTitle")
                continue
            if t == "custom-title":
                s["customTitle"] = rec.get("customTitle")
                continue
            if t == "cost-state":
                s["cost"] = rec.get("totalCostUSD") or 0.0
                if rec.get("startTime") and rec.get("totalDuration") is not None:
                    # Written as the process exits: start + wall-clock duration = when it closed.
                    s["costEnd"] = (rec["startTime"] + rec["totalDuration"]) / 1000
                continue
            if t not in ("user", "assistant") or rec.get("isSidechain"):
                continue
            ts = rec.get("timestamp")
            if ts:
                s["start"] = s["start"] or ts
                s["end"] = ts
            s["cwd"] = s["cwd"] or rec.get("cwd")
            s["entrypoint"] = s["entrypoint"] or rec.get("entrypoint")
            if rec.get("gitBranch"):
                s["branch"] = rec["gitBranch"]
            msg = rec.get("message") or {}
            content = msg.get("content")
            if t == "user":
                if END_SESSION_COMMAND in text_of(content):
                    s["endedSession"] = True
                    s["promptsAfterEnd"] = 0
                if is_human_prompt(rec):
                    txt = text_of(content)
                    s["firstPrompt"] = s["firstPrompt"] or clip(txt, 300)
                    s["lastPrompt"] = clip(txt, 400)
                    s["prompts"] += 1
                    s["lastEvent"] = "prompt"
                    if s["endedSession"]:
                        s["promptsAfterEnd"] += 1
                if isinstance(content, list):
                    for b in content:
                        if not isinstance(b, dict) or b.get("type") != "tool_result":
                            continue
                        s["lastEvent"] = "tool_result"
                        h = pending.pop(b.get("tool_use_id"), None)
                        if h is None or b.get("is_error"):
                            continue
                        res = result_text(b.get("content"))
                        written = HANDOVER_WRITTEN.search(res)
                        if written:
                            where = HANDOVER_PATH.search(res)
                            s["handovers"].append(dict(h, id=written.group(1), path=where.group(1) if where else ""))
                if text_of(content).lstrip().startswith(INTERRUPT_MARK):
                    s["lastEvent"] = "interrupt"
                continue
            usage = msg.get("usage") or {}
            ctx = sum(int(usage.get(k) or 0) for k in ("input_tokens", "cache_read_input_tokens", "cache_creation_input_tokens"))
            if ctx > 0:
                # What the last request carried: the context a resume starts from.
                s["ctx"] = ctx
            for b in content if isinstance(content, list) else []:
                if not isinstance(b, dict):
                    continue
                bt = b.get("type")
                if bt == "text" and b.get("text", "").strip():
                    s["lastReply"] = clip(b["text"], 600)
                    s["lastEvent"] = "text"
                elif bt == "tool_use":
                    s["lastEvent"] = "tool_use"
                    name = b.get("name") or ""
                    inp = b.get("input") if isinstance(b.get("input"), dict) else {}
                    if name == "Skill" and inp.get("skill") == END_SESSION_SKILL:
                        s["endedSession"] = True
                        s["promptsAfterEnd"] = 0
                    if name.startswith(TM_TOOL):
                        touch(s["taskIds"], inp.get("task_id"))
                        touch(s["taskIds"], inp.get("task_ids"))
                    if name == HANDOVER_TOOL:
                        pending[b.get("id")] = {"tldr": clip(inp.get("tldr"), 200), "next": clip(inp.get("next_action"), 300)}
            if msg.get("stop_reason") == "end_turn" and s["lastEvent"] == "text":
                s["lastEvent"] = "end_turn"
    s["taskIds"] = s["taskIds"][-MAX_TASK_IDS:]
    return s


def live_sessions(cfg: Path) -> set[str]:
    """Session ids whose Claude Code process is still alive, from <config>/sessions/<pid>.json."""
    reg = cfg / "sessions"
    if not reg.is_dir():
        return set()
    by_pid: dict[int, str] = {}
    for f in reg.glob("*.json"):
        try:
            d = json.loads(f.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
            by_pid[int(d["pid"])] = d["sessionId"]
        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
            continue
    return {sid for pid, sid in by_pid.items() if pid_alive(pid)}


def pid_alive(pid: int) -> bool:
    # Per-pid probe: tasklist can take 10 s and more, and a timeout used to mark every session closed.
    if os.name == "nt":
        import ctypes

        k32 = ctypes.windll.kernel32
        handle = k32.OpenProcess(0x1000, False, pid)  # PROCESS_QUERY_LIMITED_INFORMATION
        if not handle:
            return False
        try:
            code = ctypes.c_ulong()
            return bool(k32.GetExitCodeProcess(handle, ctypes.byref(code))) and code.value == 259  # STILL_ACTIVE
        finally:
            k32.CloseHandle(handle)
    try:
        os.kill(pid, 0)
        return True
    except PermissionError:
        return True
    except OSError:
        return False


def load_cache(path: Path | None) -> dict:
    if path is None or not path.is_file():
        return {}
    try:
        cache = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    except (OSError, ValueError):
        return {}
    return cache.get("files", {}) if isinstance(cache, dict) and cache.get("v") == CACHE_VERSION else {}


def facts(s: dict, mtime: float, live: set[str]) -> dict:
    end = iso_to_epoch(s["end"]) or 0
    return {
        "id": s["id"],
        "cwd": s["cwd"],
        "cwdExists": Path(s["cwd"]).is_dir(),
        "branch": s["branch"] or "",
        "title": s["customTitle"] or s["aiTitle"] or clip(s["firstPrompt"], 70) or s["id"][:8],
        "firstPrompt": s["firstPrompt"] or "",
        "lastPrompt": s["lastPrompt"] or "",
        "lastReply": s["lastReply"] or "",
        "lastEvent": s["lastEvent"] or "",
        "interrupted": s["lastEvent"] in MID_TURN,
        "prompts": s["prompts"],
        "ctx": s["ctx"],
        "cost": round(float(s["cost"] or 0), 2),
        # Background monitors append to transcripts after exit, so mtime is the last resort for the close time.
        "closedEpoch": s["costEnd"] or end or mtime,
        "hasClosed": s["costEnd"] is not None,
        "running": s["id"] in live,
        "resumedAfterClose": s["costEnd"] is not None and end > s["costEnd"] + 60,
        "taskIds": s["taskIds"],
        "handovers": s["handovers"],
        "endedSession": s["endedSession"],
        "promptsAfterEnd": s["promptsAfterEnd"],
    }


def scan(args) -> dict:
    cfg = Path(args.config_dir)
    projects = cfg / "projects"
    root = os.path.normpath(args.root)
    prefix = encode_dir(root).lower()
    cutoff = time.time() - args.days * 86400
    cache_path = Path(args.cache) if args.cache else None
    entries = load_cache(cache_path)
    fresh: dict = {}
    found: list[tuple[dict, float]] = []
    if projects.is_dir():
        for pdir in projects.iterdir():
            # The folder name is the encoded working directory: anything under the root starts with the root's encoding.
            if not pdir.is_dir() or not pdir.name.lower().startswith(prefix):
                continue
            for f in pdir.glob("*.jsonl"):
                st = f.stat()
                if st.st_mtime < cutoff or f.stem == args.exclude:
                    continue
                key = str(f)
                sig = f"{st.st_mtime_ns}:{st.st_size}"
                hit = entries.get(key)
                s = hit["s"] if isinstance(hit, dict) and hit.get("sig") == sig else scan_file(f)
                fresh[key] = {"sig": sig, "s": s}
                if s["prompts"] > 0 and is_under(s["cwd"], root) and not str(s["entrypoint"] or "").startswith("sdk"):
                    found.append((s, st.st_mtime))
    if cache_path:
        try:
            cache_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
            cache_path.write_text(json.dumps({"v": CACHE_VERSION, "files": fresh}), encoding="utf-8")
        except OSError:
            pass
    live = live_sessions(cfg)
    sessions = sorted((facts(s, mtime, live) for s, mtime in found), key=lambda x: -x["closedEpoch"])
    return {"v": CACHE_VERSION, "scannedAt": time.time(), "root": root, "sessions": sessions}


def digest(args) -> dict:
    """The condensed tail of one transcript (human prompts and assistant prose), for a model brief."""
    path = next((Path(args.config_dir) / "projects").glob(f"*/{args.session}.jsonl"), None)
    if path is None:
        return {"error": "session not found"}
    rows: list[str] = []
    with path.open(encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as fh:
        for line in fh:
            try:
                rec = json.loads(line)
            except ValueError:
                continue
            if not isinstance(rec, dict) or rec.get("isSidechain"):
                continue
            if is_human_prompt(rec):
                rows.append("USER: " + clip(text_of(rec["message"].get("content")), 1200))
            elif rec.get("type") == "assistant":
                for b in (rec.get("message") or {}).get("content") or []:
                    if isinstance(b, dict) and b.get("type") == "text" and b.get("text", "").strip():
                        rows.append("ASSISTANT: " + clip(b["text"], 1500))
                    elif isinstance(b, dict) and b.get("type") == "tool_use":
                        rows.append(f"TOOL: {b.get('name')}")
    out: list[str] = []
    for r in rows:
        if r.startswith("TOOL:") and out and out[-1].startswith("TOOL:"):
            continue
        out.append(r)
    text, used = [], 0
    for r in reversed(out):
        if used + len(r) > args.max_chars:
            break
        text.append(r)
        used += len(r)
    head = next((r for r in out if r.startswith("USER:")), "")
    return {"first": head, "tail": "\n".join(reversed(text))}


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    ap = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    ap.add_argument("mode", choices=["scan", "digest"])
    ap.add_argument("--config-dir", required=True)
    ap.add_argument("--root", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--days", type=float, default=4)
    ap.add_argument("--exclude", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--cache", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--session", default="")
    ap.add_argument("--max-chars", type=int, default=24000)
    args = ap.parse_args(argv)
    if args.mode == "scan" and not args.root:
        ap.error("scan needs --root")
    result = scan(args) if args.mode == "scan" else digest(args)
    sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    json.dump(result, sys.stdout, ensure_ascii=False)
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    raise SystemExit(main())
```

### Task 5.2: Status from tm: the scan types, one `backlog_query`, the classification

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/types/index.d.ts` (after `TmHandoverGuard`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/tm.ts` (`bounded` and `callTm` take `Pick<TmHost, 'call' | 'sleep'>`; every caller still passes a whole `TmHost`)
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/model.ts`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/scan.ts`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/fixtures/restore.ts`, `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-model.test.ts`, `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-scan.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `callTm`, `Parsed`, `isRefusal`, `firstParagraph`, `NO_TASK`, `RrTone`.
- Produces: the scan types; `scanOf`, `refsSql`, `parseRefs`, `classify`, `rowOf`, `rowsOf`, `phaseCounts`, `RestoreRow`, `RestoreStatus`, `STATUS_*`; and `runScan`, `runDigest`, `resolveRefs`, `RestoreHost`.

The query's row shape keeps every cell under the server's 80-character cut: a task is `["t",id,status,lane,phase]` with its title in the second column, and a handover is `["h",id,status]`. Ids go into the SQL as literals only if they match `^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,120}$`, so a transcript can never shape the query. `backlog_query`'s guard allows `UNION ALL`, which taskmaster/query_guard.py checks: one SELECT, no write keywords.
