```ts
// User intent: small, readable restore-pane inputs for tests — a scanned session with every fact defaulted, a scan, and
// backlog_query reply tables shaped as the server prints them — so each test states only the facts it is about.
import type { TmRefs, TmScan, TmScannedSession } from '../../types'

export const ROOT = 'C:\\work\\proj'
/** 2026-10-10 16:31:00 UTC, the demo burst's last close; tests pin `now` from it. */
export const T0 = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 10, 16, 31) / 1000

export function session(id: string, over: Partial<TmScannedSession> = {}): TmScannedSession {
  return {
    id,
    cwd: ROOT,
    cwdExists: true,
    branch: 'main',
    title: `Session ${id}`,
    firstPrompt: `start ${id}`,
    lastPrompt: `keep going on ${id}`,
    lastReply: `Done with step 2 of ${id}.`,
    lastEvent: 'end_turn',
    interrupted: false,
    prompts: 4,
    ctx: 120_000,
    cost: 3,
    closedEpoch: T0,
    hasClosed: true,
    running: false,
    resumedAfterClose: false,
    taskIds: [],
    handovers: [],
    endedSession: false,
    promptsAfterEnd: 0,
    ...over,
  }
}

export const scanOf = (sessions: readonly TmScannedSession[]): TmScan => ({ scannedAt: T0, root: ROOT, sessions })

export const NO_REFS: TmRefs = { tasks: {}, handovers: {} }

/** A refsSql reply: the header, one padded line a row, the footer. */
export function refsReply(rows: readonly string[]): string {
  return ['ref                                           title', ...rows, `${rows.length} ${rows.length === 1 ? 'row' : 'rows'}`].join('\n')
}
```

```ts
// User intent: prove the restore pane calls a session unfinished by Taskmaster's word (spec §10.2) — the scanner's JSON is
// read safely, one query resolves every id it found, and the status order is DONE > HANDED OFF > INTERRUPTED > WIP.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { NO_TASK } from '../hooks/phases'
import { classify, parseRefs, phaseCounts, refsSql, rowOf, scanOf } from '../hooks/restore/model'
import { NO_REFS, refsReply, ROOT, scanOf as scanWith, session } from './fixtures/restore'

const REFS = {
  tasks: {
    'a-1': { id: 'a-1', status: 'done', lane: 'full', phase: 'p1', title: 'Old work' },
    'b-2': { id: 'b-2', status: 'in-progress', lane: 'full', phase: 'p2', title: 'Current work' },
    'c-3': { id: 'c-3', status: 'todo', lane: '', phase: '', title: 'Phaseless' },
  },
  handovers: { 'h-open': 'open', 'h-closed': 'closed', 'h-old': 'superseded' },
}
const handover = (id: string) => ({ id, path: `${ROOT}\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\${id}.md`, tldr: `tldr ${id}`, next: `next ${id}` })

describe('reading the scanner', () => {
  test('a session missing fields takes empty values; a non-session or a non-scan is dropped', () => {
    const scan = scanOf({ scannedAt: 5, root: ROOT, sessions: [{ id: 'x', taskIds: ['a-1', 3, ''], handovers: [{ id: 'h' }, {}] }, { title: 'no id' }, 7] })
    expect(scan?.sessions).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(scan?.sessions[0]).toMatchObject({ id: 'x', title: 'x', cwd: '', ctx: 0, running: false, taskIds: ['a-1'] })
    expect(scan?.sessions[0]?.handovers).toEqual([{ id: 'h', path: '', tldr: '', next: '' }])
    expect(scanOf({ sessions: 'no' })).toBeNull()
    expect(scanOf(null)).toBeNull()
  })
})

describe('the one backlog_query', () => {
  test('asks for every task and handover id once, and only ids that look like ids', () => {
    const scan = scanWith([
      session('s1', { taskIds: ['a-1', 'b-2'], handovers: [handover('h-open')] }),
      session('s2', { taskIds: ["b-2", "x'); DROP TABLE entities; --"], handovers: [handover('h-closed')] }),
    ])
    expect(refsSql(scan)).toBe(
      "SELECT json_array('t', e.id, e.status, coalesce(json_extract(e.doc,'$.lane'),''), coalesce(json_extract(e.doc,'$.phase'),'')) AS ref, json_extract(e.doc,'$.title') AS title FROM entities e WHERE e.kind='task' AND e.deleted=0 AND e.id IN ('a-1','b-2')" +
        " UNION ALL SELECT json_array('h', e.id, e.status) AS ref, '' AS title FROM entities e WHERE e.kind='handover' AND e.deleted=0 AND e.id IN ('h-open','h-closed')",
    )
    expect(refsSql(scanWith([session('s3', { handovers: [handover('h-open')] })]))).toMatch(/^SELECT json_array\('h'/)
    expect(refsSql(scanWith([session('s4')]))).toBeNull()
  })

  test('its table reads into tasks and handover statuses; a refusal or a cut table does not read', () => {
    const reply = refsReply([
      '["t","b-2","in-progress","full","p2"]          Current work',
      '["t","c-3","todo","",""]                       Phaseless',
      '["h","h-open","open"]',
      '["t","broken"',
    ])
    expect(parseRefs(reply)).toEqual({
      ok: true,
      value: {
        tasks: {
          'b-2': { id: 'b-2', status: 'in-progress', lane: 'full', phase: 'p2', title: 'Current work' },
          'c-3': { id: 'c-3', status: 'todo', lane: '', phase: '', title: 'Phaseless' },
        },
        handovers: { 'h-open': 'open' },
      },
    })
    expect(parseRefs('Error: forbidden keyword DROP: this tool is read-only\n\nSchema: …')).toEqual({
      ok: false,
      reason: 'backlog_query: Error: forbidden keyword DROP: this tool is read-only',
    })
    expect(parseRefs('ref  title\n["h","h-open","open"]').ok).toBe(false)
  })
})

describe('classification (spec §10.2, first match wins)', () => {
  const cases: readonly [string, Parameters<typeof session>[1], string, string][] = [
    ['end-session with nothing after wins over an open handover', { endedSession: true, handovers: [handover('h-open')], interrupted: true }, 'done', 'ran end-session'],
    ['the bound task is done', { taskIds: ['b-2', 'a-1'] }, 'done', 'a-1 is done'],
    ['an earlier task being done does not count', { taskIds: ['a-1', 'b-2'] }, 'wip', 'no end-session and no open handover'],
    ['its last handover was closed', { handovers: [handover('h-open'), handover('h-closed')] }, 'done', 'handover h-closed is closed'],
    ['its last handover was superseded', { handovers: [handover('h-old')] }, 'done', 'handover h-old is superseded'],
    ['an open handover wins over an interrupt', { handovers: [handover('h-open')], interrupted: true }, 'handed-off', 'left handover h-open open'],
    ['closed mid-turn', { interrupted: true, handovers: [handover('h-gone')] }, 'interrupted', 'closed mid-turn: the agent was still working'],
    ['kept working after end-session', { endedSession: true, promptsAfterEnd: 2 }, 'wip', 'kept working after end-session'],
    ['nothing said', {}, 'wip', 'no end-session and no open handover'],
  ]
  for (const [name, over, status, why] of cases) {
    test(name, () => {
      expect(classify(session('s', over), REFS)).toEqual({ status, why })
    })
  }

  test('without tm only end-session can be told; the rest read ?', () => {
    expect(classify(session('s', { endedSession: true }), null).status).toBe('done')
    expect(classify(session('s', { interrupted: true }), null)).toEqual({ status: 'unknown', why: 'Taskmaster not reached: status unknown' })
  })
})

describe('rows and phase counts', () => {
  test('a row carries its bound task, phase and last handover status', () => {
    expect(rowOf(session('s', { taskIds: ['a-1', 'b-2'], handovers: [handover('h-open')] }), REFS)).toMatchObject({
      status: 'handed-off',
      boundId: 'b-2',
      task: { id: 'b-2', phase: 'p2' },
      phase: 'p2',
      handover: { id: 'h-open' },
      handoverStatus: 'open',
    })
    expect(rowOf(session('s', { taskIds: ['c-3'] }), REFS).phase).toBe(NO_TASK)
    expect(rowOf(session('s', { taskIds: ['zz-9'] }), REFS)).toMatchObject({ boundId: 'zz-9', task: null, phase: NO_TASK })
    expect(rowOf(session('s', { taskIds: ['b-2'], handovers: [handover('h-open')] }), null)).toMatchObject({ task: null, phase: NO_TASK, handoverStatus: '' })
  })

  test('phases count the closed sessions by bound task; running ones and other ids count nowhere', () => {
    const rows = [
      session('s1', { taskIds: ['b-2'] }),
      session('s2', { taskIds: ['b-2'] }),
      session('s3', { taskIds: ['b-2'], running: true }),
      session('s4', { taskIds: ['c-3'] }),
      session('s5'),
    ].map(s => rowOf(s, REFS))
    expect([...phaseCounts(rows)]).toEqual([
      ['p2', 2],
      [NO_TASK, 2],
    ])
    expect(phaseCounts(rows.map(r => rowOf(r.s, NO_REFS))).get(NO_TASK)).toBe(4)
  })
})
```

`mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-scan.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: prove the restore pane's outside readers fail to a reason, never a crash — scan.py under the first Python
// that runs, isolated, with this repo's root; the digest for a brief; one backlog_query that may be refused or slow.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import type { TmReply } from '../hooks/host'
import { type RestoreHost, resolveRefs, runDigest, runScan, type RunOut } from '../hooks/restore/scan'
import { refsReply, ROOT, scanOf, session } from './fixtures/restore'

const PATHS = { configDir: 'C:\\Users\\me\\.claude', cache: 'C:\\tmp\\tm-restore-scan-cache.json', script: 'C:\\mods\\taskmaster-mods/scanner/scan.py' }

function hostOf(answer: (argv: readonly string[]) => RunOut | Error): { rh: RestoreHost; runs: (readonly string[])[] } {
  const runs: (readonly string[])[] = []
  return {
    runs,
    rh: {
      paths: async () => PATHS,
      run: async argv => {
        runs.push(argv)
        const out = answer(argv)
        if (out instanceof Error) throw out
        return out
      },
    },
  }
}

const ok = (value: unknown): RunOut => ({ exitCode: 0, stdout: JSON.stringify(value), stderr: '' })

function tmOf(reply: (args: Record<string, unknown>) => TmReply) {
  const calls: Record<string, unknown>[] = []
  const logs: string[] = []
  return {
    calls,
    logs,
    tm: {
      call: async (tool: string, args: Record<string, unknown>) => {
        calls.push({ tool, ...args })
        return reply(args)
      },
      sleep: () => new Promise<void>(() => undefined),
      log: (text: string) => void logs.push(text),
    },
  }
}

describe('runScan', () => {
  test('runs the script isolated with the repo root, the window, the cache and this session left out', async () => {
    const { rh, runs } = hostOf(() => ok({ scannedAt: 1, root: ROOT, sessions: [session('a')] }))
    const scan = await runScan(rh, { root: ROOT, days: 3, exclude: 'me' })
    expect(runs).toEqual([
      ['python', '-I', PATHS.script, 'scan', '--config-dir', PATHS.configDir, '--root', ROOT, '--days', '3', '--cache', PATHS.cache, '--exclude', 'me'],
    ])
    expect(scan.sessions.map(s => s.id)).toEqual(['a'])
  })

  test('falls through python, python3 and py; the reason is the last failure', async () => {
    const tried = hostOf(argv =>
      argv[0] === 'python' ? new Error('spawn python ENOENT') : argv[0] === 'python3' ? { exitCode: 1, stdout: '', stderr: 'Traceback\nValueError: bad root\n' } : ok({ sessions: [] }),
    )
    expect((await runScan(tried.rh, { root: ROOT, days: 4, exclude: '' })).sessions).toEqual([])
    expect(tried.runs.map(argv => argv[0])).toEqual(['python', 'python3', 'py'])
    expect(tried.runs[2]).not.toContain('--exclude')
    const none = hostOf(argv => (argv[0] === 'py' ? { exitCode: 9, stdout: '', stderr: '' } : new Error('spawn ENOENT')))
    await expect(runScan(none.rh, { root: ROOT, days: 4, exclude: '' })).rejects.toThrow('py exited 9')
  })

  test('output that is not a scan is an error', async () => {
    await expect(runScan(hostOf(() => ({ exitCode: 0, stdout: 'oops', stderr: '' })).rh, { root: ROOT, days: 4, exclude: '' })).rejects.toThrow(
      'the scanner printed no JSON',
    )
    await expect(runScan(hostOf(() => ok({ nope: 1 })).rh, { root: ROOT, days: 4, exclude: '' })).rejects.toThrow('the scanner answered no session list')
  })
})

describe('runDigest', () => {
  test('reads the first prompt and the tail; a missing session is an error', async () => {
    const found = hostOf(() => ok({ first: 'USER: start', tail: 'USER: start\nASSISTANT: done' }))
    expect(await runDigest(found.rh, 'abc')).toEqual({ first: 'USER: start', tail: 'USER: start\nASSISTANT: done' })
    expect(found.runs[0]).toEqual(['python', '-I', PATHS.script, 'digest', '--config-dir', PATHS.configDir, '--session', 'abc'])
    await expect(runDigest(hostOf(() => ok({ error: 'session not found' })).rh, 'zz')).rejects.toThrow('session not found')
  })
})

describe('resolveRefs', () => {
  test('nothing to ask resolves empty without a call', async () => {
    const { tm, calls } = tmOf(() => ({ text: '', isError: false }))
    expect(await resolveRefs(tm, scanOf([session('a')]))).toEqual({ tasks: {}, handovers: {} })
    expect(calls).toEqual([])
  })

  test('one backlog_query, its table read', async () => {
    const { tm, calls } = tmOf(() => ({ text: refsReply(['["t","b-2","in-review","full","p2"]  Sign off', '["h","h-1","closed"]']), isError: false }))
    const refs = await resolveRefs(tm, scanOf([session('a', { taskIds: ['b-2'], handovers: [{ id: 'h-1', path: '', tldr: '', next: '' }] })]))
    expect(refs).toEqual({ tasks: { 'b-2': { id: 'b-2', status: 'in-review', lane: 'full', phase: 'p2', title: 'Sign off' } }, handovers: { 'h-1': 'closed' } })
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(calls[0]).toMatchObject({ tool: 'backlog_query', limit: 500 })
  })

  test('an error reply or a refusal is null, with the reason logged', async () => {
    const scan = scanOf([session('a', { taskIds: ['b-2'] })])
    const failing = tmOf(() => ({ text: 'store locked', isError: true }))
    expect(await resolveRefs(failing.tm, scan)).toBeNull()
    expect(failing.logs).toEqual(['taskmaster-mods: restore refs not read: backlog_query: store locked'])
    const refused = tmOf(() => ({ text: 'Error: no such column: e.lane\n\nSchema: …', isError: false }))
    expect(await resolveRefs(refused.tm, scan)).toBeNull()
    expect(refused.logs).toEqual(['taskmaster-mods: restore refs unreadable: backlog_query: Error: no such column: e.lane'])
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

In `mods/taskmaster-mods/types/index.d.ts`, replace:

```ts
export type TmHandoverGuard = { readonly lastTurnEnd: number | null; readonly latch: 'none' | 'fired' | 'handover' }
```

with:

```ts
export type TmHandoverGuard = { readonly lastTurnEnd: number | null; readonly latch: 'none' | 'fired' | 'handover' }
/** A handover a scanned session wrote: its backlog_handover_create input and the server's receipt (spec §10.1). */
export type TmScannedHandover = { readonly id: string; readonly path: string; readonly tldr: string; readonly next: string }
/** One of this repo's sessions as scanner/scan.py reports it: transcript facts only (spec §10.1). */
export type TmScannedSession = {
  readonly id: string
  readonly cwd: string
  readonly cwdExists: boolean
  readonly branch: string
  readonly title: string
  readonly firstPrompt: string
  readonly lastPrompt: string
  readonly lastReply: string
  readonly lastEvent: string
  /** Closed mid-turn: an interrupt, or a prompt or tool call the agent never answered. */
  readonly interrupted: boolean
  readonly prompts: number
  /** The context its last request carried: what a --resume reopens. */
  readonly ctx: number
  readonly cost: number
  /** Epoch seconds the process exited (its cost-state), else its last record's time. */
  readonly closedEpoch: number
  /** It wrote a cost-state: it closed at least once (a running one was resumed since). */
  readonly hasClosed: boolean
  /** Open in a terminal now (its pid file names a live process). */
  readonly running: boolean
  readonly resumedAfterClose: boolean
  /** The tm task ids its own tool calls named, last touched last: the last is its bound task (spec §10.2). */
  readonly taskIds: readonly string[]
  readonly handovers: readonly TmScannedHandover[]
  readonly endedSession: boolean
  readonly promptsAfterEnd: number
}
export type TmScan = { readonly scannedAt: number; readonly root: string; readonly sessions: readonly TmScannedSession[] }
/** A scanned task as one backlog_query read it (spec §10.2): `phase` '' when it has none. */
export type TmRefTask = { readonly id: string; readonly status: string; readonly lane: string; readonly phase: string; readonly title: string }
/** What tm said of the scanned ids: tasks by id, handover statuses by id; an id tm does not hold is absent. */
export type TmRefs = { readonly tasks: Readonly<Record<string, TmRefTask>>; readonly handovers: Readonly<Record<string, string>> }
/**
 * The restore pane's data: the last scan (null before the first), what tm said of it (null: not resolved, so statuses read
 * `?`), why the last scan failed ('' when it did not), and the window it covered, in days.
 */
export type TmRestoreData = { readonly scan: TmScan | null; readonly refs: TmRefs | null; readonly error: string; readonly days: number }
export type TmResumeMode = 'fresh' | 'resume'
/**
 * The restore pane's own state: the page and focused card, explicit ticks and resume modes (absent: the default rule),
 * when each session was reopened, `◐ compact`, the busy line, and per-card busy flags and briefs.
 */
export type TmRestoreView = {
  readonly page: number
  readonly focus: string
  readonly selected: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>
  readonly modes: Readonly<Record<string, TmResumeMode>>
  readonly opened: Readonly<Record<string, number>>
  readonly compact: boolean
  readonly busy: string
  readonly launching: readonly string[]
  readonly briefing: readonly string[]
  readonly briefs: Readonly<Record<string, string>>
}
```

`mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/model.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: what the restore pane knows about a closed session, as plain functions (spec §10, §10.2) — the scanner's
// facts read safely, the one backlog_query that resolves their task and handover ids, and the status Taskmaster gives
// them — so a session is called unfinished by tm's word, never by a regex.
import type { TmRefs, TmRefTask, TmScan, TmScannedHandover, TmScannedSession } from '../../types'
import { firstParagraph, isRefusal, type Parsed } from '../parse'
import { NO_TASK } from '../phases'
import type { RrTone } from '../rr'

export type RestoreStatus = 'interrupted' | 'wip' | 'unknown' | 'handed-off' | 'done'
/** The burst page's order (spec §10): the most urgent first; `?` (tm not reached) sits with the unfinished. */
export const STATUS_ORDER: readonly RestoreStatus[] = ['interrupted', 'wip', 'unknown', 'handed-off', 'done']
export const STATUS_LABEL: Readonly<Record<RestoreStatus, string>> = {
  interrupted: 'interrupted',
  wip: 'wip',
  unknown: '?',
  'handed-off': 'handed off',
  done: 'done',
}
export const STATUS_TONE: Readonly<Record<RestoreStatus, RrTone>> = {
  interrupted: 'critical',
  wip: 'warning',
  unknown: 'signature',
  'handed-off': 'info',
  done: 'success',
}
const DONE_TASK = new Set(['done', 'archived'])
const SETTLED_HANDOVER = new Set(['closed', 'superseded'])

/** A closed session as the pane draws it: its facts, tm's status for it, its bound task and phase, its last handover. */
export type RestoreRow = {
  readonly s: TmScannedSession
  readonly status: RestoreStatus
  readonly why: string
  /** The last task id it touched ('' none): its bound task. */
  readonly boundId: string
  /** The bound task as tm holds it (null: none, or tm not reached, or tm does not hold it). */
  readonly task: TmRefTask | null
  /** The bound task's phase id; NO_TASK when there is none to name. */
  readonly phase: string
  /** The last handover it wrote (null: none). */
  readonly handover: TmScannedHandover | null
  /** That handover's status in tm ('' unknown). */
  readonly handoverStatus: string
}

const str = (v: unknown): string => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : '')
const num = (v: unknown): number => (typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v) ? v : 0)
const isObject = (v: unknown): v is Record<string, unknown> => typeof v === 'object' && v !== null && !Array.isArray(v)

function handoverOf(v: unknown): TmScannedHandover | null {
  if (!isObject(v) || str(v.id) === '') return null
  return { id: str(v.id), path: str(v.path), tldr: str(v.tldr), next: str(v.next) }
}

function sessionOf(v: unknown): TmScannedSession | null {
  if (!isObject(v) || str(v.id) === '') return null
  return {
    id: str(v.id),
    cwd: str(v.cwd),
    cwdExists: v.cwdExists === true,
    branch: str(v.branch),
    title: str(v.title) || str(v.id).slice(0, 8),
    firstPrompt: str(v.firstPrompt),
    lastPrompt: str(v.lastPrompt),
    lastReply: str(v.lastReply),
    lastEvent: str(v.lastEvent),
    interrupted: v.interrupted === true,
    prompts: num(v.prompts),
    ctx: num(v.ctx),
    cost: num(v.cost),
    closedEpoch: num(v.closedEpoch),
    hasClosed: v.hasClosed === true,
    running: v.running === true,
    resumedAfterClose: v.resumedAfterClose === true,
    taskIds: Array.isArray(v.taskIds) ? v.taskIds.filter((id): id is string => typeof id === 'string' && id !== '') : [],
    handovers: Array.isArray(v.handovers) ? v.handovers.map(handoverOf).filter((h): h is TmScannedHandover => h !== null) : [],
    endedSession: v.endedSession === true,
    promptsAfterEnd: num(v.promptsAfterEnd),
  }
}

/** The scanner's JSON, read field by field (a missing or mistyped field takes its empty value); null when it is no scan. */
export function scanOf(value: unknown): TmScan | null {
  if (!isObject(value) || !Array.isArray(value.sessions)) return null
  const sessions = value.sessions.map(sessionOf).filter((s): s is TmScannedSession => s !== null)
  return { scannedAt: num(value.scannedAt), root: str(value.root), sessions }
}

// Ids go into SQL as literals: only what a tm id can look like is sent, so a transcript can never shape the query.
const SAFE_ID = /^[A-Za-z0-9._:-]{1,120}$/
const literals = (ids: readonly string[]): string => [...new Set(ids.filter(id => SAFE_ID.test(id)))].map(id => `'${id}'`).join(',')

/**
 * The one backlog_query that resolves a scan (spec §10.2): a row per held task, `["t",id,status,lane,phase]` and its title,
 * and per held handover `["h",id,status]` (cells kept short of the server's 80-character cut). Null when there is nothing
 * to ask.
 */
export function refsSql(scan: TmScan): string | null {
  const tasks = literals(scan.sessions.flatMap(s => s.taskIds))
  const handovers = literals(scan.sessions.flatMap(s => s.handovers.map(h => h.id)))
  const parts = [
    ...(tasks === ''
      ? []
      : [
          `SELECT json_array('t', e.id, e.status, coalesce(json_extract(e.doc,'$.lane'),''), coalesce(json_extract(e.doc,'$.phase'),'')) AS ref, json_extract(e.doc,'$.title') AS title FROM entities e WHERE e.kind='task' AND e.deleted=0 AND e.id IN (${tasks})`,
        ]),
    ...(handovers === ''
      ? []
      : [`SELECT json_array('h', e.id, e.status) AS ref, '' AS title FROM entities e WHERE e.kind='handover' AND e.deleted=0 AND e.id IN (${handovers})`]),
  ]
  return parts.length === 0 ? null : parts.join(' UNION ALL ')
}

const REF_ROW = /^(\[\S*\])(?:\s+(.*))?$/
const ROWS_FOOTER = /^\d+ rows?(?: \(capped\))?$/

/** refsSql's table: a header, a row per held id (one whose cell does not read is left out), then `N rows`. */
export function parseRefs(raw: string): Parsed<TmRefs> {
  if (isRefusal(raw)) return { ok: false, reason: `backlog_query: ${firstParagraph(raw)}` }
  const lines = raw.replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').trim().split('\n')
  if (!ROWS_FOOTER.test((lines[lines.length - 1] ?? '').trim())) return { ok: false, reason: 'backlog_query: no "N rows" footer' }
  const tasks: Record<string, TmRefTask> = {}
  const handovers: Record<string, string> = {}
  for (const line of lines.slice(1, -1)) {
    const row = REF_ROW.exec(line.trim())
    if (row === null) continue
    let cells: unknown
    try {
      cells = JSON.parse(row[1] ?? '')
    } catch {
      continue
    }
    if (!Array.isArray(cells) || typeof cells[1] !== 'string' || cells[1] === '') continue
    if (cells[0] === 't') {
      tasks[cells[1]] = { id: cells[1], status: str(cells[2]), lane: str(cells[3]), phase: str(cells[4]), title: (row[2] ?? '').trim() }
    } else if (cells[0] === 'h') {
      handovers[cells[1]] = str(cells[2])
    }
  }
  return { ok: true, value: { tasks, handovers } }
}

/**
 * Spec §10.2, first match wins: DONE (end-session ran with no prompt after it, its bound task is done, or its last handover
 * was closed or superseded) → HANDED OFF (that handover is open) → INTERRUPTED (closed mid-turn) → WIP. Without tm only
 * the end-session rule can be told; anything else reads `?`.
 */
export function classify(s: TmScannedSession, refs: TmRefs | null): { readonly status: RestoreStatus; readonly why: string } {
  if (s.endedSession && s.promptsAfterEnd === 0) return { status: 'done', why: 'ran end-session' }
  if (refs === null) return { status: 'unknown', why: 'Taskmaster not reached: status unknown' }
  const bound = s.taskIds.at(-1)
  const task = bound === undefined ? undefined : refs.tasks[bound]
  if (task !== undefined && DONE_TASK.has(task.status)) return { status: 'done', why: `${task.id} is ${task.status}` }
  const handover = s.handovers.at(-1)
  const state = handover === undefined ? '' : (refs.handovers[handover.id] ?? '')
  if (handover !== undefined && SETTLED_HANDOVER.has(state)) return { status: 'done', why: `handover ${handover.id} is ${state}` }
  if (handover !== undefined && state === 'open') return { status: 'handed-off', why: `left handover ${handover.id} open` }
  if (s.interrupted) return { status: 'interrupted', why: 'closed mid-turn: the agent was still working' }
  return { status: 'wip', why: s.endedSession ? 'kept working after end-session' : 'no end-session and no open handover' }
}

export function rowOf(s: TmScannedSession, refs: TmRefs | null): RestoreRow {
  const { status, why } = classify(s, refs)
  const boundId = s.taskIds.at(-1) ?? ''
  const task = refs === null || boundId === '' ? null : (refs.tasks[boundId] ?? null)
  const handover = s.handovers.at(-1) ?? null
  return {
    s,
    status,
    why,
    boundId,
    task,
    phase: task !== null && task.phase !== '' ? task.phase : NO_TASK,
    handover,
    handoverStatus: handover === null || refs === null ? '' : (refs.handovers[handover.id] ?? ''),
  }
}

export function rowsOf(scan: TmScan | null, refs: TmRefs | null): RestoreRow[] {
  return scan === null ? [] : scan.sessions.map(s => rowOf(s, refs))
}

/** Restore's phase counts (spec §5): the closed sessions per bound task's phase, NO_TASK for the rest. */
export function phaseCounts(rows: readonly RestoreRow[]): Map<string, number> {
  const counts = new Map<string, number>()
  for (const row of rows) if (!row.s.running) counts.set(row.phase, (counts.get(row.phase) ?? 0) + 1)
  return counts
}
```

`mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/scan.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: the restore pane's two outside readers — scanner/scan.py run as its own process (transcripts outgrow the mod
// runtime's 4 MiB read) and the one backlog_query that resolves what it found — each failing to a reason, never a crash.
import type { TmRefs, TmScan } from '../../types'
import type { TmHost } from '../host'
import { callTm } from '../tm'
import { parseRefs, refsSql, scanOf } from './model'

export type RunOut = { readonly exitCode: number; readonly stdout: string; readonly stderr: string }
/** Where the scanner lives and what it reads: the config folder, its cache file, the script. */
export type RestorePaths = { readonly configDir: string; readonly cache: string; readonly script: string }
/** What the scan needs from the engine, bound once from `$` in hooks.tsx. */
export type RestoreHost = {
  readonly run: (argv: readonly string[], timeoutMs: number) => Promise<RunOut>
  readonly paths: () => Promise<RestorePaths>
}
export type Digest = { readonly first: string; readonly tail: string }

export const SCAN_TIMEOUT_MS = 120_000
export const DIGEST_TIMEOUT_MS = 60_000
/** Tried in order: the first that runs the script answers. */
export const PYTHONS: readonly string[] = ['python', 'python3', 'py']

/** Runs scan.py isolated (`-I`) under the first Python that starts; throws an Error naming why none answered. */
async function runPython(rh: RestoreHost, args: readonly string[], timeoutMs: number): Promise<unknown> {
  const { script } = await rh.paths()
  let reason = 'no Python found (python, python3, py)'
  for (const py of PYTHONS) {
    let out: RunOut
    try {
      out = await rh.run([py, '-I', script, ...args], timeoutMs)
    } catch (error) {
      reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
      continue
    }
    if (out.exitCode !== 0) {
      reason = out.stderr.trim().split('\n').at(-1)?.trim() || `${py} exited ${out.exitCode}`
      continue
    }
    try {
      return JSON.parse(out.stdout) as unknown
    } catch {
      throw new Error('the scanner printed no JSON')
    }
  }
  throw new Error(reason)
}

/** This repo's sessions of the last `days` days, less `exclude` (spec §10.1). */
export async function runScan(rh: RestoreHost, a: { readonly root: string; readonly days: number; readonly exclude: string }): Promise<TmScan> {
  const { configDir, cache } = await rh.paths()
  const args = ['scan', '--config-dir', configDir, '--root', a.root, '--days', String(a.days), '--cache', cache]
  const scan = scanOf(await runPython(rh, a.exclude === '' ? args : [...args, '--exclude', a.exclude], SCAN_TIMEOUT_MS))
  if (scan === null) throw new Error('the scanner answered no session list')
  return scan
}

/** One transcript's condensed tail for a brief; an Error names why it could not be read. */
export async function runDigest(rh: RestoreHost, sessionId: string): Promise<Digest> {
  const { configDir } = await rh.paths()
  const out = await runPython(rh, ['digest', '--config-dir', configDir, '--session', sessionId], DIGEST_TIMEOUT_MS)
  const v = typeof out === 'object' && out !== null ? (out as Record<string, unknown>) : {}
  if (typeof v.error === 'string') throw new Error(v.error)
  return { first: typeof v.first === 'string' ? v.first : '', tail: typeof v.tail === 'string' ? v.tail : '' }
}

/**
 * The scan's task and handover ids as tm holds them, from one backlog_query (spec §10.2). Nothing to ask resolves empty
 * without a call; a refusal, a timeout or an unreadable reply is null (statuses then read `?`), its reason logged.
 */
export async function resolveRefs(tm: Pick<TmHost, 'call' | 'sleep' | 'log'>, scan: TmScan): Promise<TmRefs | null> {
  const sql = refsSql(scan)
  if (sql === null) return { tasks: {}, handovers: {} }
  try {
    const text = (await callTm(tm, 'backlog_query', { sql, limit: 500 })).text
    const parsed = parseRefs(text)
    if (parsed.ok) return parsed.value
    tm.log(`taskmaster-mods: restore refs unreadable: ${parsed.reason}`)
    return null
  } catch (error) {
    tm.log(`taskmaster-mods: restore refs not read: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`)
    return null
  }
}
```

`mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-paging.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: prove the restore pane opens on the sessions closed together last time, unfinished first, ticks what should
// reopen and picks fresh or --resume sensibly (spec §10, cm-restore's pure helpers and tests, ported), and that the band's
// restore keycap and the Session tab's burst line follow the spec §10.4 rule.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import type { TmScannedSession } from '../types'
import { NO_TASK } from '../hooks/phases'
import {
  burstsOf,
  ctxTone,
  defaultBurst,
  detailOf,
  formatTokens,
  isTicked,
  pageHeader,
  pageRows,
  pagesOf,
  plain,
  restorePendingOf,
  type RestoreRow,
  type RestoreStatus,
  resumeModeOf,
  rowOf,
  runningRows,
  sessionBurstOf,
  spanLabel,
} from '../hooks/restore/model'
import { NO_REFS, session } from './fixtures/restore'

const row = (id: string, status: RestoreStatus, closedEpoch: number, over: Partial<TmScannedSession> = {}): RestoreRow => ({
  ...rowOf(session(id, { closedEpoch, ...over }), NO_REFS),
  status,
})
const HO = { id: 'h-1', path: 'C:\\p\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\h-1.md', tldr: 'Half done', next: 'Finish the **error rows**' }

// A lone session closed after a burst of three; the burst page keeps it rather than cost it a page of its own.
const ROWS: readonly RestoreRow[] = [
  row('solo', 'wip', 2000),
  row('a', 'done', 1000),
  row('b', 'interrupted', 990),
  row('c', 'wip', 980),
]

describe('bursts', () => {
  test('sessions closed within ten minutes of each other burst together, newest first; open counts the unfinished', () => {
    const bursts = burstsOf(ROWS)
    expect(bursts.map(b => b.ids)).toEqual([['solo'], ['a', 'b', 'c']])
    expect(bursts[1]).toMatchObject({ from: 980, to: 1000, open: 2 })
    expect(defaultBurst(bursts)).toBe(1)
    expect(defaultBurst(burstsOf([row('x', 'wip', 10)]))).toBe(0)
  })

  test('a running session that closed before keeps its burst place but is not open; one that never closed is in none', () => {
    const rows = [...ROWS, row('back', 'wip', 985, { running: true, hasClosed: true }), row('new', 'wip', 5000, { running: true, hasClosed: false })]
    const bursts = burstsOf(rows)
    expect(bursts.map(b => b.ids)).toEqual([['solo'], ['a', 'b', 'back', 'c']])
    expect(bursts[1]?.open).toBe(2)
    expect(runningRows(rows).map(r => r.s.id)).toEqual(['new', 'back'])
  })
})

describe('pages', () => {
  test('page 1 is the closing burst plus what closed after; older sessions page newest first, five a page', () => {
    // Twenty minutes apart: each older session is a burst of its own.
    const older = [5, 4, 3, 2, 1, 0, -1].map(n => row(`o${n}`, n % 2 ? 'done' : 'wip', -10_000 + n * 1200))
    const rows = [...ROWS, ...older]
    const pages = pagesOf(rows, burstsOf(rows), '')
    expect(pages.map(p => p.isBurst)).toEqual([true, false, false])
    expect(pages[0]).toMatchObject({ burstSize: 3, since: 1, from: 980, to: 2000 })
    expect(pageRows(rows, pages[0]).map(r => r.s.id)).toEqual(['b', 'solo', 'c', 'a'])
    expect(pageRows(rows, pages[1]).map(r => r.s.id)).toEqual(['o5', 'o4', 'o3', 'o2', 'o1'])
    expect(pageRows(rows, pages[2]).map(r => r.s.id)).toEqual(['o0', 'o-1'])
    expect(pagesOf([], [], '')).toEqual([])
    expect(pageRows(rows, undefined)).toEqual([])
  })

  test('within the burst page: interrupted, wip, ?, handed off, done, then newest', () => {
    const rows = [row('d', 'done', 100), row('h', 'handed-off', 101), row('u', 'unknown', 102), row('w', 'wip', 103), row('i', 'interrupted', 99)]
    expect(pageRows(rows, pagesOf(rows, burstsOf(rows), '')[0]).map(r => r.s.id)).toEqual(['i', 'w', 'u', 'h', 'd'])
  })

  test('under a phase: that phase only, newest first, no burst page; running sessions are on no page', () => {
    const rows = [
      { ...row('p1', 'wip', 300), phase: 'patch-1-9' },
      { ...row('p2', 'wip', 200), phase: 'patch-1-9' },
      { ...row('p3', 'wip', 250), phase: 'release-2' },
      { ...row('p4', 'wip', 400, { running: true }), phase: 'patch-1-9' },
      row('n1', 'wip', 100),
    ]
    const bursts = burstsOf(rows)
    expect(pagesOf(rows, bursts, 'patch-1-9')).toEqual([{ ids: ['p1', 'p2'], from: 200, to: 300, isBurst: false, burstSize: 0, since: 0 }])
    expect(pagesOf(rows, bursts, NO_TASK).flatMap(p => p.ids)).toEqual(['n1'])
  })

  test('the header names the burst, its span, its age and what closed since; an older page its span', () => {
    const noon = new Date(2026, 9, 8, 12, 0).getTime() / 1000
    expect(spanLabel(noon - 600, noon, noon)).toBe('today 11:50–12:00')
    expect(spanLabel(noon - 86_400, noon, noon)).toBe('yesterday 12:00 – 12:00')
    expect(pageHeader({ ids: ['a', 'b', 'c', 'd', 'e'], from: noon - 1020, to: noon, isBurst: true, burstSize: 4, since: 1 }, noon + 720)).toBe(
      '4 closed together · today 11:43–12:00 · 12m ago · 1 closed since',
    )
    expect(pageHeader({ ids: ['x'], from: noon, to: noon, isBurst: true, burstSize: 1, since: 0 }, noon)).toBe('Closed alone · today 12:00 · 0m ago')
    expect(pageHeader({ ids: ['x', 'y'], from: noon - 600, to: noon, isBurst: false, burstSize: 0, since: 0 }, noon)).toBe('2 sessions · newest first · today 11:50–12:00')
  })
})

describe('ticks and resume modes', () => {
  test('unfinished sessions start ticked; done, reopened, running and folder-less ones do not; a toggle wins', () => {
    const [, a, b] = ROWS
    expect(isTicked(b!, {}, {})).toBe(true)
    expect(isTicked(a!, {}, {})).toBe(false)
    expect(isTicked(a!, { a: true }, {})).toBe(true)
    expect(isTicked(b!, {}, { b: 1 })).toBe(false)
    expect(isTicked(b!, { b: false }, {})).toBe(false)
    expect(isTicked(row('m', 'wip', 1, { cwdExists: false }), { m: true }, {})).toBe(false)
    expect(isTicked(row('r', 'wip', 1, { running: true }), { r: true }, {})).toBe(false)
  })

  test('with a handover: fresh past 400k context, else --resume, a toggle winning; without one always --resume', () => {
    const heavy = { ...row('h', 'handed-off', 1, { ctx: 431_000 }), handover: HO }
    const light = { ...row('l', 'handed-off', 1, { ctx: 120_000 }), handover: HO }
    expect(resumeModeOf(heavy, {})).toBe('fresh')
    expect(resumeModeOf(light, {})).toBe('resume')
    expect(resumeModeOf(heavy, { h: 'resume' })).toBe('resume')
    expect(resumeModeOf(row('n', 'wip', 1, { ctx: 900_000 }), { n: 'fresh' })).toBe('resume')
  })

  test('context colours, token counts and plain prose', () => {
    expect([ctxTone(399_999), ctxTone(400_000), ctxTone(700_000)]).toEqual([null, 'warning', 'critical'])
    expect(formatTokens(431_000)).toBe('431k')
    expect(formatTokens(1_250_000)).toBe('1.3M')
    expect(plain("fg-store-002's wrap-up: - **Docs:** commit `abc`")).toBe("fg-store-002's wrap-up: Docs: commit abc")
  })

  test('the card line: the handover next step, the interrupted prompt, the last reply, else why', () => {
    expect(detailOf({ ...row('h', 'handed-off', 1), handover: HO })).toEqual({ label: 'Next', text: 'Finish the error rows' })
    expect(detailOf(row('i', 'interrupted', 1, { lastPrompt: 'port the `counter`' }))).toEqual({ label: 'Working on', text: 'port the counter' })
    expect(detailOf(row('w', 'wip', 1))).toEqual({ label: 'Last reply', text: 'Done with step 2 of w.' })
    expect(detailOf({ ...row('z', 'wip', 1, { lastReply: '' }), why: 'no end-session and no open handover' })).toEqual({
      label: 'Note',
      text: 'no end-session and no open handover',
    })
  })
})

describe('the band keycap and the Session tab line', () => {
  const now = 1000 + 3600
  test('a burst of two or more, one unfinished, under 48 hours old; anything less is null', () => {
    expect(restorePendingOf(burstsOf(ROWS), now)).toEqual({ epoch: 1000, unfinished: 2 })
    expect(restorePendingOf(burstsOf(ROWS), 1000 + 48 * 3600)).toBeNull()
    expect(restorePendingOf(burstsOf([row('a', 'done', 1000), row('b', 'done', 990)]), now)).toBeNull()
    expect(restorePendingOf(burstsOf([row('a', 'wip', 1000)]), now)).toBeNull()
    expect(restorePendingOf([], now)).toBeNull()
  })

  test('membership by its own id (a --resume) or by the handover it resumed from (fresh); others are the unfinished rest', () => {
    const at = new Date(2026, 9, 10, 16, 14).getTime() / 1000
    const rows = [
      row('a', 'wip', at + 600, { running: true, hasClosed: true }),
      { ...row('b', 'handed-off', at + 300, { handovers: [HO] }), handover: HO },
      row('c', 'interrupted', at),
      row('d', 'done', at + 100),
    ]
    const bursts = burstsOf(rows)
    expect(sessionBurstOf(bursts, rows, 'a', null)).toEqual({ at: '16:14', others: 2 })
    expect(sessionBurstOf(bursts, rows, 'fresh-new', 'h-1')).toEqual({ at: '16:14', others: 1 })
    expect(sessionBurstOf(bursts, rows, 'stranger', null)).toBeNull()
    expect(sessionBurstOf(burstsOf([row('solo', 'wip', 5)]), [row('solo', 'wip', 5)], 'solo', null)).toBeNull()
  })
})
```

````ts
// ── Bursts, pages, ticks and resume modes (ported from cm-restore's pure helpers) ─────────────────────────────────────

/** Sessions closed within this many minutes of the next one belong to one burst. */
export const BURST_GAP_MIN = 10
/** Older sessions page this many at a time, newest first (spec §10). */
export const RESTORE_PAGE_SIZE = 5
/** Context a resume reopens: warning once heavy, critical near a 1M window's end. */
export const CTX_WARN = 400_000
export const CTX_HIGH = 700_000
/** The band's restore keycap shows for a burst this recent at most (spec §10.4). */
export const PENDING_WITHIN_S = 48 * 3600

/** Sessions closed together. `open`: members not running and not done. Newest burst first. */
export type RestoreBurst = { readonly ids: readonly string[]; readonly from: number; readonly to: number; readonly open: number }
/** A page: page 1 is the closing burst and what closed after it; `burstSize` counts the burst, `since` what closed after. */
export type RestorePage = {
  readonly ids: readonly string[]
  readonly from: number
  readonly to: number
  readonly isBurst: boolean
  readonly burstSize: number
  readonly since: number
}

const pad = (n: number): string => String(n).padStart(2, '0')
export function hhmm(epoch: number): string {
  const d = new Date(epoch * 1000)
  return `${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`
}
export function dayLabel(epoch: number, now: number): string {
  const d = new Date(epoch * 1000)
  const t = new Date(now * 1000)
  const start = (x: Date): number => new Date(x.getFullYear(), x.getMonth(), x.getDate()).getTime()
  const diff = Math.round((start(t) - start(d)) / 86_400_000)
  if (diff === 0) return 'today'
  if (diff === 1) return 'yesterday'
  return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`
}
/** `16:31` today, `yesterday 16:31`, else the date and time. */
export function when(epoch: number, now: number): string {
  const day = dayLabel(epoch, now)
  return day === 'today' ? hhmm(epoch) : `${day} ${hhmm(epoch)}`
}
/** `today 16:14–16:31` within a day, `yesterday 22:10 – 09:05` across days. */
export function spanLabel(from: number, to: number, now: number): string {
  if (from === to) return `${dayLabel(to, now)} ${hhmm(to)}`
  if (dayLabel(from, now) === dayLabel(to, now)) return `${dayLabel(to, now)} ${hhmm(from)}–${hhmm(to)}`
  return `${dayLabel(from, now)} ${hhmm(from)} – ${when(to, now)}`
}
export function ago(epoch: number, now: number): string {
  const m = Math.max(0, Math.round((now - epoch) / 60))
  if (m < 60) return `${m}m ago`
  const hours = Math.round(m / 60)
  return hours < 48 ? `${hours}h ago` : `${Math.round(hours / 24)}d ago`
}
export function formatTokens(n: number): string {
  return n >= 1_000_000 ? `${(n / 1_000_000).toFixed(1)}M` : `${Math.round(n / 1000)}k`
}
/** The ctx figure's tone: critical from CTX_HIGH, warning from CTX_WARN, else none (subtle text). */
export function ctxTone(ctx: number): 'critical' | 'warning' | null {
  return ctx >= CTX_HIGH ? 'critical' : ctx >= CTX_WARN ? 'warning' : null
}
/** One line of prose from markdown-ish text. */
export function plain(text: string): string {
  return text
    .replace(/```[\s\S]*?```/g, ' ')
    .replace(/[*_`#>]+/g, '')
    .replace(/(^|\s)[-•]\s+/gm, '$1')
    .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
    .trim()
}

/**
 * Closing bursts, newest first: closed sessions (and running ones that closed before, by that close) whose close times sit
 * within `gapMin` minutes of the next. A session that never closed (running, no cost-state) is in none.
 */
export function burstsOf(rows: readonly RestoreRow[], gapMin = BURST_GAP_MIN): RestoreBurst[] {
  const closed = rows.filter(r => !r.s.running || r.s.hasClosed).sort((a, b) => b.s.closedEpoch - a.s.closedEpoch)
  const bursts: { ids: string[]; from: number; to: number; open: number }[] = []
  for (const r of closed) {
    const last = bursts.at(-1)
    if (last !== undefined && last.from - r.s.closedEpoch <= gapMin * 60) {
      last.ids.push(r.s.id)
      last.from = r.s.closedEpoch
    } else {
      bursts.push({ ids: [r.s.id], from: r.s.closedEpoch, to: r.s.closedEpoch, open: 0 })
    }
    if (!r.s.running && r.status !== 'done') bursts[bursts.length - 1]!.open += 1
  }
  return bursts
}

/** Page 1's burst: the newest of two or more sessions, else the newest. */
export function defaultBurst(bursts: readonly RestoreBurst[]): number {
  const at = bursts.findIndex(b => b.ids.length >= 2)
  return at >= 0 ? at : 0
}

/** A row under the strip's choice: All, a phase id, or NO_TASK. */
export const inPhase = (row: RestoreRow, choice: string): boolean => choice === '' || row.phase === choice

const chunks = (rows: readonly RestoreRow[], size: number): RestorePage[] => {
  const pages: RestorePage[] = []
  for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i += size) {
    const chunk = rows.slice(i, i + size)
    pages.push({ ids: chunk.map(r => r.s.id), from: chunk.at(-1)!.s.closedEpoch, to: chunk[0]!.s.closedEpoch, isBurst: false, burstSize: 0, since: 0 })
  }
  return pages
}

/**
 * Burst-first paging (spec §10): under All, page 1 is the closing burst plus anything closed after it, then the rest newest
 * first, RESTORE_PAGE_SIZE a page. Under a phase, that phase's sessions newest first. Running sessions are on no page.
 */
export function pagesOf(rows: readonly RestoreRow[], bursts: readonly RestoreBurst[], choice: string, size = RESTORE_PAGE_SIZE): RestorePage[] {
  const closed = rows.filter(r => !r.s.running && inPhase(r, choice)).sort((a, b) => b.s.closedEpoch - a.s.closedEpoch)
  if (choice !== '' || bursts.length === 0) return chunks(closed, size)
  const at = defaultBurst(bursts)
  const burst = bursts[at]!
  const head = new Set(bursts.slice(0, at + 1).flatMap(b => b.ids))
  const first = closed.filter(r => head.has(r.s.id))
  const members = new Set(burst.ids)
  const page: RestorePage = {
    ids: first.map(r => r.s.id),
    from: burst.from,
    to: bursts[0]!.to,
    isBurst: true,
    burstSize: burst.ids.length,
    since: first.filter(r => !members.has(r.s.id)).length,
  }
  return [page, ...chunks(closed.filter(r => !head.has(r.s.id)), size)]
}

/** A page's rows: the burst page unfinished first (STATUS_ORDER, then newest); other pages strictly newest first. */
export function pageRows(rows: readonly RestoreRow[], page: RestorePage | undefined): RestoreRow[] {
  if (page === undefined) return []
  const byId = new Map(rows.map(r => [r.s.id, r]))
  const list = page.ids.map(id => byId.get(id)).filter((r): r is RestoreRow => r !== undefined)
  const rank = (r: RestoreRow): number => STATUS_ORDER.indexOf(r.status)
  return page.isBurst ? list.sort((a, b) => rank(a) - rank(b) || b.s.closedEpoch - a.s.closedEpoch) : list.sort((a, b) => b.s.closedEpoch - a.s.closedEpoch)
}

/** The page's header: `4 closed together · today 16:14–16:31 · 12m ago · 1 closed since`, or an older page's span. */
export function pageHeader(page: RestorePage, now: number): string {
  const span = spanLabel(page.from, page.to, now)
  if (!page.isBurst) return `${page.ids.length} ${page.ids.length === 1 ? 'session' : 'sessions'} · newest first · ${span}`
  return [
    page.burstSize === 1 ? 'Closed alone' : `${page.burstSize} closed together`,
    span,
    ago(page.to, now),
    page.since > 0 ? `${page.since} closed since` : '',
  ]
    .filter(Boolean)
    .join(' · ')
}

/** Sessions open in another terminal now, most recently active first. */
export function runningRows(rows: readonly RestoreRow[]): RestoreRow[] {
  return rows.filter(r => r.s.running).sort((a, b) => b.s.closedEpoch - a.s.closedEpoch)
}

/** Why a session cannot be reopened from here, or '' when it can. */
export function blockedWhy(row: RestoreRow): string {
  if (row.s.running) return `${row.s.title} is open in another terminal`
  if (!row.s.cwdExists) return `${row.s.title}: its folder is gone (${row.s.cwd || 'unknown'})`
  return ''
}

/** Default ticks (spec §10): unfinished, resumable and not yet reopened from here; an explicit toggle wins. */
export function isTicked(row: RestoreRow, selected: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>, opened: Readonly<Record<string, number>>): boolean {
  if (blockedWhy(row) !== '') return false
  return selected[row.s.id] ?? (row.status !== 'done' && opened[row.s.id] === undefined)
}

/**
 * How a session reopens (spec §10.3): with a handover, `fresh` (a new session told to resume from it) when its context is
 * past CTX_WARN, else `resume`; an explicit toggle wins. Without a handover, always `resume`.
 */
export function resumeModeOf(row: RestoreRow, modes: Readonly<Record<string, TmResumeMode>>): TmResumeMode {
  if (row.handover === null) return 'resume'
  return modes[row.s.id] ?? (row.s.ctx > CTX_WARN ? 'fresh' : 'resume')
}

/** The card's one line of what was going on: the handover's next step, the interrupted prompt, the last reply, or why. */
export function detailOf(row: RestoreRow): { readonly label: string; readonly text: string } {
  if (row.handover !== null && row.status === 'handed-off') return { label: 'Next', text: plain(row.handover.next || row.handover.tldr) }
  if (row.status === 'interrupted' && row.s.lastPrompt !== '') return { label: 'Working on', text: plain(row.s.lastPrompt) }
  if (row.s.lastReply !== '') return { label: 'Last reply', text: plain(row.s.lastReply) }
  return { label: 'Note', text: row.why }
}

/**
 * The band's restore keycap (spec §10.4): page 1's burst when it holds two or more sessions, at least one unfinished, and
 * closed under 48 hours ago; null otherwise. `epoch` names the burst (its last close, whole seconds).
 */
export function restorePendingOf(bursts: readonly RestoreBurst[], now: number): TmRestorePending | null {
  const burst = bursts[defaultBurst(bursts)]
  if (burst === undefined || burst.ids.length < 2 || burst.open < 1 || now - burst.to >= PENDING_WITHIN_S) return null
  return { epoch: Math.round(burst.to), unfinished: burst.open }
}

/**
 * The Session tab's burst line (spec §7): the burst this session closed in (a --resume keeps its id), or the burst of the
 * session that wrote the handover it resumed from; `others`: the rest of that burst still unfinished and not open.
 */
export function sessionBurstOf(
  bursts: readonly RestoreBurst[],
  rows: readonly RestoreRow[],
  sessionId: string,
  resumedFrom: string | null,
): TmSessionBurst | null {
  const writer = resumedFrom === null ? undefined : rows.find(r => r.s.handovers.some(h => h.id === resumedFrom))?.s.id
  const mine = new Set([sessionId, ...(writer === undefined ? [] : [writer])])
  const burst = bursts.find(b => b.ids.some(id => mine.has(id)))
  if (burst === undefined || burst.ids.length < 2) return null
  const byId = new Map(rows.map(r => [r.s.id, r]))
  const others = burst.ids.filter(id => {
    const r = byId.get(id)
    return !mine.has(id) && r !== undefined && !r.s.running && r.status !== 'done'
  }).length
  return { at: hhmm(burst.from), others }
}
````

```ts
import type { LaunchResult, LaunchSpec } from '../../hooks/launch'
```

Append to the end of `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/fixtures/restore.ts`:

```ts
/**
 * The restore flows over memory: `scan` is what scan.py prints (an Error: every Python fails with it), `tm` answers the
 * refs query in turn (a string: the reply text; 'offline': the call throws), and every effect is recorded.
 */
export type FakeRestore = {
  readonly deps: RestoreDeps
  data: TmRestoreData
  view: TmRestoreView
  readonly signals: { readonly pending: TmRestorePending | null; readonly burst: TmSessionBurst | null }[]
  readonly toasts: string[]
  readonly logs: string[]
  readonly runs: (readonly string[])[]
  readonly waits: number[]
  readonly launches: (readonly LaunchSpec[])[]
  readonly copies: string[]
  readonly prompts: string[]
  readonly store: Map<string, unknown>
  opened: number
  phasesAsked: number
  launchResult: (specs: readonly LaunchSpec[]) => LaunchResult
  complete: (prompt: string) => { readonly ok: true; readonly text: string } | { readonly ok: false; readonly reason: string }
}

export function fakeRestore(
  o: {
    readonly source?: 'tm' | 'demo'
    readonly scan?: TmScan | Error
    readonly tm?: readonly (string | 'offline')[]
    readonly now?: number
    readonly self?: string
    readonly store?: Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>
    readonly digest?: { readonly first: string; readonly tail: string } | { readonly error: string }
  } = {},
): FakeRestore {
  const answers = [...(o.tm ?? [])]
  const f: FakeRestore = {
    data: FRESH_RESTORE_DATA,
    view: FRESH_RESTORE_VIEW,
    signals: [],
    toasts: [],
    logs: [],
    runs: [],
    waits: [],
    launches: [],
    copies: [],
    prompts: [],
    store: new Map(Object.entries(o.store ?? {})),
    opened: 0,
    phasesAsked: 0,
    launchResult: specs => ({ opened: specs.length, copied: null, failed: [] }),
    complete: () => ({ ok: true, text: '<state>Stopped at the parser.</state><next>Finish the error rows.</next><blocker>none</blocker>' }),
    deps: undefined as unknown as RestoreDeps,
  }
  const scan = o.scan ?? scanOf([])
  const out = (argv: readonly string[]): RunOut =>
    argv[3] === 'digest'
      ? { exitCode: 0, stdout: JSON.stringify(o.digest ?? { first: 'USER: start', tail: 'USER: start\nASSISTANT: stopped' }), stderr: '' }
      : scan instanceof Error
        ? { exitCode: 1, stdout: '', stderr: `Traceback\n${scan.message}\n` }
        : { exitCode: 0, stdout: JSON.stringify(scan), stderr: '' }
  ;(f as { deps: RestoreDeps }).deps = {
    source: o.source ?? 'tm',
    rh: {
      paths: async () => ({ configDir: 'C:\\Users\\me\\.claude', cache: 'C:\\tmp\\tm-restore-scan-cache.json', script: 'C:\\mod\\scanner\\scan.py' }),
      run: async argv => {
        f.runs.push(argv)
        return out(argv)
      },
    },
    tm: {
      call: async () => {
        const next = answers.length > 1 ? answers.shift()! : (answers[0] ?? refsReply([]))
        if (next === 'offline') throw new Error('$.mcp.call: no connected MCP tool "backlog_query" on a server named "plugin:taskmaster:tm"')
        return { text: next, isError: false }
      },
      sleep: () => new Promise<void>(() => undefined),
      log: text => void f.logs.push(text),
    },
    store: {
      get: async key => f.store.get(key),
      set: async (key, value) => void f.store.set(key, value),
      delete: async key => void f.store.delete(key),
    },
    read: { data: async () => f.data, view: async () => f.view },
    write: {
      // $.state hands back copies: so does this, or a flow keyed on object identity would pass here and fail live.
      data: async change => {
        f.data = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(change(f.data))) as TmRestoreData
      },
      view: async change => {
        f.view = change(f.view)
      },
      signals: async (pending, burst) => void f.signals.push({ pending, burst }),
    },
    now: async () => o.now ?? (T0 + 3600) * 1000,
    wait: async ms => void f.waits.push(ms),
    sessionId: async () => o.self ?? 'me',
    repoRoot: async () => ROOT,
    toast: text => void f.toasts.push(text),
    openPane: async () => {
      f.opened += 1
      return true
    },
    loadPhases: () => {
      f.phasesAsked += 1
    },
  }
  return f
}
```

`mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-flows.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: prove what the restore pane's keys and the session-start scan do (spec §10, §10.4): open scans and names the
// burst, a failed scan says why, tm's silence reads `?`, the start scan waits for tm and feeds the band and the Session tab,
// and ticks, modes, compact and paging change only the pane's own state.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { createRestoreFlows, RESOLVE_RETRY_MS, SCANNING } from '../hooks/restore/flows'
import { paneRows } from '../hooks/restore/model'
import { fakeRestore, refsReply, ROOT, scanOf, session, T0 } from './fixtures/restore'

const HO = { id: 'h-1', path: 'C:\\work\\proj\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\h-1.md', tldr: 'Half done', next: 'Finish the rows' }
// Three sessions closed together half an hour before `now` (one handed off, one interrupted, one done), one an hour earlier.
const SCAN = scanOf([
  session('u5', { closedEpoch: T0, taskIds: ['t-1'], handovers: [HO], ctx: 431_000 }),
  session('audit', { closedEpoch: T0 - 120, taskIds: ['t-2'], interrupted: true }),
  session('counter', { closedEpoch: T0 - 300, endedSession: true }),
  session('old', { closedEpoch: T0 - 3600 }),
  session('live', { closedEpoch: T0 + 1800, running: true, hasClosed: false }),
])
const REFS = refsReply(['["t","t-1","in-review","full","p1"]  Sign off', '["t","t-2","in-progress","full","p1"]  Supersede', '["h","h-1","open"]'])

describe('open and scan', () => {
  test('/tm-restore opens at page 1, asks for the phases, scans the repo and names the burst', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: [REFS] })
    f.view = { ...f.view, page: 2, focus: 'old' }
    const flows = createRestoreFlows(f.deps)
    expect(await flows.open(4)).toBe('Restore opened: 3 sessions closed together, 2 unfinished.')
    expect([f.opened, f.phasesAsked]).toEqual([1, 1])
    expect(f.view).toMatchObject({ page: 0, focus: '', busy: '' })
    expect(f.runs[0]).toEqual(['python', '-I', 'C:\\mod\\scanner\\scan.py', 'scan', '--config-dir', 'C:\\Users\\me\\.claude', '--root', ROOT, '--days', '4', '--cache', 'C:\\tmp\\tm-restore-scan-cache.json'])
    expect(f.data).toMatchObject({ scan: SCAN, error: '', days: 4 })
    expect(paneRows(f.data, 'me').map(r => [r.s.id, r.status])).toEqual([
      ['u5', 'handed-off'],
      ['audit', 'interrupted'],
      ['counter', 'done'],
      ['old', 'wip'],
      ['live', 'wip'],
    ])
  })

  test('the busy line shows while the scan runs', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: [REFS] })
    const seen: string[] = []
    const write = f.deps.write
    ;(f.deps as { write: typeof write }).write = { ...write, data: async change => {
      seen.push(f.view.busy)
      await write.data(change)
    } }
    await createRestoreFlows(f.deps).open(4)
    expect(seen.every(busy => busy === SCANNING)).toBe(true)
    expect(f.view.busy).toBe('')
  })

  test('a scan that fails says why in the pane and the answer, and is logged', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: new Error('ValueError: bad root') })
    expect(await createRestoreFlows(f.deps).open(4)).toBe('Scan failed: ValueError: bad root')
    expect(f.data.error).toBe('Scan failed: ValueError: bad root')
    expect(f.logs).toContain('taskmaster-mods: restore scan failed: ValueError: bad root')
    expect(f.view.busy).toBe('')
  })

  test('tm not answering leaves the facts listed with status ?', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: ['offline'] })
    await createRestoreFlows(f.deps).open(4)
    expect(f.data.refs).toBeNull()
    expect(paneRows(f.data, 'me').map(r => r.status)).toEqual(['unknown', 'unknown', 'done', 'unknown', 'unknown'])
    expect(f.waits).toEqual([])
  })

  test('u rescans the same window and keeps the page', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: [REFS] })
    const flows = createRestoreFlows(f.deps)
    await flows.open(7)
    await flows.turnPage(1)
    await flows.rescan()
    expect(f.runs.at(-1)).toContain('7')
    expect(f.view.page).toBe(1)
  })
})

describe('the session-start scan (spec §10.4)', () => {
  test('3 days, tm asked again while it connects, then the band keycap and the burst line are written', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: ['offline', 'offline', REFS] })
    await createRestoreFlows(f.deps).startScan()
    expect(f.runs[0]).toContain('3')
    expect(f.waits).toEqual([RESOLVE_RETRY_MS, RESOLVE_RETRY_MS])
    expect(f.data.refs).not.toBeNull()
    expect(f.signals).toEqual([{ pending: { epoch: T0, unfinished: 2 }, burst: null }])
  })

  test('a session resumed from a burst member is told its burst and how many others are open', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: [REFS], self: 'fresh-one', store: { 'resumed-from:fresh-one': 'h-1' } })
    await createRestoreFlows(f.deps).startScan()
    expect(f.signals.at(-1)?.burst).toEqual({ at: expect.any(String), others: 1 })
  })

  test('demo reads the demo sessions: a burst of four, three unfinished', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ source: 'demo' })
    await createRestoreFlows(f.deps).startScan()
    expect(f.runs).toEqual([])
    expect(f.data.refs).not.toBeNull()
    expect(f.signals.at(-1)?.pending).toMatchObject({ unfinished: 3 })
  })
})

describe('the pane state', () => {
  test('s flips a tick from its default; a session that cannot reopen says why', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: [REFS] })
    const flows = createRestoreFlows(f.deps)
    await flows.open(4)
    await flows.toggle('audit')
    await flows.toggle('counter')
    expect(f.view.selected).toEqual({ audit: false, counter: true })
    await flows.toggle('live')
    expect(f.toasts).toEqual(['Session live is open in another terminal'])
    await flows.select({ audit: true, old: false })
    expect(f.view.selected).toEqual({ audit: true, counter: true, old: false })
  })

  test('m flips fresh and --resume from the default; without a handover it says so', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: [REFS] })
    const flows = createRestoreFlows(f.deps)
    await flows.open(4)
    await flows.toggleMode('u5')
    expect(f.view.modes).toEqual({ u5: 'resume' })
    await flows.toggleMode('u5')
    expect(f.view.modes).toEqual({ u5: 'fresh' })
    await flows.toggleMode('audit')
    expect(f.toasts).toEqual(['Session audit wrote no handover: it reopens with --resume'])
  })

  test('z toggles compact; a page turn drops the focus; focus moves', async () => {
    const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: [REFS] })
    const flows = createRestoreFlows(f.deps)
    await flows.toggleCompact()
    await flows.focus('audit')
    expect(f.view).toMatchObject({ compact: true, focus: 'audit' })
    await flows.turnPage(1)
    expect(f.view).toMatchObject({ page: 1, focus: '' })
  })
})
```

```ts
/** What tm says of the demo sessions' ids (spec §10.2), as if one backlog_query had read them. */
export const DEMO_REFS: TmRefs = {
  tasks: {
    'unified-chat-022': { id: 'unified-chat-022', status: 'in-review', lane: 'full', phase: 'patch-1-9', title: 'Unified chat pre-build gets the full supervisor toolset' },
    'tm-audit-031': { id: 'tm-audit-031', status: 'in-progress', lane: 'full', phase: 'patch-1-9', title: 'Handover auto-supersede keeps the newest per thread' },
    'docs-012': { id: 'docs-012', status: 'todo', lane: '', phase: 'release-2', title: 'README mentions the review queue' },
    'tm-audit-029': { id: 'tm-audit-029', status: 'done', lane: 'full', phase: 'patch-1-8', title: 'Port the row counter' },
  },
  handovers: { '2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022': 'open' },
}
export const DEMO_BRIEF =
  'State: The handover guard forks and files its handover; compaction after it is not wired yet.\nNext: Wire the compaction call behind the fork and run the guard tests.\nBlocker: none'

/**
 * The restore pane's demo scan: four sessions closed together an hour ago (handed off, interrupted, wip, done), one closed
 * after them, two older ones (one whose folder is gone) and one open in another terminal now.
 */
export function demoRestore(now: number): { readonly scan: TmScan; readonly refs: TmRefs } {
  const at = (minutes: number) => now / 1000 - minutes * 60
  const base = (id: string, title: string, closedMinutes: number, over: Partial<TmScannedSession>): TmScannedSession => ({
    id,
    cwd: DEMO_ROOT,
    cwdExists: true,
    branch: 'master',
    title,
    firstPrompt: title,
    lastPrompt: `Carry on with ${title.toLowerCase()}`,
    lastReply: `Stopped after the tests for ${title.toLowerCase()}.`,
    lastEvent: 'end_turn',
    interrupted: false,
    prompts: 12,
    ctx: 150_000,
    cost: 4,
    closedEpoch: at(closedMinutes),
    hasClosed: true,
    running: false,
    resumedAfterClose: false,
    taskIds: [],
    handovers: [],
    endedSession: false,
    promptsAfterEnd: 0,
    ...over,
  })
  const shipped = '2026-10-05-shipped-unified-chat-022'
  return {
    refs: DEMO_REFS,
    scan: {
      scannedAt: now / 1000,
      root: DEMO_ROOT,
      sessions: [
        base('demo-live', 'Plan the restore pane', 1, { running: true, hasClosed: false, ctx: 210_000 }),
        base('demo-late', 'Spike: viewer colours', 20, { ctx: 40_000, branch: 'spike/colours' }),
        base('demo-u5', 'U5 brief approval', 43, {
          ctx: 431_000,
          taskIds: ['unified-chat-022'],
          handovers: [{ id: shipped, path: handoverPath(DEMO_ROOT, shipped), tldr: 'Shipped unified-chat-022 to dev', next: 'Live check, then sign off in the review queue' }],
        }),
        base('demo-audit', 'Handover auto-supersede', 48, {
          ctx: 712_000,
          branch: 'feat/tm-audit-031',
          interrupted: true,
          lastEvent: 'interrupt',
          lastPrompt: 'Make the supersede keep the newest handover per thread',
          taskIds: ['tm-audit-031'],
        }),
        base('demo-docs', 'README review queue', 55, { ctx: 95_000, taskIds: ['docs-012'] }),
        base('demo-counter', 'Port the row counter', 60, { taskIds: ['tm-audit-029'], endedSession: true }),
        base('demo-old-1', 'Release 7.0.0 notes', 26 * 60, { endedSession: true }),
        base('demo-old-2', 'Store read hang', 30 * 60, { cwd: 'C:\\Users\\demo\\gone', cwdExists: false, taskIds: ['tm-audit-031'] }),
      ],
    },
  }
}
```

`mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/flows.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: what every key in the restore pane does, and the session-start scan behind the band's restore keycap, in
// one place over injected closures (no `$`), so the pane, the band and the Session tab never disagree about a burst.
import type { TmRefs, TmRestoreData, TmRestorePending, TmRestoreView, TmSessionBurst } from '../../types'
import { demoRestore } from '../demo'
import type { TmHost } from '../host'
import type { PhaseStore } from '../phases'
import { readResumedFrom } from '../resumed'
import {
  blockedWhy,
  burstsOf,
  defaultBurst,
  isTicked,
  paneRows,
  restorePendingOf,
  type RestoreRow,
  resumeModeOf,
  rowsOf,
  sessionBurstOf,
  SIGNAL_DAYS,
} from './model'
import { type RestoreHost, resolveRefs, runScan } from './scan'

/** tm connects seconds after a session starts: the start scan asks this many more times, this far apart. */
export const RESOLVE_RETRIES = 3
export const RESOLVE_RETRY_MS = 5_000
export const SCANNING = 'Scanning sessions…'

export type RestoreWriter = {
  readonly data: (change: (d: TmRestoreData) => TmRestoreData) => Promise<void>
  readonly view: (change: (v: TmRestoreView) => TmRestoreView) => Promise<void>
  /** The band's restore keycap and the Session tab's burst line (spec §10.4, §7). */
  readonly signals: (pending: TmRestorePending | null, burst: TmSessionBurst | null) => Promise<void>
}

export type RestoreDeps = {
  readonly source: 'tm' | 'demo'
  readonly rh: RestoreHost
  readonly tm: Pick<TmHost, 'call' | 'sleep' | 'log'>
  readonly store: PhaseStore
  readonly read: { readonly data: () => Promise<TmRestoreData>; readonly view: () => Promise<TmRestoreView> }
  readonly write: RestoreWriter
  /** Milliseconds, as $.clock.now. */
  readonly now: () => Promise<number>
  readonly wait: (ms: number) => Promise<void>
  readonly sessionId: () => Promise<string>
  readonly repoRoot: () => Promise<string>
  readonly toast: (text: string) => void
  readonly openPane: () => Promise<boolean>
  /** Reads restore's phase list fresh into the strip, when the main flows are built (a no-op before). */
  readonly loadPhases: () => void
}

export type RestoreFlows = ReturnType<typeof createRestoreFlows>

export function createRestoreFlows(d: RestoreDeps) {
  const rowsNow = async (): Promise<RestoreRow[]> => paneRows(await d.read.data(), await d.sessionId())
  const rowById = async (id: string): Promise<RestoreRow | undefined> => (await rowsNow()).find(r => r.s.id === id)

  /** After every scan: page 1's burst for the band, this session's burst for the Session tab. */
  const signal = async (): Promise<void> => {
    const data = await d.read.data()
    const self = await d.sessionId()
    const rows = rowsOf(data.scan, data.refs)
    const bursts = burstsOf(rows)
    const now = (await d.now()) / 1000
    await d.write.signals(restorePendingOf(bursts, now), sessionBurstOf(bursts, rows, self, await readResumedFrom(d.store, self)))
  }

  /** One scan (demo: the demo sessions), then tm's word on it; `retries` more asks while tm does not answer. */
  const scan = async (days: number, retries: number): Promise<void> => {
    if (d.source === 'demo') {
      const demo = demoRestore(await d.now())
      await d.write.data(() => ({ scan: demo.scan, refs: demo.refs, error: '', days }))
      return
    }
    const found = await runScan(d.rh, { root: await d.repoRoot(), days, exclude: '' })
    // The last refs stay drawn until the new ones land: a rescan never flickers every status to `?`.
    await d.write.data(prev => ({ ...prev, scan: found, error: '', days }))
    let refs: TmRefs | null = await resolveRefs(d.tm, found)
    for (let i = 0; refs === null && i < retries; i++) {
      await d.wait(RESOLVE_RETRY_MS)
      refs = await resolveRefs(d.tm, found)
    }
    // Only onto the scan they were read for ($.state hands back copies, so the scan is known by its time, not its identity).
    await d.write.data(prev => (prev.scan?.scannedAt === found.scannedAt ? { ...prev, refs } : prev))
  }

  const rescan = async (days: number, retries = 0): Promise<void> => {
    await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, busy: SCANNING }))
    try {
      await scan(days, retries)
      await signal()
    } catch (error) {
      const reason = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)
      await d.write.data(prev => ({ ...prev, error: `Scan failed: ${reason}`, days }))
      d.tm.log(`taskmaster-mods: restore scan failed: ${reason}`)
    } finally {
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, busy: '' }))
    }
  }

  return {
    /** `/tm-restore [days]`: the pane opens at once on page 1 and fills when the scan lands; the answer names the burst. */
    open: async (days: number): Promise<string> => {
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, page: 0, focus: '' }))
      if (!(await d.openPane())) d.toast('taskmaster-mods: widen the terminal to see the pane')
      d.loadPhases()
      await rescan(days)
      const data = await d.read.data()
      if (data.error !== '') return data.error
      const bursts = burstsOf(await rowsNow())
      const burst = bursts[defaultBurst(bursts)]
      if (burst === undefined) return 'Restore opened: no recent sessions in this repo.'
      return `Restore opened: ${burst.ids.length} ${burst.ids.length === 1 ? 'session' : 'sessions'} closed together, ${burst.open} unfinished.`
    },
    /** `u`: the pane's window again, the page kept. */
    rescan: async (): Promise<void> => {
      await rescan((await d.read.data()).days)
    },
    /** session.start (spec §10.4): a quiet 3-day scan for the band, asking tm again while it is still connecting. */
    startScan: async (): Promise<void> => {
      await rescan(SIGNAL_DAYS, RESOLVE_RETRIES)
    },
    turnPage: async (page: number): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, page, focus: '' }))
    },
    focus: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.view(v => (v.focus === id ? v : { ...v, focus: id }))
    },
    /** `s` / a click on the box: flips the tick; a session that cannot reopen says why instead. */
    toggle: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      const row = await rowById(id)
      if (row === undefined) return
      const why = blockedWhy(row)
      if (why !== '') {
        d.toast(why)
        return
      }
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, selected: { ...v.selected, [id]: !isTicked(row, v.selected, v.opened) } }))
    },
    /** all · unfinished · none: explicit ticks for the page's sessions. */
    select: async (changes: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, selected: { ...v.selected, ...changes } }))
    },
    /** `m`: fresh from the handover ⇄ --resume; a session without a handover only has --resume. */
    toggleMode: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      const row = await rowById(id)
      if (row === undefined) return
      if (row.handover === null) {
        d.toast(`${row.s.title} wrote no handover: it reopens with --resume`)
        return
      }
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, modes: { ...v.modes, [id]: resumeModeOf(row, v.modes) === 'fresh' ? 'resume' : 'fresh' } }))
    },
    /** `z`: --resume launches ask the session to compact first. */
    toggleCompact: async (): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, compact: !v.compact }))
    },
  }
}
```

### Task 5.5: Resume (fresh or --resume), resumed-from records, copy, Brief

**Files:**
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/model.ts` (imports; `launchSpecOf`, `copyTextOf`, `briefKey`, `briefPrompt` and `briefText` before `restorePendingOf`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/flows.ts` (imports; deps `launch`, `copy` and `complete`; `briefOf` and `runBrief`; `resume`, `copy`, `toggleBrief` and `brief`)
- Modify: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/fixtures/restore.ts` (the three new deps)
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-launch.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Stage 2's `LaunchSpec`, `LaunchResult`, `resumePrompt` and `recordResumedFrom`, and `TmFlows.launch` (through `RestoreDeps.launch`); `runDigest`; `handoverPath`.
- Produces: `RestoreFlows.resume(ids)`, `copy(id)`, `toggleBrief(id)` and `brief(ids)`; `RestoreDeps.launch`, `copy` and `complete`; the pure `launchSpecOf`, `copyTextOf`, `briefKey`, `briefPrompt` and `briefText`.

Spec §10.5: before a `--resume` of a session with a handover, `resumed-from:<session id>` = handover id is stored (demo stores nothing). The resumed session keeps its id, so Stage 2's start hook reads it back. A fresh launch is recorded by Stage 2's prompt hook when the `Resume from handover <id>` prompt arrives. The brief's prompt is tag-structured (`<task>`, `<session>`, `<handover>`, `<opening>`, `<transcript_tail>`, `<output_format>`). Its reply is parsed from `<state>`, `<next>` and `<blocker>`, and cached as `brief:<id>:<closedEpoch>`.
