```ts
// User intent: prove the restore pane reopens sessions the way the spec says (§10.3, §10.5, §10.6): fresh from a handover
// or --resume, a --resume recorded as `resumed-from` before it launches, one staggered launch for the ticked, a paste-ready
// copy, and a cached state / next / blocker brief from the briefModel.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { DEMO_BRIEF } from '../hooks/demo'
import { createRestoreFlows } from '../hooks/restore/flows'
import { briefKey, briefPrompt, briefText, copyTextOf, launchSpecOf, rowOf } from '../hooks/restore/model'
import { fakeRestore, NO_REFS, refsReply, ROOT, scanOf, session, T0 } from './fixtures/restore'

const HO = { id: 'h-1', path: 'C:\\work\\proj\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\h-1.md', tldr: 'Half done', next: 'Finish the rows' }
const SCAN = scanOf([
  session('heavy', { closedEpoch: T0, title: 'Heavy one', handovers: [HO], ctx: 431_000, taskIds: ['t-1'] }),
  session('light', { closedEpoch: T0 - 60, title: 'Light one', handovers: [{ ...HO, id: 'h-2', path: '' }], ctx: 90_000 }),
  session('plain', { closedEpoch: T0 - 120, title: 'Plain one', interrupted: true }),
  session('gone', { closedEpoch: T0 - 180, title: 'Gone one', cwdExists: false, cwd: 'C:\\gone' }),
])
const REFS = refsReply(['["t","t-1","in-progress","full","p1"]  Work', '["h","h-1","open"]', '["h","h-2","open"]'])

async function opened(over: Parameters<typeof fakeRestore>[0] = {}) {
  const f = fakeRestore({ scan: SCAN, tm: [REFS], ...over })
  const flows = createRestoreFlows(f.deps)
  await flows.open(4)
  return { f, flows }
}

describe('launch specs and the copy line (pure)', () => {
  const heavy = rowOf(SCAN.sessions[0]!, NO_REFS)
  const light = rowOf(SCAN.sessions[1]!, NO_REFS)
  test('fresh: a new session told to resume from the handover; --resume: the session id, compact only there', () => {
    expect(launchSpecOf(heavy, 'fresh', true, ROOT)).toEqual({ cwd: ROOT, title: 'Heavy one', mode: 'fresh', prompt: `Resume from handover h-1 (${HO.path})` })
    expect(launchSpecOf(heavy, 'resume', true, ROOT)).toEqual({ cwd: ROOT, title: 'Heavy one', mode: 'resume', sessionId: 'heavy', compact: true })
    expect(launchSpecOf(light, 'fresh', false, ROOT).prompt).toBe('Resume from handover h-2 (C:\\work\\proj\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\h-2.md)')
  })
  test('the copy line changes into the folder first', () => {
    expect(copyTextOf(heavy, 'resume', ROOT)).toBe('cd "C:\\work\\proj"; claude --resume heavy')
    expect(copyTextOf(heavy, 'fresh', ROOT)).toBe(`cd "C:\\work\\proj"; claude "Resume from handover h-1 (${HO.path})"`)
  })
})

describe('resume', () => {
  test('r on a heavy handed-off session opens it fresh; nothing is recorded; it is marked reopened and unticked', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened()
    await flows.resume(['heavy'])
    expect(f.launches).toEqual([[{ cwd: ROOT, title: 'Heavy one', mode: 'fresh', prompt: `Resume from handover h-1 (${HO.path})` }]])
    expect([...f.store.keys()].filter(k => k.startsWith('resumed-from:'))).toEqual([])
    expect(f.view.opened).toEqual({ heavy: T0 + 3600 })
    expect(f.view.selected).toEqual({ heavy: false })
    expect(f.view.launching).toEqual([])
  })

  test('a --resume records resumed-from:<id> before the launch; compact rides along', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened()
    await flows.toggleCompact()
    let recordedFirst = false
    f.launchResult = specs => {
      recordedFirst = f.store.get('resumed-from:light') === 'h-2'
      return { opened: specs.length, copied: null, failed: [] }
    }
    await flows.resume(['light'])
    expect(recordedFirst).toBe(true)
    expect(f.launches[0]).toEqual([{ cwd: ROOT, title: 'Light one', mode: 'resume', sessionId: 'light', compact: true }])
  })

  test('g launches every ticked session at once; what cannot reopen says why and stays out', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened()
    await flows.resume(['heavy', 'gone', 'plain'])
    expect(f.toasts).toEqual(['Gone one: its folder is gone (C:\\gone)'])
    expect(f.launches[0]?.map(s => s.title)).toEqual(['Heavy one', 'Plain one'])
    await flows.resume([])
    expect(f.toasts.at(-1)).toBe('Nothing ticked to resume')
  })

  test('a launch that opened fewer tabs than asked marks nothing reopened', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened()
    f.launchResult = specs => ({ opened: specs.length - 1, copied: null, failed: ['Plain one'] })
    await flows.resume(['heavy', 'plain'])
    expect(f.view.opened).toEqual({})
    expect(f.view.launching).toEqual([])
  })

  test('demo records nothing in the store', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened({ source: 'demo' })
    await flows.resume(['demo-docs'])
    expect(f.launches).toHaveLength(1)
    expect([...f.store.keys()]).toEqual([])
  })
})

describe('copy', () => {
  test('c copies the reopen line and says so; a clipboard that refuses still shows it', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened()
    await flows.copy('plain')
    expect(f.copies).toEqual(['cd "C:\\work\\proj"; claude --resume plain'])
    expect(f.toasts).toEqual(['Copied: cd "C:\\work\\proj"; claude --resume plain'])
  })
})

describe('brief', () => {
  test('the prompt is tag-structured over the digest; the reply becomes State / Next / Blocker lines', () => {
    const row = { ...rowOf(SCAN.sessions[0]!, NO_REFS), status: 'handed-off' as const, handoverStatus: 'open' }
    const prompt = briefPrompt(row, { first: 'USER: start', tail: 'ASSISTANT: stopped' })
    expect(prompt).toContain('<session title="Heavy one" branch="main" status="handed-off" task="t-1"/>')
    expect(prompt).toContain('<handover id="h-1" status="open">Half done | next: Finish the rows</handover>')
    expect(prompt).toContain('<transcript_tail>ASSISTANT: stopped</transcript_tail>')
    expect(prompt).toContain('<output_format>')
    expect(briefText('<state>Parser half done.</state><next>Finish rows.</next><blocker>none</blocker>')).toBe('State: Parser half done.\nNext: Finish rows.')
    expect(briefText('<state>s</state><blocker>Needs a key.</blocker>')).toBe('State: s\nNext: ?\nBlocker: Needs a key.')
    expect(briefText('  free text  ')).toBe('free text')
    expect(briefKey(row)).toBe(`brief:heavy:${T0}`)
  })

  test('b briefs once, caches by close, and hides on the second press', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened()
    await flows.toggleBrief('plain')
    expect(f.view.briefs).toEqual({ plain: 'State: Stopped at the parser.\nNext: Finish the error rows.' })
    expect(f.prompts).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(f.runs.at(-1)?.slice(3)).toEqual(['digest', '--config-dir', 'C:\\Users\\me\\.claude', '--session', 'plain'])
    expect(f.store.get(`brief:plain:${T0 - 120}`)).toBe('State: Stopped at the parser.\nNext: Finish the error rows.')
    await flows.toggleBrief('plain')
    expect(f.view.briefs).toEqual({})
    await flows.toggleBrief('plain')
    expect(f.prompts).toHaveLength(1)
    expect(f.view.briefing).toEqual([])
  })

  test('v briefs every ticked one without a brief; failures read as the brief', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened({ digest: { error: 'session not found' } })
    f.complete = () => ({ ok: false, reason: 'api-error' })
    await flows.brief(['plain', 'light'])
    expect(f.view.briefs).toEqual({ plain: 'Brief failed: session not found', light: 'Brief failed: session not found' })
    await flows.brief([])
    expect(f.toasts.at(-1)).toBe('Nothing ticked to brief')
  })

  test('demo briefs are canned and never run the scanner or a model', async () => {
    const { f, flows } = await opened({ source: 'demo' })
    await flows.toggleBrief('demo-u5')
    expect(f.view.briefs['demo-u5']).toBe(DEMO_BRIEF)
    expect(f.prompts).toEqual([])
  })
})
```

```ts
const handoverPathOf = (h: TmScannedHandover, root: string): string => h.path || handoverPath(root, h.id)

/** What the launcher opens for a session (spec §10.3): a fresh session told to resume from its handover, or --resume. */
export function launchSpecOf(row: RestoreRow, mode: TmResumeMode, compact: boolean, root: string): LaunchSpec {
  const base = { cwd: row.s.cwd, title: row.s.title }
  if (mode === 'fresh' && row.handover !== null) {
    return { ...base, mode: 'fresh', prompt: resumePrompt(row.handover.id, handoverPathOf(row.handover, root)) }
  }
  return { ...base, mode: 'resume', sessionId: row.s.id, ...(compact ? { compact: true } : {}) }
}

/** `c`: one line to paste in a new terminal (`;` runs in PowerShell, bash and zsh alike). */
export function copyTextOf(row: RestoreRow, mode: TmResumeMode, root: string): string {
  const run =
    mode === 'fresh' && row.handover !== null ? `claude "${resumePrompt(row.handover.id, handoverPathOf(row.handover, root))}"` : `claude --resume ${row.s.id}`
  return `cd "${row.s.cwd}"; ${run}`
}

/** A brief is cached per session and close (spec §10.6): a session closed again gets a new one. */
export const briefKey = (row: RestoreRow): string => `brief:${row.s.id}:${Math.round(row.s.closedEpoch)}`
const attr = (text: string): string => text.replace(/[<>"&]/g, ' ')

/** The brief's prompt (spec §10.6): the scanner's digest and what tm said, tag-structured, asking for state / next / blocker. */
export function briefPrompt(row: RestoreRow, digest: { readonly first: string; readonly tail: string }): string {
  const h = row.handover
  return [
    '<task>A developer closed this Claude Code session and wants to resume it. From the transcript excerpt, say where the work stopped and the single next step. Be concrete: name files, task ids, branches, open questions. No preamble.</task>',
    `<session title="${attr(row.s.title)}" branch="${attr(row.s.branch)}" status="${row.status}" task="${attr(row.boundId)}"/>`,
    ...(h === null ? [] : [`<handover id="${attr(h.id)}" status="${attr(row.handoverStatus)}">${h.tldr} | next: ${h.next}</handover>`]),
    `<opening>${digest.first}</opening>`,
    `<transcript_tail>${digest.tail}</transcript_tail>`,
    '<output_format>Reply with exactly: <state>one or two sentences</state><next>one sentence</next><blocker>one sentence or none</blocker></output_format>',
  ].join('\n')
}

/** The reply's tags as `State:` / `Next:` / `Blocker:` lines (a `none` blocker left out); an untagged reply as it came. */
export function briefText(reply: string): string {
  const pick = (tag: string): string | undefined => new RegExp(`<${tag}>([\\s\\S]*?)</${tag}>`).exec(reply)?.[1]?.trim()
  const state = pick('state')
  if (state === undefined) return reply.trim()
  const blocker = pick('blocker')
  return [`State: ${state}`, `Next: ${pick('next') ?? '?'}`, ...(blocker !== undefined && !/^none\.?$/i.test(blocker) ? [`Blocker: ${blocker}`] : [])].join('\n')
}
```

```ts
  const briefOf = async (row: RestoreRow): Promise<string> => {

    await d.store.set(key, text)
```

with:

```ts
    toggleCompact: async (): Promise<void> => {
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, compact: !v.compact }))
    },
    /**
     * `r` on a card, `b` for the ticked: what cannot reopen says why; a --resume of a session with a handover records
     * `resumed-from:<id>` first (spec §10.5); then one staggered launch. Only a launch that opened every tab marks them
     * reopened and unticks them; anything less leaves the ticks for another try.
     */
    resume: async (ids: readonly string[]): Promise<void> => {
      if (ids.length === 0) {
        d.toast('Nothing ticked to resume')
        return
      }
      const view = await d.read.view()
      const rows = await rowsNow()
      const ready: RestoreRow[] = []
      for (const id of ids) {
        const row = rows.find(r => r.s.id === id)
        if (row === undefined || view.launching.includes(id)) continue
        const why = blockedWhy(row)
        if (why !== '') d.toast(why)
        else ready.push(row)
      }
      if (ready.length === 0) return
      const root = await d.repoRoot()
      const specs: LaunchSpec[] = []
      for (const row of ready) {
        const mode = resumeModeOf(row, view.modes)
        if (mode === 'resume' && row.handover !== null && d.source === 'tm') await recordResumedFrom(d.store, row.s.id, row.handover.id)
        specs.push(launchSpecOf(row, mode, view.compact, root))
      }
      const launched = ready.map(r => r.s.id)
      await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, launching: [...v.launching, ...launched] }))
      try {
        const out = await d.launch(specs)
        if (out.opened === specs.length) {
          const at = (await d.now()) / 1000
          await d.write.view(v => ({
            ...v,
            opened: { ...v.opened, ...Object.fromEntries(launched.map(id => [id, at])) },
            selected: { ...v.selected, ...Object.fromEntries(launched.map(id => [id, false])) },
          }))
        }
      } catch (error) {
        d.toast(`taskmaster-mods: could not open the sessions (${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)})`)
      } finally {
        await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, launching: v.launching.filter(id => !launched.includes(id)) }))
      }
    },
    /** `c`: the command that reopens it, to paste in a new terminal. */
    copy: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      const row = await rowById(id)
      if (row === undefined) return
      const text = copyTextOf(row, resumeModeOf(row, (await d.read.view()).modes), await d.repoRoot())
      d.toast((await d.copy(text)) ? `Copied: ${text}` : `Clipboard unavailable: ${text}`)
    },
    /** `i` on a card: shows its brief (asking for one the first time), or hides it. */
    toggleBrief: async (id: string): Promise<void> => {
      const view = await d.read.view()
      if (view.briefs[id] !== undefined) {
        await d.write.view(v => ({ ...v, briefs: Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v.briefs).filter(([k]) => k !== id)) }))
        return
      }
      const row = await rowById(id)
      if (row !== undefined && !view.briefing.includes(id)) await runBrief(row)
    },
    /** `g`: a brief for each ticked session that has none, one after another. */
    brief: async (ids: readonly string[]): Promise<void> => {
      if (ids.length === 0) {
        d.toast('Nothing ticked to brief')
        return
      }
      const rows = await rowsNow()
      for (const id of ids) {
        const view = await d.read.view()
        const row = rows.find(r => r.s.id === id)
        if (row !== undefined && view.briefs[id] === undefined && !view.briefing.includes(id)) await runBrief(row)
      }
    },
  }
}
```

### Task 5.6: Drawing the restore pane

**Files:**
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/draw.tsx`
- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-draw.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the Stage 1 kit and strip; the model (Tasks 5.2–5.5); `demoRestore` and `demoPhases` in tests.
- Produces: `restorePaneTree(k, v, on, width)`, `RestorePaneView = { data, view, rows, strip, now, source }`, and `RestoreHandlers`.

Layout (spec §10): the `◆ TASKMASTER · RESTORE` panel, with its health signal (`tm ok`, `tm offline`, `scan failed` or `demo`) at the top right. Inside it come a toolbar (`last N days`, the pager, `z ◐ compact`, `u rescan`) and the phase strip. Under the strip go the page header, the status badges, the selection bar (`b Resume N`, `g Brief N`, then all · unfinished · none over this page), the busy line, and the cards on the raised ground. A card's row is its box, status badge, bold title and ctx (warning past 400k, critical past 700k). Only the focused card opens: where and when it closed, its task's status badge, id and phase name, the `Next` / `Working on` / `Last reply` line, its brief, and `via [m ◐ fresh from H | ○ --resume] [r resume] [i brief] [c copy]`. Then come "Running now", and a footer with `j` / `k` and the help line. The draw has no rails, no motion on hover and no shadows: hovers change colour only, and the test checks it.

```ts
// User intent: pin the restore pane's drawing (spec §10) on the demo sessions — the burst page unfinished first, only the
// focused card open with its keys, the fresh / --resume choice, the selection bar's counts, Running now, the failure lines
// — and the user's rules: every key one letter or digit, none twice, hovers colour-only.
import { describe, expect, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import type { TmRestoreData, TmRestoreView } from '../types'
import { DEMO_BRIEF, demoPhases, demoRestore } from '../hooks/demo'
import { HOTKEY, type StripView } from '../hooks/kit'
import { FRESH_PHASE_VIEW, withCounts } from '../hooks/phases'
import { type RestoreHandlers, restorePaneTree } from '../hooks/restore/draw'
import { FRESH_RESTORE_VIEW, paneRows, phaseCounts } from '../hooks/restore/model'
import { byKey, hoversOf, testKit, textsOf, walk } from './fixtures/kit-ui'

const NOW_MS = Date.UTC(2026, 9, 10, 17, 31)
const DEMO = demoRestore(NOW_MS)
const DATA: TmRestoreData = { scan: DEMO.scan, refs: DEMO.refs, error: '', days: 4 }
const noop = (): void => undefined
const ON: RestoreHandlers = {
  strip: { choose: noop, toggleDone: noop, overlay: noop },
  turnPage: noop,
  rescan: noop,
  toggleCompact: noop,
  focus: noop,
  toggle: noop,
  select: noop,
  toggleMode: noop,
  resume: noop,
  toggleBrief: noop,
  brief: noop,
  copy: noop,
}
const STYLE_ONLY = new Set(['backgroundColor', 'borderColor', 'bold', 'color', 'dimColor'])

function draw(over: { data?: TmRestoreData; view?: Partial<TmRestoreView>; source?: 'tm' | 'demo'; surface?: 'terminal' | 'desktop' } = {}) {
  const data = over.data ?? DATA
  const rows = paneRows(data, 'me')
  const strip: StripView = {
    pane: 'restore',
    view: { ...FRESH_PHASE_VIEW, phases: withCounts(demoPhases('restore'), phaseCounts(rows)), loaded: true },
    allCount: rows.filter(r => !r.s.running).length,
    focused: true,
    digits: true,
    empty: 'No sessions',
  }
  return restorePaneTree(
    testKit(over.surface ?? 'terminal'),
    { data, view: { ...FRESH_RESTORE_VIEW, ...over.view }, rows, strip, now: NOW_MS / 1000, source: over.source ?? 'tm' },
    ON,
    110,
  )
}
const cardIds = (tree: unknown): string[] =>
  walk(tree)
    .map(e => e.props?.key)
    .filter((key): key is string => typeof key === 'string' && key.startsWith('card:'))
const button = (tree: unknown, key: string) => walk(tree).find(e => e.type === 'Button' && e.props?.key === key)

describe('the burst page', () => {
  test('lists the closing burst and what closed after it, unfinished first; the first card is open', () => {
    const tree = draw()
    expect(cardIds(tree)).toEqual(['card:demo-audit', 'card:demo-late', 'card:demo-docs', 'card:demo-u5', 'card:demo-counter'])
    expect(textsOf(tree).some(x => x.startsWith('4 closed together · today') && x.endsWith('20m ago · 1 closed since'))).toBe(true)
    expect(textsOf(tree)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([' 1 INTERRUPTED ', ' 2 WIP ', ' 1 HANDED OFF ', ' 1 DONE ']))
    expect(button(tree, 'resume:demo-audit')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'r', label: 'resume' })
    expect(button(tree, 'resume:demo-docs')).toBeUndefined()
    expect(button(tree, 'sel:demo-audit')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 's', label: '■' })
    expect(button(tree, 'sel:demo-counter')?.props).toMatchObject({ label: '□' })
    expect(button(tree, 'sel:demo-counter')?.props?.hotkey).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('the selection bar counts the default ticks; compact names itself on the resume action', () => {
    expect(button(draw(), 'restore-resume-all')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'b', label: 'Resume 4' })
    expect(button(draw(), 'restore-brief-all')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'g', label: 'Brief 4' })
    const compact = draw({ view: { compact: true, selected: { 'demo-late': false } } })
    expect(button(compact, 'restore-resume-all')?.props?.label).toBe('Resume + compact 3')
    expect(button(compact, 'restore-compact')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'z', label: '◐ compact' })
    expect(button(draw({ view: { selected: Object.fromEntries(cardIds(draw()).map(id => [id.slice(5), false])) } }), 'restore-resume-all')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
  })

  test('a heavy handed-off session offers fresh from its handover; one without a handover only --resume', () => {
    const u5 = draw({ view: { focus: 'demo-u5' } })
    expect(button(u5, 'mode:demo-u5')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'm', label: '◐ fresh from 2026-10-05-shipped-unified-…' })
    expect(textsOf(u5)).toContain('  Live check, then sign off in the review queue')
    const docs = draw({ view: { focus: 'demo-docs' } })
    expect(button(docs, 'mode:demo-docs')?.props).toMatchObject({ label: '○ --resume', dimColor: true })
    expect(textsOf(docs)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([' TODO ', 'docs-012 · Release 2.0']))
  })

  test('the context figure takes the warning and critical tones past 400k and 700k', () => {
    const texts = walk(draw()).filter(e => e.type === 'Text')
    const ctx = (label: string) => texts.find(e => (e.children ?? []).includes(label))?.props?.color
    expect(ctx('712k ctx')).toBe('#d14343')
    expect(ctx('431k ctx')).toBe('#c4881d')
    expect(ctx('95k ctx')).toBe('#a09c95')
  })

  test('a brief shows under the card; briefing says so and dims b', () => {
    const tree = draw({ view: { focus: 'demo-u5', briefs: { 'demo-u5': DEMO_BRIEF } } })
    expect(textsOf(tree)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining(['State', 'Next', '  Wire the compaction call behind the fork and run the guard tests.']))
    expect(button(tree, 'brief:demo-u5')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'i', label: 'hide brief' })
    const busy = draw({ view: { focus: 'demo-u5', briefing: ['demo-u5'] } })
    expect(textsOf(busy)).toContain('Briefing…')
    expect(button(busy, 'brief:demo-u5')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
  })

  test('Running now lists the open session for orientation, never as a card', () => {
    const tree = draw()
    expect(textsOf(tree)).toContain('Running now · 1 · open in other terminals')
    expect(byKey(tree, 'run:demo-live')).toBeDefined()
    expect(cardIds(tree)).not.toContain('card:demo-live')
  })
})

describe('older pages, failures, keys', () => {
  test('page 2 is older sessions newest first; a gone folder dims its box and resume', () => {
    const tree = draw({ view: { page: 1, focus: 'demo-old-2' } })
    expect(cardIds(tree)).toEqual(['card:demo-old-1', 'card:demo-old-2'])
    expect(textsOf(tree)).toContain('folder missing')
    expect(button(tree, 'resume:demo-old-2')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
    expect(button(tree, 'sel:demo-old-2')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
    expect(button(tree, 'page-prev')?.props?.dimColor).toBeUndefined()
  })

  test('a failed scan says why; tm not reached says statuses read ?; demo says demo', () => {
    const failed = draw({ data: { ...DATA, error: 'Scan failed: no Python found (python, python3, py)' } })
    expect(textsOf(failed)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([' Scan failed', '  no Python found (python, python3, py)', ' scan failed']))
    expect(cardIds(failed)).toEqual([])
    const offline = draw({ data: { ...DATA, refs: null } })
    expect(textsOf(offline)).toEqual(expect.arrayContaining([' tm offline', '  statuses read ? · resume still works']))
    expect(textsOf(draw({ source: 'demo' }))).toContain(' demo')
    expect(textsOf(draw({ data: { ...DATA, scan: null } }))).toContain('No scan yet: u scans this repo’s sessions')
  })

  test('j and k move between cards and dim at the ends', () => {
    expect(button(draw(), 'restore-up')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'k', dimColor: true })
    expect(button(draw(), 'restore-down')?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'j' })
    expect(button(draw({ view: { focus: 'demo-counter' } }), 'restore-down')?.props?.dimColor).toBe(true)
  })

  test('every hotkey is one letter or digit and none is taken twice; hovers change colour only, on both surfaces', () => {
    for (const surface of ['terminal', 'desktop'] as const) {
      const tree = draw({ surface, view: { focus: 'demo-u5' } })
      const keys = walk(tree).flatMap(e => (e.type === 'Button' && typeof e.props?.hotkey === 'string' ? [e.props.hotkey] : []))
      expect(keys.every(key => HOTKEY.test(key))).toBe(true)
      expect(keys.length).toBe(new Set(keys).size)
      expect(new Set(keys.filter(key => !/\d/.test(key)))).toEqual(new Set(['h', 'l', 'f', 'p', 'n', 'z', 'u', 'b', 'g', 's', 'm', 'r', 'i', 'c', 'j', 'k']))
      expect(keys.filter(key => /\d/.test(key))).toEqual(['0', '1', '2', '3'])
      expect(hoversOf(tree).every(h => Object.keys(h).every(prop => STYLE_ONLY.has(prop)))).toBe(true)
    }
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/draw.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: the restore pane (spec §10) drawn with the kit — the closing burst's sessions as stepped cards, unfinished
// first, each with its tm status, bound task and phase, a fresh / --resume choice and resume, brief and copy keycaps — so a
// burst of closed sessions comes back in one press. JSX compiles to h(...): never name a variable `h` in this file.
import type { RenderNode } from 'claude-code'

import type { TmRestoreData, TmRestoreView } from '../../types'
import {
  badge,
  type BulkAction,
  card,
  checkbox,
  type Ghost,
  helpFooter,
  keycap,
  type Kit,
  panel,
  panelTitle,
  pager,
  phaseEmpty,
  phaseSection,
  row,
  selectionBar,
  signal,
  type StripHandlers,
  type StripView,
  toolbar,
} from '../kit'
import { phaseName, truncate } from '../model'
import type { RrSignalKind } from '../rr'
import {
  blockedWhy,
  burstsOf,
  ctxTone,
  detailOf,
  formatTokens,
  isTicked,
  pageHeader,
  pageRows,
  pagesOf,
  type RestoreRow,
  type RestoreStatus,
  resumeModeOf,
  runningRows,
  STATUS_LABEL,
  STATUS_ORDER,
  STATUS_TONE,
  TASK_TONE,
  when,
} from './model'

export type RestorePaneView = {
  readonly data: TmRestoreData
  readonly view: TmRestoreView
  /** The rows the pane lists (paneRows: this session left out), computed once with the strip's counts. */
  readonly rows: readonly RestoreRow[]
  readonly strip: StripView
  /** Epoch seconds. */
  readonly now: number
  readonly source: 'tm' | 'demo'
}

export type RestoreHandlers = {
  readonly strip: StripHandlers
  readonly turnPage: (page: number) => void
  readonly rescan: () => void
  readonly toggleCompact: () => void
  readonly focus: (id: string) => void
  readonly toggle: (id: string) => void
  readonly select: (changes: Readonly<Record<string, boolean>>) => void
  readonly toggleMode: (id: string) => void
  readonly resume: (ids: readonly string[]) => void
  readonly toggleBrief: (id: string) => void
  readonly brief: (ids: readonly string[]) => void
  readonly copy: (id: string) => void
}

/** The panel's top-right signal: demo, a failed scan, tm not reached (statuses `?`), or tm ok. */
function healthOf(v: RestorePaneView): { readonly kind: RrSignalKind; readonly word: string } {
  if (v.source === 'demo') return { kind: 'info', word: 'demo' }
  if (v.data.error !== '') return { kind: 'critical', word: 'scan failed' }
  if (v.data.scan !== null && v.data.refs === null && v.view.busy === '') return { kind: 'critical', word: 'tm offline' }
  return { kind: 'success', word: 'tm ok' }
}

const shortId = (id: string): string => truncate(id, 28)

/** A card's first row: its box (`s` while focused), the status badge, the title, the context it would reopen. */
function headRow(k: Kit, r: RestoreRow, a: { readonly ticked: boolean; readonly focused: boolean }, on: RestoreHandlers, width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const why = blockedWhy(r)
  const box = checkbox(k, {
    id: `sel:${r.s.id}`,
    selected: a.ticked,
    on: 'overlay',
    ...(a.focused ? { key: 's' } : {}),
    ...(why === '' ? {} : { dim: why }),
    onPress: () => on.toggle(r.s.id),
  })
  const status = badge(k, STATUS_LABEL[r.status], STATUS_TONE[r.status], { on: 'overlay' })
  const tone = ctxTone(r.s.ctx)
  const ctx = r.s.ctx > 0 ? `${formatTokens(r.s.ctx)} ctx` : ''
  const room = Math.max(6, width - box.width - status.width - ctx.length - 6)
  return (
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} alignItems="flex-start">
      {box.el}
      {status.el}
      <Box flexGrow={1} flexShrink={1}>
        <Text color={r.status === 'done' ? k.t.fg.subtle : k.t.fg.bold} bold>
          {truncate(r.s.title, room)}
        </Text>
      </Box>
      {ctx === '' ? null : <Text color={tone === null ? k.t.fg.subtle : k.t.tone[tone]}>{ctx}</Text>}
    </Box>
  )
}

/** The focused card's body: where and when it closed with its task, what was going on, its brief, and its keys. */
function bodyOf(k: Kit, r: RestoreRow, v: RestorePaneView, on: RestoreHandlers, width: number): RenderNode[] {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const id = r.s.id
  const where = `closed ${when(r.s.closedEpoch, v.now)} · ${r.s.branch || 'no branch'}`
  const phase = r.task === null ? undefined : v.strip.view.phases.find(p => p.id === r.task?.phase)
  const phaseWord = r.task === null ? '' : phase !== undefined ? phaseName(phase) : r.task.phase || 'no phase'
  const task: RenderNode[] =
    r.task !== null
      ? [
          badge(k, r.task.status, TASK_TONE[r.task.status] ?? 'signature', { strength: 12, on: 'overlay' }).el,
          <Text color={t.fg.default}>{truncate(`${r.task.id} · ${phaseWord}`, Math.max(8, width - where.length - r.task.status.length - 4))}</Text>,
        ]
      : r.boundId !== ''
        ? [<Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{`· ${r.boundId}`}</Text>]
        : []
  const detail = detailOf(r)
  const mode = resumeModeOf(r, v.view.modes)
  const why = blockedWhy(r)
  const launching = v.view.launching.includes(id)
  const briefing = v.view.briefing.includes(id)
  const brief = v.view.briefs[id]
  const openedAt = v.view.opened[id]
  const via = keycap(k, {
    id: `mode:${id}`,
    key: 'm',
    label: mode === 'fresh' && r.handover !== null ? `◐ fresh from ${shortId(r.handover.id)}` : '○ --resume',
    selected: mode === 'fresh',
    on: 'overlay',
    ...(r.handover === null ? { dim: `${r.s.title} wrote no handover: it reopens with --resume` } : {}),
    onPress: () => on.toggleMode(id),
  })
  const resume = keycap(k, {
    id: `resume:${id}`,
    key: 'r',
    label: launching ? 'opening…' : 'resume',
    tone: 'success',
    on: 'overlay',
    ...(why !== '' ? { dim: why } : launching ? { dim: `${r.s.title} is opening` } : {}),
    onPress: () => on.resume([id]),
  })
  const briefCap = keycap(k, {
    id: `brief:${id}`,
    key: 'i',
    label: brief === undefined ? 'brief' : 'hide brief',
    on: 'overlay',
    ...(briefing ? { dim: `Briefing ${r.s.title}…` } : {}),
    onPress: () => on.toggleBrief(id),
  })
  const copy = keycap(k, { id: `copy:${id}`, key: 'c', label: 'copy', on: 'overlay', onPress: () => on.copy(id) })
  const marks = [
    ...(openedAt === undefined ? [] : [<Text color={t.tone.info}>{`opened ${when(openedAt, v.now)}`}</Text>]),
    ...(r.s.resumedAfterClose && !r.s.running ? [<Text color={t.fg.subtle}>resumed since</Text>] : []),
    ...(r.s.cwdExists ? [] : [<Text color={t.tone.critical}>folder missing</Text>]),
  ]
  return [
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} alignItems="flex-start">
      <Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{where}</Text>
      {task}
    </Box>,
    <Text wrap="truncate-end">
      <Text color={t.fg.subtle} bold>
        {detail.label}
      </Text>
      <Text color={t.fg.default}>{`  ${truncate(detail.text, Math.max(8, width - detail.label.length - 4))}`}</Text>
    </Text>,
    ...(briefing ? [<Text color={t.tone.info}>Briefing…</Text>] : []),
    ...(brief === undefined
      ? []
      : brief.split('\n').map(line => {
          const [head, ...rest] = line.split(': ')
          return rest.length === 0 ? (
            <Text color={t.fg.default}>{line}</Text>
          ) : (
            <Text wrap="wrap">
              <Text color={t.signatureText} bold>
                {head}
              </Text>
              <Text color={t.fg.default}>{`  ${rest.join(': ')}`}</Text>
            </Text>
          )
        })),
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={2} alignItems="flex-start" flexWrap="wrap">
      <Text color={t.fg.subtle}>via</Text>
      {via.el}
      {resume.el}
      {briefCap.el}
      {copy.el}
      {marks}
    </Box>,
  ]
}

/** A running session: not closed, so not restorable — listed for orientation only. */
function runningRow(k: Kit, r: RestoreRow, width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const meta = [r.s.branch || 'no branch', r.boundId, r.s.ctx > 0 ? `${formatTokens(r.s.ctx)} ctx` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')
  const tag = badge(k, 'running', 'signature', { strength: 12, on: 'raised' })
  return (
    <Box key={`run:${r.s.id}`} flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} paddingX={2} alignItems="flex-start">
      {tag.el}
      <Box flexGrow={1} flexShrink={1}>
        <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{truncate(r.s.title, Math.max(6, width - tag.width - meta.length - 8))}</Text>
      </Box>
      <Text color={k.t.fg.subtle}>{meta}</Text>
    </Box>
  )
}

/** The whole pane inside its panel (`◆ TASKMASTER · RESTORE`), `width` cells across. */
export function restorePaneTree(k: Kit, v: RestorePaneView, on: RestoreHandlers, width: number): RenderNode {
  const { Box, Text } = k.ui
  const { t } = k
  const inner = Math.max(20, width - 4)
  const rows = v.rows
  const pages = pagesOf(rows, burstsOf(rows), v.strip.view.choice)
  const at = Math.min(Math.max(0, v.view.page), Math.max(0, pages.length - 1))
  const page = pages[at]
  const list = pageRows(rows, page)
  const focusId = list.some(r => r.s.id === v.view.focus) ? v.view.focus : (list[0]?.s.id ?? '')
  const ticked = list.filter(r => isTicked(r, v.view.selected, v.view.opened))
  const tickedIds = ticked.map(r => r.s.id)
  const running = runningRows(rows)

  const slots = [
    pager(k, { page: at, pages: pages.length, onTurn: on.turnPage, on: 'raised' }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'restore-compact', key: 'z', label: `${v.view.compact ? '◐' : '○'} compact`, selected: v.view.compact, on: 'raised', onPress: on.toggleCompact }),
    keycap(k, { id: 'restore-rescan', key: 'u', label: 'rescan', on: 'raised', onPress: on.rescan }),
  ]
  const top = toolbar(k, { title: `last ${v.data.days} ${v.data.days === 1 ? 'day' : 'days'}`, slots, width: inner })

  const body: RenderNode[] = []
  if (v.data.error !== '') {
    body.push(signal(k, 'critical', 'Scan failed', v.data.error.replace(/^Scan failed: /, '')).el)
    body.push(<Text color={t.fg.subtle}>u scans again</Text>)
  } else if (v.data.scan === null) {
    body.push(<Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{v.view.busy !== '' ? v.view.busy : 'No scan yet: u scans this repo’s sessions'}</Text>)
  } else {
    if (v.source === 'tm' && v.data.refs === null && v.view.busy === '') {
      body.push(signal(k, 'critical', 'tm offline', 'statuses read ? · resume still works').el)
    }
    if (page !== undefined) body.push(<Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{truncate(pageHeader(page, v.now), inner)}</Text>)
    const counts = STATUS_ORDER.map(s => [s, list.filter(r => r.status === s).length] as const).filter(([, n]) => n > 0)
    if (counts.length > 0) body.push(row(k, counts.map(([s, n]) => badge(k, `${n} ${STATUS_LABEL[s as RestoreStatus]}`, STATUS_TONE[s], { on: 'raised' }))).el)
    const actions: BulkAction[] = [
      { id: 'restore-resume-all', key: 'b', verb: v.view.compact ? 'Resume + compact' : 'Resume', count: tickedIds.length, tone: 'success', onPress: () => on.resume(tickedIds) },
      { id: 'restore-brief-all', key: 'g', verb: 'Brief', count: tickedIds.length, onPress: () => on.brief(tickedIds) },
    ]
    const pick = (keep: (r: RestoreRow) => boolean) => () =>
      on.select(Object.fromEntries(list.map(r => [r.s.id, blockedWhy(r) === '' && keep(r)])))
    const ghosts: Ghost[] = [
      { id: 'restore-all', label: 'all', onPress: pick(() => true) },
      { id: 'restore-unfinished', label: 'unfinished', onPress: pick(r => r.status !== 'done') },
      { id: 'restore-none', label: 'none', onPress: pick(() => false) },
    ]
    body.push(selectionBar(k, { actions, ghosts, width: inner, on: 'raised' }))
    if (v.view.busy !== '') body.push(<Text color={t.tone.info}>{v.view.busy}</Text>)
    if (list.length === 0) body.push(phaseEmpty(k, { ...v.strip, on: 'raised' }, on.strip))
    for (const r of list) {
      const focused = r.s.id === focusId
      const isTick = ticked.includes(r)
      body.push(card(k, { id: r.s.id, selected: isTick, focused, on: 'raised' }, headRow(k, r, { ticked: isTick, focused }, on, inner - 4), bodyOf(k, r, v, on, inner - 4)))
    }
    if (running.length > 0) {
      body.push(<Text color={t.fg.subtle}>{`Running now · ${running.length} · open in other terminals`}</Text>)
      for (const r of running) body.push(runningRow(k, r, inner))
    }
  }

  const index = list.findIndex(r => r.s.id === focusId)
  const down = keycap(k, {
    id: 'restore-down',
    key: 'j',
    label: '↓',
    on: 'raised',
    ...(index < 0 || index >= list.length - 1 ? { dim: 'Already on the last card' } : {}),
    onPress: () => on.focus(list[index + 1]?.s.id ?? focusId),
  })
  const up = keycap(k, {
    id: 'restore-up',
    key: 'k',
    label: '↑',
    on: 'raised',
    ...(index <= 0 ? { dim: 'Already on the first card' } : {}),
    onPress: () => on.focus(list[index - 1]?.s.id ?? focusId),
  })
  const footer = (
    <Box flexDirection="row" columnGap={1} alignItems="flex-start">
      {down.el}
      {up.el}
      {helpFooter(k, ['s tick', 'r resume', 'm fresh/--resume', 'i brief', 'c copy', 'b resume ticked', 'g brief ticked', 'h l phase', 'p n page'], Math.max(10, inner - down.width - up.width - 2))}
    </Box>
  )
  return panel(k, { title: panelTitle('restore'), health: healthOf(v), width }, [
    top,
    ...phaseSection(k, { ...v.strip, on: 'raised' }, on.strip, inner, body),
    footer,
  ])
}
```

- Create: `mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-pane.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: everything above; `TmFlows` through `RestoreLink` (`launch`, `loadPhases`, `choosePhase`, `toggleDone`, `setOverlay`, Stage 3's `refresh`); `$.process.run`, `$.env.get` (`USERPROFILE`, `HOME`, `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, `TEMP`, `TMPDIR`), `$.plugin.root` and `$.model.complete`.
- Produces: `onRestore`, `RestoreLink`, `refreshRestore`, `BRIEF_MODEL`; `TmFlows.refresh('restore')`; the `/tm-restore [days]` command (default 4 days); the `tm-restore` pane; and the `restorePending` and `sessionBurst` atoms, written after every scan. The interactive `session.start` scan runs off the start with `$.clock.after(0)`, for 3 days, and asks tm three more times while it connects.

A test's own `on('process.run', …)` beside `worldOf`'s did not take effect in the probe: the world's hook answered. So the world takes a whole answer through `world.answer`, which a test sets, and the environment through Stage 2's `world.env`. The pane test answers both queries: Stage 1's restore `phaseSql` and the refs query.

`mods/taskmaster-mods/tests/restore-pane.test.ts`:

```ts
// User intent: the restore pane as a person uses it (spec §10): /tm-restore scans this repo, tm names each session's status,
// the pane draws on the terminal and the desktop, its keys tick, move, flip modes and page, a --resume is recorded before it
// launches, Brief asks the configured model — and the session-start scan feeds the band's restore keycap (spec §10.4).
import { describe, expect, mock, test } from 'claude-code/testing'

import { command, pane, PLUGIN, SESSION } from './fixtures/inputs'
import { refsReply, scanOf, session } from './fixtures/restore'
import { RR_STUB } from './fixtures/rr-stub'
import { stateOf, type World, worldOf } from './fixtures/world'

const DEMO = { options: { source: 'demo' }, plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const TM = { plugins: [RR_STUB] }
const RESTORE_PANE = { ...pane('tm-restore'), props: { ...pane('tm-restore').props, bodyColumns: 120 } }
const ENV = { USERPROFILE: 'C:\\Users\\me', TEMP: 'C:\\tmp' }
const HO = { id: 'h-1', path: 'C:\\work\\proj\\.taskmaster\\handovers\\h-1.md', tldr: 'Half done', next: 'Finish the rows' }

/** Three sessions closed together a quarter hour before the mock clock's now, plus an older one. */
function tmWorld(world: World, nowS: number): void {
  const scan = scanOf([
    session('u5', { closedEpoch: nowS - 900, title: 'U5 brief approval', handovers: [HO], ctx: 120_000, taskIds: ['t-1'], cwd: world.root }),
    session('audit', { closedEpoch: nowS - 1000, title: 'Audit supersede', interrupted: true, taskIds: ['t-2'], cwd: world.root }),
    session('counter', { closedEpoch: nowS - 1100, title: 'Row counter', endedSession: true, cwd: world.root }),
    session('old', { closedEpoch: nowS - 90_000, title: 'Old work', cwd: world.root }),
  ])
  world.answer = argv =>
    String(argv[2] ?? '').endsWith('scan.py')
      ? argv[3] === 'digest'
        ? { exitCode: 0, stdout: JSON.stringify({ first: 'USER: start', tail: 'ASSISTANT: stopped' }) }
        : { exitCode: 0, stdout: JSON.stringify(scan) }
      : null
  world.mcp = (tool, args) =>
    tool !== 'backlog_query'
      ? 'offline'
      : String(args.sql).includes("p.kind='phase'")
        ? { text: 'phase                                   name\n["p1","active",1,0,0,"2026-10-01"]    Patch 1 — Restore\n1 row' }
        : { text: refsReply(['["t","t-1","in-review","full","p1"]  Sign off', '["t","t-2","in-progress","full","p1"]  Supersede', '["h","h-1","open"]']) }
}

describe('/tm-restore in demo mode', () => {
  test('opens the pane, names the burst, and draws on the terminal and the desktop without running the scanner', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    expect(await $.command.run(command('tm-restore'))).toEqual({ text: 'Restore opened: 4 sessions closed together, 3 unfinished.' })
    expect(world.opened).toEqual(['tm-restore'])
    expect(world.runs.some(argv => argv.some(a => a.endsWith('scan.py')))).toBe(false)
    for (const surface of ['terminal', 'desktop'] as const) {
      const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...RESTORE_PANE, surface })
      expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'restore-resume-all' }))?.props).toMatchObject({ hotkey: 'b', label: 'Resume 4' })
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'resume:demo-audit' })).toBeDefined()
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: /4 closed together/ })).toBeDefined()
      expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: 'Running now · 1 · open in other terminals' })).toBeDefined()
      await ui.unmount()
    }
  })

  test('the pane draws the demo sessions even before the command ran', DEMO, async ($, on) => {
    worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...RESTORE_PANE })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:demo-audit' })).toBeDefined()
  })
})

describe('/tm-restore over tm', () => {
  test('scans this repo isolated, resolves the ids with one backlog_query, and says what it found', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on, { now: 1_791_700_000_000 })
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.env = ENV
    tmWorld(world, 1_791_700_000)
    expect(await $.command.run({ ...command('tm-restore'), args: '2' })).toEqual({ text: 'Restore opened: 3 sessions closed together, 2 unfinished.' })
    const scanRun = world.runs.find(argv => argv[3] === 'scan')
    expect(scanRun?.slice(0, 2)).toEqual(['python', '-I'])
    expect(scanRun?.slice(3)).toEqual(['scan', '--config-dir', 'C:\\Users\\me/.claude', '--root', world.root, '--days', '2', '--cache', 'C:\\tmp/tm-restore-scan-cache.json'])
    expect(world.calls.filter(c => c.tool === 'backlog_query')).toHaveLength(1)
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...RESTORE_PANE })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' 1 INTERRUPTED ' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: ' 1 HANDED OFF ' })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'restore-resume-all' }))?.props?.label).toBe('Resume 2')
  })

  test('keys: s ticks, j moves the open card, m flips the mode, z compacts, u rescans, n pages', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on, { now: 1_791_700_000_000 })
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.env = ENV
    tmWorld(world, 1_791_700_000)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-restore'))
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...RESTORE_PANE })
    const label = async (key: string) => (await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key }))?.props?.label
    await ui.press({ key: 'sel:audit' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await label('restore-resume-all')).toBe('Resume 1')
    await ui.press({ key: 'restore-down' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'resume:u5' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await label('mode:u5')).toBe('○ --resume')
    await ui.press({ key: 'mode:u5' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await label('mode:u5')).toBe('◐ fresh from h-1')
    await ui.press({ key: 'restore-compact' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await label('restore-compact')).toBe('◐ compact')
    // u goes through the shared TmFlows.refresh('restore'): one more scan of the same window.
    const scans = () => world.runs.filter(argv => argv[3] === 'scan').length
    const before = scans()
    await ui.press({ key: 'restore-rescan' })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(scans()).toBe(before + 1)
    await ui.press({ key: 'page-next' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:old' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:u5' })).toBeUndefined()
    // A phase tab narrows to that phase's sessions and starts at its page 1.
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'phase-tab:p1' }))?.props?.label).toBe('Patch 1 · active 2')
    await ui.press({ key: 'phase-tab:p1' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:u5' })).toBeDefined()
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'sel:counter' })).toBeUndefined()
    expect(world.store.get(`phase:restore:${world.root}`)).toEqual({ phase: 'p1' })
  })

  test('r on a --resume session records resumed-from before the launcher opens it', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on, { now: 1_791_700_000_000 })
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.env = ENV
    tmWorld(world, 1_791_700_000)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-restore'))
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...RESTORE_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'restore-down' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    await ui.press({ key: 'resume:u5' })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(world.store.get('resumed-from:u5')).toBe('h-1')
  })

  test('b asks the configured brief model and shows State / Next under the card', { ...TM, options: { briefModel: 'claude-sonnet-5-5' } }, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on, { now: 1_791_700_000_000 })
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    world.env = ENV
    tmWorld(world, 1_791_700_000)
    const models: string[] = []
    on('model.complete', ($$, e) => {
      models.push(e.model)
      return { value: { isAnswered: true, text: '<state>Stopped mid-port.</state><next>Finish the port.</next><blocker>none</blocker>', usage: {} } } as never
    })
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    await $.command.run(command('tm-restore'))
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...RESTORE_PANE })
    await ui.press({ key: 'brief:audit' })
    await clock.settle()
    await ui.redraw()
    expect(models).toEqual(['claude-sonnet-5-5'])
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: '  Finish the port.' })).toBeDefined()
    expect((await ui.find({ type: 'Button', key: 'brief:audit' }))?.props?.label).toBe('hide brief')
  })

  test('a scanner that cannot run says so in the pane and the answer', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const world = worldOf(on, mock.clock(on))
    world.env = ENV
    world.answer = argv => (String(argv[2] ?? '').endsWith('scan.py') ? { exitCode: 1, stdout: '', stderr: 'boom\nOSError: no projects folder' } : null)
    expect((await $.command.run(command('tm-restore'))).text).toBe('Scan failed: OSError: no projects folder')
    const ui = await $.ui.mount({ plugin: PLUGIN, ...RESTORE_PANE })
    expect(await ui.find({ type: 'Text', text: '  OSError: no projects folder' })).toBeDefined()
  })
})

describe('the session-start scan (spec §10.4)', () => {
  test('an interactive start scans 3 days off the start and writes the band keycap and the burst line', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on, { now: 1_791_700_000_000 })
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    const state = stateOf(on)
    world.env = ENV
    tmWorld(world, 1_791_700_000)
    await $.session.start(SESSION)
    expect(world.runs.some(argv => argv[3] === 'scan')).toBe(false)
    await clock.settle()
    expect(world.runs.find(argv => argv[3] === 'scan')).toContain('3')
    expect(state.get('taskmaster-mods.restorePending')).toEqual({ epoch: 1_791_700_000 - 900, unfinished: 2 })
    expect(state.get('taskmaster-mods.sessionBurst')).toBeNull()
  })

  test('a -p run scans nothing', TM, async ($, on) => {
    const clock = mock.clock(on)
    const world = worldOf(on, clock)
    await $.session.start({ ...SESSION, surface: null, isInteractive: false })
    await clock.settle()
    expect(world.runs.some(argv => argv[3] === 'scan')).toBe(false)
  })
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement and wire**

`mods/taskmaster-mods/hooks/restore/hooks.tsx`:

```tsx
// User intent: the restore pane's own hooks (spec §10) — /tm-restore, its pane, its keys, and the quiet session-start scan
// that tells the band a burst of sessions closed unfinished — kept out of register.tsx, which lends only its flows (never `$`).
// JSX compiles to h(...): never name a variable `h` in this file.
import { atom, read, update } from 'claude-code'
import type { EngineInterface, On, PluginOptions } from 'claude-code'

import type { TmPhasePane, TmPhaseView, TmRestorePending, TmSessionBurst } from '../../types'
import { demoPhases, demoRestore } from '../demo'
import type { TmFlows } from '../flows'
import { kitOf, type KitRr, type Ui } from '../kit'
import { RESTORE } from '../model'
import { replyText } from '../parse'
import { FRESH_PHASE_VIEWS, withCounts } from '../phases'
import { restorePaneTree } from './draw'
import { createRestoreFlows, type RestoreDeps, type RestoreFlows } from './flows'
import { DEFAULT_DAYS, FRESH_RESTORE_DATA, FRESH_RESTORE_VIEW, paneRows, phaseCounts } from './model'
import type { RestoreHost } from './scan'

/** userConfig `briefModel`'s default (spec §10.6). */
export const BRIEF_MODEL = 'claude-haiku-5-5'

const DATA = atom({ plugin: 'taskmaster-mods', key: 'restoreData' } as const, FRESH_RESTORE_DATA)
const VIEW = atom({ plugin: 'taskmaster-mods', key: 'restoreView' } as const, FRESH_RESTORE_VIEW)
// Written here, read by the band and the Session tab (Stage 2 declares both keys).
const PENDING = atom({ plugin: 'taskmaster-mods', key: 'restorePending' } as const, null as TmRestorePending | null)
const BURST = atom({ plugin: 'taskmaster-mods', key: 'sessionBurst' } as const, null as TmSessionBurst | null)
const PHASE_VIEWS = atom({ plugin: 'taskmaster-mods', key: 'phaseViews' } as const, FRESH_PHASE_VIEWS as Readonly<Record<TmPhasePane, TmPhaseView>>)
const RR_POLARITY = { plugin: 'rr-tui', key: 'polarity' } as const

/** What register.tsx lends: its flows once an acting hook built them (Stage 2's launch, the strip's phase flows). */
export type RestoreLink = { readonly flows: () => TmFlows | null }

// Like register.tsx's `mod`: rebuilt by each register(), the flows built by the first restore hook that acts.
const rs: { source: 'tm' | 'demo'; briefModel: string; link: RestoreLink | null; flows: RestoreFlows | null } = {
  source: 'tm',
  briefModel: BRIEF_MODEL,
  link: null,
  flows: null,
}

function restoreHostOf($: EngineInterface): RestoreHost {
  return {
    run: async (argv, timeoutMs) => {
      const r = await $.process.run(argv, { timeoutMs })
      return { exitCode: r.exitCode, stdout: r.stdout, stderr: r.stderr }
    },
    paths: async () => {
      const home = (await $.env.get('USERPROFILE')) ?? (await $.env.get('HOME')) ?? '.'
      const configDir = (await $.env.get('CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR')) || `${home}/.claude`
      const temp = (await $.env.get('TEMP')) ?? (await $.env.get('TMPDIR')) ?? configDir
      return { configDir, cache: `${temp}/tm-restore-scan-cache.json`, script: `${$.plugin.root}/scanner/scan.py` }
    },
  }
}

function depsOf($: EngineInterface): RestoreDeps {
  return {
    source: rs.source,
    rh: restoreHostOf($),
    tm: {
      call: async (tool, args) => {
        const r = await $.mcp.call('plugin:taskmaster:tm', tool, args)
        const text = r.content.map(block => (block.type === 'text' ? String((block as { text?: unknown }).text ?? '') : '')).join('\n')
        return { text: replyText(text, r.structuredContent), isError: r.isError }
      },
      sleep: (ms, signal) => $.clock.sleep(ms, signal ? { signal } : undefined),
      log: text => $.ui.log(text, { to: 'debug' }),
    },
    store: { get: key => $.store.get(key), set: (key, value) => $.store.set(key, value), delete: key => $.store.delete(key) },
    read: { data: () => read($, DATA), view: () => read($, VIEW) },
    write: {
      data: async change => {
        await update($, DATA, change)
      },
      view: async change => {
        await update($, VIEW, change)
      },
      signals: async (pending, burst) => {
        await update($, PENDING, () => pending)
        await update($, BURST, () => burst)
      },
    },
    now: () => $.clock.now(),
    wait: ms => $.clock.sleep(ms),
    sessionId: () => $.session.id(),
    repoRoot: async () => (await $.session.repo())?.root ?? (await $.session.root()),
    toast: text => $.ui.toast(text),
    openPane: async () => (await $.ui.open({ id: RESTORE, title: 'Restore', focus: true, closeOnEscape: true })).isPlaced,
    loadPhases: () => {
      const main = rs.link?.flows() ?? null
      if (main !== null) void main.loadPhases('restore').catch(() => undefined)
    },
    launch: async specs => {
      const main = rs.link?.flows() ?? null
      if (main === null) {
        $.ui.toast('taskmaster-mods: still starting; press resume again')
        return { opened: 0, copied: null, failed: specs.map(s => s.title) }
      }
      return main.launch(specs)
    },
    copy: async text => (await $.ui.copy({ text })).isCopied,
    complete: async prompt => {
      const r = await $.model.complete({ model: rs.briefModel, prompt, maxTokens: 400, timeoutMs: 90_000 })
      return r.isAnswered ? { ok: true, text: r.text } : { ok: false, reason: r.reason }
    },
  }
}

function flowsOf($: EngineInterface): RestoreFlows {
  rs.flows ??= createRestoreFlows(depsOf($))
  return rs.flows
}

function kitRrOf($: EngineInterface): KitRr {
  return { tokens: () => $.rr.tokens(), badgeProps: a => $.rr.badgeProps(a) }
}

function sayOf($: EngineInterface): (text: string) => void {
  return text => $.ui.toast(text)
}

// Press handlers run after the ui.press hook below built the flows; they read rs.flows then.
const act = (run: (flows: RestoreFlows) => Promise<void>): void => {
  if (rs.flows !== null) void run(rs.flows)
}
const actMain = (run: (flows: TmFlows) => Promise<void>): void => {
  const main = rs.link?.flows() ?? null
  if (main !== null) void run(main)
}

/** `u` on the restore pane (TmFlows.refresh('restore')): rescans the pane's window; a no-op before the flows exist. */
export async function refreshRestore(): Promise<void> {
  if (rs.flows !== null) await rs.flows.rescan()
}

export function onRestore(on: On, options: PluginOptions, link: RestoreLink): void {
  rs.source = options.source === 'demo' ? 'demo' : 'tm'
  rs.briefModel = typeof options.briefModel === 'string' && options.briefModel.trim() !== '' ? options.briefModel.trim() : BRIEF_MODEL
  rs.link = link
  rs.flows = null

  // An interactive start only (a `-p` run restores nothing): the command, then a quiet 3-day scan off the start (§10.4).
  on('session.start', { isInteractive: true }, async ($, e, next) => {
    const started = await next(e)
    try {
      await $.command.register({ name: RESTORE, description: 'Sessions closed together last time, unfinished first: resume them', argumentHint: '[days]' })
    } catch {
      // already registered by an earlier load of this module: commands persist across reloads
    }
    const flows = flowsOf($)
    $.clock.after(0, () => {
      void flows.startScan().catch(() => undefined)
    })
    return started
  })

  on('command.run', { command: RESTORE }, async ($, e) => {
    const days = Number.parseFloat(e.args.trim())
    return { text: await flowsOf($).open(Number.isFinite(days) && days > 0 ? days : DEFAULT_DAYS) }
  })

  on('ui.press', { requestId: RESTORE }, async ($, e, next) => {
    flowsOf($)
    return next(e)
  })

  on('ui.render', { component: 'Pane', requestId: RESTORE }, async ($, e) => {
    const { Box, Text } = $.ui.resolve(e)
    if (e.surface === 'mobile') return <Text>Open the restore pane in the terminal or the desktop app.</Text>
    await $.state.get(RR_POLARITY)
    const now = await $.clock.now()
    const stored = await read($, DATA)
    // Demo draws its sessions before the first scan lands, as the other panes draw their seed (a render writes nothing).
    const data = rs.source === 'demo' && stored.scan === null ? { ...stored, ...demoRestore(now) } : stored
    const rows = paneRows(data, await $.session.id())
    const views = await read($, PHASE_VIEWS)
    const phases = rs.source === 'demo' && !views.restore.loaded ? demoPhases('restore') : views.restore.phases
    const strip = {
      pane: 'restore' as const,
      view: { ...views.restore, phases: withCounts(phases, phaseCounts(rows)) },
      allCount: rows.filter(r => !r.s.running).length,
      focused: e.props.isFocused,
      digits: true,
      empty: 'No sessions',
    }
    const k = await kitOf($.ui.resolve(e) as unknown as Ui, kitRrOf($), e.surface, sayOf($))
    const tree = restorePaneTree(
      k,
      { data, view: await read($, VIEW), rows, strip, now: now / 1000, source: rs.source },
      {
        strip: {
          // A new phase starts at its page 1, as review and handovers do.
          choose: id => {
            actMain(f => f.choosePhase('restore', id))
            act(f => f.turnPage(0))
          },
          toggleDone: () => actMain(f => f.toggleDone('restore')),
          overlay: open => actMain(f => f.setOverlay('restore', open)),
        },
        turnPage: page => act(f => f.turnPage(page)),
        rescan: () => actMain(f => f.refresh('restore')),
        toggleCompact: () => act(f => f.toggleCompact()),
        focus: id => act(f => f.focus(id)),
        toggle: id => act(f => f.toggle(id)),
        select: changes => act(f => f.select(changes)),
        toggleMode: id => act(f => f.toggleMode(id)),
        resume: ids => act(f => f.resume(ids)),
        toggleBrief: id => act(f => f.toggleBrief(id)),
        brief: ids => act(f => f.brief(ids)),
        copy: id => act(f => f.copy(id)),
      },
      e.props.bodyColumns,
    )
    const ground = await $.rr.surfaceProps({ level: 'page' })
    return (
      <Box {...ground} flexGrow={1}>
        {tree}
      </Box>
    )
  })

  // Tab onto a card's box focuses that card (opens it), as j / k do.
  on('ui.focus', { requestId: RESTORE }, async ($, e, next) => {
    const moved = await next(e)
    const element = e.element
    if (element !== undefined && element.startsWith('sel:')) {
      const id = element.slice(4)
      await update($, VIEW, v => (v.focus === id ? v : { ...v, focus: id }))
    }
    return moved
  }).catch(($, e, next) => next(e))
}
```
